# 1. Create Network Endpoint Group
gcloud compute network-endpoint-groups create sqleditorapi-neg-prod \
  --region=us-east4 \
  --network-endpoint-type=serverless \
  --cloud-run-service=sqleditorapi \
  --project=vz-it-pr-voev-voevdo-0
# 2. Create Backend Service
gcloud compute backend-services create sqleditorapi-backend-prod \
  --load-balancing-scheme=INTERNAL_MANAGED \
  --protocol=HTTPS \
  --region=us-east4 \
  --enable-logging \
  --project=vz-it-pr-voev-voevdo-0
# 3. Add Backend to Backend Service
gcloud compute backend-services add-backend sqleditorapi-backend-prod \
  --region=us-east4 \
  --network-endpoint-group=sqleditorapi-neg-prod \
  --network-endpoint-group-region=us-east4 \
  --project=vz-it-pr-voev-voevdo-0
# 4. Create URL Map
gcloud compute url-maps create sqleditorapi-urlmap-prod \
  --default-service=sqleditorapi-backend-prod \
  --region=us-east4 \
  --project=vz-it-pr-voev-voevdo-0
# 4.5, Create SSL
gcloud compute ssl-certificates create sqleditorapi-ssl-prod \
  --certificate=sqleditorapi_verizon_com.pem \
  --private-key=sqleditorapi_verizon_com.key \
  --region=us-east4 \
  --project=vz-it-pr-voev-voevdo-0
# 5. Create HTTPS Proxy (using your SSL certificate)
gcloud compute target-https-proxies create sqleditorapi-https-proxy-prod \
  --ssl-certificates=sqleditorapi-ssl-prod \
  --url-map=sqleditorapi-urlmap-prod \
  --region=us-east4 \
  --project=vz-it-pr-voev-voevdo-0
# 6. Create Forwarding Rule
gcloud compute forwarding-rules create sqleditorapi-fw-rule-prod \
  --load-balancing-scheme=INTERNAL_MANAGED \
  --network=projects/vz-it-pr-exhv-sharedvpc-228020/global/networks/shared-pr-east \
  --subnet=projects/vz-it-pr-exhv-sharedvpc-228020/regions/us-east4/subnetworks/shared-pr-east-green-subnet-2 \
  --target-https-proxy=sqleditorapi-https-proxy-prod \
  --target-https-proxy-region=us-east4 \
  --region=us-east4 \
  --ports=443 \
  --project=vz-it-pr-voev-voevdo-0

Dockerfile

# Use the official lightweight Python image.
# https://hub.docker.com/_/python
FROM python:3.10-slim

# Allow statements and log messages to immediately appear in the logs
ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED True

# Copy local code to the container image.
ENV APP_HOME /app
WORKDIR $APP_HOME

# Copy requirements first (for better layer caching)
COPY requirements.txt ./

# Copy application code
COPY . ./

USER root

# Install system dependencies for pymssql (FreeTDS) and other libs
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
    freetds-dev freetds-bin gcc g++ && \
    rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Update pip and install dependencies
RUN pip install --upgrade pip && \
    pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt && \
    python -c "import vertexai; print('✓ vertexai installed successfully')" || echo "Warning: vertexai import check" && \
    python -c "import pymssql; print('✓ pymssql installed successfully')" || echo "Warning: pymssql import check"

# Set environment variables
ENV PORT=8080
ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1
ENV PYTHONPATH=/app

# Run the web service on container startup
# Use uvicorn with FastAPI
CMD ["sh", "-c", "if [ -n \"$SCHEDULER_JOB_ID\" ]; then python main.py; else uvicorn main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port ${PORT}; fi"]

# ── User identity helper ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def _get_user_id(request: Request) -> str:
    """Extract VZID from X-User-Id header.  Returns '' if missing."""
    return (request.headers.get("x-user-id") or "").strip().lower()


def _require_user(request: Request) -> str:
    """Extract VZID or raise 401."""
    uid = _get_user_id(request)
    if not uid:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="X-User-Id header required")
    return uid


def _is_super_admin(user_id: str) -> bool:
    return user_id == SUPER_ADMIN_VZID


# ── Audit + User-data tables ─────────────────────────────────────────
AUDIT_TABLE = "sql_editor_audit_log"
USER_FAVORITES_TABLE = "sql_editor_user_favorites"
USER_HISTORY_TABLE = "sql_editor_user_history"


def _ensure_user_tables():
    """Create audit, favorites, and history tables."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        # Audit log
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {AUDIT_TABLE} (
                id          SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                user_id     VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                action      VARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'query',
                database_name VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                table_name  VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                query_text  TEXT DEFAULT '',
                db_type     VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT 'cloudsql',
                details     TEXT DEFAULT '',
                ip_address  VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT '',
                created_at  TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_user_ts
            ON {AUDIT_TABLE} (user_id, created_at DESC)
        """)
        # Additional indexes for audit search & filter performance
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_action
            ON {AUDIT_TABLE} (action)
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_db
            ON {AUDIT_TABLE} (database_name)
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_created
            ON {AUDIT_TABLE} (created_at DESC)
        """)
        # User favorites
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {USER_FAVORITES_TABLE} (
                id          SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                user_id     VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                query_text  TEXT NOT NULL,
                label       VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                created_at  TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_fav_user
            ON {USER_FAVORITES_TABLE} (user_id)
        """)
        # User query history
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {USER_HISTORY_TABLE} (
                id          SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                user_id     VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                query_text  TEXT NOT NULL,
                database_name VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                db_type     VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT 'cloudsql',
                row_count   INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                execution_time_ms FLOAT DEFAULT 0,
                created_at  TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hist_user_ts
            ON {USER_HISTORY_TABLE} (user_id, created_at DESC)
        """)
        # Add created_by column to existing tables (safe ALTER — no-op if exists)
        for tbl in ["sql_editor_apis", "sql_editor_servers", "sql_editor_scheduler_jobs"]:
            try:
                cursor.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {tbl} ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS created_by VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT ''")
            except Exception:
                pass
        # Add dependencies column to scheduler_jobs (JSON array of job IDs)
        try:
            cursor.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {SCHEDULER_JOBS_TABLE} ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS dependencies TEXT DEFAULT '[]'")
        except Exception:
            pass
        connection.commit()
        logger.info("User tables (audit, favorites, history) ensured")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to create user tables: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


_ensure_user_tables()


def _log_audit(user_id: str, action: str, database_name: str = "", table_name: str = "",
               query_text: str = "", db_type: str = "cloudsql", details: str = "", ip_address: str = ""):
    """Insert an audit log entry (fire-and-forget)."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {AUDIT_TABLE}
                (user_id, action, database_name, table_name, query_text, db_type, details, ip_address)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
        """, (user_id, action, database_name, table_name,
              query_text[:4000] if query_text else "", db_type, details, ip_address))
        connection.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Audit log failed: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


async def _log_audit_async(user_id: str, action: str, **kwargs):
    await asyncio.to_thread(_log_audit, user_id, action, **kwargs)


# Pydantic models
class QueryRequest(BaseModel):
    database: str = Field(..., description="Database name to connect to")
    query: str = Field(..., description="SQL query to execute")
    limit: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="Limit number of rows returned", ge=1, le=10000)
    
    @field_validator('query')
    @classmethod
    def validate_query(cls, v):
        if not v or not v.strip():
            raise ValueError('Query cannot be empty')
        # Basic validation - you can add more restrictions
        dangerous_keywords = ['DROP', 'TRUNCATE', 'ALTER', 'CREATE', 'GRANT', 'REVOKE']
        query_upper = v.upper()
        for keyword in dangerous_keywords:
            if keyword in query_upper:
                logger.warning(f"Potentially dangerous query attempted: {keyword}")
                # Uncomment the next line if you want to block these queries
                # raise ValueError(f'Query contains restricted keyword: {keyword}')
        return v


class QueryResponse(BaseModel):
    success: bool
    data: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None
    columns: Optional[List[str]] = None
    row_count: int
    execution_time_ms: float
    message: Optional[str] = None
    query: str
    database: str


class DatabaseInfo(BaseModel):
    name: str
    size: Optional[str] = None


class DatabaseListResponse(BaseModel):
    success: bool
    databases: List[DatabaseInfo]
    count: int


class RowUpdateRequest(BaseModel):
    database: str = Field(..., description="Database name")
    table: str = Field(..., description="Table name")
    schema_name: str = Field("public", description="Schema name")
    primary_key: Dict[str, Any] = Field(..., description="Primary key column(s) and value(s) to identify the row")
    updates: Dict[str, Any] = Field(..., description="Column-value pairs to update")


class RowInsertRequest(BaseModel):
    database: str = Field(..., description="Database name")
    table: str = Field(..., description="Table name")
    schema_name: str = Field("public", description="Schema name")
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(..., description="List of row dicts to insert")


class RowDeleteRequest(BaseModel):
    database: str = Field(..., description="Database name")
    table: str = Field(..., description="Table name")
    schema_name: str = Field("public", description="Schema name")
    primary_key_column: str = Field(..., description="Primary key column name")
    primary_key_values: List[Any] = Field(..., description="List of primary key values to delete")


class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    message: str = Field(..., description="User message for the chatbot")
    selected_query: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Currently selected SQL query")
    database: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Current database context")
    table: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Current table context")
    action: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Action type: 'chat', 'fix', 'enhance', 'suggest', 'explain'")


class ChatResponse(BaseModel):
    success: bool
    message: str
    action: str
    suggested_query: Optional[str] = None
    explanation: Optional[str] = None
    error_details: Optional[str] = None
    timestamp: str


# ── API Builder Models ───────────────────────────────────────────────────


class FunctionLayer(BaseModel):
    """Optional transformation/validation layer applied to API logic."""
    id: Optional[str] = None
    name: str = Field(..., description="Layer name, e.g. 'mask_pii', 'validate_email'")
    type: str = Field(..., description="Layer type: 'filter', 'transform', 'validate', 'aggregate', 'custom_sql'")
    config: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict, description="Layer-specific configuration")
    order: int = Field(0, description="Execution order (lower = first)")
    enabled: bool = True


class RelationshipDef(BaseModel):
    """Defines a relationship between two tables (same or cross-DB)."""
    id: Optional[str] = None
    name: str = Field(..., description="Human-friendly name for this relationship")
    source_database: str
    source_table: str
    source_column: str
    target_database: str
    target_table: str
    target_column: str
    join_type: str = Field("INNER", description="JOIN type: INNER, LEFT, RIGHT, FULL")
    source_db_type: str = Field("cloudsql", description="cloudsql or bigquery")
    target_db_type: str = Field("cloudsql", description="cloudsql or bigquery")
    raw_on_clause: str = Field("", description="Raw ON clause for function-based JOINs (e.g. right(t1.col,32)=t2.col)")
    alias: str = Field("", description="Table alias for this JOIN (required when same table is joined multiple times)")


class InlineRelationshipDef(BaseModel):
    """Relationship definition embedded inside CustomAPIConfig.
    Extends RelationshipDef with join_source_table for chained JOIN support.
    """
    id: Optional[str] = None
    name: str = Field("", description="Optional human-friendly name")
    source_database: Optional[str] = None
    source_table: str = Field("", description="Table whose column is used on the left side of the ON clause. "
                              "Defaults to the API base_table when empty. "
                              "Set this for chained JOINs where the source is a previously joined table.")
    source_column: str = Field("", description="Column on the source/left side of the ON clause")
    target_database: Optional[str] = None
    target_table: str = Field(..., description="Table to JOIN into")
    target_column: str = Field("", description="Column on the target/right side of the ON clause")
    join_type: str = Field("INNER", description="JOIN type: INNER, LEFT, RIGHT, FULL")
    source_db_type: str = Field("cloudsql", description="cloudsql or bigquery")
    target_db_type: str = Field("cloudsql", description="cloudsql or bigquery")
    raw_on_clause: str = Field("", description="Raw ON clause overrides source/target columns when set")
    alias: str = Field("", description="Table alias for the joined table")


class CustomAPIConfig(BaseModel):
    """Full configuration for a user-defined API endpoint."""
    id: Optional[str] = None
    name: str = Field(..., description="API name / endpoint slug, e.g. 'active-users'")
    description: str = Field("", description="What this API does")
    database: str
    db_type: str = Field("cloudsql", description="cloudsql or bigquery")
    server_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Server ID for PostgreSQL connections; null = use default DB_HOST")
    base_table: str
    schema_name: str = Field("public")
    relationships: List[InlineRelationshipDef] = Field(default_factory=list)
    function_layers: List[FunctionLayer] = Field(default_factory=list)
    select_columns: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Columns to expose; empty = all")
    default_limit: int = Field(100, ge=1, le=10000)
    allowed_filters: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Columns the caller can filter on")
    allowed_sorts: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Columns the caller can sort on")
    enable_crud: bool = Field(True, description="Whether POST/PUT/DELETE are enabled")
    # ── Auth & Encryption ─────────────────────────────────────────
    auth_enabled: bool = Field(False, description="Require Basic Auth")
    auth_username: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Basic Auth username")
    auth_password: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Basic Auth password")
    encryption_enabled: bool = Field(False, description="Encrypt/decrypt payloads")
    encryption_key: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Fernet key (auto-generated if blank)")
    public_alias: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Public alias for clean URL, e.g. 'users' → /api/data/users")
    base_filter: str = Field("", description="Raw WHERE clause always applied (legacy / advanced)")
    where_conditions: List[dict] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Structured WHERE conditions: [{column, operator, value}]")
    select_column_aliases: List[dict] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Column output aliases: [{expr, output_alias}]")
    base_table_alias: str = Field("", description="Alias for the base table from original query, e.g. 'inc' for vea_sn_incident AS inc")
    cache_ttl: int = Field(0, ge=0, le=3600, description="Response cache TTL in seconds; 0 = no caching")
    created_at: Optional[str] = None
    updated_at: Optional[str] = None


# ── API Builder PostgreSQL persistence ────────────────────────────────
API_BUILDER_TABLE = "sql_editor_apis"
API_METRICS_TABLE = "sql_editor_api_metrics"
_api_store: Dict[str, dict] = {}
_relationship_store: Dict[str, dict] = {}

# ── Response Cache (in-memory TTL) ────────────────────────────────────
import time as _time
_response_cache: Dict[str, dict] = {}  # {cache_key: {"data": ..., "expires_at": float}}

def _cache_get(key: str):
    """Return cached data if key exists and not expired, else None."""
    entry = _response_cache.get(key)
    if entry and entry["expires_at"] > _time.time():
        return entry["data"]
    if entry:
        del _response_cache[key]
    return None

def _cache_set(key: str, data: dict, ttl: int):
    """Store data in cache with TTL in seconds."""
    if ttl <= 0:
        return
    _response_cache[key] = {"data": data, "expires_at": _time.time() + ttl}
    # Evict old entries if cache grows too large (max 500 entries)
    if len(_response_cache) > 500:
        now = _time.time()
        expired = [k for k, v in _response_cache.items() if v["expires_at"] <= now]
        for k in expired:
            del _response_cache[k]


def _ensure_api_builder_table():
    """Create the api builder table in PostgreSQL if it doesn't exist."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {API_BUILDER_TABLE} (
                id              VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
                name            VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                description     TEXT DEFAULT '',
                database_name   VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                db_type         VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT 'cloudsql',
                base_table      VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                schema_name     VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT 'public',
                config          TEXT DEFAULT '{{}}',
                public_alias    VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT NULL,
                created_at      VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT '',
                updated_at      VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT ''
            )
        """)
        connection.commit()
        logger.info(f"Ensured {API_BUILDER_TABLE} table exists")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to create API builder table: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def _load_all_apis() -> Dict[str, dict]:
    """Load all custom APIs from PostgreSQL."""
    store = {}
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {API_BUILDER_TABLE} ORDER BY created_at DESC")
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        for row in rows:
            cfg = json.loads(row.get("config") or "{}")
            api = {
                "id": row["id"],
                "name": row["name"],
                "description": row.get("description", ""),
                "database": row.get("database_name", ""),
                "db_type": row.get("db_type", "cloudsql"),
                "base_table": row.get("base_table", ""),
                "schema_name": row.get("schema_name", "public"),
                "public_alias": row.get("public_alias"),
                "created_at": row.get("created_at", ""),
                "updated_at": row.get("updated_at", ""),
                "created_by": row.get("created_by", ""),
                **cfg,
            }
            store[api["id"]] = api
        logger.info(f"Loaded {len(store)} custom APIs from {API_BUILDER_TABLE}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to load custom APIs: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()
    return store


def _save_api(api: dict):
    """Save a single API config to PostgreSQL (upsert)."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        # Store complex nested fields in config JSON
        config_fields = {
            k: v for k, v in api.items()
            if k not in ("id", "name", "description", "database", "db_type",
                         "base_table", "schema_name", "public_alias",
                         "created_at", "updated_at", "created_by")
        }
        cursor.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {API_BUILDER_TABLE}
                (id, name, description, database_name, db_type, base_table, schema_name, config, public_alias, created_at, updated_at, created_by)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
            ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
                name = EXCLUDED.name,
                description = EXCLUDED.description,
                database_name = EXCLUDED.database_name,
                db_type = EXCLUDED.db_type,
                base_table = EXCLUDED.base_table,
                schema_name = EXCLUDED.schema_name,
                config = EXCLUDED.config,
                public_alias = EXCLUDED.public_alias,
                updated_at = EXCLUDED.updated_at,
                created_by = EXCLUDED.created_by
        """, (
            api["id"], api.get("name", ""), api.get("description", ""),
            api.get("database", ""), api.get("db_type", "cloudsql"),
            api.get("base_table", ""), api.get("schema_name", "public"),
            json.dumps(config_fields, default=str),
            api.get("public_alias") or None,
            api.get("created_at", ""), api.get("updated_at", ""),
            api.get("created_by", ""),
        ))
        connection.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to save API {api.get('id')}: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def _delete_api_from_db(api_id: str):
    """Delete an API from PostgreSQL."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"DELETE FROM {API_BUILDER_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (api_id,))
        connection.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to delete API {api_id}: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── API Metrics PostgreSQL persistence ────────────────────────────────

def _ensure_api_metrics_table():
    """Create the api metrics table in PostgreSQL if it doesn't exist."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {API_METRICS_TABLE} (
                id              SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                api_id          VARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL,
                hit_timestamp   TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
                method          VARCHAR(10)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'GET',
                path            TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                caller_ip       VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT '',
                user_agent      TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                response_time_ms FLOAT       DEFAULT 0,
                status_code     INTEGER      DEFAULT 200,
                query_params    TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                row_count       INTEGER      DEFAULT 0,
                error_message   TEXT         DEFAULT ''
            )
        """)
        # Add index for fast lookups by api_id + timestamp
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_api_metrics_api_ts
            ON {API_METRICS_TABLE} (api_id, hit_timestamp DESC)
        """)
        connection.commit()
        logger.info(f"Ensured {API_METRICS_TABLE} table exists")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to create API metrics table: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def _log_api_metric(api_id: str, method: str, path: str, caller_ip: str,
                    user_agent: str, response_time_ms: float, status_code: int,
                    query_params: str = "", row_count: int = 0, error_message: str = ""):
    """Log a single API hit metric to PostgreSQL (fire-and-forget)."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {API_METRICS_TABLE}
                (api_id, method, path, caller_ip, user_agent, response_time_ms,
                 status_code, query_params, row_count, error_message)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
        """, (api_id, method, path, caller_ip, user_agent, response_time_ms,
              status_code, query_params, row_count, error_message))
        connection.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Failed to log API metric for {api_id}: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


async def _log_api_metric_async(api_id: str, method: str, path: str,
                                caller_ip: str, user_agent: str,
                                response_time_ms: float, status_code: int,
                                query_params: str = "", row_count: int = 0,
                                error_message: str = ""):
    """Async wrapper — logs metric in a background thread so it won't block."""
    await asyncio.to_thread(
        _log_api_metric, api_id, method, path, caller_ip, user_agent,
        response_time_ms, status_code, query_params, row_count, error_message
    )


# Initialize: create table, load saved APIs
_ensure_api_builder_table()
_ensure_api_metrics_table()
_api_store = _load_all_apis()

# ── Server credential persistence (PostgreSQL-backed) ─────────────────
SERVERS_TABLE = "sql_editor_servers"


def _ensure_servers_table():
    """Create the servers table in the default PostgreSQL if it doesn't exist."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {SERVERS_TABLE} (
                id          VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
                name        VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                host        VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                port        INTEGER DEFAULT 5432,
                username    VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                password    VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                database    VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                db_type     VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT 'cloudsql',
                color       VARCHAR(20)  DEFAULT '#3b82f6',
                ssl_mode    VARCHAR(20)  DEFAULT 'prefer',
                is_default  BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
                created_at  VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT ''
            )
        """)
        connection.commit()
        logger.info(f"Ensured {SERVERS_TABLE} table exists")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to create servers table: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def _load_servers() -> Dict[str, dict]:
    """Load all servers from the PostgreSQL table."""
    store = {}
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {SERVERS_TABLE}")
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        for row in rows:
            srv = dict(row)
            srv["connected"] = False  # always start disconnected
            if srv.get("created_at") and hasattr(srv["created_at"], "isoformat"):
                srv["created_at"] = srv["created_at"].isoformat()
            store[srv["id"]] = srv
        logger.info(f"Loaded {len(store)} servers from {SERVERS_TABLE} table")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to load servers from database: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()
    return store


def _save_servers():
    """Sync the entire _server_store to the PostgreSQL table (delete + re-insert)."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"DELETE FROM {SERVERS_TABLE}")
        for srv in _server_store.values():
            cursor.execute(f"""
                INSERT INTO {SERVERS_TABLE}
                    (id, name, host, port, username, password, database, db_type, color, ssl_mode, is_default, created_at, created_by)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
            """, (
                srv["id"], srv["name"], srv["host"], srv.get("port", 5432),
                srv["username"], srv["password"], srv.get("database", ""),
                srv.get("db_type", "cloudsql"), srv.get("color", "#3b82f6"),
                srv.get("ssl_mode", "prefer"), srv.get("is_default", False),
                srv.get("created_at", datetime.now().isoformat()),
                srv.get("created_by", ""),
            ))
        connection.commit()
        logger.info(f"Saved {len(_server_store)} servers to {SERVERS_TABLE} table")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to save servers to database: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# Initialize: create table, load saved servers, ensure default exists
_ensure_servers_table()
_server_store: Dict[str, dict] = _load_servers()
_default_server_id = "default"
if _default_server_id not in _server_store:
    _server_store[_default_server_id] = {
        "id": _default_server_id,
        "name": "Default CloudSQL",
        "host": DB_HOST,
        "port": 5432,
        "username": DB_USERNAME,
        "password": DB_PASSWORD,
        "database": "",
        "db_type": "cloudsql",
        "color": "#3b82f6",
        "is_default": True,
        "connected": False,
        "created_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
    }
    _save_servers()


# ── Encryption helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────

def generate_fernet_key() -> str:
    """Generate a new Fernet encryption key."""
    return Fernet.generate_key().decode()


def encrypt_payload(data: Any, key: str) -> str:
    """Encrypt data with Fernet. Returns base64-encoded ciphertext."""
    f = Fernet(key.encode() if isinstance(key, str) else key)
    plaintext = json.dumps(data, default=str).encode("utf-8")
    return f.encrypt(plaintext).decode("utf-8")


def decrypt_payload(token: str, key: str) -> Any:
    """Decrypt a Fernet token back to Python object."""
    f = Fernet(key.encode() if isinstance(key, str) else key)
    plaintext = f.decrypt(token.encode("utf-8"))
    return json.loads(plaintext.decode("utf-8"))


# ── Basic Auth dependency ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def verify_basic_auth(api_cfg: dict, authorization: Optional[str] = None):
    """Verify Basic Auth header against the API config."""
    if not api_cfg.get("auth_enabled"):
        return  # Auth not required
    if not authorization:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Authorization header required", headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Basic"})
    try:
        scheme, _, credentials = authorization.partition(" ")
        if scheme.lower() != "basic":
            raise ValueError("Not Basic")
        decoded = base64.b64decode(credentials).decode("utf-8")
        username, _, password = decoded.partition(":")
        if username != api_cfg.get("auth_username") or password != api_cfg.get("auth_password"):
            raise ValueError("Bad credentials")
    except Exception:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid credentials", headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Basic"})


# Database connection functions
def get_db_connection(database: str):
    """Establish database connection"""
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST,
            database=database,
            user=DB_USERNAME,
            password=DB_PASSWORD,
            connect_timeout=10
        )
        logger.info(f"Successfully connected to database: {database}")
        return connection
    except psycopg2.OperationalError as e:
        logger.error(f"Error connecting to database {database}: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=f"Cannot connect to database '{database}': {str(e)}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Unexpected error connecting to database {database}: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Database connection error: {str(e)}")


def normalize_query(query: str, database: str) -> str:
    """
    Convert BigQuery/MySQL style queries to PostgreSQL syntax
    - Removes backticks
    - Converts database.table notation to just table
    """
    # Replace backticks with nothing (remove them)
    normalized = query.replace('`', '')
    
    # If query contains database.table notation, convert to just table
    # For example: usecasescenario_prod.tbl_admin_users -> tbl_admin_users
    if '.' in normalized:
        # Split by spaces and process each token
        tokens = normalized.split()
        for i, token in enumerate(tokens):
            if '.' in token:
                # Check if it starts with the database name
                if token.startswith(f"{database}."):
                    # Replace database.table with just table
                    tokens[i] = token.replace(f"{database}.", "")
        normalized = ' '.join(tokens)
    
    return normalized


def get_bigquery_datasets(search: str = None) -> List[Dict]:
    """Get list of BigQuery datasets"""
    try:
        datasets = list(bq_client.list_datasets())
        dataset_list = []
        
        for dataset in datasets:
            dataset_id = dataset.dataset_id
            # Filter by search term if provided
            if search and search.lower() not in dataset_id.lower():
                continue
            
            dataset_list.append({
                "name": dataset_id,
                "project": dataset.project,
                "size": "N/A"  # BigQuery doesn't easily expose dataset size
            })
        
        return dataset_list
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error listing BigQuery datasets: {e}")
        raise


def get_bigquery_tables(dataset_id: str, search: str = None) -> List[Dict]:
    """Get list of BigQuery tables in a dataset"""
    try:
        dataset = bq_client.get_dataset(dataset_id)
        tables = list(bq_client.list_tables(dataset))
        table_list = []
        
        for table in tables:
            table_name = table.table_id
            # Filter by search term if provided
            if search and search.lower() not in table_name.lower():
                continue
            
            table_list.append({
                "schema": dataset_id,
                "table_name": table_name,
                "size": "N/A"
            })
        
        return table_list
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error listing BigQuery tables: {e}")
        raise


def _normalize_bq_query(query: str) -> str:
    """Convert double-quoted identifiers to backtick-quoted for BigQuery.
    BigQuery uses backticks for identifiers; double quotes are string literals."""
    # Replace double-quoted identifiers with backtick-quoted
    # Pattern: "word" or "word"."word" (identifiers, not string values)
    # This converts e.g. "empan_dev_tbls"."vea_sn_incident" → `empan_dev_tbls`.`vea_sn_incident`
    normalized = re.sub(r'"([A-Za-z_][\w]*)"', r'`\1`', query)
    return normalized


def execute_bigquery(query: str, database: str = None, limit: Optional[int] = None) -> Dict:
    """Execute BigQuery query and return results"""
    start_time = datetime.now()
    
    try:
        # Normalize: convert double-quoted identifiers to backticks for BigQuery
        query = _normalize_bq_query(query)

        # Apply limit if specified (only for SELECT queries)
        query_upper = query.strip().upper()
        is_select = query_upper.startswith('SELECT') or query_upper.startswith('WITH')
        has_limit = 'LIMIT' in query_upper
        
        if limit and is_select and not has_limit:
            query = query.rstrip(';').rstrip() + f" LIMIT {limit}"
        
        # Set default dataset so unqualified table names auto-resolve
        job_config = None
        if database:
            job_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(
                default_dataset=f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{database}"
            )
        
        # Execute query
        query_job = bq_client.query(query, job_config=job_config)
        results = query_job.result()
        
        # Convert results to list of dicts
        data = [dict(row) for row in results]
        columns = [field.name for field in results.schema]
        row_count = len(data)
        
        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        
        return {
            "success": True,
            "data": data,
            "columns": columns,
            "row_count": row_count,
            "execution_time_ms": round(execution_time, 2),
            "query": query,
            "database": database or "BigQuery"
        }
    
    except Exception as e:
        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        logger.error(f"BigQuery error: {e}")
        return {
            "success": False,
            "data": None,
            "columns": None,
            "row_count": 0,
            "execution_time_ms": round(execution_time, 2),
            "message": f"Query error: {str(e)}",
            "query": query,
            "database": database or "BigQuery"
        }



def execute_query(database: str, query: str, limit: Optional[int] = None) -> Dict:
    """Execute SQL query and return results"""
    connection = None
    start_time = datetime.now()
    
    try:
        # Normalize query to PostgreSQL syntax
        query = normalize_query(query, database)
        
        connection = get_db_connection(database)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        
        # Apply limit if specified and query is a SELECT (not DDL/DML like CREATE, INSERT, etc.)
        query_upper = query.strip().upper()
        is_select = query_upper.startswith('SELECT') or query_upper.startswith('WITH')
        has_limit = 'LIMIT' in query_upper
        
        if limit and is_select and not has_limit:
            query = query.rstrip(';').rstrip() + f" LIMIT {limit}"
            if query.strip().upper().endswith(';'):
                pass
            else:
                query = query + ";"
        
        cursor.execute(query)
        
        # Check if query returns data
        if cursor.description:
            results = cursor.fetchall()
            columns = [desc[0] for desc in cursor.description]
            data = [dict(row) for row in results]
            row_count = len(results)
        else:
            # For queries that don't return data (INSERT, UPDATE, etc.)
            connection.commit()
            columns = []
            data = []
            row_count = cursor.rowcount if cursor.rowcount >= 0 else 0
        
        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        
        return {
            "success": True,
            "data": data,
            "columns": columns,
            "row_count": row_count,
            "execution_time_ms": round(execution_time, 2),
            "query": query,
            "database": database
        }
        
    except psycopg2.Error as e:
        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        logger.error(f"PostgreSQL error executing query: {e}")
        return {
            "success": False,
            "data": None,
            "columns": None,
            "row_count": 0,
            "execution_time_ms": round(execution_time, 2),
            "message": f"Query error: {str(e)}",
            "query": query,
            "database": database
        }
    except Exception as e:
        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        logger.error(f"Error executing query: {e}")
        return {
            "success": False,
            "data": None,
            "columns": None,
            "row_count": 0,
            "execution_time_ms": round(execution_time, 2),
            "message": f"Execution error: {str(e)}",
            "query": query,
            "database": database
        }
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def convert_to_csv(data: List[Dict], columns: List[str]) -> str:
    """Convert query results to CSV format"""
    output = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.DictWriter(output, fieldnames=columns)
    writer.writeheader()
    
    for row in data:
        # Convert datetime objects to strings
        cleaned_row = {}
        for key, value in row.items():
            if isinstance(value, datetime):
                cleaned_row[key] = value.isoformat()
            elif value is None:
                cleaned_row[key] = ''
            else:
                cleaned_row[key] = value
        writer.writerow(cleaned_row)
    
    return output.getvalue()


def convert_to_json_string(data: List[Dict]) -> str:
    """Convert query results to JSON string"""
    # Handle datetime serialization
    def json_serial(obj):
        if isinstance(obj, datetime):
            return obj.isoformat()
        raise TypeError(f"Type {type(obj)} not serializable")
    
    return json.dumps(data, indent=2, default=json_serial)

@app.get("/api/databases", response_model=DatabaseListResponse)
def list_databases(type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$"), search: str = Query(None)):
    """List all available databases or BigQuery datasets"""
    try:
        if type == "bigquery":
            datasets = get_bigquery_datasets(search)
            databases = [DatabaseInfo(name=ds["name"], size=ds["size"]) for ds in datasets]
        elif type == "mssql":
            # Find the first connected MSSQL server from _server_store
            mssql_srv = None
            for sid, srv in _server_store.items():
                if srv.get("db_type") == "mssql" and srv.get("connected"):
                    mssql_srv = (sid, srv)
                    break
            if not mssql_srv:
                # Try any MSSQL server even if not marked connected
                for sid, srv in _server_store.items():
                    if srv.get("db_type") == "mssql":
                        mssql_srv = (sid, srv)
                        break
            if not mssql_srv:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="No MSSQL server configured. Add one via Server Manager.")
            sid, srv = mssql_srv
            connection = _get_server_connection(sid, srv.get("database") or "master")
            try:
                cursor = connection.cursor()
                cursor.execute("""
                    SELECT d.name,
                           CAST(SUM(mf.size) * 8 / 1024 AS VARCHAR) + ' MB' AS size
                    FROM sys.databases d
                    LEFT JOIN sys.master_files mf ON d.database_id = mf.database_id
                    WHERE d.database_id > 0
                    GROUP BY d.name
                    ORDER BY d.name
                """)
                rows = cursor.fetchall()
                databases = [DatabaseInfo(name=r["name"], size=r["size"]) for r in rows]
                if search:
                    databases = [db for db in databases if search.lower() in db.name.lower()]
            finally:
                connection.close()
            _server_store[sid]["connected"] = True
            _save_servers()
        else:  # cloudsql
            # Connect to postgres database to list all databases
            connection = psycopg2.connect(
                host=DB_HOST,
                database="postgres",
                user=DB_USERNAME,
                password=DB_PASSWORD,
                connect_timeout=10
            )
            try:
                cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)

                query = """
                SELECT
                    datname as name,
                    pg_size_pretty(pg_database_size(datname)) as size
                FROM pg_database
                WHERE datistemplate = false
                ORDER BY datname
                """

                cursor.execute(query)
                results = cursor.fetchall()

                databases = [DatabaseInfo(name=row['name'], size=row['size']) for row in results]

                # Filter by search if provided
                if search:
                    databases = [db for db in databases if search.lower() in db.name.lower()]
            finally:
                connection.close()

        return DatabaseListResponse(
            success=True,
            databases=databases,
            count=len(databases)
        )

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error listing databases: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error listing databases: {str(e)}")


# ── Create Database (default server / BigQuery) ───────────────────────
@app.post("/api/databases/create")
async def create_database(body: dict, request: Request):
    """Create a new database on the default CloudSQL server or a BigQuery dataset."""
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    db_name = (body.get("name") or "").strip()
    db_type = body.get("type", "cloudsql")  # cloudsql | bigquery

    if not db_name:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Database name is required")
    # Validate name: alphanumeric, underscores, hyphens, max 63 chars
    import re as _re_db
    if not _re_db.match(r'^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,62}$', db_name):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid database name. Use letters, digits, underscores, hyphens. Must start with a letter or underscore. Max 63 characters.")

    try:
        if db_type == "bigquery":
            if not bq_client:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="BigQuery client not initialized")
            from google.cloud.bigquery import Dataset
            dataset_ref = bq_client.dataset(db_name)
            dataset = Dataset(dataset_ref)
            dataset.location = GOOGLE_CLOUD_LOCATION
            bq_client.create_dataset(dataset, exists_ok=False)
            logger.info(f"[{user_id}] Created BigQuery dataset: {db_name}")
            await _log_audit_async(user_id, "create_database", database_name=db_name,
                                   query_text=f"CREATE DATASET {db_name}", ip_address=request.client.host if request.client else "")
            return {"success": True, "message": f"BigQuery dataset '{db_name}' created successfully", "database": db_name}
        else:
            # CloudSQL (PostgreSQL) – must connect to 'postgres' maintenance db
            connection = psycopg2.connect(
                host=DB_HOST,
                database="postgres",
                user=DB_USERNAME,
                password=DB_PASSWORD,
                connect_timeout=10,
            )
            connection.autocommit = True  # CREATE DATABASE cannot run inside a transaction
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            # Use identifier quoting to prevent SQL injection
            cursor.execute(f'CREATE DATABASE "{db_name}"')
            connection.close()
            logger.info(f"[{user_id}] Created PostgreSQL database: {db_name}")
            await _log_audit_async(user_id, "create_database", database_name=db_name,
                                   query_text=f"CREATE DATABASE \"{db_name}\"", ip_address=request.client.host if request.client else "")
            return {"success": True, "message": f"Database '{db_name}' created successfully", "database": db_name}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Create database error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to create database: {str(e)}")


@app.post("/api/query", response_model=QueryResponse)
async def execute_sql_query(request: QueryRequest, req: Request, type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$")):
    """Execute SQL query and return results as JSON"""
    user_id = _get_user_id(req)
    logger.info(f"[{user_id}] Executing query on {type} database '{request.database}': {request.query[:100]}...")
    
    # Block DELETE queries in SQL Editor (Production safety)
    stripped = request.query.strip().upper()
    if stripped.startswith("DELETE ") or stripped.startswith("DELETE\n") or stripped == "DELETE":
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=403,
            detail="Delete functions are temporarily disabled. Please contact admin for delete operations on Production."
        )
    
    if type == "bigquery":
        result = execute_bigquery(request.query, request.database, request.limit)
    else:
        result = execute_query(request.database, request.query, request.limit)
    
    if not result["success"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=result["message"])
    
    # Audit log + user history (fire-and-forget)
    if user_id:
        ip = req.client.host if req.client else ""
        # Extract table name from query (best-effort)
        tbl = ""
        import re as _re
        m = _re.search(r'\bFROM\s+[`"]?(\S+?)[`"]?(?:\s|$|;)', request.query, _re.IGNORECASE)
        if m:
            tbl = m.group(1)
        await _log_audit_async(user_id, "query", database_name=request.database,
                               table_name=tbl, query_text=request.query,
                               db_type=type, ip_address=ip)
        # Save to user query history
        asyncio.get_event_loop().run_in_executor(None, _save_user_history,
            user_id, request.query, request.database, type,
            result.get("row_count", 0), result.get("execution_time_ms", 0))
    
    return QueryResponse(**result)


@app.post("/api/query/csv")
async def download_query_results_csv(request: QueryRequest, type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$")):
    """Execute SQL query and return results as CSV file"""
    logger.info(f"Executing query for CSV download on {type} database '{request.database}'")
    
    if type == "bigquery":
        result = execute_bigquery(request.query, request.database, request.limit)
    else:
        result = execute_query(request.database, request.query, request.limit)
    
    if not result["success"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=result["message"])
    
    if not result["data"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="No data returned from query")
    
    csv_content = convert_to_csv(result["data"], result["columns"])
    
    # Generate filename with timestamp
    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    filename = f"query_results_{timestamp}.csv"
    
    return StreamingResponse(
        io.StringIO(csv_content),
        media_type="text/csv",
        headers={"Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename={filename}"}
    )


@app.post("/api/query/json")
async def download_query_results_json(request: QueryRequest, type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$")):
    """Execute SQL query and return results as JSON file"""
    logger.info(f"Executing query for JSON download on {type} database '{request.database}'")
    
    if type == "bigquery":
        result = execute_bigquery(request.query, request.database, request.limit)
    else:
        result = execute_query(request.database, request.query, request.limit)
    
    if not result["success"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=result["message"])
    
    if not result["data"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="No data returned from query")
    
    json_content = convert_to_json_string(result["data"])
    
    # Generate filename with timestamp
    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    filename = f"query_results_{timestamp}.json"
    
    return StreamingResponse(
        io.StringIO(json_content),
        media_type="application/json",
        headers={"Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename={filename}"}
    )


@app.post("/api/query/clipboard")
async def get_query_results_for_clipboard(request: QueryRequest, format: str = Query("json", pattern="^(json|csv)$")):
    """Execute SQL query and return results formatted for clipboard (JSON or CSV)"""
    logger.info(f"Executing query for clipboard in {format} format on database '{request.database}'")
    
    result = execute_query(request.database, request.query, request.limit)
    
    if not result["success"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=result["message"])
    
    if not result["data"]:
        return JSONResponse(content={"success": True, "data": "", "message": "No data returned from query"})
    
    if format == "csv":
        content = convert_to_csv(result["data"], result["columns"])
    else:  # json
        content = convert_to_json_string(result["data"])
    
    return JSONResponse(content={
        "success": True,
        "data": content,
        "format": format,
        "row_count": result["row_count"]
    })


def _parse_where_conditions(where_text: str, alias_map: dict = None, base_table: str = "") -> list:
    """Parse a raw WHERE clause text into structured conditions.
    Returns list of {column, operator, value} dicts.
    Handles: col = 'val', col LIKE '%val%', col > 5, col IS NULL,
             col IN ('a','b'), col != 'x', col BETWEEN a AND b, etc.
    Falls back to a single raw condition if it cannot be parsed.

    alias_map: {alias -> real_table_name} e.g. {'req': 'vea_sn_change_request', 'appl': 'vea_sn_cmdb_ci_appl'}
    base_table: the main FROM table name — columns from this table need no qualifier.
    """
    if not where_text or not where_text.strip():
        return []

    results = []
    _alias_map = alias_map or {}
    _base_table = base_table

    # Helper: resolve alias prefix to real table name
    # - appl.u_owning_entity -> vea_sn_cmdb_ci_appl.u_owning_entity  (joined table, needs qualifier)
    # - req.state            -> state                                 (base table, no qualifier needed)
    # - col_name             -> col_name                              (no prefix, keep as-is)
    def _resolve_col(col_name: str) -> str:
        col_name = col_name.strip('`"')
        if '.' in col_name:
            parts = col_name.split('.', 1)
            alias = parts[0]
            col = parts[1]
            real_table = _alias_map.get(alias, alias)
            # If column belongs to the base table, no qualifier needed
            if real_table == _base_table:
                return col
            # Preserve alias if it differs from table name (for self-joins)
            if alias != real_table and alias in _alias_map:
                return f"{alias}.{col}"
            # Otherwise qualify with the real table name
            return f"{real_table}.{col}"
        return col_name

    # Split on top-level AND (not inside parentheses or quotes)
    parts = re.split(r'\bAND\b', where_text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    for part in parts:
        part = part.strip()
        if not part:
            continue

        # IS NULL / IS NOT NULL
        m = re.match(r'^(\S+)\s+(IS\s+NOT\s+NULL|IS\s+NULL)\s*$', part, re.IGNORECASE)
        if m:
            results.append({"column": _resolve_col(m.group(1)), "operator": m.group(2).upper().replace("  ", " "), "value": ""})
            continue

        # NOT LIKE
        m = re.match(r'^(\S+)\s+(NOT\s+LIKE)\s+(.+)$', part, re.IGNORECASE)
        if m:
            val = m.group(3).strip().strip("'\"")
            results.append({"column": _resolve_col(m.group(1)), "operator": "NOT LIKE", "value": val})
            continue

        # LIKE / ILIKE
        m = re.match(r'^(\S+)\s+(I?LIKE)\s+(.+)$', part, re.IGNORECASE)
        if m:
            val = m.group(3).strip().strip("'\"")
            results.append({"column": _resolve_col(m.group(1)), "operator": m.group(2).upper(), "value": val})
            continue

        # IN (...)
        m = re.match(r'^(\S+)\s+(NOT\s+IN|IN)\s*\((.+)\)\s*$', part, re.IGNORECASE)
        if m:
            raw_vals = m.group(3)
            # strip individual quotes
            items = [v.strip().strip("'\"") for v in raw_vals.split(",")]
            results.append({"column": _resolve_col(m.group(1)), "operator": m.group(2).upper().replace("  ", " "), "value": ", ".join(items)})
            continue

        # BETWEEN  col BETWEEN a AND b  (already split on AND so this won't work perfectly — put as raw)
        # Standard operators: >=, <=, !=, <>, =, >, <
        m = re.match(r'^(\S+)\s*(>=|<=|!=|<>|=|>|<)\s*(.+)$', part, re.IGNORECASE)
        if m:
            val = m.group(3).strip().strip("'\"")
            results.append({"column": _resolve_col(m.group(1)), "operator": m.group(2), "value": val})
            continue

        # Could not parse — add as raw base_filter-style entry
        results.append({"column": "", "operator": "RAW", "value": part})

    return results


@app.post("/api/query/parse")
async def parse_query_for_api(request: QueryRequest, type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$")):
    """Parse a SQL query and extract table name, columns, JOINs, and other
    metadata to pre-fill the API Builder form.

    Supported query types
    ─────────────────────
    SELECT (simple, DISTINCT, aggregates, CASE, sub-selects, star)
    JOIN   (INNER, LEFT, RIGHT, FULL, CROSS, self-join, multi-join, schema-qualified)
    CTE    (WITH … AS (…) SELECT …)
    UNION / UNION ALL  (extracts from first SELECT)
    GROUP BY / HAVING / ORDER BY / LIMIT / OFFSET
    Sub-queries in WHERE (EXISTS, IN)
    INSERT INTO … (…)
    UPDATE … SET …
    DELETE FROM …
    BigQuery backtick-quoted identifiers (`project.dataset.table`)
    BigQuery 3-part names, UNNEST, STRUCT, ARRAY functions
    """
    query = request.query.strip()
    database = request.database
    is_bigquery = (type == "bigquery")

    # Strip backticks (BigQuery uses `project.dataset.table`)
    clean_query = query.replace('`', '')

    table_name = ""
    columns_used = []
    schema_name = database if is_bigquery else "public"
    query_type = "SELECT"
    joins = []  # list of detected JOIN clauses

    # ── Helper: decide which SELECT…FROM to use for the *main* query ──
    def _main_query(sql: str) -> str:
        """For CTE (WITH) queries return the final SELECT statement so we
        extract the real table, not the CTE alias."""
        upper = sql.upper()
        if not upper.lstrip().startswith("WITH"):
            return sql
        # Walk forward counting parenthesis depth to skip CTE bodies
        depth = 0
        i = 0
        while i < len(sql):
            ch = sql[i]
            if ch == '(':
                depth += 1
            elif ch == ')':
                depth -= 1
            elif depth == 0 and sql[i:i+6].upper() == "SELECT":
                # Make sure it's not the SELECT inside the CTE definition
                prefix = sql[:i].rstrip()
                if prefix and prefix[-1] == ')':
                    return sql[i:]
            i += 1
        return sql  # fallback: return as-is

    try:
        upper = clean_query.upper()

        # Detect query type
        for qt in ["SELECT", "INSERT", "UPDATE", "DELETE", "WITH"]:
            if upper.startswith(qt):
                query_type = qt
                break

        # For CTE queries get the final SELECT so FROM targets the real table
        work_query = _main_query(clean_query) if query_type == "WITH" else clean_query

        # ── Build alias → real table map ──────────────────────────────
        alias_map = {}  # alias -> table_name

        # ── Extract base table from FROM clause ───────────────────────
        # 3-part (BigQuery): FROM project.dataset.table
        from_match_3 = re.search(
            r'\bFROM\s+([\w-]+)\s*\.\s*(\w+)\s*\.\s*(\w+)',
            work_query, re.IGNORECASE
        )
        if from_match_3:
            # project = group(1) (ignored), dataset = group(2), table = group(3)
            schema_name = from_match_3.group(2)
            table_name = from_match_3.group(3)
        else:
            # 2-part: FROM schema.table  or  FROM dataset.table
            from_match = re.search(
                r'\bFROM\s+["\']?(\w+)["\']?\s*\.\s*["\']?(\w+)["\']?',
                work_query, re.IGNORECASE
            )
            if from_match:
                schema_name = from_match.group(1)
                table_name = from_match.group(2)
            else:
                # Unqualified: FROM table  (skip subqueries like  FROM ( … ))
                from_match = re.search(
                    r'\bFROM\s+(?!\s*\()["\']?(\w+)["\']?',
                    work_query, re.IGNORECASE
                )
                if from_match:
                    table_name = from_match.group(1)

        # Capture alias for the FROM table  (FROM tbl AS a  or  FROM tbl a)
        if table_name:
            alias_pattern = (
                r'\bFROM\s+["\']?\w+["\']?'
                r'(?:\s*\.\s*["\']?\w+["\']?)*'
                r'\s+(?:AS\s+)?([a-zA-Z_]\w*)\b'
            )
            alias_match = re.search(alias_pattern, work_query, re.IGNORECASE)
            if alias_match:
                candidate = alias_match.group(1).upper()
                _sql_keywords = {
                    'JOIN', 'INNER', 'LEFT', 'RIGHT', 'FULL', 'CROSS',
                    'NATURAL', 'WHERE', 'ORDER', 'GROUP', 'HAVING',
                    'LIMIT', 'UNION', 'ON', 'SET', 'VALUES', 'INTO',
                    'OFFSET', 'FETCH', 'WINDOW', 'PARTITION', 'RETURNING',
                    'UNNEST',
                }
                if candidate not in _sql_keywords:
                    alias_map[alias_match.group(1)] = table_name

        # If no FROM found, try INSERT INTO, UPDATE, DELETE FROM
        # Helper to extract table from dotted reference (handles 1/2/3-part names)
        def _extract_table_parts(ref: str):
            parts = ref.split('.')
            tbl = parts[-1]
            sch = parts[-2] if len(parts) >= 2 else None
            return tbl, sch

        if not table_name:
            insert_match = re.search(
                r'\bINSERT\s+INTO\s+(\w+(?:\.\w+)*)',
                clean_query, re.IGNORECASE)
            if insert_match:
                table_name, sch = _extract_table_parts(insert_match.group(1))
                if sch:
                    schema_name = sch
        if not table_name:
            update_match = re.search(
                r'\bUPDATE\s+(\w+(?:\.\w+)*)',
                clean_query, re.IGNORECASE)
            if update_match:
                table_name, sch = _extract_table_parts(update_match.group(1))
                if sch:
                    schema_name = sch
        if not table_name:
            delete_match = re.search(
                r'\bDELETE\s+FROM\s+(\w+(?:\.\w+)*)',
                clean_query, re.IGNORECASE)
            if delete_match:
                table_name, sch = _extract_table_parts(delete_match.group(1))
                if sch:
                    schema_name = sch

        # ── Extract JOIN clauses ──────────────────────────────────────
        join_pattern = re.compile(
            r'\b(INNER|LEFT\s+OUTER|LEFT|RIGHT\s+OUTER|RIGHT|FULL\s+OUTER|FULL|CROSS|NATURAL)?\s*'
            r'JOIN\s+(\w+(?:\.\w+)*)'                         # target table ref (may be dotted: project.dataset.table)
            r'(?:\s+(?:AS\s+)?([a-zA-Z_]\w*))?'               # optional alias
            r'(?:\s+ON\s+(.*?))?'                              # optional ON clause (CROSS JOIN has none)
            r'(?=\s+(?:INNER|LEFT|RIGHT|FULL|CROSS|NATURAL)?\s*\bJOIN\b|\bWHERE\b|\bGROUP\b|\bORDER\b|\bLIMIT\b|\bHAVING\b|\bUNION\b|;|\Z)',
            re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL
        )
        for jm in join_pattern.finditer(work_query):
            join_type_raw = (jm.group(1) or "INNER").strip().upper()
            join_type = join_type_raw.replace(" OUTER", "")

            # Parse dotted table reference (project.dataset.table or schema.table or table)
            table_ref_parts = jm.group(2).split('.')
            target_table = table_ref_parts[-1]

            target_alias = jm.group(3) or target_table
            alias_map[target_alias] = target_table

            on_clause = (jm.group(4) or "").strip().rstrip(';').strip()

            # Parse ON condition:  a.col = b.col
            source_column = ""
            target_column = ""
            if on_clause:
                on_match = re.search(
                    r'([a-zA-Z_]\w*)\.([a-zA-Z_]\w*)\s*=\s*([a-zA-Z_]\w*)\.([a-zA-Z_]\w*)',
                    on_clause
                )
                if on_match:
                    left_alias = on_match.group(1)
                    left_col = on_match.group(2)
                    right_alias = on_match.group(3)
                    right_col = on_match.group(4)

                    left_table = alias_map.get(left_alias, left_alias)
                    right_table = alias_map.get(right_alias, right_alias)

                    if left_table == target_table:
                        target_column = left_col
                        source_column = right_col
                    else:
                        source_column = left_col
                        target_column = right_col

            # Resolve aliases in the ON clause to real table names
            resolved_on = on_clause
            for al, tbl in alias_map.items():
                resolved_on = re.sub(r'\b' + re.escape(al) + r'\.', tbl + '.', resolved_on)

            # Determine if an alias is meaningful (differs from table name)
            effective_alias = target_alias if target_alias != target_table else ""
            # Also capture the base table alias for source_alias
            base_alias = ""
            for al, tbl in alias_map.items():
                if tbl == table_name and al != table_name:
                    base_alias = al
                    break

            joins.append({
                "join_type": join_type,
                "target_table": target_table,
                "target_column": target_column,
                "source_column": source_column,
                "name": f"{table_name}-to-{target_table}" + (f"-{effective_alias}" if effective_alias else ""),
                "raw_on_clause": resolved_on if (not source_column or not target_column) else "",
                "alias": effective_alias,
                "source_alias": base_alias,
            })

        # ── Extract selected columns ──────────────────────────────────
        # Also build select_column_aliases: [{expr, output_alias}] for columns
        # that use "alias.col AS name" (e.g. inc_mgr.email AS incident_manager_email)
        select_column_aliases = []  # [{expr: "alias.col", output_alias: "name"}, ...]
        if query_type in ("SELECT", "WITH"):
            # Use the *original* query so WITH…SELECT columns are captured
            # but prefer the last SELECT for CTE queries
            col_query = work_query if query_type == "WITH" else clean_query
            select_match = re.search(
                r'\bSELECT\s+(.*?)\s+FROM\b',
                col_query, re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL
            )
            if select_match:
                cols_str = select_match.group(1).strip()
                # Strip leading DISTINCT / ALL keywords
                cols_str = re.sub(r'^(?:DISTINCT|ALL)\s+', '', cols_str, flags=re.IGNORECASE).strip()

                if cols_str != '*':
                    # Smart split by top-level commas (respects parentheses)
                    raw_cols = []
                    depth = 0
                    buf = []
                    for ch in cols_str:
                        if ch == '(':
                            depth += 1
                            buf.append(ch)
                        elif ch == ')':
                            depth -= 1
                            buf.append(ch)
                        elif ch == ',' and depth == 0:
                            raw_cols.append(''.join(buf).strip())
                            buf = []
                        else:
                            buf.append(ch)
                    if buf:
                        raw_cols.append(''.join(buf).strip())

                    for rc in raw_cols:
                        # Detect output alias: ... AS output_name
                        output_alias = ""
                        as_match = re.search(r'\s+AS\s+(\w+)\s*$', rc, re.IGNORECASE)
                        if as_match:
                            output_alias = as_match.group(1)

                        # Strip the AS alias for column analysis
                        col = re.sub(r'\s+AS\s+\w+', '', rc, flags=re.IGNORECASE).strip()
                        # Skip function calls: COUNT(*), SUM(x), COALESCE(a,b), etc.
                        if re.match(r'^[A-Za-z_]\w*\s*\(', col):
                            continue
                        # Skip CASE expressions
                        if col.upper().startswith('CASE'):
                            continue
                        # Skip sub-selects
                        if col.upper().lstrip().startswith('('):
                            continue
                        # Skip arithmetic / expressions (contain operators)
                        if re.search(r'[+\-*/]', col):
                            continue

                        # Resolve alias-qualified columns
                        if '.' in col:
                            parts = col.split('.', 1)
                            prefix = parts[0].strip().strip('"').strip("'")
                            col_name = parts[1].strip().strip('"').strip("'")
                            real_table = alias_map.get(prefix, prefix)
                            if real_table == table_name:
                                # Base table column — use bare name
                                final_col = col_name
                            else:
                                # Joined table column — find the alias for this table
                                # to preserve which specific JOIN instance it comes from
                                alias_for_join = prefix if prefix in alias_map and prefix != real_table else ""
                                if alias_for_join:
                                    final_col = f"{alias_for_join}.{col_name}"
                                else:
                                    final_col = f"{real_table}.{col_name}"
                        else:
                            final_col = col.strip().strip('"').strip("'")

                        # Final guard: parts must be valid identifiers
                        check_parts = final_col.split('.')
                        if all(p and re.match(r'^[A-Za-z_]\w*$', p) for p in check_parts):
                            if final_col not in columns_used:
                                columns_used.append(final_col)
                            if output_alias:
                                select_column_aliases.append({
                                    "expr": final_col,
                                    "output_alias": output_alias,
                                })

        # ── Extract WHERE clause ──────────────────────────────────────
        where_clause = ""
        where_conditions = []
        if query_type in ("SELECT", "WITH"):
            where_src = work_query if query_type == "WITH" else clean_query
            # Grab everything after WHERE up to the next top-level clause or end of query
            where_match = re.search(
                r'\bWHERE\s+(.+?)(?:\s+(?:GROUP\s+BY|HAVING|ORDER\s+BY|LIMIT|UNION|OFFSET|FETCH|WINDOW)\b|;|\s*$)',
                where_src, re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL
            )
            if where_match:
                where_clause = where_match.group(1).strip().rstrip(';').strip()
                # Parse individual conditions from the WHERE clause
                # Pass alias_map so joined-table columns keep their table qualifier
                where_conditions = _parse_where_conditions(where_clause, alias_map=alias_map, base_table=table_name)

        # ── Fetch actual table columns from the database ──────────────
        actual_columns = []
        if database and table_name:
            try:
                if is_bigquery and bq_client:
                    # BigQuery: use bq_client to get table schema
                    bq_dataset = schema_name if schema_name != "public" else database
                    table_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{bq_dataset}.{table_name}"
                    bq_table_obj = bq_client.get_table(table_ref)
                    actual_columns = [field.name for field in bq_table_obj.schema]
                else:
                    # CloudSQL / PostgreSQL
                    connection = psycopg2.connect(
                        host=DB_HOST, database=database,
                        user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5,
                    )
                    cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
                    cursor.execute("""
                        SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns
                        WHERE table_schema = %s AND table_name = %s
                        ORDER BY ordinal_position
                    """, (schema_name, table_name))
                    actual_columns = [r["column_name"] for r in cursor.fetchall()]
                    connection.close()
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Build base table alias for frontend
        base_table_alias = ""
        for al, tbl in alias_map.items():
            if tbl == table_name and al != table_name:
                base_table_alias = al
                break

        return {
            "success": True,
            "table_name": table_name,
            "schema_name": schema_name,
            "columns_used": columns_used,
            "actual_columns": actual_columns,
            "query_type": query_type,
            "database": database,
            "db_type": type,
            "joins": joins,
            "where_clause": where_clause,
            "where_conditions": where_conditions,
            "select_column_aliases": select_column_aliases,
            "base_table_alias": base_table_alias,
        }
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error parsing query: {e}")
        return {
            "success": False,
            "table_name": table_name,
            "schema_name": schema_name,
            "columns_used": [],
            "actual_columns": [],
            "query_type": query_type,
            "database": database,
            "db_type": type,
            "joins": [],
            "where_clause": "",
            "where_conditions": [],
            "error": str(e),
        }


@app.get("/api/tables")
async def list_tables(database: str = Query(..., description="Database name"), type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$"), search: str = Query(None)):
    """List all tables in a database or BigQuery dataset"""
    connection = None
    try:
        if type == "bigquery":
            tables = get_bigquery_tables(database, search)
        else:  # cloudsql
            connection = get_db_connection(database)
            cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
            
            query = """
            SELECT 
                table_schema,
                table_name,
                (SELECT pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size(quote_ident(table_schema) || '.' || quote_ident(table_name)))) as size
            FROM information_schema.tables
            WHERE table_schema NOT IN ('pg_catalog', 'information_schema')
            ORDER BY table_schema, table_name
            """
            
            cursor.execute(query)
            results = cursor.fetchall()
            
            tables = [dict(row) for row in results]
            
            # Filter by search if provided
            if search:
                tables = [t for t in tables if search.lower() in t['table_name'].lower()]
        
        return {
            "success": True,
            "database": database,
            "tables": tables,
            "count": len(tables)
        }
        
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error listing tables: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error listing tables: {str(e)}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.get("/api/table/schema")
async def get_table_schema(
    database: str = Query(..., description="Database name"),
    table_name: str = Query(..., description="Table name"),
    schema: str = Query("public", description="Schema name"),
    type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$"),
):
    """Get schema information for a specific table"""
    if type == "bigquery":
        try:
            table_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{database}.{table_name}"
            bq_table = bq_client.get_table(table_ref)
            columns = []
            for field in bq_table.schema:
                columns.append({
