                    "column_name": field.name,
                    "data_type": field.field_type,
                    "character_maximum_length": None,
                    "is_nullable": "YES" if field.mode != "REQUIRED" else "NO",
                    "column_default": None,
                })
            return {
                "success": True,
                "database": database,
                "schema": database,
                "table": table_name,
                "columns": columns,
                "count": len(columns),
            }
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error getting BigQuery table schema: {e}")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error getting table schema: {str(e)}")

    connection = None
    try:
        connection = get_db_connection(database)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        
        query = """
        SELECT 
            column_name,
            data_type,
            character_maximum_length,
            is_nullable,
            column_default
        FROM information_schema.columns
        WHERE table_schema = %s AND table_name = %s
        ORDER BY ordinal_position
        """
        
        cursor.execute(query, (schema, table_name))
        results = cursor.fetchall()
        
        columns = [dict(row) for row in results]
        
        return {
            "success": True,
            "database": database,
            "schema": schema,
            "table": table_name,
            "columns": columns,
            "count": len(columns)
        }
        
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error getting table schema: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error getting table schema: {str(e)}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.post("/api/chat", response_model=ChatResponse)
async def chat_with_ai(request: ChatRequest):
    """Chat with Gemini AI for SQL/BigQuery support"""
    logger.info(f"Chatbot request: action={request.action}, message={request.message[:50]}...")
    
    result = get_chatbot_response(
        user_message=request.message,
        selected_query=request.selected_query,
        database=request.database,
        table=request.table,
        action=request.action or "chat"
    )
    
    if not result["success"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=result["message"])
    
    return ChatResponse(**result)


# ── CRUD Endpoints ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


@app.get("/api/table/primary-key")
async def get_primary_key(
    database: str = Query(...),
    table_name: str = Query(...),
    schema: str = Query("public"),
    type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$"),
):
    """Return the primary-key columns for a table."""
    if type == "bigquery":
        # BigQuery tables do not have traditional primary keys
        return {"success": True, "primary_keys": []}

    connection = None
    try:
        connection = get_db_connection(database)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT a.attname AS column_name
            FROM   pg_index i
            JOIN   pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid = i.indrelid AND a.attnum = ANY(i.indkey)
            WHERE  i.indrelid = (quote_ident(%s) || '.' || quote_ident(%s))::regclass
            AND    i.indisprimary;
            """,
            (schema, table_name),
        )
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        pk_cols = [r["column_name"] for r in rows]
        return {"success": True, "primary_keys": pk_cols}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error fetching primary key: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.put("/api/row")
async def update_row(request: RowUpdateRequest, type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$")):
    """Update a single row identified by its primary key."""
    if type == "bigquery":
        try:
            set_clause = ", ".join(
                f"`{col}` = @{col}" for col in request.updates
            )
            where_clause = " AND ".join(
                f"`{col}` = @pk_{col}" for col in request.primary_key
            )
            query = f"UPDATE `{request.table}` SET {set_clause} WHERE {where_clause}"

            job_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(
                query_parameters=[
                    bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter(col, "STRING", str(val))
                    for col, val in request.updates.items()
                ]
                + [
                    bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter(f"pk_{col}", "STRING", str(val))
                    for col, val in request.primary_key.items()
                ]
            )
            bq_client.query(query, job_config=job_config).result()
            return {"success": True, "message": "Row updated (BigQuery)"}
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"BigQuery update error: {e}")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

    # Cloud SQL / PostgreSQL
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = get_db_connection(request.database)
        cursor = connection.cursor()

        set_parts = []
        values = []
        for col, val in request.updates.items():
            set_parts.append(f'"{col}" = %s')
            values.append(val)

        where_parts = []
        for col, val in request.primary_key.items():
            where_parts.append(f'"{col}" = %s')
            values.append(val)

        qualified = f'"{request.schema_name}"."{request.table}"'
        sql = f"UPDATE {qualified} SET {', '.join(set_parts)} WHERE {' AND '.join(where_parts)}"

        cursor.execute(sql, values)
        connection.commit()
        affected = cursor.rowcount

        return {"success": True, "message": f"{affected} row(s) updated", "affected_rows": affected}
    except Exception as e:
        if connection:
            connection.rollback()
        logger.error(f"Update error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.post("/api/row")
async def insert_rows(request: RowInsertRequest, type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$")):
    """Insert one or more rows into a table."""
    if not request.rows:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No rows provided")

    if type == "bigquery":
        try:
            table_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{request.database}.{request.table}"
            errors = bq_client.insert_rows_json(table_ref, request.rows)
            if errors:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(errors))
            return {"success": True, "message": f"{len(request.rows)} row(s) inserted (BigQuery)"}
        except HTTPException:
            raise
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"BigQuery insert error: {e}")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

    # Cloud SQL / PostgreSQL
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = get_db_connection(request.database)
        cursor = connection.cursor()

        columns = list(request.rows[0].keys())
        col_str = ", ".join(f'"{c}"' for c in columns)
        placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(columns))
        qualified = f'"{request.schema_name}"."{request.table}"'
        sql = f"INSERT INTO {qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders})"

        total = 0
        for row in request.rows:
            vals = [row.get(c) for c in columns]
            cursor.execute(sql, vals)
            total += cursor.rowcount

        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True, "message": f"{total} row(s) inserted", "affected_rows": total}
    except Exception as e:
        if connection:
            connection.rollback()
        logger.error(f"Insert error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.delete("/api/rows")
async def delete_rows(request: RowDeleteRequest, type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$")):
    """Delete rows by primary key values."""
    if not request.primary_key_values:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No primary key values provided")

    if type == "bigquery":
        try:
            placeholders = ", ".join(f"@v{i}" for i in range(len(request.primary_key_values)))
            query = f"DELETE FROM `{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{request.database}.{request.table}` WHERE `{request.primary_key_column}` IN ({placeholders})"
            params = [
                bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter(f"v{i}", "STRING", str(v))
                for i, v in enumerate(request.primary_key_values)
            ]
            job_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(query_parameters=params)
            bq_client.query(query, job_config=job_config).result()
            return {"success": True, "message": f"Rows deleted (BigQuery)"}
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"BigQuery delete error: {e}")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

    # Cloud SQL / PostgreSQL
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = get_db_connection(request.database)
        cursor = connection.cursor()

        qualified = f'"{request.schema_name}"."{request.table}"'
        placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(request.primary_key_values))
        sql = f'DELETE FROM {qualified} WHERE "{request.primary_key_column}" IN ({placeholders})'

        cursor.execute(sql, request.primary_key_values)
        connection.commit()
        affected = cursor.rowcount

        return {"success": True, "message": f"{affected} row(s) deleted", "affected_rows": affected}
    except Exception as e:
        if connection:
            connection.rollback()
        logger.error(f"Delete error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def get_chatbot_response(user_message: str, selected_query: Optional[str] = None, 
                        database: Optional[str] = None, table: Optional[str] = None,
                        action: str = "chat") -> Dict:
    """
    Get response from Gemini AI for SQL/BigQuery support
    """
    try:
        model = GenerativeModel(GEMINI_MODEL)
        
        # Build the context-aware prompt
        context = "You are an expert SQL and BigQuery assistant. Help users write, fix, and optimize SQL queries."
        
        if selected_query:
            context += f"\n\nCurrent query:\n```sql\n{selected_query}\n```"
        
        if database:
            context += f"\nDatabase: {database}"
        
        if table:
            context += f"\nTable: {table}"
        
        # Action-specific prompts
        if action == "fix":
            prompt = f"{context}\n\nPlease fix any syntax errors in the query above. Provide:\n1. The corrected query\n2. Explanation of what was wrong\n3. The fixed query wrapped in ```sql tags"
        elif action == "enhance":
            prompt = f"{context}\n\nPlease enhance/optimize the query above. Provide:\n1. Performance suggestions\n2. The optimized query\n3. Explanation of improvements wrapped in ```sql tags"
        elif action == "suggest":
            prompt = f"{context}\n\nBased on the query above, provide:\n1. Best practices for this type of query\n2. Common mistakes to avoid\n3. Suggestions for next optimizations"
        elif action == "explain":
            prompt = f"{context}\n\nPlease explain the query above:\n1. What it does step by step\n2. Which tables and columns it uses\n3. What the output represents"
        else:  # chat
            prompt = f"{context}\n\nUser question: {user_message}"
        
        # Set safety settings
        safety_settings = [
            SafetySetting(
                category=HarmCategory.HARM_CATEGORY_HATE_SPEECH,
                threshold=HarmBlockThreshold.BLOCK_NONE,
            ),
            SafetySetting(
                category=HarmCategory.HARM_CATEGORY_DANGEROUS_CONTENT,
                threshold=HarmBlockThreshold.BLOCK_NONE,
            ),
            SafetySetting(
                category=HarmCategory.HARM_CATEGORY_SEXUALLY_EXPLICIT,
                threshold=HarmBlockThreshold.BLOCK_MEDIUM_AND_ABOVE,
            ),
            SafetySetting(
                category=HarmCategory.HARM_CATEGORY_HARASSMENT,
                threshold=HarmBlockThreshold.BLOCK_MEDIUM_AND_ABOVE,
            ),
        ]
        
        # Generate response
        response = model.generate_content(
            prompt,
            safety_settings=safety_settings
        )
        
        response_text = response.text
        
        # Extract suggested query if present
        suggested_query = None
        if "```sql" in response_text:
            start = response_text.find("```sql") + 6
            end = response_text.find("```", start)
            if end > start:
                suggested_query = response_text[start:end].strip()
        
        logger.info(f"Chatbot response generated for action: {action}")
        
        return {
            "success": True,
            "message": response_text,
            "action": action,
            "suggested_query": suggested_query,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error getting chatbot response: {e}")
        return {
            "success": False,
            "message": f"Error: {str(e)}",
            "action": action,
            "error_details": str(e),
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  DYNAMIC CRUD  –  /api/crud/{database}/{table}
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════


def _apply_function_layers(data: List[Dict], layers: List[Dict], phase: str = "post") -> List[Dict]:
    """Apply function layers to data.  phase = 'pre' (before query) or 'post' (after query)."""
    sorted_layers = sorted(layers, key=lambda l: l.get("order", 0))
    for layer in sorted_layers:
        if not layer.get("enabled", True):
            continue
        ltype = layer.get("type", "")
        config = layer.get("config", {})

        if ltype == "filter" and phase == "post":
            # config: {"column": "status", "operator": "eq", "value": "active"}
            col = config.get("column")
            op = config.get("operator", "eq")
            val = config.get("value")
            if col and val is not None:
                if op == "eq":
                    data = [r for r in data if str(r.get(col)) == str(val)]
                elif op == "neq":
                    data = [r for r in data if str(r.get(col)) != str(val)]
                elif op == "contains":
                    data = [r for r in data if str(val).lower() in str(r.get(col, "")).lower()]
                elif op == "gt":
                    data = [r for r in data if r.get(col) is not None and float(r.get(col, 0)) > float(val)]
                elif op == "lt":
                    data = [r for r in data if r.get(col) is not None and float(r.get(col, 0)) < float(val)]

        elif ltype == "transform" and phase == "post":
            # config: {"column": "email", "action": "lowercase"}
            col = config.get("column")
            action = config.get("action", "")
            if col:
                for row in data:
                    if col in row and row[col] is not None:
                        if action == "lowercase":
                            row[col] = str(row[col]).lower()
                        elif action == "uppercase":
                            row[col] = str(row[col]).upper()
                        elif action == "trim":
                            row[col] = str(row[col]).strip()
                        elif action == "mask":
                            val_str = str(row[col])
                            row[col] = val_str[:2] + "*" * max(0, len(val_str) - 4) + val_str[-2:] if len(val_str) > 4 else "****"
                        elif action == "hash":
                            import hashlib
                            row[col] = hashlib.sha256(str(row[col]).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]

        elif ltype == "aggregate" and phase == "post":
            # config: {"group_by": "department", "agg_column": "salary", "agg_func": "avg"}
            group_col = config.get("group_by")
            agg_col = config.get("agg_column")
            agg_func = config.get("agg_func", "count")
            if group_col and agg_col:
                groups = {}
                for row in data:
                    key = row.get(group_col, "NULL")
                    if key not in groups:
                        groups[key] = []
                    groups[key].append(row.get(agg_col, 0))
                result = []
                for key, vals in groups.items():
                    nums = [float(v) for v in vals if v is not None]
                    agg_val = 0
                    if agg_func == "sum":
                        agg_val = sum(nums)
                    elif agg_func == "avg":
                        agg_val = sum(nums) / len(nums) if nums else 0
                    elif agg_func == "count":
                        agg_val = len(vals)
                    elif agg_func == "min":
                        agg_val = min(nums) if nums else 0
                    elif agg_func == "max":
                        agg_val = max(nums) if nums else 0
                    result.append({group_col: key, f"{agg_func}_{agg_col}": round(agg_val, 2)})
                data = result

        elif ltype == "validate" and phase == "pre":
            # Validation happens before insert/update  — handled separately
            pass

    return data


@app.get("/api/crud/{database}/{table}")
async def crud_read(
    database: str = Path(...),
    table: str = Path(...),
    type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$"),
    schema: str = Query("public"),
    limit: int = Query(100, ge=1, le=10000),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
    sort_by: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Column to sort by"),
    sort_order: str = Query("asc", pattern="^(asc|desc)$"),
    filters: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="JSON-encoded filters: [{col, op, val}]"),
    columns: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Comma-separated column names to select"),
    server_id: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Server ID for custom PostgreSQL connections"),
):
    """Generic READ for any database/table with filtering, sorting, pagination."""
    start_time = datetime.now()

    try:
        # Build SELECT columns
        select_cols = "*"
        if columns:
            col_list = [c.strip() for c in columns.split(",") if c.strip()]
            if type == "bigquery":
                select_cols = ", ".join(f"`{c}`" for c in col_list)
            else:
                select_cols = ", ".join(f'"{c}"' for c in col_list)

        # Build WHERE clause from filters
        where_clause = ""
        where_values = []
        if filters:
            try:
                filter_list = json.loads(filters)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid filters JSON")
            conditions = []
            for f in filter_list:
                col, op, val = f.get("col"), f.get("op", "eq"), f.get("val")
                if not col:
                    continue
                if type == "bigquery":
                    col_ref = f"`{col}`"
                else:
                    col_ref = f'"{col}"'

                if op == "eq":
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} = %s" if type != "bigquery" else f"{col_ref} = @p{len(conditions)}")
                    where_values.append(val)
                elif op == "neq":
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} != %s" if type != "bigquery" else f"{col_ref} != @p{len(conditions)}")
                    where_values.append(val)
                elif op == "gt":
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} > %s" if type != "bigquery" else f"{col_ref} > @p{len(conditions)}")
                    where_values.append(val)
                elif op == "lt":
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} < %s" if type != "bigquery" else f"{col_ref} < @p{len(conditions)}")
                    where_values.append(val)
                elif op == "like":
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} ILIKE %s" if type != "bigquery" else f"LOWER({col_ref}) LIKE LOWER(@p{len(conditions)})")
                    where_values.append(f"%{val}%")
                elif op == "in":
                    vals = val if isinstance(val, list) else [val]
                    ph = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(vals)) if type != "bigquery" else ", ".join([f"@p{len(conditions)+i}" for i in range(len(vals))])
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} IN ({ph})")
                    where_values.extend(vals)
                elif op == "is_null":
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} IS NULL")
                elif op == "is_not_null":
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} IS NOT NULL")

            if conditions:
                where_clause = "WHERE " + " AND ".join(conditions)

        # Build ORDER BY
        order_clause = ""
        if sort_by:
            col_ref = f'"{sort_by}"' if type != "bigquery" else f"`{sort_by}`"
            order_clause = f"ORDER BY {col_ref} {sort_order.upper()}"

        if type == "bigquery":
            table_ref = f"`{database}.{table}`"
            query = f"SELECT {select_cols} FROM {table_ref} {where_clause} {order_clause} LIMIT {limit} OFFSET {offset}"

            # Replace %s placeholders with BigQuery params
            bq_params = []
            for i, v in enumerate(where_values):
                bq_params.append(bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter(f"p{i}", "STRING", str(v)))

            job_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(query_parameters=bq_params) if bq_params else None
            if not bq_params and database:
                job_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(
                    default_dataset=f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{database}"
                )

            results = bq_client.query(query, job_config=job_config).result()
            data = [dict(row) for row in results]
            cols = [field.name for field in results.schema]
        else:
            table_ref = f'"{schema}"."{table}"'
            query = f"SELECT {select_cols} FROM {table_ref} {where_clause} {order_clause} LIMIT {limit} OFFSET {offset}"

            # Use server-aware connection if server_id is set
            if server_id:
                connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
            else:
                connection = get_db_connection(database)
            try:
                cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
                cursor.execute(query, where_values if where_values else None)
                data = [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
                cols = [desc[0] for desc in cursor.description] if cursor.description else []
            finally:
                connection.close()

        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000

        return {
            "success": True,
            "data": data,
            "columns": cols,
            "row_count": len(data),
            "execution_time_ms": round(execution_time, 2),
            "endpoint": f"/api/crud/{database}/{table}",
            "database": database,
            "table": table,
        }

    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"CRUD read error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@app.post("/api/crud/{database}/{table}")
async def crud_create(
    database: str = Path(...),
    table: str = Path(...),
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$"),
    schema: str = Query("public"),
):
    """Generic INSERT for any database/table."""
    if not rows:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Request body must be a JSON array of row objects")

    if type == "bigquery":
        try:
            table_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{database}.{table}"
            errors = bq_client.insert_rows_json(table_ref, rows)
            if errors:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(errors))
            return {"success": True, "message": f"{len(rows)} row(s) inserted", "affected_rows": len(rows)}
        except HTTPException:
            raise
        except Exception as e:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

    connection = None
    try:
        connection = get_db_connection(database)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        columns = list(rows[0].keys())
        col_str = ", ".join(f'"{c}"' for c in columns)
        placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(columns))
        qualified = f'"{schema}"."{table}"'
        sql = f"INSERT INTO {qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders})"
        total = 0
        for row in rows:
            cursor.execute(sql, [row.get(c) for c in columns])
            total += cursor.rowcount
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True, "message": f"{total} row(s) inserted", "affected_rows": total}
    except Exception as e:
        if connection:
            connection.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.put("/api/crud/{database}/{table}")
async def crud_update(
    database: str = Path(...),
    table: str = Path(...),
    primary_key: Dict[str, Any] = None,
    updates: Dict[str, Any] = None,
    type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$"),
    schema: str = Query("public"),
):
    """Generic UPDATE for any database/table. Body: { primary_key: {...}, updates: {...} }"""
    from fastapi import Request
    if not primary_key or not updates:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Body must include 'primary_key' and 'updates'")

    if type == "bigquery":
        try:
            set_clause = ", ".join(f"`{col}` = @u{i}" for i, col in enumerate(updates))
            where_clause = " AND ".join(f"`{col}` = @pk{i}" for i, col in enumerate(primary_key))
            query = f"UPDATE `{database}.{table}` SET {set_clause} WHERE {where_clause}"
            params = [bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter(f"u{i}", "STRING", str(v)) for i, (_, v) in enumerate(updates.items())]
            params += [bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter(f"pk{i}", "STRING", str(v)) for i, (_, v) in enumerate(primary_key.items())]
            job_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(query_parameters=params)
            bq_client.query(query, job_config=job_config).result()
            return {"success": True, "message": "Row updated (BigQuery)"}
        except Exception as e:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

    connection = None
    try:
        connection = get_db_connection(database)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        set_parts, values = [], []
        for col, val in updates.items():
            set_parts.append(f'"{col}" = %s')
            values.append(val)
        where_parts = []
        for col, val in primary_key.items():
            where_parts.append(f'"{col}" = %s')
            values.append(val)
        sql = f'UPDATE "{schema}"."{table}" SET {", ".join(set_parts)} WHERE {" AND ".join(where_parts)}'
        cursor.execute(sql, values)
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True, "message": f"{cursor.rowcount} row(s) updated", "affected_rows": cursor.rowcount}
    except Exception as e:
        if connection:
            connection.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.delete("/api/crud/{database}/{table}")
async def crud_delete(
    database: str = Path(...),
    table: str = Path(...),
    pk_column: str = Query(..., description="Primary key column name"),
    pk_values: str = Query(..., description="Comma-separated PK values to delete"),
    type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$"),
    schema: str = Query("public"),
):
    """Generic DELETE for any database/table."""
    values = [v.strip() for v in pk_values.split(",") if v.strip()]
    if not values:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No primary key values provided")

    if type == "bigquery":
        try:
            placeholders = ", ".join(f"@v{i}" for i in range(len(values)))
            query = f"DELETE FROM `{database}.{table}` WHERE `{pk_column}` IN ({placeholders})"
            params = [bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter(f"v{i}", "STRING", str(v)) for i, v in enumerate(values)]
            job_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(query_parameters=params)
            bq_client.query(query, job_config=job_config).result()
            return {"success": True, "message": f"Rows deleted (BigQuery)"}
        except Exception as e:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

    connection = None
    try:
        connection = get_db_connection(database)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(values))
        sql = f'DELETE FROM "{schema}"."{table}" WHERE "{pk_column}" IN ({placeholders})'
        cursor.execute(sql, values)
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True, "message": f"{cursor.rowcount} row(s) deleted", "affected_rows": cursor.rowcount}
    except Exception as e:
        if connection:
            connection.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  RELATIONSHIP APIs  –  Join tables within or across databases
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════


@app.post("/api/builder/relationships")
async def create_relationship(rel: RelationshipDef):
    """Save a table-to-table relationship definition."""
    rel.id = rel.id or str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
    _relationship_store[rel.id] = rel.model_dump()
    logger.info(f"Relationship created: {rel.id} – {rel.name}")
    return {"success": True, "relationship": _relationship_store[rel.id]}


@app.get("/api/builder/relationships")
async def list_relationships():
    """List all saved relationships."""
    return {"success": True, "relationships": list(_relationship_store.values()), "count": len(_relationship_store)}


@app.delete("/api/builder/relationships/{rel_id}")
async def delete_relationship(rel_id: str = Path(...)):
    """Delete a relationship definition."""
    if rel_id not in _relationship_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Relationship not found")
    deleted = _relationship_store.pop(rel_id)
    return {"success": True, "deleted": deleted}


@app.post("/api/builder/relationships/{rel_id}/query")
async def execute_relationship_query(
    rel_id: str = Path(...),
    limit: int = Query(100, ge=1, le=10000),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
    filters: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    columns: Optional[str] = Query(None),
):
    """Execute a JOIN query based on a saved relationship."""
    if rel_id not in _relationship_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Relationship not found")

    rel = _relationship_store[rel_id]
    start_time = datetime.now()

    try:
        same_db = (rel["source_database"] == rel["target_database"] and
                   rel["source_db_type"] == rel["target_db_type"])

        if same_db and rel["source_db_type"] == "cloudsql":
            # Same PostgreSQL database – standard JOIN
            s_table = f'"public"."{rel["source_table"]}"'
            t_table = f'"public"."{rel["target_table"]}"'
            select_cols = columns if columns else f'{s_table}.*, {t_table}.*'

            join_type = rel.get("join_type", "INNER").upper()
            query = (
                f"SELECT {select_cols} FROM {s_table} "
                f"{join_type} JOIN {t_table} "
                f'ON {s_table}."{rel["source_column"]}" = {t_table}."{rel["target_column"]}" '
                f"LIMIT {limit} OFFSET {offset}"
            )
            connection = get_db_connection(rel["source_database"])
            try:
                cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
                cursor.execute(query)
                data = [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
                cols = [desc[0] for desc in cursor.description] if cursor.description else []
            finally:
                connection.close()

        elif same_db and rel["source_db_type"] == "bigquery":
            # Same BigQuery dataset – standard JOIN
            s_table = f'`{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{rel["source_database"]}.{rel["source_table"]}`'
            t_table = f'`{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{rel["target_database"]}.{rel["target_table"]}`'
            select_cols = columns if columns else f'{s_table}.*, {t_table}.*'
            join_type = rel.get("join_type", "INNER").upper()
            query = (
                f"SELECT {select_cols} FROM {s_table} "
                f"{join_type} JOIN {t_table} "
                f'ON {s_table}.{rel["source_column"]} = {t_table}.{rel["target_column"]} '
                f"LIMIT {limit} OFFSET {offset}"
            )
            results = bq_client.query(query).result()
            data = [dict(row) for row in results]
            cols = [field.name for field in results.schema]

        else:
            # Cross-database join – fetch from both sides, join in Python
            # Fetch source data
            if rel["source_db_type"] == "bigquery":
                s_query = f'SELECT * FROM `{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{rel["source_database"]}.{rel["source_table"]}` LIMIT {limit * 2}'
                s_result = bq_client.query(s_query).result()
                source_data = [dict(row) for row in s_result]
            else:
                conn = get_db_connection(rel["source_database"])
                try:
                    cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
                    cur.execute(f'SELECT * FROM "public"."{rel["source_table"]}" LIMIT {limit * 2}')
                    source_data = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
                finally:
                    conn.close()

            # Fetch target data
            if rel["target_db_type"] == "bigquery":
                t_query = f'SELECT * FROM `{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{rel["target_database"]}.{rel["target_table"]}` LIMIT {limit * 2}'
                t_result = bq_client.query(t_query).result()
                target_data = [dict(row) for row in t_result]
            else:
                conn = get_db_connection(rel["target_database"])
                try:
                    cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
                    cur.execute(f'SELECT * FROM "public"."{rel["target_table"]}" LIMIT {limit * 2}')
                    target_data = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
                finally:
                    conn.close()

            # Python-side join
            target_index = {}
            for row in target_data:
                key = str(row.get(rel["target_column"], ""))
                if key not in target_index:
                    target_index[key] = []
                target_index[key].append(row)

            data = []
            join_type = rel.get("join_type", "INNER").upper()
            for s_row in source_data:
                key = str(s_row.get(rel["source_column"], ""))
                matches = target_index.get(key, [])
                if matches:
                    for t_row in matches:
                        merged = {f"src_{k}": v for k, v in s_row.items()}
                        merged.update({f"tgt_{k}": v for k, v in t_row.items()})
                        data.append(merged)
                elif join_type in ("LEFT", "FULL"):
                    merged = {f"src_{k}": v for k, v in s_row.items()}
                    merged.update({f"tgt_{k}": None for k in (target_data[0].keys() if target_data else [])})
                    data.append(merged)

            data = data[offset: offset + limit]
            cols = list(data[0].keys()) if data else []

        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        return {
            "success": True,
            "data": data,
            "columns": cols,
            "row_count": len(data),
            "execution_time_ms": round(execution_time, 2),
            "relationship": rel,
        }

    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Relationship query error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  CUSTOM API BUILDER  –  /api/builder/apis
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════


@app.post("/api/builder/apis")
async def create_custom_api(config: CustomAPIConfig, request: Request):
    """Create a new custom API endpoint configuration."""
    user_id = _get_user_id(request)
    config.id = config.id or str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
    config.created_at = datetime.now().isoformat()
    config.updated_at = config.created_at
    # Assign IDs to nested objects
    for r in config.relationships:
        r.id = r.id or str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
    for fl in config.function_layers:
        fl.id = fl.id or str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
    # Auto-generate encryption key if encryption enabled but no key given
    if config.encryption_enabled and not config.encryption_key:
        config.encryption_key = generate_fernet_key()
    # Validate alias uniqueness
    if config.public_alias:
        config.public_alias = config.public_alias.strip().lower().replace(' ', '-')
        for existing in _api_store.values():
            if existing.get("public_alias") == config.public_alias:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=f"Alias '{config.public_alias}' is already in use")
    api_data = config.model_dump()
    api_data["created_by"] = user_id
    _api_store[config.id] = api_data
    _save_api(api_data)
    logger.info(f"Custom API created by {user_id}: {config.id} – {config.name}")
    return {"success": True, "api": _api_store[config.id]}


@app.get("/api/builder/apis")
async def list_custom_apis(request: Request):
    """List custom APIs — scoped to user; super-admin sees all."""
    user_id = _get_user_id(request)
    if _is_super_admin(user_id):
        apis = list(_api_store.values())
    else:
        apis = [a for a in _api_store.values() if a.get("created_by", "") == user_id or a.get("created_by", "") == ""]
    return {"success": True, "apis": apis, "count": len(apis)}


@app.get("/api/builder/apis/{api_id}")
async def get_custom_api(api_id: str = Path(...)):
    """Get a single custom API configuration."""
    if api_id not in _api_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="API not found")
    return {"success": True, "api": _api_store[api_id]}


@app.put("/api/builder/apis/{api_id}")
async def update_custom_api(api_id: str = Path(...), config: CustomAPIConfig = None, request: Request = None):
    """Update an existing custom API configuration."""
    user_id = _get_user_id(request) if request else ""
    if api_id not in _api_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="API not found")
    owner = _api_store[api_id].get("created_by", "")
    if owner and not _is_super_admin(user_id) and owner != user_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not authorised to update this API")
    config.id = api_id
    config.created_at = _api_store[api_id].get("created_at")
    config.updated_at = datetime.now().isoformat()
    for r in config.relationships:
        r.id = r.id or str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
    for fl in config.function_layers:
        fl.id = fl.id or str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
    # Auto-generate encryption key if encryption enabled but no key given
    if config.encryption_enabled and not config.encryption_key:
        config.encryption_key = generate_fernet_key()
    # Validate alias uniqueness (exclude self)
    if config.public_alias:
        config.public_alias = config.public_alias.strip().lower().replace(' ', '-')
        for eid, existing in _api_store.items():
            if eid != api_id and existing.get("public_alias") == config.public_alias:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=f"Alias '{config.public_alias}' is already in use")
    api_data = config.model_dump()
    api_data["created_by"] = _api_store[api_id].get("created_by", user_id)
    _api_store[api_id] = api_data
    _save_api(api_data)
    return {"success": True, "api": _api_store[api_id]}


@app.delete("/api/builder/apis/{api_id}")
async def delete_custom_api(api_id: str = Path(...), request: Request = None):
    """Delete a custom API configuration."""
    user_id = _get_user_id(request) if request else ""
    if api_id not in _api_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="API not found")
    owner = _api_store[api_id].get("created_by", "")
    if owner and not _is_super_admin(user_id) and owner != user_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not authorised to delete this API")
    deleted = _api_store.pop(api_id)
    _delete_api_from_db(api_id)
    return {"success": True, "deleted": deleted}


# ── Helper: qualify bare table names in raw ON clauses for BigQuery ───
def _bq_qualify_on_clause(raw_on: str, schema_name: str, known_tables: set) -> str:
    """Replace bare  table_name.col  references with  `schema.table_name`.col
    inside a raw ON clause so BigQuery can resolve them.
    """
    result = raw_on
    # Process longest table names first to avoid partial replacements
    for tbl in sorted(known_tables, key=len, reverse=True):
        # Match  table_name.column_name  (word-boundary before table, dot + ident after)
        pattern = re.compile(r'\b' + re.escape(tbl) + r'\.([A-Za-z_]\w*)')
        result = pattern.sub(f'`{schema_name}.{tbl}`.\\1', result)
    return result


# ── Helper: discover which BigQuery table owns each column ───────────
def _bq_get_column_owners(schema_name: str, table_names: set) -> dict:
    """Query INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS to build {column_name: set(table_names)}.
    Used to correctly qualify bare column names in SELECT / WHERE when JOINs
    make a column potentially ambiguous.
    """
    if not table_names:
        return {}
    table_list = ", ".join(f"'{t}'" for t in table_names)
    query = (
        f"SELECT table_name, column_name "
        f"FROM `{schema_name}.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS` "
        f"WHERE table_name IN ({table_list})"
    )
    try:
        rows = bq_client.query(query).result()
        col_map: dict[str, set] = {}
        for row in rows:
            col_map.setdefault(row["column_name"], set()).add(row["table_name"])
        return col_map
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"INFORMATION_SCHEMA lookup failed: {e}")
        return {}


# ── Preview / Test API (without saving) ──────────────────────────────
@app.post("/api/builder/apis/preview")
async def preview_custom_api(
    config: CustomAPIConfig,
    limit: Optional[int] = Query(10, ge=1, le=1000),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
):
    """Execute a custom API config as a preview WITHOUT saving it.
    Returns the same format as the GET execute endpoint, but capped at 1000 rows.
    Useful for testing the API before creating it.
    """
    api_cfg = config.model_dump()
    db = api_cfg["database"]
    db_type = api_cfg.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    base_table = api_cfg["base_table"]
    schema_name = api_cfg.get("schema_name", "public")
    effective_limit = min(limit or api_cfg.get("default_limit", 100), 1000)
    select_cols = api_cfg.get("select_columns", [])
    relationships = api_cfg.get("relationships", [])

    if not db or not base_table:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="database and base_table are required")

    start_time = datetime.now()

    try:
        # ── Build alias → table map from relationships ────────────────
        # alias_to_table: {alias -> real_table_name} e.g. {"inc_mgr": "vea_sn_sys_user_all_cols"}
        alias_to_table = {}
        for rel in relationships:
            a = (rel.get("alias") or "").strip()
            if a:
                alias_to_table[a] = rel["target_table"]
        # Also map the base table alias (e.g. inc → vea_sn_incident)
        bta = (api_cfg.get("base_table_alias") or "").strip()
        if bta and bta != base_table:
            alias_to_table[bta] = base_table

        # select_column_aliases from config (column output renaming)
        sc_aliases = {a["expr"]: a["output_alias"] for a in api_cfg.get("select_column_aliases", [])}

        # Build SELECT
        def quote_col(c, dt):
            """Quote a column ref.  Handles:
            - alias.col  (resolve alias to real table)
            - table.col
            - bare col
            """
            if "." in c:
                parts = c.split(".", 1)
                prefix = parts[0]
                col_part = parts[1]
                real_table = alias_to_table.get(prefix, prefix)
                if dt == "bigquery":
                    # Use alias if defined, else fully-qualified table ref
                    if prefix in alias_to_table:
                        return f"{prefix}.`{col_part}`"
                    if "." not in real_table:
                        return f'`{schema_name}.{real_table}`.`{col_part}`'
                    return f'`{real_table}`.`{col_part}`'
                if prefix in alias_to_table:
                    return f'"{prefix}"."{col_part}"'
                return f'"{real_table}"."{col_part}"'
            return f"`{c}`" if dt == "bigquery" else f'"{c}"'

        col_str = ", ".join(quote_col(c, db_type) for c in select_cols) if select_cols else "*"

        # Collect all known table names for BQ ON-clause qualification
        all_table_names = {base_table}
        for _r in relationships:
            all_table_names.add(_r.get("target_table", ""))
        all_table_names.discard("")

        # Discover column ownership via INFORMATION_SCHEMA when BQ + JOINs
        col_owner_map = {}
        if db_type == "bigquery" and relationships:
            col_owner_map = _bq_get_column_owners(schema_name, all_table_names)

        def _bq_qualify_bare(col_name):
            """Resolve a bare column to its fully-qualified `dataset.table`.`col` form."""
            owners = col_owner_map.get(col_name)
            if owners:
                # Prefer base table when column exists there
                if base_table in owners:
                    return f'`{schema_name}.{base_table}`.`{col_name}`'
                # Column only in a joined table — pick it
                tbl = next(iter(owners))
                return f'`{schema_name}.{tbl}`.`{col_name}`'
            # Not found in schema — leave bare
            return f"`{col_name}`"

        def _smart_col_ref(col):
            """Resolve any column ref: alias.col, table.col, or bare col."""
            if "." in col:
                return quote_col(col, db_type)
            if db_type == "bigquery" and col_owner_map:
                return _bq_qualify_bare(col)
            return quote_col(col, db_type)

        # Re-build SELECT with smart column qualification + output aliases
        if select_cols:
            select_parts = []
            for c in select_cols:
                ref = _smart_col_ref(c)
                output_alias = sc_aliases.get(c, "")
                if output_alias:
                    select_parts.append(f"{ref} AS {output_alias}")
                else:
                    select_parts.append(ref)
            col_str = ", ".join(select_parts)

        # Build JOINs
        join_clause = ""
        for rel in relationships:
            jtype = rel.get("join_type", "INNER").upper()
            tgt_dt = rel.get("target_db_type", db_type)
            raw_on = (rel.get("raw_on_clause") or "").strip()
            src_col = (rel.get("source_column") or "").strip()
            tgt_col = (rel.get("target_column") or "").strip()
            join_alias = (rel.get("alias") or "").strip()

            if tgt_dt == "bigquery":
                t_table = f'`{rel["target_database"]}.{rel["target_table"]}`'
            else:
                t_table = f'"{rel.get("target_database", schema_name)}"."{rel["target_table"]}"'

            # Append alias to table ref when defined
            alias_clause = f" AS {join_alias}" if join_alias else ""

            if raw_on:
                # For BigQuery raw ON clauses:
                # - If aliases are used, resolve alias.col to `schema.table`.col
                # - If bare table names, qualify normally
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    # Replace alias references with fully-qualified BQ refs
                    qualified_on = raw_on
                    for a, tbl in alias_to_table.items():
                        pattern = re.compile(r'\b' + re.escape(a) + r'\.([A-Za-z_]\w*)')
                        qualified_on = pattern.sub(f'{a}.\\1', qualified_on)
                    # Also qualify any remaining bare table.col references
                    qualified_on = _bq_qualify_on_clause(qualified_on, schema_name, all_table_names)
                    raw_on = qualified_on
                join_clause += f" {jtype} JOIN {t_table}{alias_clause} ON {raw_on}"
            elif src_col and tgt_col:
                # source_table can be specified for chained JOINs (A→B→C where B is not base_table).
                # If not specified, defaults to base_table for backward compatibility.
                join_src_table = (rel.get("source_table") or "").strip() or base_table
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    if join_src_table in alias_to_table:
                        s_col = f'{join_src_table}.`{src_col}`'
                    else:
                        s_col = f'`{schema_name}.{join_src_table}`.`{src_col}`'
                else:
                    s_col = f'"{join_src_table}"."{src_col}"'
                if tgt_dt == "bigquery":
                    t_col_ref = join_alias if join_alias else t_table
                    t_col = f'{t_col_ref}.`{tgt_col}`'
                else:
                    t_col_ref = f'"{join_alias}"' if join_alias else f'"{rel["target_table"]}"'
                    t_col = f'{t_col_ref}."{tgt_col}"'
                join_clause += f" {jtype} JOIN {t_table}{alias_clause} ON {s_col} = {t_col}"
            else:
                logger.warning(f"Preview: skipping JOIN to {rel.get('target_table')}")

        # Build WHERE from where_conditions
        conditions = []
        where_vals = []
        wc_list = api_cfg.get("where_conditions", [])
        for wc in wc_list:
            wc_col = (wc.get("column") or "").strip()
            wc_op = (wc.get("operator") or "").strip().upper()
            wc_val = wc.get("value", "")
            if not wc_col or not wc_op:
                continue
            # Smart column resolution — use INFORMATION_SCHEMA map for bare BQ cols
            col_ref = _smart_col_ref(wc_col)
            if wc_op in ("IS NULL", "IS NOT NULL"):
                conditions.append(f"{col_ref} {wc_op}")
            elif wc_op in ("IN", "NOT IN"):
                in_items = [v.strip() for v in str(wc_val).split(",") if v.strip()]
                if not in_items:
                    continue
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    base_idx = len(where_vals)
                    placeholders = ", ".join(f"@fp{base_idx + i}" for i in range(len(in_items)))
                else:
                    placeholders = ", ".join("%s" for _ in in_items)
                conditions.append(f"{col_ref} {wc_op} ({placeholders})")
                where_vals.extend(in_items)
            elif wc_op in ("LIKE", "NOT LIKE", "ILIKE"):
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    placeholder = f"@fp{len(where_vals)}"
                    if wc_op == "ILIKE":
                        conditions.append(f"LOWER({col_ref}) LIKE LOWER({placeholder})")
                    else:
                        conditions.append(f"{col_ref} {wc_op} {placeholder}")
                else:
                    placeholder = "%s"
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} {wc_op} {placeholder}")
                where_vals.append(str(wc_val))
            else:
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    placeholder = f"@fp{len(where_vals)}"
                else:
                    placeholder = "%s"
                conditions.append(f"{col_ref} {wc_op} {placeholder}")
                where_vals.append(str(wc_val))

        where_clause = ("WHERE " + " AND ".join(conditions)) if conditions else ""

        # base_filter
        base_filter = api_cfg.get("base_filter", "").strip()
        if base_filter:
            where_clause = (where_clause + f" AND ({base_filter})") if where_clause else f"WHERE ({base_filter})"

        # Table ref
        if db_type == "bigquery":
            table_ref = f"`{db}.{base_table}`"
        else:
            table_ref = f'"{schema_name}"."{base_table}"'
        # Append base table alias if provided (e.g. FROM `schema.table` AS inc)
        if bta:
            table_ref += f" AS {bta}"

        query = f"SELECT {col_str} FROM {table_ref}{join_clause} {where_clause} LIMIT {effective_limit} OFFSET {offset}"

        # Execute
        if db_type == "bigquery":
            bq_params = [bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter(f"fp{i}", "STRING", str(v)) for i, v in enumerate(where_vals)]
            job_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(query_parameters=bq_params) if bq_params else bigquery.QueryJobConfig(
                default_dataset=f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{db}"
            )
            results = bq_client.query(query, job_config=job_config).result()
            data = [dict(row) for row in results]
            cols = [field.name for field in results.schema]
        else:
            server_id = api_cfg.get("server_id")
            if server_id:
                connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, db)
            else:
                connection = get_db_connection(db)
            try:
                cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
                cursor.execute(query, where_vals if where_vals else None)
                data = [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
                cols = [desc[0] for desc in cursor.description] if cursor.description else []
            finally:
                connection.close()

        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000

        return {
            "success": True,
            "preview": True,
            "api_name": api_cfg.get("name", "preview"),
            "data": data,
            "columns": cols,
            "row_count": len(data),
            "execution_time_ms": round(execution_time, 2),
            "query_generated": query,
            "database": db,
        }

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Preview API error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@app.get("/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute")
async def execute_custom_api(
    api_id: str = Path(...),
    request: Request = None,
    limit: Optional[int] = Query(None, ge=1, le=10000),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
    sort_by: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    sort_order: str = Query("asc", pattern="^(asc|desc)$"),
    filters: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    authorization: Optional[str] = Header(None),
    _body_filters: Optional[dict] = None,
):
    """Execute a custom API (GET/READ) — runs the built query with relationships and function layers.
    Supports 3 ways to filter:
      1. ?filters=[{"col":"eid","op":"eq","val":"123"}]  (advanced JSON)
      2. ?eid=123&name=John  (simple query params — auto equality filter)
      3. POST /search with JSON body {"eid":"123"}  (via _body_filters)
    """
    if api_id not in _api_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="API not found")

    api_cfg = _api_store[api_id]
    verify_basic_auth(api_cfg, authorization)

    # ── Response Caching ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    cache_ttl = api_cfg.get("cache_ttl", 0)
    if cache_ttl > 0:
        # Build cache key from API id + all query parameters
        qs = str(request.query_params) if request else ""
        bf = str(_body_filters) if _body_filters else ""
        cache_key = f"api_cache:{api_id}:{qs}:{bf}"
        cached = _cache_get(cache_key)
        if cached is not None:
            cached["_cached"] = True
            return cached

    db = api_cfg["database"]
    db_type = api_cfg.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    base_table = api_cfg["base_table"]
    schema_name = api_cfg.get("schema_name", "public")
    effective_limit = limit or api_cfg.get("default_limit", 100)
    select_cols = api_cfg.get("select_columns", [])
    relationships = api_cfg.get("relationships", [])
    layers = api_cfg.get("function_layers", [])

    start_time = datetime.now()

    try:
        # ── Build alias → table map from relationships ────────────────
        alias_to_table = {}
        for rel in relationships:
            a = (rel.get("alias") or "").strip()
            if a:
                alias_to_table[a] = rel["target_table"]
        # Also map the base table alias (e.g. inc → vea_sn_incident)
        bta = (api_cfg.get("base_table_alias") or "").strip()
        if bta and bta != base_table:
            alias_to_table[bta] = base_table

        sc_aliases = {a["expr"]: a["output_alias"] for a in api_cfg.get("select_column_aliases", [])}

        # Build SELECT — handles "alias.col", "table.col", and bare "col"
        def quote_col(c, dt):
            if "." in c:
                parts = c.split(".", 1)
                prefix = parts[0]
                col_part = parts[1]
                real_table = alias_to_table.get(prefix, prefix)
                if dt == "bigquery":
                    if prefix in alias_to_table:
                        return f"{prefix}.`{col_part}`"
                    if "." not in real_table:
                        return f'`{schema_name}.{real_table}`.`{col_part}`'
                    return f'`{real_table}`.`{col_part}`'
                if prefix in alias_to_table:
                    return f'"{prefix}"."{col_part}"'
                return f'"{real_table}"."{col_part}"'
            return f"`{c}`" if dt == "bigquery" else f'"{c}"'

        if select_cols:
            col_str = ", ".join(quote_col(c, db_type) for c in select_cols)
        else:
            col_str = "*"

        # Collect all known table names for BQ ON-clause qualification
        all_table_names = {base_table}
        for _r in relationships:
            all_table_names.add(_r.get("target_table", ""))
        all_table_names.discard("")

        # Discover column ownership via INFORMATION_SCHEMA when BQ + JOINs
        col_owner_map = {}
        if db_type == "bigquery" and relationships:
            col_owner_map = _bq_get_column_owners(schema_name, all_table_names)

        def _bq_qualify_bare(col_name):
            """Resolve a bare column to its fully-qualified `dataset.table`.`col` form."""
            owners = col_owner_map.get(col_name)
            if owners:
                if base_table in owners:
                    return f'`{schema_name}.{base_table}`.`{col_name}`'
                tbl = next(iter(owners))
                return f'`{schema_name}.{tbl}`.`{col_name}`'
            return f"`{col_name}`"

        def _smart_col_ref(col):
            """Resolve any column ref: alias.col, table.col, or bare col."""
            if "." in col:
                return quote_col(col, db_type)
            if db_type == "bigquery" and col_owner_map:
                return _bq_qualify_bare(col)
            return quote_col(col, db_type)

        # Re-build SELECT with smart column qualification + output aliases
        if select_cols:
            select_parts = []
            for c in select_cols:
                ref = _smart_col_ref(c)
                output_alias = sc_aliases.get(c, "")
                if output_alias:
                    select_parts.append(f"{ref} AS {output_alias}")
                else:
                    select_parts.append(ref)
            col_str = ", ".join(select_parts)

        # Build JOINs from relationships
        join_clause = ""
        for rel in relationships:
            jtype = rel.get("join_type", "INNER").upper()
            tgt_dt = rel.get("target_db_type", db_type)
            raw_on = (rel.get("raw_on_clause") or "").strip()
            src_col = (rel.get("source_column") or "").strip()
            tgt_col = (rel.get("target_column") or "").strip()
            join_alias = (rel.get("alias") or "").strip()

            # Target table quoting
            if tgt_dt == "bigquery":
                t_table = f'`{rel["target_database"]}.{rel["target_table"]}`'
            else:
                t_table = f'"{rel.get("target_database", schema_name)}"."{rel["target_table"]}"'

            alias_clause = f" AS {join_alias}" if join_alias else ""

            # If we have a raw ON clause (function-based JOINs), qualify for BQ
            if raw_on:
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    qualified_on = raw_on
                    for a, tbl in alias_to_table.items():
                        pattern = re.compile(r'\b' + re.escape(a) + r'\.([A-Za-z_]\w*)')
                        qualified_on = pattern.sub(f'{a}.\\1', qualified_on)
                    qualified_on = _bq_qualify_on_clause(qualified_on, schema_name, all_table_names)
                    raw_on = qualified_on
                join_clause += f" {jtype} JOIN {t_table}{alias_clause} ON {raw_on}"
            elif src_col and tgt_col:
                # Standard column-based ON clause.
                # source_table can be specified for chained JOINs (A→B→C where B is not base_table).
                # If not specified, defaults to base_table for backward compatibility.
                join_src_table = (rel.get("source_table") or "").strip() or base_table
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    # Resolve source table reference: use alias if defined, else fully qualify with schema
                    if join_src_table in alias_to_table:
                        s_col = f'{join_src_table}.`{src_col}`'
                    else:
                        s_col = f'`{schema_name}.{join_src_table}`.`{src_col}`'
                else:
                    s_col = f'"{join_src_table}"."{src_col}"'
                if tgt_dt == "bigquery":
                    t_col_ref = join_alias if join_alias else t_table
                    t_col = f'{t_col_ref}.`{tgt_col}`'
                else:
                    t_col_ref = f'"{join_alias}"' if join_alias else f'"{rel["target_table"]}"'
                    t_col = f'{t_col_ref}."{tgt_col}"'
                join_clause += f" {jtype} JOIN {t_table}{alias_clause} ON {s_col} = {t_col}"
            else:
                # No ON clause info — skip this JOIN to avoid empty identifiers
                logger.warning(f"Skipping JOIN to {rel.get('target_table')} — missing column info and raw_on_clause")

        # Build WHERE from allowed filters
        where_clause = ""
        where_vals = []
        allowed = set(api_cfg.get("allowed_filters", []))
        conditions = []

        # Method 1: Advanced JSON filters  ?filters=[{"col":"eid","op":"eq","val":"123"}]
        if filters:
            try:
                filter_list = json.loads(filters)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid filters JSON")
            for f in filter_list:
                col = f.get("col")
                if allowed and col not in allowed:
                    continue
                op, val = f.get("op", "eq"), f.get("val")
                col_ref = _smart_col_ref(col)
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    placeholder = f"@fp{len(conditions)}"
                else:
                    placeholder = "%s"
                if op == "eq":
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} = {placeholder}")
                    where_vals.append(val)
                elif op == "like":
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} ILIKE {placeholder}" if db_type != "bigquery" else f"LOWER({col_ref}) LIKE LOWER({placeholder})")
                    where_vals.append(f"%{val}%")
                elif op == "gt":
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} > {placeholder}")
                    where_vals.append(val)
                elif op == "lt":
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} < {placeholder}")
                    where_vals.append(val)

        # Method 2: Simple query params  ?eid=123&name=John
        _reserved_params = {"limit", "offset", "sort_by", "sort_order", "filters"}
        if request:
            for param_key, param_val in request.query_params.items():
                if param_key in _reserved_params:
                    continue
                if allowed and param_key not in allowed:
                    continue
                col_ref = _smart_col_ref(param_key)
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    placeholder = f"@fp{len(conditions)}"
                else:
                    placeholder = "%s"
                conditions.append(f"{col_ref} = {placeholder}")
                where_vals.append(param_val)

        # Method 3: JSON body filters (from /search endpoint)  {"eid":"123"}
        if _body_filters and isinstance(_body_filters, dict):
            for col, val in _body_filters.items():
                if allowed and col not in allowed:
                    continue
                col_ref = _smart_col_ref(col)
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    placeholder = f"@fp{len(conditions)}"
                else:
                    placeholder = "%s"
                conditions.append(f"{col_ref} = {placeholder}")
                where_vals.append(val)

        if conditions:
            where_clause = "WHERE " + " AND ".join(conditions)

        # Inject structured where_conditions (column / operator / value rows)
        wc_list = api_cfg.get("where_conditions", [])
        for wc in wc_list:
            wc_col = (wc.get("column") or "").strip()
            wc_op  = (wc.get("operator") or "").strip().upper()
            wc_val = wc.get("value", "")
            if not wc_col or not wc_op:
                continue
            # Smart column resolution — use INFORMATION_SCHEMA map for bare BQ cols
            col_ref = _smart_col_ref(wc_col)
            if wc_op in ("IS NULL", "IS NOT NULL"):
                conditions.append(f"{col_ref} {wc_op}")
            elif wc_op == "IN":
                # value should be comma-separated
                in_items = [v.strip() for v in str(wc_val).split(",") if v.strip()]
                if not in_items:
                    continue
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    base_idx = len(where_vals)
                    placeholders = ", ".join(f"@fp{base_idx + i}" for i in range(len(in_items)))
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} IN ({placeholders})")
                    where_vals.extend(in_items)
                else:
                    placeholders = ", ".join("%s" for _ in in_items)
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} IN ({placeholders})")
                    where_vals.extend(in_items)
            elif wc_op in ("LIKE", "NOT LIKE"):
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    placeholder = f"@fp{len(where_vals)}"
                else:
                    placeholder = "%s"
                conditions.append(f"{col_ref} {wc_op} {placeholder}")
                where_vals.append(str(wc_val))
            elif wc_op == "ILIKE":
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    placeholder = f"@fp{len(where_vals)}"
                    conditions.append(f"LOWER({col_ref}) LIKE LOWER({placeholder})")
                else:
                    placeholder = "%s"
                    conditions.append(f"{col_ref} ILIKE {placeholder}")
                where_vals.append(str(wc_val))
            else:
                # =, !=, <>, >, <, >=, <=
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    placeholder = f"@fp{len(where_vals)}"
                else:
                    placeholder = "%s"
                conditions.append(f"{col_ref} {wc_op} {placeholder}")
                where_vals.append(str(wc_val))

        # Re-evaluate WHERE clause after where_conditions
        if conditions and not where_clause:
            where_clause = "WHERE " + " AND ".join(conditions)
        elif conditions:
            where_clause = "WHERE " + " AND ".join(conditions)

        # Inject raw base_filter (legacy / advanced free-text WHERE)
        base_filter = api_cfg.get("base_filter", "").strip()
        if base_filter:
            if where_clause:
                where_clause += f" AND ({base_filter})"
            else:
                where_clause = f"WHERE ({base_filter})"

        # Build ORDER BY
        order_clause = ""
        allowed_sorts = set(api_cfg.get("allowed_sorts", []))
        if sort_by and (not allowed_sorts or sort_by in allowed_sorts):
            col_ref = quote_col(sort_by, db_type)
            order_clause = f"ORDER BY {col_ref} {sort_order.upper()}"

        # Assemble final query
        if db_type == "bigquery":
            table_ref = f"`{db}.{base_table}`"
        else:
            table_ref = f'"{schema_name}"."{base_table}"'
        # Append base table alias if provided (e.g. FROM `schema.table` AS inc)
        if bta:
            table_ref += f" AS {bta}"

        query = f"SELECT {col_str} FROM {table_ref}{join_clause} {where_clause} {order_clause} LIMIT {effective_limit} OFFSET {offset}"

        # Execute
        if db_type == "bigquery":
            bq_params = [bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter(f"fp{i}", "STRING", str(v)) for i, v in enumerate(where_vals)]
            job_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(query_parameters=bq_params) if bq_params else bigquery.QueryJobConfig(
                default_dataset=f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{db}"
            )
            results = bq_client.query(query, job_config=job_config).result()
            data = [dict(row) for row in results]
            cols = [field.name for field in results.schema]
        else:
            # Use server-aware connection if server_id is set
            server_id = api_cfg.get("server_id")
            if server_id:
                connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, db)
            else:
                connection = get_db_connection(db)
            try:
                cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
                cursor.execute(query, where_vals if where_vals else None)
                data = [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
                cols = [desc[0] for desc in cursor.description] if cursor.description else []
            finally:
                connection.close()

        # Apply post-query function layers
        if layers:
            data = _apply_function_layers(data, layers, phase="post")

        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000

        result_payload = {
            "success": True,
            "api_name": api_cfg["name"],
            "data": data,
            "columns": cols if not layers else (list(data[0].keys()) if data else cols),
            "row_count": len(data),
            "execution_time_ms": round(execution_time, 2),
            "query_generated": query,
            "database": db,
        }

        # Encrypt response if enabled
        if api_cfg.get("encryption_enabled") and api_cfg.get("encryption_key"):
            result_payload = {
                "encrypted": True,
                "payload": encrypt_payload(result_payload, api_cfg["encryption_key"]),
            }

        # Log metric (fire-and-forget)
        _caller_ip = request.client.host if request and request.client else ""
        _user_agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "") if request else ""
        _path = str(request.url.path) if request else f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute"
        _qp = str(request.url.query) if request else ""
        asyncio.ensure_future(_log_api_metric_async(
            api_id, "GET", _path, _caller_ip, _user_agent,
            round(execution_time, 2), 200, _qp, len(data),
        ))

        # Cache the result if TTL is configured
        if cache_ttl > 0:
            _cache_set(cache_key, result_payload, cache_ttl)

        return result_payload

    except HTTPException:
        _caller_ip = request.client.host if request and request.client else ""
        _user_agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "") if request else ""
        _path = str(request.url.path) if request else f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute"
        exec_ms = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        asyncio.ensure_future(_log_api_metric_async(
            api_id, "GET", _path, _caller_ip, _user_agent,
            round(exec_ms, 2), 400, "", 0, "HTTP error",
        ))
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Custom API execute error: {e}")
        _caller_ip = request.client.host if request and request.client else ""
        _user_agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "") if request else ""
        _path = str(request.url.path) if request else f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute"
        exec_ms = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        asyncio.ensure_future(_log_api_metric_async(
            api_id, "GET", _path, _caller_ip, _user_agent,
            round(exec_ms, 2), 500, "", 0, str(e),
        ))
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


# ── POST (Insert) for custom API ─────────────────────────────────────
@app.post("/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute")
async def execute_custom_api_post(
    api_id: str = Path(...),
    request: Request = None,
    authorization: Optional[str] = Header(None),
):
    """Insert rows via a custom API endpoint."""
    if api_id not in _api_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="API not found")
    api_cfg = _api_store[api_id]
    verify_basic_auth(api_cfg, authorization)

    if not api_cfg.get("enable_crud", True):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="CRUD is disabled for this API")

    db = api_cfg["database"]
    db_type = api_cfg.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    base_table = api_cfg["base_table"]
    schema_name = api_cfg.get("schema_name", "public")

    body = await request.json()

    # Decrypt if encryption is enabled
    if api_cfg.get("encryption_enabled") and api_cfg.get("encryption_key"):
        if isinstance(body, dict) and body.get("encrypted"):
            body = decrypt_payload(body["payload"], api_cfg["encryption_key"])

    rows = body if isinstance(body, list) else [body]
    if not rows:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No rows provided")

    start_time = datetime.now()
    try:
        if db_type == "bigquery":
            table_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{db}.{base_table}"
            errors = bq_client.insert_rows_json(table_ref, rows)
            if errors:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"BigQuery insert errors: {errors}")
            affected = len(rows)
        else:
            # Use server-aware connection if server_id is set
            server_id = api_cfg.get("server_id")
            if server_id:
                connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, db)
            else:
                connection = get_db_connection(db)
            try:
                cursor = connection.cursor()
                columns = list(rows[0].keys())
                col_str = ", ".join(f'"{c}"' for c in columns)
                placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(columns))
                qualified = f'"{schema_name}"."{base_table}"'
                sql = f"INSERT INTO {qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders})"
                affected = 0
                for row in rows:
                    cursor.execute(sql, [row.get(c) for c in columns])
                    affected += cursor.rowcount
                connection.commit()
            finally:
                connection.close()

        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        result_payload = {
            "success": True,
            "api_name": api_cfg["name"],
            "message": f"{affected} row(s) inserted",
            "affected_rows": affected,
            "execution_time_ms": round(execution_time, 2),
        }
        if api_cfg.get("encryption_enabled") and api_cfg.get("encryption_key"):
            result_payload = {"encrypted": True, "payload": encrypt_payload(result_payload, api_cfg["encryption_key"])}

        # Log metric
        _caller_ip = request.client.host if request and request.client else ""
        _user_agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "") if request else ""
        asyncio.ensure_future(_log_api_metric_async(
            api_id, "POST", f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute", _caller_ip, _user_agent,
            round(execution_time, 2), 200, "", affected,
        ))
        return result_payload

    except HTTPException:
        exec_ms = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        _caller_ip = request.client.host if request and request.client else ""
        _user_agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "") if request else ""
        asyncio.ensure_future(_log_api_metric_async(
            api_id, "POST", f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute", _caller_ip, _user_agent,
            round(exec_ms, 2), 400, "", 0, "HTTP error",
        ))
        raise
    except psycopg2.errors.UniqueViolation as e:
        logger.warning(f"Custom API POST duplicate key: {e}")
        exec_ms = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        _caller_ip = request.client.host if request and request.client else ""
        _user_agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "") if request else ""
        asyncio.ensure_future(_log_api_metric_async(
            api_id, "POST", f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute", _caller_ip, _user_agent,
            round(exec_ms, 2), 409, "", 0, str(e),
        ))
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=f"Duplicate entry: {e.diag.message_detail or e}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Custom API POST error: {e}")
        exec_ms = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        _caller_ip = request.client.host if request and request.client else ""
        _user_agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "") if request else ""
        asyncio.ensure_future(_log_api_metric_async(
            api_id, "POST", f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute", _caller_ip, _user_agent,
            round(exec_ms, 2), 500, "", 0, str(e),
        ))
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
