# ── PUT (Update) for custom API ──────────────────────────────────────
@app.put("/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute")
async def execute_custom_api_put(
    api_id: str = Path(...),
    request: Request = None,
    authorization: Optional[str] = Header(None),
):
    """Update rows via a custom API endpoint. Body: {primary_key: {...}, updates: {...}}"""
    if api_id not in _api_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="API not found")
    api_cfg = _api_store[api_id]
    verify_basic_auth(api_cfg, authorization)

    if not api_cfg.get("enable_crud", True):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="CRUD is disabled for this API")

    db = api_cfg["database"]
    db_type = api_cfg.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    base_table = api_cfg["base_table"]
    schema_name = api_cfg.get("schema_name", "public")

    body = await request.json()

    # Decrypt if encryption is enabled
    if api_cfg.get("encryption_enabled") and api_cfg.get("encryption_key"):
        if isinstance(body, dict) and body.get("encrypted"):
            body = decrypt_payload(body["payload"], api_cfg["encryption_key"])

    primary_key = body.get("primary_key")
    updates = body.get("updates")
    if not primary_key or not updates:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Body must include 'primary_key' and 'updates'")

    start_time = datetime.now()
    try:
        if db_type == "bigquery":
            set_clause = ", ".join(f"`{col}` = @u{i}" for i, col in enumerate(updates))
            where_clause = " AND ".join(f"`{col}` = @pk{i}" for i, col in enumerate(primary_key))
            query = f"UPDATE `{db}.{base_table}` SET {set_clause} WHERE {where_clause}"
            params = [bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter(f"u{i}", "STRING", str(v)) for i, (_, v) in enumerate(updates.items())]
            params += [bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter(f"pk{i}", "STRING", str(v)) for i, (_, v) in enumerate(primary_key.items())]
            job_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(query_parameters=params)
            bq_client.query(query, job_config=job_config).result()
            affected = 1
        else:
            # Use server-aware connection if server_id is set
            server_id = api_cfg.get("server_id")
            if server_id:
                connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, db)
            else:
                connection = get_db_connection(db)
            try:
                cursor = connection.cursor()
                set_parts, values = [], []
                for col, val in updates.items():
                    set_parts.append(f'"' + col + '" = %s')
                    values.append(val)
                where_parts = []
                for col, val in primary_key.items():
                    where_parts.append(f'"' + col + '" = %s')
                    values.append(val)
                sql = f'UPDATE "{schema_name}"."{base_table}" SET {", ".join(set_parts)} WHERE {" AND ".join(where_parts)}'
                cursor.execute(sql, values)
                connection.commit()
                affected = cursor.rowcount
            finally:
                connection.close()

        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        result_payload = {
            "success": True,
            "api_name": api_cfg["name"],
            "message": f"{affected} row(s) updated",
            "affected_rows": affected,
            "execution_time_ms": round(execution_time, 2),
        }
        if api_cfg.get("encryption_enabled") and api_cfg.get("encryption_key"):
            result_payload = {"encrypted": True, "payload": encrypt_payload(result_payload, api_cfg["encryption_key"])}

        # Log metric
        _caller_ip = request.client.host if request and request.client else ""
        _user_agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "") if request else ""
        asyncio.ensure_future(_log_api_metric_async(
            api_id, "PUT", f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute", _caller_ip, _user_agent,
            round(execution_time, 2), 200, "", affected,
        ))
        return result_payload

    except HTTPException:
        exec_ms = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        _caller_ip = request.client.host if request and request.client else ""
        _user_agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "") if request else ""
        asyncio.ensure_future(_log_api_metric_async(
            api_id, "PUT", f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute", _caller_ip, _user_agent,
            round(exec_ms, 2), 400, "", 0, "HTTP error",
        ))
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Custom API PUT error: {e}")
        exec_ms = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        _caller_ip = request.client.host if request and request.client else ""
        _user_agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "") if request else ""
        asyncio.ensure_future(_log_api_metric_async(
            api_id, "PUT", f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute", _caller_ip, _user_agent,
            round(exec_ms, 2), 500, "", 0, str(e),
        ))
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


# ── DELETE for custom API ────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.delete("/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute")
async def execute_custom_api_delete(
    api_id: str = Path(...),
    request: Request = None,
    authorization: Optional[str] = Header(None),
):
    """Delete rows via a custom API endpoint. Body: {pk_column: '...', pk_values: [...]}"""
    if api_id not in _api_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="API not found")
    api_cfg = _api_store[api_id]
    verify_basic_auth(api_cfg, authorization)

    if not api_cfg.get("enable_crud", True):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="CRUD is disabled for this API")

    db = api_cfg["database"]
    db_type = api_cfg.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    base_table = api_cfg["base_table"]
    schema_name = api_cfg.get("schema_name", "public")

    body = await request.json()

    # Decrypt if encryption is enabled
    if api_cfg.get("encryption_enabled") and api_cfg.get("encryption_key"):
        if isinstance(body, dict) and body.get("encrypted"):
            body = decrypt_payload(body["payload"], api_cfg["encryption_key"])

    pk_column = body.get("pk_column")
    pk_values = body.get("pk_values", [])
    if not pk_column or not pk_values:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Body must include 'pk_column' and 'pk_values'")

    start_time = datetime.now()
    try:
        if db_type == "bigquery":
            placeholders = ", ".join(f"@v{i}" for i in range(len(pk_values)))
            query = f"DELETE FROM `{db}.{base_table}` WHERE `{pk_column}` IN ({placeholders})"
            params = [bigquery.ScalarQueryParameter(f"v{i}", "STRING", str(v)) for i, v in enumerate(pk_values)]
            job_config = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(query_parameters=params)
            bq_client.query(query, job_config=job_config).result()
            affected = len(pk_values)
        else:
            # Use server-aware connection if server_id is set
            server_id = api_cfg.get("server_id")
            if server_id:
                connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, db)
            else:
                connection = get_db_connection(db)
            try:
                cursor = connection.cursor()
                placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(pk_values))
                sql = f'DELETE FROM "{schema_name}"."{base_table}" WHERE "{pk_column}" IN ({placeholders})'
                cursor.execute(sql, pk_values)
                connection.commit()
                affected = cursor.rowcount
            finally:
                connection.close()

        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        result_payload = {
            "success": True,
            "api_name": api_cfg["name"],
            "message": f"{affected} row(s) deleted",
            "affected_rows": affected,
            "execution_time_ms": round(execution_time, 2),
        }
        if api_cfg.get("encryption_enabled") and api_cfg.get("encryption_key"):
            result_payload = {"encrypted": True, "payload": encrypt_payload(result_payload, api_cfg["encryption_key"])}

        # Log metric
        _caller_ip = request.client.host if request and request.client else ""
        _user_agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "") if request else ""
        asyncio.ensure_future(_log_api_metric_async(
            api_id, "DELETE", f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute", _caller_ip, _user_agent,
            round(execution_time, 2), 200, "", affected,
        ))
        return result_payload

    except HTTPException:
        exec_ms = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        _caller_ip = request.client.host if request and request.client else ""
        _user_agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "") if request else ""
        asyncio.ensure_future(_log_api_metric_async(
            api_id, "DELETE", f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute", _caller_ip, _user_agent,
            round(exec_ms, 2), 400, "", 0, "HTTP error",
        ))
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Custom API DELETE error: {e}")
        exec_ms = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        _caller_ip = request.client.host if request and request.client else ""
        _user_agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "") if request else ""
        asyncio.ensure_future(_log_api_metric_async(
            api_id, "DELETE", f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute", _caller_ip, _user_agent,
            round(exec_ms, 2), 500, "", 0, str(e),
        ))
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


# ── Generate Encryption Key endpoint ─────────────────────────────────
@app.get("/api/builder/generate-key")
async def generate_encryption_key():
    """Generate a new Fernet encryption key."""
    return {"success": True, "key": generate_fernet_key()}


# ── Encrypt / Decrypt utility endpoints ──────────────────────────────
@app.post("/api/builder/encrypt")
async def encrypt_data(request: Request):
    """Encrypt arbitrary JSON data with a given key."""
    body = await request.json()
    key = body.get("key")
    data = body.get("data")
    if not key or data is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="'key' and 'data' are required")
    try:
        encrypted = encrypt_payload(data, key)
        return {"success": True, "encrypted": encrypted}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Encryption error: {str(e)}")


@app.post("/api/builder/decrypt")
async def decrypt_data(request: Request):
    """Decrypt Fernet-encrypted data with a given key."""
    body = await request.json()
    key = body.get("key")
    payload = body.get("payload")
    if not key or not payload:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="'key' and 'payload' are required")
    try:
        decrypted = decrypt_payload(payload, key)
        return {"success": True, "data": decrypted}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Decryption error: {str(e)}")


@app.get("/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/docs")
async def get_custom_api_docs(api_id: str = Path(...)):
    """Generate documentation for a custom API endpoint."""
    if api_id not in _api_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="API not found")

    api_cfg = _api_store[api_id]
    crud_on = api_cfg.get("enable_crud", True)
    methods = ["GET"]
    if crud_on:
        methods.extend(["POST", "PUT", "DELETE"])
    docs = {
        "api_name": api_cfg["name"],
        "description": api_cfg.get("description", ""),
        "base_url": f"/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute",
        "methods": methods,
        "database": api_cfg["database"],
        "db_type": api_cfg.get("db_type", "cloudsql"),
        "base_table": api_cfg["base_table"],
        "columns_exposed": api_cfg.get("select_columns", ["*"]),
        "filterable_columns": api_cfg.get("allowed_filters", []),
        "sortable_columns": api_cfg.get("allowed_sorts", []),
        "default_limit": api_cfg.get("default_limit", 100),
        "where_conditions": api_cfg.get("where_conditions", []),
        "base_filter": api_cfg.get("base_filter", ""),
        "crud_enabled": crud_on,
        "auth_enabled": api_cfg.get("auth_enabled", False),
        "encryption_enabled": api_cfg.get("encryption_enabled", False),
        "relationships": [
            {
                "name": r.get("name", ""),
                "type": r.get("join_type", "INNER"),
                "source": f'{r["source_database"]}.{r["source_table"]}.{r["source_column"]}',
                "target": f'{r["target_database"]}.{r["target_table"]}.{r["target_column"]}',
            }
            for r in api_cfg.get("relationships", [])
        ],
        "function_layers": [
            {"name": l.get("name"), "type": l.get("type"), "enabled": l.get("enabled", True)}
            for l in api_cfg.get("function_layers", [])
        ],
        "query_parameters": {
            "limit": {"type": "integer", "default": api_cfg.get("default_limit", 100), "max": 10000},
            "offset": {"type": "integer", "default": 0},
            "sort_by": {"type": "string", "allowed_values": api_cfg.get("allowed_sorts", [])},
            "sort_order": {"type": "string", "values": ["asc", "desc"]},
            "filters": {"type": "JSON array", "format": '[{"col":"name","op":"eq","val":"John"}]'},
        },
        "example_url": f'/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute?limit=10&sort_by={api_cfg.get("allowed_sorts", ["id"])[0] if api_cfg.get("allowed_sorts") else "id"}&sort_order=asc',
        "public_alias": api_cfg.get("public_alias"),
        "public_url": f'/api/data/{api_cfg["public_alias"]}' if api_cfg.get("public_alias") else None,
        "created_at": api_cfg.get("created_at"),
    }
    return {"success": True, "documentation": docs}


# ── API Metrics Endpoints ─────────────────────────────────────────────

@app.get("/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/metrics")
async def get_api_metrics(
    api_id: str = Path(...),
    date_from: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Start date YYYY-MM-DD"),
    date_to: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="End date YYYY-MM-DD"),
    limit: int = Query(500, ge=1, le=5000),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
):
    """Get raw metric logs for a custom API with optional date filtering."""
    if api_id not in _api_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="API not found")

    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)

        where_parts = ["api_id = %s"]
        params: list = [api_id]

        if date_from:
            where_parts.append("hit_timestamp >= %s")
            params.append(f"{date_from} 00:00:00")
        if date_to:
            where_parts.append("hit_timestamp <= %s")
            params.append(f"{date_to} 23:59:59")

        where_clause = " AND ".join(where_parts)

        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT id, api_id, hit_timestamp, method, path, caller_ip,
                   user_agent, response_time_ms, status_code,
                   query_params, row_count, error_message
            FROM {API_METRICS_TABLE}
            WHERE {where_clause}
            ORDER BY hit_timestamp DESC
            LIMIT %s OFFSET %s
        """, params + [limit, offset])
        rows = cursor.fetchall()

        # Get total count
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM {API_METRICS_TABLE} WHERE {where_clause}", params)
        total = cursor.fetchone()["cnt"]

        # Serialize timestamps
        for row in rows:
            if row.get("hit_timestamp"):
                row["hit_timestamp"] = row["hit_timestamp"].isoformat()

        return {
            "success": True,
            "api_id": api_id,
            "api_name": _api_store[api_id].get("name", ""),
            "hits": rows,
            "total": total,
            "limit": limit,
            "offset": offset,
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to get metrics for {api_id}: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.get("/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/metrics/summary")
async def get_api_metrics_summary(
    api_id: str = Path(...),
    date_from: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Start date YYYY-MM-DD"),
    date_to: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="End date YYYY-MM-DD"),
):
    """Get aggregated metrics summary for a custom API."""
    if api_id not in _api_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="API not found")

    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)

        where_parts = ["api_id = %s"]
        params: list = [api_id]

        if date_from:
            where_parts.append("hit_timestamp >= %s")
            params.append(f"{date_from} 00:00:00")
        if date_to:
            where_parts.append("hit_timestamp <= %s")
            params.append(f"{date_to} 23:59:59")

        wc = " AND ".join(where_parts)

        # 1. Overall stats
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT
                COUNT(*)                                  AS total_hits,
                COUNT(DISTINCT caller_ip)                 AS unique_callers,
                ROUND(AVG(response_time_ms)::numeric, 2) AS avg_response_ms,
                ROUND(MIN(response_time_ms)::numeric, 2) AS min_response_ms,
                ROUND(MAX(response_time_ms)::numeric, 2) AS max_response_ms,
                ROUND(percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY response_time_ms)::numeric, 2) AS p50_response_ms,
                ROUND(percentile_cont(0.95) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY response_time_ms)::numeric, 2) AS p95_response_ms,
                SUM(CASE WHEN status_code >= 200 AND status_code < 300 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS success_count,
                SUM(CASE WHEN status_code >= 400 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS error_count,
                SUM(row_count)                            AS total_rows_served,
                MIN(hit_timestamp)                        AS first_hit,
                MAX(hit_timestamp)                        AS last_hit
            FROM {API_METRICS_TABLE}
            WHERE {wc}
        """, params)
        stats = dict(cursor.fetchone())
        for k in ("first_hit", "last_hit"):
            if stats.get(k):
                stats[k] = stats[k].isoformat()
        # Convert Decimal to float for JSON serialization
        for k in ("avg_response_ms", "min_response_ms", "max_response_ms", "p50_response_ms", "p95_response_ms"):
            if stats.get(k) is not None:
                stats[k] = float(stats[k])

        # 2. Hits per day (for chart)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT DATE(hit_timestamp) AS day,
                   COUNT(*)            AS hits,
                   ROUND(AVG(response_time_ms)::numeric, 2) AS avg_ms
            FROM {API_METRICS_TABLE}
            WHERE {wc}
            GROUP BY DATE(hit_timestamp)
            ORDER BY day
        """, params)
        daily = []
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            daily.append({
                "day": row["day"].isoformat() if row.get("day") else "",
                "hits": row["hits"],
                "avg_ms": float(row["avg_ms"]) if row.get("avg_ms") is not None else 0,
            })

        # 3. Hits by method
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT method, COUNT(*) AS hits
            FROM {API_METRICS_TABLE}
            WHERE {wc}
            GROUP BY method ORDER BY hits DESC
        """, params)
        by_method = [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]

        # 4. Top callers (by IP)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT caller_ip, COUNT(*) AS hits,
                   MAX(hit_timestamp) AS last_seen
            FROM {API_METRICS_TABLE}
            WHERE {wc}
            GROUP BY caller_ip ORDER BY hits DESC LIMIT 20
        """, params)
        top_callers = []
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            top_callers.append({
                "caller_ip": row["caller_ip"],
                "hits": row["hits"],
                "last_seen": row["last_seen"].isoformat() if row.get("last_seen") else "",
            })

        # 5. Hits by status code
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT status_code, COUNT(*) AS hits
            FROM {API_METRICS_TABLE}
            WHERE {wc}
            GROUP BY status_code ORDER BY status_code
        """, params)
        by_status = [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]

        # 6. Hits by hour of day (usage pattern)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT EXTRACT(HOUR FROM hit_timestamp)::int AS hour,
                   COUNT(*) AS hits
            FROM {API_METRICS_TABLE}
            WHERE {wc}
            GROUP BY hour ORDER BY hour
        """, params)
        by_hour = [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]

        # 7. Top user agents
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT user_agent, COUNT(*) AS hits
            FROM {API_METRICS_TABLE}
            WHERE {wc}
            GROUP BY user_agent ORDER BY hits DESC LIMIT 10
        """, params)
        top_agents = [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]

        return {
            "success": True,
            "api_id": api_id,
            "api_name": _api_store[api_id].get("name", ""),
            "summary": stats,
            "daily": daily,
            "by_method": by_method,
            "top_callers": top_callers,
            "by_status": by_status,
            "by_hour": by_hour,
            "top_agents": top_agents,
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to get metrics summary for {api_id}: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.delete("/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/metrics")
async def clear_api_metrics(api_id: str = Path(...)):
    """Clear all metrics for a specific API."""
    if api_id not in _api_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="API not found")
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"DELETE FROM {API_METRICS_TABLE} WHERE api_id = %s", (api_id,))
        deleted = cursor.rowcount
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True, "deleted": deleted}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to clear metrics for {api_id}: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── Public Alias Endpoints — /api/data/<alias> ───────────────────────

def _find_api_by_alias(alias: str) -> Optional[dict]:
    """Look up an API config by its public alias."""
    for api in _api_store.values():
        if api.get("public_alias") == alias:
            return api
    return None


# ── Search / Filter endpoint (POST body) ─────────────────────────────
@app.post("/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/search")
async def search_custom_api(
    api_id: str = Path(...),
    request: Request = None,
    limit: Optional[int] = Query(None, ge=1, le=10000),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
    sort_by: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    sort_order: str = Query("asc", pattern="^(asc|desc)$"),
    authorization: Optional[str] = Header(None),
):
    """Search/filter a custom API by POST body. Send {"eid":"123"} to filter."""
    body = await request.json() if request else {}
    return await execute_custom_api(
        api_id=api_id, request=None, limit=limit, offset=offset,
        sort_by=sort_by, sort_order=sort_order, filters=None,
        authorization=authorization, _body_filters=body,
    )


@app.get("/api/data/{alias}")
async def public_api_get(
    alias: str = Path(...),
    request: Request = None,
    limit: Optional[int] = Query(None, ge=1, le=10000),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
    sort_by: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    sort_order: str = Query("asc", pattern="^(asc|desc)$"),
    filters: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    authorization: Optional[str] = Header(None),
):
    """Public GET endpoint using clean alias URL."""
    api_cfg = _find_api_by_alias(alias)
    if not api_cfg:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No API found with alias '{alias}'")
    return await execute_custom_api(
        api_id=api_cfg["id"], request=request, limit=limit, offset=offset,
        sort_by=sort_by, sort_order=sort_order, filters=filters,
        authorization=authorization,
    )

):

    )

# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  SERVER MANAGEMENT  –  Add / Test / List / Delete PostgreSQL servers
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════


class ServerConnection(BaseModel):
    """User-defined database server connection details (PostgreSQL, MS SQL, etc.)."""
    id: Optional[str] = None
    name: str = Field(..., description="Display name for the server, e.g. 'Production DB'")
    host: str = Field(..., description="Server hostname or IP address")
    port: int = Field(5432, description="PostgreSQL port (default 5432) or MSSQL port (1433)")
    username: str = Field(..., description="Database username")
    password: str = Field(..., description="Database password")
    database: str = Field("", description="Default database to connect to (optional)")
    db_type: str = Field("cloudsql", description="cloudsql, bigquery, or mssql")
    color: str = Field("#3b82f6", description="Display color for the server badge")
    ssl_mode: str = Field("prefer", description="SSL mode: disable, allow, prefer, require")
    created_at: Optional[str] = None


def _get_server_connection(server_id: str, database: str = None):
    """Get a database connection using a server's stored credentials.
    Supports PostgreSQL (cloudsql) and MS SQL Server (mssql)."""
    if server_id not in _server_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Server '{server_id}' not found")
    srv = _server_store[server_id]
    srv_type = srv.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    try:
        if srv_type == "mssql":
            if not _HAS_PYMSSQL:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="pymssql driver is not installed on the server")
            db_name = database or srv.get("database") or "master"
            connection = pymssql.connect(
                server=srv["host"],
                port=str(srv.get("port", 1433)),
                user=srv["username"],
                password=srv["password"],
                database=db_name,
                login_timeout=10,
                as_dict=True,
            )
            return connection
        else:
            db_name = database or srv.get("database") or "postgres"
            connection = psycopg2.connect(
                host=srv["host"],
                port=srv.get("port", 5432),
                database=db_name,
                user=srv["username"],
                password=srv["password"],
                connect_timeout=10,
            )
            return connection
    except psycopg2.OperationalError as e:
        logger.error(f"Server connection error [{server_id}]: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=f"Cannot connect to server '{srv['name']}': {str(e)}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Unexpected server connection error [{server_id}]: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Connection error: {str(e)}")


@app.post("/api/servers")
async def add_server(server: ServerConnection, request: Request):
    """Register a new database server connection (PostgreSQL or MSSQL)."""
    user_id = _get_user_id(request)
    server.id = server.id or str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
    server.created_at = datetime.now().isoformat()
    srv = server.model_dump()
    srv["is_default"] = False
    srv["connected"] = False
    srv["created_by"] = user_id
    # Ensure correct default port for db_type
    if srv.get("db_type") == "mssql" and srv.get("port") == 5432:
        srv["port"] = 1433
    _server_store[server.id] = srv
    _save_servers()
    logger.info(f"Server added by {user_id}: {server.id} – {server.name} ({server.host}:{srv['port']}) [{srv.get('db_type','cloudsql')}]")
    return {"success": True, "server": {**srv, "password": "***"}}


@app.get("/api/servers")
def list_servers(request: Request):
    """List server connections — scoped to user; super-admin sees all."""
    user_id = _get_user_id(request)
    servers = []
    for srv in _server_store.values():
        owner = srv.get("created_by", "")
        # Default server visible to all; others only visible to their owner (or super-admin)
        if srv.get("is_default") or _is_super_admin(user_id) or owner == user_id:
            safe = {**srv, "password": "***"}
            servers.append(safe)
    return {"success": True, "servers": servers, "count": len(servers)}


@app.get("/api/servers/{server_id}")
async def get_server(server_id: str = Path(...), request: Request = None):
    """Get a single server's config (password masked)."""
    if server_id not in _server_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Server not found")
    srv = _server_store[server_id]
    user_id = _get_user_id(request) if request else ""
    owner = srv.get("created_by", "")
    if not srv.get("is_default") and owner and owner != user_id and not _is_super_admin(user_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not authorised to view this server")
    return {"success": True, "server": {**srv, "password": "***"}}


@app.put("/api/servers/{server_id}")
async def update_server(server_id: str = Path(...), server: ServerConnection = None, request: Request = None):
    """Update an existing server connection."""
    if server_id not in _server_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Server not found")
    existing = _server_store[server_id]
    if existing.get("is_default"):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Cannot modify the default server")
    user_id = _get_user_id(request) if request else ""
    owner = existing.get("created_by", "")
    if owner and owner != user_id and not _is_super_admin(user_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not authorised to modify this server")
    updated = server.model_dump()
    updated["id"] = server_id
    updated["is_default"] = False
    updated["connected"] = existing.get("connected", False)
    updated["created_at"] = existing.get("created_at")
    updated["created_by"] = existing.get("created_by", "")
    _server_store[server_id] = updated
    _save_servers()
    logger.info(f"Server updated by {user_id}: {server_id} – {server.name}")
    await _log_audit_async(user_id, "update_server", query_text=f"Updated server {server.name} [{server_id}]",
                           ip_address=request.client.host if request and request.client else "")
    return {"success": True, "server": {**updated, "password": "***"}}


@app.delete("/api/servers/{server_id}")
async def delete_server(server_id: str = Path(...), request: Request = None):
    """Remove a registered server connection."""
    user_id = _get_user_id(request) if request else ""
    if server_id not in _server_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Server not found")
    if _server_store[server_id].get("is_default"):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Cannot delete the default server")
    owner = _server_store[server_id].get("created_by", "")
    if owner and not _is_super_admin(user_id) and owner != user_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not authorised to delete this server")
    deleted = _server_store.pop(server_id)
    _save_servers()
    logger.info(f"Server deleted: {server_id} – {deleted['name']}")
    import json as _json_del
    await _log_audit_async(user_id, "delete_server", query_text=f"Deleted server {deleted['name']} [{server_id}]",
                           ip_address=request.client.host if request and request.client else "",
                           details=_json_del.dumps({"server_id": server_id, "server_name": deleted['name']}))
    return {"success": True, "deleted": {**deleted, "password": "***"}}


@app.post("/api/servers/{server_id}/test")
async def test_server_connection(server_id: str = Path(...)):
    """Test connectivity to a registered server (PostgreSQL or MSSQL)."""
    if server_id not in _server_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Server not found")
    srv = _server_store[server_id]
    srv_type = srv.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    connection = None
    start = datetime.now()
    try:
        if srv_type == "mssql":
            if not _HAS_PYMSSQL:
                raise Exception("pymssql driver is not installed")
            connection = pymssql.connect(
                server=srv["host"],
                port=str(srv.get("port", 1433)),
                user=srv["username"],
                password=srv["password"],
                database=srv.get("database") or "master",
                login_timeout=10,
                as_dict=True,
            )
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT @@VERSION AS version")
            version = cursor.fetchone()["version"]
        else:
            connection = psycopg2.connect(
                host=srv["host"],
                port=srv.get("port", 5432),
                database=srv.get("database") or "postgres",
                user=srv["username"],
                password=srv["password"],
                connect_timeout=10,
            )
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT version();")
            version = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        elapsed = (datetime.now() - start).total_seconds() * 1000
        _server_store[server_id]["connected"] = True
        _save_servers()
        return {
            "success": True,
            "message": f"Connected successfully to {srv['name']}",
            "version": version,
            "latency_ms": round(elapsed, 2),
            "server": {**_server_store[server_id], "password": "***"},
        }
    except Exception as e:
        elapsed = (datetime.now() - start).total_seconds() * 1000
        _server_store[server_id]["connected"] = False
        _save_servers()
        logger.error(f"Server test failed [{server_id}]: {e}")
        return {
            "success": False,
            "message": f"Connection failed: {str(e)}",
            "latency_ms": round(elapsed, 2),
            "server": {**_server_store[server_id], "password": "***"},
        }
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.post("/api/servers/test-connection")
async def test_connection_direct(server: ServerConnection):
    """Test connectivity with raw credentials (before saving). Supports PostgreSQL and MSSQL."""
    connection = None
    start = datetime.now()
    srv_type = server.db_type if server.db_type else "cloudsql"
    try:
        if srv_type == "mssql":
            if not _HAS_PYMSSQL:
                raise Exception("pymssql driver is not installed on the server")
            connection = pymssql.connect(
                server=server.host,
                port=str(server.port or 1433),
                user=server.username,
                password=server.password,
                database=server.database or "master",
                login_timeout=10,
                as_dict=True,
            )
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT @@VERSION AS version")
            version = cursor.fetchone()["version"]
        else:
            connection = psycopg2.connect(
                host=server.host,
                port=server.port,
                database=server.database or "postgres",
                user=server.username,
                password=server.password,
                connect_timeout=10,
            )
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT version();")
            version = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        elapsed = (datetime.now() - start).total_seconds() * 1000
        return {
            "success": True,
            "message": "Connection successful!",
            "version": version,
            "latency_ms": round(elapsed, 2),
        }
    except Exception as e:
        elapsed = (datetime.now() - start).total_seconds() * 1000
        return {
            "success": False,
            "message": f"Connection failed: {str(e)}",
            "latency_ms": round(elapsed, 2),
        }
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.post("/api/servers/{server_id}/reconnect")
async def reconnect_server(server_id: str = Path(...)):
    """Reconnect to a server using saved credentials. Tests connection and updates status."""
    if server_id not in _server_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Server not found")
    srv = _server_store[server_id]
    srv_type = srv.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    connection = None
    start = datetime.now()
    try:
        if srv_type == "mssql":
            if not _HAS_PYMSSQL:
                raise Exception("pymssql driver is not installed")
            connection = pymssql.connect(
                server=srv["host"],
                port=str(srv.get("port", 1433)),
                user=srv["username"],
                password=srv["password"],
                database=srv.get("database") or "master",
                login_timeout=10,
                as_dict=True,
            )
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT @@VERSION AS version")
            version = cursor.fetchone()["version"]
        else:
            connection = psycopg2.connect(
                host=srv["host"],
                port=srv.get("port", 5432),
                database=srv.get("database") or "postgres",
                user=srv["username"],
                password=srv["password"],
                connect_timeout=10,
            )
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT version();")
            version = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        elapsed = (datetime.now() - start).total_seconds() * 1000

        _save_servers()
        logger.error(f"Reconnect failed [{server_id}]: {e}")
        return {
            "success": False,
            "message": f"Reconnect failed: {str(e)}",
            "latency_ms": round(elapsed, 2),
            "server": {**_server_store[server_id], "password": "***"},
        }
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.get("/api/servers/{server_id}/databases")
async def list_server_databases(server_id: str = Path(...), search: str = Query(None)):
    """List all databases on a specific server (PostgreSQL or MSSQL)."""
    if server_id not in _server_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Server not found")
    srv = _server_store[server_id]
    srv_type = srv.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    connection = None
    try:
        if srv_type == "mssql":
            connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, srv.get("database") or "master")
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT d.name,
                       CAST(SUM(mf.size) * 8 / 1024 AS VARCHAR) + ' MB' AS size
                FROM sys.databases d
                LEFT JOIN sys.master_files mf ON d.database_id = mf.database_id
                WHERE d.database_id > 0
                GROUP BY d.name
                ORDER BY d.name
            """)
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            databases = [dict(r) for r in rows]
        else:
            connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, "postgres")
            cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT datname AS name,
                       pg_size_pretty(pg_database_size(datname)) AS size
                FROM pg_database
                WHERE datistemplate = false
                ORDER BY datname
            """)
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            databases = [dict(r) for r in rows]
        if search:
            databases = [d for d in databases if search.lower() in d["name"].lower()]
        # Mark connected
        _server_store[server_id]["connected"] = True
        _save_servers()
        return {"success": True, "databases": databases, "count": len(databases), "server_id": server_id}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        _server_store[server_id]["connected"] = False
        _save_servers()
        logger.error(f"List databases error [{server_id}]: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── Create Database on a custom server ────────────────────────────────
@app.post("/api/servers/{server_id}/databases/create")
async def create_server_database(server_id: str = Path(...), body: dict = {}, request: Request = None):
    """Create a new database on a specific connected server (PostgreSQL or MSSQL)."""
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    if server_id not in _server_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Server not found")
    srv = _server_store[server_id]
    srv_type = srv.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    db_name = (body.get("name") or "").strip()

    if not db_name:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Database name is required")
    import re as _re_sdb
    if not _re_sdb.match(r'^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,62}$', db_name):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid database name. Use letters, digits, underscores, hyphens. Must start with a letter or underscore. Max 63 characters.")

    connection = None
    try:
        if srv_type == "mssql":
            # MSSQL – connect to master
            connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, "master")
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            # MSSQL CREATE DATABASE cannot be inside a user transaction
            cursor.execute(f"CREATE DATABASE [{db_name}]")
            connection.commit()
        else:
            # PostgreSQL – connect to postgres maintenance db
            connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, "postgres")
            connection.autocommit = True
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            cursor.execute(f'CREATE DATABASE "{db_name}"')

        logger.info(f"[{user_id}] Created database '{db_name}' on server {srv['name']} [{server_id}]")
        import json as _json_cdb
        await _log_audit_async(user_id, "create_database", database_name=db_name,
                               query_text=f"CREATE DATABASE {db_name} on server {srv['name']}",
                               ip_address=request.client.host if request.client else "",
                               details=_json_cdb.dumps({"server_id": server_id, "server_name": srv['name']}))
        return {"success": True, "message": f"Database '{db_name}' created on {srv['name']}", "database": db_name, "server_id": server_id}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Create database error on server [{server_id}]: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to create database: {str(e)}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.get("/api/servers/{server_id}/databases/{database}/tables")
async def list_server_tables(
    server_id: str = Path(...),
    database: str = Path(...),
    search: str = Query(None),
):
    """List tables for a specific database on a server (PostgreSQL or MSSQL)."""
    if server_id not in _server_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Server not found")
    srv = _server_store[server_id]
    srv_type = srv.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
        if srv_type == "mssql":
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            # Use INFORMATION_SCHEMA for broad compatibility, then enrich with size
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT TABLE_SCHEMA AS table_schema,
                       TABLE_NAME   AS table_name,
                       TABLE_TYPE   AS table_type
                FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
                ORDER BY TABLE_SCHEMA, TABLE_NAME
            """)
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            tables = []
            for r in rows:
                d = dict(r)
                d["size"] = "N/A"
                tables.append(d)
        else:
            cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT table_schema,
                       table_name,
                       (SELECT pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size(
                           quote_ident(table_schema) || '.' || quote_ident(table_name)
                       ))) AS size
                FROM information_schema.tables
                WHERE table_schema NOT IN ('pg_catalog', 'information_schema')
                ORDER BY table_schema, table_name
            """)
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            tables = [dict(r) for r in rows]
        if search:
            tables = [t for t in tables if search.lower() in t["table_name"].lower()]
        return {"success": True, "tables": tables, "count": len(tables), "server_id": server_id, "database": database}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"List tables error [{server_id}/{database}]: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.get("/api/servers/{server_id}/databases/{database}/tables/{table_name}/schema")
async def get_server_table_schema(
    server_id: str = Path(...),
    database: str = Path(...),
    table_name: str = Path(...),
    schema: str = Query("public"),
):
    """Get column schema for a table on a specific server (PostgreSQL or MSSQL)."""
    if server_id not in _server_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Server not found")
    srv = _server_store[server_id]
    srv_type = srv.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
        if srv_type == "mssql":
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT c.name AS column_name,
                       t.name AS data_type,
                       c.max_length AS character_maximum_length,
                       CASE WHEN c.is_nullable = 1 THEN 'YES' ELSE 'NO' END AS is_nullable,
                       dc.definition AS column_default
                FROM sys.columns c
                INNER JOIN sys.types t ON c.user_type_id = t.user_type_id
                INNER JOIN sys.tables tbl ON c.object_id = tbl.object_id
                INNER JOIN sys.schemas s ON tbl.schema_id = s.schema_id
                LEFT JOIN sys.default_constraints dc ON c.default_object_id = dc.object_id
                WHERE s.name = %s AND tbl.name = %s
                ORDER BY c.column_id
            """, (schema or "dbo", table_name))
            columns = [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]
        else:
            cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT column_name, data_type, character_maximum_length,
                       is_nullable, column_default
                FROM information_schema.columns
                WHERE table_schema = %s AND table_name = %s
                ORDER BY ordinal_position
            """, (schema, table_name))
            columns = [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]
        return {
            "success": True,
            "columns": columns,
            "count": len(columns),
            "server_id": server_id,
            "database": database,
            "table": table_name,
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Table schema error [{server_id}/{database}/{table_name}]: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.post("/api/servers/{server_id}/query")
async def execute_server_query(
    server_id: str = Path(...),
    request: QueryRequest = None,
    req: Request = None,
):
    """Execute a SQL query on a specific server's database (PostgreSQL or MSSQL)."""
    user_id = _get_user_id(req) if req else ""
    if server_id not in _server_store:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Server not found")
    srv = _server_store[server_id]
    srv_type = srv.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    connection = None
    start_time = datetime.now()
    try:
        # Block DELETE queries in SQL Editor (Production safety)
        stripped = request.query.strip().upper()
        if stripped.startswith("DELETE ") or stripped.startswith("DELETE\n") or stripped == "DELETE":
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=403,
                detail="Delete functions are temporarily disabled. Please contact admin for delete operations on Production."
            )

        connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, request.database)
        query = request.query
        query_upper = query.strip().upper()
        is_select = query_upper.startswith('SELECT') or query_upper.startswith('WITH')

        if srv_type == "mssql":
            # Auto-convert PostgreSQL LIMIT/OFFSET to MSSQL TOP syntax
            import re as _re_q
            limit_match = _re_q.search(r'\bLIMIT\s+(\d+)\b', query, _re_q.IGNORECASE)
            if limit_match and is_select:
                limit_val = limit_match.group(1)
                # Remove the LIMIT clause
                query = _re_q.sub(r'\bLIMIT\s+\d+\b', '', query, flags=_re_q.IGNORECASE).strip().rstrip(';')
                # Also remove OFFSET if present (MSSQL doesn't support bare OFFSET)
                query = _re_q.sub(r'\bOFFSET\s+\d+\b', '', query, flags=_re_q.IGNORECASE).strip()
                # Insert TOP N after SELECT if not already present
                if 'TOP' not in query.upper():
                    query = _re_q.sub(r'(?i)^SELECT', f'SELECT TOP {limit_val}', query.lstrip(), count=1)
            # MSSQL uses TOP instead of LIMIT
            has_top = 'TOP' in query.upper()
            if request.limit and is_select and not has_top:
                # Insert TOP N after SELECT
                query = re.sub(r'(?i)^SELECT', f'SELECT TOP {request.limit}', query.lstrip(), count=1)
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            cursor.execute(query)
            if cursor.description:
                columns = [desc[0] for desc in cursor.description]
                results = cursor.fetchall()
                data = [dict(row) for row in results]
                row_count = len(results)
            else:
                connection.commit()
                columns = []
                data = []
                row_count = cursor.rowcount if cursor.rowcount >= 0 else 0
        else:
            has_limit = 'LIMIT' in query_upper
            if request.limit and is_select and not has_limit:
                query = query.rstrip(';').rstrip() + f" LIMIT {request.limit}"
            cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
            cursor.execute(query)
            if cursor.description:
                results = cursor.fetchall()
                columns = [desc[0] for desc in cursor.description]
                data = [dict(row) for row in results]
                row_count = len(results)
            else:
                connection.commit()
                columns = []
                data = []
                row_count = cursor.rowcount if cursor.rowcount >= 0 else 0

        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000

        # Audit log + user history for server queries
        if user_id:
            ip = req.client.host if req and req.client else ""
            tbl = ""
            import re as _re_audit
            m = _re_audit.search(r'\bFROM\s+[`"\[]?(\S+?)[`"\]]?(?:\s|$|;)', request.query, _re_audit.IGNORECASE)
            if m:
                tbl = m.group(1)
            import json as _json_audit
            server_details = _json_audit.dumps({"server_id": server_id, "server_name": srv.get("name", ""), "server_host": srv.get("host", "")})
            await _log_audit_async(user_id, "query", database_name=request.database,
                                   table_name=tbl, query_text=request.query,
                                   db_type=srv_type, ip_address=ip,
                                   details=server_details)
            asyncio.get_event_loop().run_in_executor(None, _save_user_history,
                user_id, request.query, request.database, srv_type,
                row_count, round(execution_time, 2))

        return {
            "success": True,
            "data": data,
            "columns": columns,
            "row_count": row_count,
            "execution_time_ms": round(execution_time, 2),
            "query": query,
            "database": request.database,
            "server_id": server_id,
        }
    except psycopg2.Error as e:
        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        logger.error(f"Server query error [{server_id}]: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Query error: {str(e)}")
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        execution_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
        logger.error(f"Server query error [{server_id}]: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

# ── Pydantic models ──────────────────────────────────────────────────


class SchedulerStepConfig(BaseModel):
    id: Optional[str] = None
    name: str = Field(..., description="Step name")
    type: str = Field(..., description="api_call | db_mapping | email_trigger | gcs_operation")
    order: int = Field(0)
    enabled: bool = True
    config: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    conditions: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(default_factory=list)


class SchedulerJobConfig(BaseModel):
    id: Optional[str] = None
    name: str = Field(..., description="Job name")
    description: str = ""
    enabled: bool = True
    schedule_type: str = Field("manual", description="manual | interval | cron")
    schedule_config: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    steps: List[SchedulerStepConfig] = Field(default_factory=list)
    dependencies: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="List of job IDs that must succeed before this job runs")
    execution_mode: str = Field("in_app", description="in_app | cloud_job")
    cloud_job_config: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict, description="CPU, memory, timeout for Cloud Run Job mode")
    created_at: Optional[str] = None
    updated_at: Optional[str] = None
    last_run_at: Optional[str] = None
    last_run_status: Optional[str] = None


# ── PostgreSQL table setup ───────────────────────────────────────────


def _ensure_scheduler_tables():
    """Create scheduler tables if they don't exist."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {SCHEDULER_JOBS_TABLE} (
                id          VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
                name        VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                description TEXT DEFAULT '',
                enabled     BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
                schedule_type VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'manual',
                schedule_config TEXT DEFAULT '{{}}',
                steps       TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
                dependencies TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
                created_at  VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT '',
                updated_at  VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT '',
                last_run_at VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT '',
                last_run_status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT '',
                created_by  VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT ''
            )
        """)
        # Ensure columns exist for tables created before this version
        try:
            cursor.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {SCHEDULER_JOBS_TABLE} ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS created_by VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT ''")
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            cursor.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {SCHEDULER_JOBS_TABLE} ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS dependencies TEXT DEFAULT '[]'")
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            cursor.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {SCHEDULER_JOBS_TABLE} ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS execution_mode VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'in_app'")
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            cursor.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {SCHEDULER_JOBS_TABLE} ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS cloud_job_config TEXT DEFAULT '{{}}'")
        except Exception:
            pass
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {SCHEDULER_RUNS_TABLE} (
                id          VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
                job_id      VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
                status      VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'running',
                started_at  VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT '',
                finished_at VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT '',
                duration_ms FLOAT DEFAULT 0,
                steps_log   TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
                error       TEXT DEFAULT ''
            )
        """)
        connection.commit()
        logger.info("Scheduler tables ensured")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to create scheduler tables: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


_ensure_scheduler_tables()


# ── Scheduler job CRUD helpers ───────────────────────────────────────


def _load_scheduler_job(job_id: str) -> Optional[dict]:
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {SCHEDULER_JOBS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (job_id,))
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if row:
            job = dict(row)
            job["schedule_config"] = json.loads(job.get("schedule_config") or "{}")
            job["steps"] = json.loads(job.get("steps") or "[]")
            job["dependencies"] = json.loads(job.get("dependencies") or "[]")
            job["execution_mode"] = job.get("execution_mode") or "in_app"
            job["cloud_job_config"] = json.loads(job.get("cloud_job_config") or "{}")
            return job
        return None
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to load scheduler job {job_id}: {e}")
        return None
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def _load_all_scheduler_jobs() -> list:
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {SCHEDULER_JOBS_TABLE} ORDER BY created_at DESC")
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        jobs = []
        for row in rows:
            job = dict(row)
            job["schedule_config"] = json.loads(job.get("schedule_config") or "{}")
            job["steps"] = json.loads(job.get("steps") or "[]")
            job["dependencies"] = json.loads(job.get("dependencies") or "[]")
            job["execution_mode"] = job.get("execution_mode") or "in_app"
            job["cloud_job_config"] = json.loads(job.get("cloud_job_config") or "{}")
            jobs.append(job)
        return jobs
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to load scheduler jobs: {e}")
        return []
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def _save_scheduler_job(job: dict):
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {SCHEDULER_JOBS_TABLE}
                (id, name, description, enabled, schedule_type, schedule_config, steps, dependencies,
                 execution_mode, cloud_job_config, created_at, updated_at, last_run_at, last_run_status, created_by)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
            ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
                name = EXCLUDED.name,
                description = EXCLUDED.description,
                enabled = EXCLUDED.enabled,
                schedule_type = EXCLUDED.schedule_type,
                schedule_config = EXCLUDED.schedule_config,
                steps = EXCLUDED.steps,
                dependencies = EXCLUDED.dependencies,
                execution_mode = EXCLUDED.execution_mode,
                cloud_job_config = EXCLUDED.cloud_job_config,
                updated_at = EXCLUDED.updated_at,
                last_run_at = EXCLUDED.last_run_at,
                last_run_status = EXCLUDED.last_run_status,
                created_by = EXCLUDED.created_by
        """, (
            job["id"], job["name"], job.get("description", ""),
            job.get("enabled", True), job.get("schedule_type", "manual"),
            json.dumps(job.get("schedule_config", {}), default=str),
            json.dumps(job.get("steps", []), default=str),
            json.dumps(job.get("dependencies", []), default=str),
            job.get("execution_mode", "in_app"),
            json.dumps(job.get("cloud_job_config", {}), default=str),
            job.get("created_at", ""), job.get("updated_at", ""),
            job.get("last_run_at", ""), job.get("last_run_status", ""),
            job.get("created_by", ""),
        ))
        connection.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to save scheduler job: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def _delete_scheduler_job(job_id: str):
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"DELETE FROM {SCHEDULER_JOBS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (job_id,))
        cursor.execute(f"DELETE FROM {SCHEDULER_RUNS_TABLE} WHERE job_id = %s", (job_id,))
        connection.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to delete scheduler job: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def _save_run_log(run: dict):
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {SCHEDULER_RUNS_TABLE}
                (id, job_id, status, started_at, finished_at, duration_ms, steps_log, error)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
            ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
                status = EXCLUDED.status,
                finished_at = EXCLUDED.finished_at,
                duration_ms = EXCLUDED.duration_ms,
                steps_log = EXCLUDED.steps_log,
                error = EXCLUDED.error
        """, (
            run["id"], run["job_id"], run["status"],
            run["started_at"], run.get("finished_at", ""),
            run.get("duration_ms", 0),
            json.dumps(run.get("steps_log", []), default=str),
            run.get("error", ""),
        ))
        connection.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to save run log: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def _load_run_history(job_id: str, limit: int = 20) -> list:
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(
            f"SELECT * FROM {SCHEDULER_RUNS_TABLE} WHERE job_id = %s ORDER BY started_at DESC LIMIT %s",
            (job_id, limit),
        )
        runs = []
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            r = dict(row)
            r["steps_log"] = json.loads(r.get("steps_log") or "[]")
            runs.append(r)
        return runs
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to load run history: {e}")
        return []
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── Cloud Run Job helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────

CLOUD_RUN_REGION = "us-east4"
CLOUD_RUN_PROJECT = GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT
CLOUD_RUN_SA = "sa-pr-voev-app-voevdo-0@vz-it-pr-voev-voevdo-0.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
CLOUD_RUN_IMAGE = "us-east4-docker.pkg.dev/vz-it-pr-voev-voevdo-0/development-artifact/sqleditorapi:latest"
CLOUD_RUN_VPC_CONNECTOR = "projects/vz-it-pr-exhv-sharedvpc-228020/locations/us-east4/connectors/shared-pr-east"
CLOUD_RUN_KMS_KEY = "projects/vz-it-pr-d0sv-vsadkms-0/locations/us-east4/keyRings/vz-it-pr-kr-corp/cryptoKeys/vz-it-pr-kms-voev"


def _sanitize_cloud_job_name(job_id: str, custom_name: str = None) -> str:
    """Sanitize into a valid Cloud Run Job name. Uses custom_name if provided."""
    if custom_name and custom_name.strip():
        name = custom_name.strip().lower()
    else:
        name = f"scheduler-{job_id}".lower()
    name = re.sub(r'[^a-z0-9-]', '-', name)
    name = re.sub(r'-+', '-', name).strip('-')
    if not name or not name[0].isalpha():
        name = f"job-{name}"
    return name[:63]


def _create_or_update_cloud_run_job(job_id: str, job_name: str, cloud_config: dict) -> dict:
    """Create or update a Cloud Run Job for a scheduler job."""
    if not _HAS_CLOUD_RUN:
        raise RuntimeError("google-cloud-run package not installed")

    client = run_v2.JobsClient()
    parent = f"projects/{CLOUD_RUN_PROJECT}/locations/{CLOUD_RUN_REGION}"
    custom_name = cloud_config.get("cloud_job_name", "")
    cloud_job_name = _sanitize_cloud_job_name(job_id, custom_name)
    full_name = f"{parent}/jobs/{cloud_job_name}"

    cpu = cloud_config.get("cpu", "2")
    memory = cloud_config.get("memory", "2Gi")
    timeout_sec = int(cloud_config.get("timeout", 3600))
    max_retries = int(cloud_config.get("max_retries", 0))
    extra_env = cloud_config.get("env_vars", {})

    env_vars = [
        run_v2.EnvVar(name="SCHEDULER_JOB_ID", value=job_id),
        run_v2.EnvVar(name="DB_HOST", value=DB_HOST),
        run_v2.EnvVar(name="DB_USERNAME", value=DB_USERNAME),
        run_v2.EnvVar(name="DB_PASSWORD", value=DB_PASSWORD),
    ]
    for k, v in extra_env.items():
        env_vars.append(run_v2.EnvVar(name=k, value=str(v)))

    container = run_v2.Container(
        image=CLOUD_RUN_IMAGE,
        env=env_vars,
        resources=run_v2.ResourceRequirements(
            limits={"cpu": cpu, "memory": memory},
        ),
    )

    template = run_v2.ExecutionTemplate(
        template=run_v2.TaskTemplate(
            containers=[container],
            timeout=f"{timeout_sec}s",
            max_retries=max_retries,
            service_account=CLOUD_RUN_SA,
            vpc_access=run_v2.VpcAccess(
                connector=CLOUD_RUN_VPC_CONNECTOR,
                egress=run_v2.VpcAccess.VpcEgress.ALL_TRAFFIC,
            ),
            encryption_key=CLOUD_RUN_KMS_KEY,
        ),
    )

    job = run_v2.Job(
        template=template,
        labels={"managed-by": "sqleditor-scheduler", "scheduler-job-id": job_id[:63]},
    )

    try:
        existing = client.get_job(name=full_name)
        job.name = full_name
        update_mask = {"paths": ["template", "labels"]}
        result = client.update_job(job=job, update_mask=update_mask)
        logger.info(f"Cloud Run Job updated: {cloud_job_name}")
        return {"action": "updated", "name": cloud_job_name}
    except Exception as get_err:
        if "404" in str(get_err) or "NotFound" in str(get_err):
            request = run_v2.CreateJobRequest(
                parent=parent,
                job=job,
                job_id=cloud_job_name,
            )
            result = client.create_job(request=request)
            logger.info(f"Cloud Run Job created: {cloud_job_name}")
            return {"action": "created", "name": cloud_job_name}
        raise


def _execute_cloud_run_job(job_id: str, cloud_config: dict = None) -> dict:
    """Trigger execution of a Cloud Run Job and record a run."""
    if not _HAS_CLOUD_RUN:
        raise RuntimeError("google-cloud-run package not installed")

    client = run_v2.JobsClient()
    custom_name = (cloud_config or {}).get("cloud_job_name", "")
    cloud_job_name = _sanitize_cloud_job_name(job_id, custom_name)
    full_name = f"projects/{CLOUD_RUN_PROJECT}/locations/{CLOUD_RUN_REGION}/jobs/{cloud_job_name}"

    run_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:12]
    run = {
        "id": run_id,
        "job_id": job_id,
        "status": "running",
        "started_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "steps_log": [{"name": "cloud_run_job", "type": "cloud_job", "status": "submitted",
                        "message": f"Submitted to Cloud Run Job: {cloud_job_name}"}],
    }
    _save_run_log(run)

    operation = client.run_job(name=full_name)
    logger.info(f"Cloud Run Job execution triggered: {cloud_job_name}")

    return run


def _get_cloud_run_job_status(cloud_job_name: str) -> dict:
    """Get status of a Cloud Run Job and its recent executions."""
    if not _HAS_CLOUD_RUN:
        raise RuntimeError("google-cloud-run package not installed")

    client = run_v2.JobsClient()
    full_name = f"projects/{CLOUD_RUN_PROJECT}/locations/{CLOUD_RUN_REGION}/jobs/{cloud_job_name}"

    try:
        job = client.get_job(name=full_name)
        exec_client = run_v2.ExecutionsClient()
        executions = list(exec_client.list_executions(parent=full_name))

        exec_list = []
        for ex in executions[:20]:
            exec_list.append({
                "name": ex.name.split("/")[-1] if ex.name else "",
                "create_time": ex.create_time.isoformat() if ex.create_time else "",
                "completion_time": ex.completion_time.isoformat() if ex.completion_time else "",
                "succeeded_count": ex.succeeded_count,
                "failed_count": ex.failed_count,
                "running_count": ex.running_count,
            })

        return {
            "name": cloud_job_name,
            "create_time": job.create_time.isoformat() if job.create_time else "",
            "update_time": job.update_time.isoformat() if job.update_time else "",
            "execution_count": job.execution_count,
            "executions": exec_list,
        }
    except Exception as e:
        return {"name": cloud_job_name, "error": str(e)}


def _delete_cloud_run_job(cloud_job_name: str) -> bool:
    """Delete a Cloud Run Job."""
    if not _HAS_CLOUD_RUN:
        raise RuntimeError("google-cloud-run package not installed")

    client = run_v2.JobsClient()
    full_name = f"projects/{CLOUD_RUN_PROJECT}/locations/{CLOUD_RUN_REGION}/jobs/{cloud_job_name}"
    client.delete_job(name=full_name)
    logger.info(f"Cloud Run Job deleted: {cloud_job_name}")
    return True


def _list_cloud_run_jobs() -> list:
    """List all Cloud Run Jobs created by the scheduler."""
    if not _HAS_CLOUD_RUN:
        return []

    client = run_v2.JobsClient()
    parent = f"projects/{CLOUD_RUN_PROJECT}/locations/{CLOUD_RUN_REGION}"

    jobs = []
    for job in client.list_jobs(parent=parent):
        labels = dict(job.labels) if job.labels else {}
        if labels.get("managed-by") == "sqleditor-scheduler":
            config = {}
            try:
                tpl = job.template.template
                if tpl.containers:
                    c = tpl.containers[0]
                    limits = dict(c.resources.limits) if c.resources and c.resources.limits else {}
                    config["cpu"] = limits.get("cpu", "1")
                    config["memory"] = limits.get("memory", "512Mi")
                    config["image"] = c.image or ""
                    env_vars = {}
                    for ev in (c.env or []):
                        if ev.name not in ("SCHEDULER_JOB_ID", "DB_HOST", "DB_USERNAME", "DB_PASSWORD"):
                            env_vars[ev.name] = ev.value
                    config["env_vars"] = env_vars
                config["timeout"] = tpl.timeout.seconds if tpl.timeout else 3600
                config["max_retries"] = tpl.max_retries or 0
                config["service_account"] = tpl.service_account or ""
            except Exception:
                pass

            last_exec = None
            try:
                exec_client = run_v2.ExecutionsClient()
                execs = list(exec_client.list_executions(parent=job.name))
                if execs:
                    latest = execs[0]
                    last_exec = {
                        "name": latest.name.split("/")[-1] if latest.name else "",
                        "create_time": latest.create_time.isoformat() if latest.create_time else "",
                        "succeeded": latest.succeeded_count,
                        "failed": latest.failed_count,
                        "running": latest.running_count,
                    }
            except Exception:
                pass

            jobs.append({
                "name": job.name.split("/")[-1] if job.name else "",
                "full_name": job.name,
                "scheduler_job_id": labels.get("scheduler-job-id", ""),
                "create_time": job.create_time.isoformat() if job.create_time else "",
                "update_time": job.update_time.isoformat() if job.update_time else "",
                "execution_count": job.execution_count,
                "config": config,
                "last_execution": last_exec,
            })
    return jobs


def _run_cloud_run_job_by_name(cloud_job_name: str) -> dict:
    """Trigger execution of a Cloud Run Job by its name."""
    if not _HAS_CLOUD_RUN:
        raise RuntimeError("google-cloud-run package not installed")

    client = run_v2.JobsClient()
    full_name = f"projects/{CLOUD_RUN_PROJECT}/locations/{CLOUD_RUN_REGION}/jobs/{cloud_job_name}"
    operation = client.run_job(name=full_name)
    logger.info(f"Cloud Run Job manually triggered: {cloud_job_name}")
    return {"name": cloud_job_name, "status": "submitted"}


def _update_cloud_run_job(cloud_job_name: str, updates: dict) -> dict:
    """Update a Cloud Run Job's configuration (CPU, memory, timeout, retries, env vars)."""
    if not _HAS_CLOUD_RUN:
        raise RuntimeError("google-cloud-run package not installed")

    client = run_v2.JobsClient()
    full_name = f"projects/{CLOUD_RUN_PROJECT}/locations/{CLOUD_RUN_REGION}/jobs/{cloud_job_name}"

    job = client.get_job(name=full_name)

    tpl = job.template.template
    if tpl.containers:
        c = tpl.containers[0]
        if "cpu" in updates or "memory" in updates:
            limits = dict(c.resources.limits) if c.resources and c.resources.limits else {}
            if "cpu" in updates:
                limits["cpu"] = str(updates["cpu"])
            if "memory" in updates:
                limits["memory"] = str(updates["memory"])
            c.resources = run_v2.ResourceRequirements(limits=limits)
        if "env_vars" in updates:
            existing = {ev.name: ev for ev in (c.env or [])}
            for k, v in updates["env_vars"].items():
                existing[k] = run_v2.EnvVar(name=k, value=str(v))
            c.env = list(existing.values())
    if "timeout" in updates:
        tpl.timeout = f"{int(updates['timeout'])}s"
    if "max_retries" in updates:
        tpl.max_retries = int(updates["max_retries"])

    update_mask = {"paths": ["template"]}
    client.update_job(job=job, update_mask=update_mask)
    logger.info(f"Cloud Run Job updated: {cloud_job_name}")
    return {"name": cloud_job_name, "status": "updated"}


def _get_cloud_run_job_logs(cloud_job_name: str, execution_name: str = None, limit: int = 200) -> list:
    """Fetch logs for a Cloud Run Job from Cloud Logging."""
    if not _HAS_CLOUD_LOGGING:
        raise RuntimeError("google-cloud-logging package not installed")

    client = cloud_logging.Client(project=CLOUD_RUN_PROJECT)

    log_filter = (
        f'resource.type="cloud_run_job" '
        f'resource.labels.job_name="{cloud_job_name}" '
        f'resource.labels.location="{CLOUD_RUN_REGION}"'
    )
    if execution_name:
        log_filter += f' labels."run.googleapis.com/execution_name"="{execution_name}"'

    entries = []
    for entry in client.list_entries(
        filter_=log_filter,
        order_by=cloud_logging.DESCENDING,
        max_results=limit,
    ):
        payload = ""
        if isinstance(entry.payload, dict):
            payload = entry.payload.get("message", str(entry.payload))
        elif isinstance(entry.payload, str):
            payload = entry.payload
        else:
            payload = entry.text_payload or str(entry.payload)

        entries.append({
            "timestamp": entry.timestamp.isoformat() if entry.timestamp else "",
            "severity": entry.severity or "DEFAULT",
            "message": payload,
            "execution": (entry.labels or {}).get("run.googleapis.com/execution_name", ""),
        })

    entries.reverse()
    return entries


# ── Condition evaluator ──────────────────────────────────────────────


def _resolve_path(obj, path: str):
    """Resolve a dot-notation path like 'response.data.length' against an object."""
    parts = path.split(".")
    current = obj
    for part in parts:
        if part == "length" and isinstance(current, (list, dict)):
            return len(current)
        if isinstance(current, dict):
            current = current.get(part)
        elif isinstance(current, list):
            try:
                current = current[int(part)]
            except (ValueError, IndexError):
                return None
        else:
            return None
        if current is None:
            return None
    return current


def _evaluate_condition(condition: dict, context: dict) -> bool:
    """Evaluate a single condition against the execution context."""
    field = condition.get("field", "")
    op = condition.get("op", "eq")
    expected = condition.get("value")

    actual = _resolve_path(context, field)

    try:
