        if op == "eq":
            return str(actual) == str(expected)
        elif op == "neq":
            return str(actual) != str(expected)
        elif op == "gt":
            return float(actual) > float(expected)
        elif op == "lt":
            return float(actual) < float(expected)
        elif op == "gte":
            return float(actual) >= float(expected)
        elif op == "lte":
            return float(actual) <= float(expected)
        elif op == "contains":
            return str(expected) in str(actual)
        elif op == "not_empty":
            return actual is not None and actual != "" and actual != [] and actual != {}
        elif op == "is_empty":
            return actual is None or actual == "" or actual == [] or actual == {}
        elif op == "exists":
            return actual is not None
    except Exception:
        return False
    return False


def _evaluate_conditions(conditions: list, context: dict) -> bool:
    """All conditions must pass (AND logic)."""
    if not conditions:
        return True
    return all(_evaluate_condition(c, context) for c in conditions)


# ── Dynamic variable resolver ────────────────────────────────────────


def _resolve_dynamic_var(expr: str) -> str:
    """Resolve dynamic variable expressions.

    Supported syntax (all start with ``$``):
        $now                   → current datetime   YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS
        $today                 → today at 00:00
        $yesterday / $tomorrow → ±1 day at 00:00
        $week_start            → Monday 00:00 of current week
        $month_start           → 1st of current month 00:00
        $year_start            → Jan 1 of current year 00:00

    Offsets:
        $now-1d   $today+2h   $now-30m   $now+1w   $now-3M   $now+1y

    Formats (after colon):
        $now:YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm
        $now-1d:YYYY-MM-DD
        $now:epoch        → Unix timestamp (seconds)
        $now:epoch_ms     → Unix timestamp (milliseconds)
        $now:iso          → ISO-8601

    Examples:
        {{$now-1d:YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm}}  → "2026-03-05 14:30"
        {{$today:YYYY-MM-DD}}          → "2026-03-06"
        {{$month_start:YYYY-MM-DD}}    → "2026-03-01"
    """
    if not expr.startswith("$"):
        return None

    import re as _re
    match = _re.match(r'^\$(\w+)([+-]\d+[dhmwMy])?\s*(?::(.+))?$', expr.strip())
    if not match:
        return None

    var_name = match.group(1)
    offset_str = match.group(2)
    fmt = match.group(3)

    now = datetime.now()

    bases = {
        "now":         now,
        "run_date":    now,
        "today":       now.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0),
        "yesterday":   (now - timedelta(days=1)).replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0),
        "tomorrow":    (now + timedelta(days=1)).replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0),
        "week_start":  (now - timedelta(days=now.weekday())).replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0),
        "month_start": now.replace(day=1, hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0),
        "year_start":  now.replace(month=1, day=1, hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0),
        "epoch":       now,
        "epoch_ms":    now,
    }

    base = bases.get(var_name)
    if base is None:
        return None

    # Apply offset  e.g.  -1d  +2h  -30m  +1w  -3M  +1y
    if offset_str:
        sign = 1 if offset_str[0] == "+" else -1
        num = int(offset_str[1:-1])
        unit = offset_str[-1]
        if unit == "d":
            base += timedelta(days=sign * num)
        elif unit == "h":
            base += timedelta(hours=sign * num)
        elif unit == "m":
            base += timedelta(minutes=sign * num)
        elif unit == "w":
            base += timedelta(weeks=sign * num)
        elif unit == "M":
            new_month = base.month + sign * num
            new_year = base.year
            while new_month > 12:
                new_month -= 12
                new_year += 1
            while new_month < 1:
                new_month += 12
                new_year -= 1
            try:
                base = base.replace(year=new_year, month=new_month)
            except ValueError:
                # e.g. Jan 31 - 1M → clamp to last day of Dec
                import calendar
                last_day = calendar.monthrange(new_year, new_month)[1]
                base = base.replace(year=new_year, month=new_month, day=min(base.day, last_day))
        elif unit == "y":
            try:
                base = base.replace(year=base.year + sign * num)
            except ValueError:
                base = base.replace(year=base.year + sign * num, day=28)

    # Handle format-only $epoch / $epoch_ms shorthand (no colon needed)
    if var_name == "epoch" and not fmt:
        return str(int(base.timestamp()))
    if var_name == "epoch_ms" and not fmt:
        return str(int(base.timestamp() * 1000))

    # Apply format
    if fmt:
        fmt = fmt.strip()
        if fmt == "epoch":
            return str(int(base.timestamp()))
        elif fmt == "epoch_ms":
            return str(int(base.timestamp() * 1000))
        elif fmt == "iso":
            return base.isoformat()
        else:
            py_fmt = (
                fmt.replace("YYYY", "%Y").replace("YY", "%y")
                   .replace("MM", "%m").replace("DD", "%d")
                   .replace("HH", "%H").replace("mm", "%M")
                   .replace("ss", "%S")
            )
            return base.strftime(py_fmt)

    # Default format
    return base.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")


# ── Template renderer ────────────────────────────────────────────────


def _render_template(template: str, context: dict) -> str:
    """Replace {{path.to.value}} and {{$dynamic_var}} placeholders."""
    import re as _re

    def replacer(match):
        path = match.group(1).strip()
        # Dynamic variables first
        if path.startswith("$"):
            result = _resolve_dynamic_var(path)
            if result is not None:
                return result
        # Context path resolution
        val = _resolve_path(context, path)
        return str(val) if val is not None else ""

    return _re.sub(r'\{\{(.+?)\}\}', replacer, template)


def _render_template_deep(obj, context: dict):
    """Recursively render {{…}} templates in strings, dicts, and lists."""
    if isinstance(obj, str):
        return _render_template(obj, context)
    elif isinstance(obj, dict):
        return {k: _render_template_deep(v, context) for k, v in obj.items()}
    elif isinstance(obj, list):
        return [_render_template_deep(item, context) for item in obj]
    return obj


# ── Step executors ───────────────────────────────────────────────────


def _exec_api_call(step_config: dict, context: dict) -> dict:
    """Execute an HTTP API call step."""
    cfg = step_config
    url = _render_template(cfg.get("url", ""), context)
    method = cfg.get("method", "GET").upper()
    headers = {}
    for h in cfg.get("headers", []):
        headers[h.get("key", "")] = _render_template(h.get("value", ""), context)

    payload = cfg.get("payload")
    if isinstance(payload, str):
        payload = _render_template(payload, context)
        try:
            payload = json.loads(payload)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            pass
    elif isinstance(payload, (dict, list)):
        payload = _render_template_deep(payload, context)

    # Auth — accept both flat keys and nested auth_config from frontend
    auth = None
    auth_type = cfg.get("auth_type", "none")
    auth_cfg = cfg.get("auth_config", {})
    if auth_type == "basic":
        username = auth_cfg.get("username", "") or cfg.get("auth_username", "")
        password = auth_cfg.get("password", "") or cfg.get("auth_password", "")
        if username:
            auth = (username, password)
    elif auth_type == "bearer":
        token = auth_cfg.get("token", "") or cfg.get("auth_token", "")
        if token:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {token}"

    timeout = cfg.get("timeout", 30)

    # ── Self-referencing URL: route internally to avoid LB round-trip ─
    try:
        from urllib.parse import urlparse
        parsed = urlparse(url)
        if parsed.hostname in ("sqleditorapi.verizon.com", "localhost", "127.0.0.1"):
            internal_url = f"http://localhost:8080{parsed.path}"
            if parsed.query:
                internal_url += f"?{parsed.query}"
            logger.info(f"Scheduler: self-call detected → {internal_url}")
            url = internal_url
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        resp = http_requests.request(
            method, url, json=payload if method != "GET" else None,
            params=payload if method == "GET" else None,
            headers=headers, auth=auth, timeout=timeout, verify=False,
        )
        try:
            resp_data = resp.json()
        except Exception:
            resp_data = resp.text

        return {
            "status": "success" if resp.ok else "failed",
            "status_code": resp.status_code,
            "response": resp_data,
            "data": resp_data.get("data") if isinstance(resp_data, dict) else resp_data,
            "headers": dict(resp.headers),
        }
    except Exception as e:
        err_msg = getattr(e, 'detail', None) or str(e) or f"{type(e).__name__}: {repr(e)}"
        return {"status": "failed", "error": err_msg}


def _exec_api_call_upsert(step_config: dict, context: dict) -> dict:
    """Combined step: Call API (with dynamic payload) → upsert response into DB.

    This is the most common scheduler pattern: hit an external/internal API
    every day with a dynamic payload (e.g. date range) and upsert the
    returned rows into a database table using a primary-key column.

    Config keys (API part — same as api_call):
      url, method, headers, payload, auth_type, auth_config, timeout

    Config keys (DB upsert part):
      response_data_path : dot-path into the response JSON to reach the rows list
                           e.g. "data", "results", "response.records"  (default "data")
      db_type            : "cloudsql" | "bigquery" | "server:<id>"
      database           : target database / dataset name
      table              : target table name
      schema_name        : schema (default "public", ignored for BigQuery)
      pk_column          : primary-key column used for upsert conflict resolution
      mapping            : [{"source_field": "…", "target_column": "…"}, …]
                           If empty, auto-maps response keys → column names.
    """
    cfg = step_config

    # ── 1. Execute the API call (reuse full _exec_api_call with auth, templates, etc.)
    api_result = _exec_api_call(cfg, context)
    if api_result.get("status") == "failed":
        return {**api_result, "phase": "api_call"}

    # ── 2. Extract rows from response
    data_path = (cfg.get("response_data_path") or "").strip()
    resp = api_result.get("response", {})
    if not data_path:
        # No path specified — use response as-is (handles top-level arrays)
        data = resp
    elif isinstance(resp, dict):
        data = _resolve_path(resp, data_path)
    else:
        data = resp

    if data is None:
        # Try falling back to api_result["data"]
        data = api_result.get("data")
    if data is None:
        return {
            "status": "failed",
            "phase": "extract",
            "error": f"No data found at response path '{data_path}'. Top-level keys: {list(resp.keys()) if isinstance(resp, dict) else type(resp).__name__}",
            "api_status_code": api_result.get("status_code"),
        }

    if isinstance(data, dict):
        data = [data]
    if not isinstance(data, list):
        return {
            "status": "failed",
            "phase": "extract",
            "error": f"Data at '{data_path}' is {type(data).__name__}, expected list or dict",
            "api_status_code": api_result.get("status_code"),
        }
    if not data:
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "phase": "complete",
            "message": "API returned empty data — nothing to upsert",
            "rows_affected": 0,
            "api_status_code": api_result.get("status_code"),
        }

    # ── 3. Column mapping
    mapping = cfg.get("mapping", [])
    pk_column = cfg.get("pk_column", "") or cfg.get("update_key", "")
    database = cfg.get("database", "")
    table = cfg.get("table", "")
    schema_name = cfg.get("schema_name", "public")
    db_type = cfg.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    operation = cfg.get("operation", "upsert")  # "upsert" | "insert" | "truncate_insert"
    truncate_first = cfg.get("truncate_first", False) or operation == "truncate_insert"

    if not database:
        return {"status": "failed", "phase": "config", "error": "database name is required for upsert"}
    if not table:
        return {"status": "failed", "phase": "config", "error": "table name is required for upsert"}
    if operation == "upsert" and not pk_column:
        return {"status": "failed", "phase": "config", "error": "pk_column (primary key) is required for upsert"}

    valid_mapping = [m for m in mapping if m.get("source_field") and m.get("target_column")]
    if valid_mapping:
        mapped_rows = []
        for row in data:
            mapped = {}
            for m in valid_mapping:
                src = m["source_field"]
                tgt = m["target_column"]
                mapped[tgt] = _resolve_path(row, src) if isinstance(row, dict) else row
            if mapped:
                mapped_rows.append(mapped)
    else:
        # Auto-map: response keys become column names directly
        mapped_rows = [r for r in data if isinstance(r, dict)] if all(isinstance(r, dict) for r in data) else []

    if not mapped_rows:
        return {"status": "success", "phase": "complete", "message": "No rows after mapping", "rows_affected": 0}

    # ── 3b. Apply transformations
    transformations = cfg.get("transformations", [])
    if transformations:
        mapped_rows, t_log = _apply_row_transformations(mapped_rows, transformations, context)
        if not mapped_rows:
            return {"status": "success", "phase": "complete", "message": "No rows after transformations", "rows_affected": 0, "transform_stats": t_log}

    # ── 4. Resolve server connection
    server_id = None
    if db_type.startswith("server:"):
        extracted = db_type.replace("server:", "")
        if extracted not in ("cloudsql", "bigquery", ""):
            server_id = extracted
            db_type = "server"
        else:
            db_type = extracted or "cloudsql"

    connection = None
    try:
        if server_id:
            connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
            # Detect if MSSQL (pymssql) vs PostgreSQL (psycopg2)
            is_mssql = hasattr(connection, 'tds_version') or type(connection).__module__.startswith('pymssql')
        elif db_type == "bigquery":
            # BigQuery upsert via streaming insert
            table_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{database}.{table}"
            clean_rows = []
            for row in mapped_rows:
                clean = {}
                for k, v in row.items():
                    if hasattr(v, 'isoformat'):
                        clean[k] = v.isoformat()
                    else:
                        clean[k] = v
                clean_rows.append(clean)
            errors = bq_client.insert_rows_json(table_ref, clean_rows)
            if errors:
                return {"status": "failed", "phase": "upsert", "error": str(errors), "rows_affected": 0,
                        "api_status_code": api_result.get("status_code")}
            return {
                "status": "success", "phase": "complete",
                "rows_affected": len(clean_rows), "operation": "upsert",
                "api_status_code": api_result.get("status_code"),
                "message": f"Upserted {len(clean_rows)} rows into BigQuery {database}.{table}",
                "data": clean_rows[:5],
            }
        else:
            connection = get_db_connection(database)
            is_mssql = False

        if connection:
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            columns = list(mapped_rows[0].keys())
            total = 0

            # ── Truncate first (if requested) ──
            if truncate_first:
                if is_mssql:
                    tgt_tbl = f"[{schema_name}].[{table}]" if schema_name else f"[{table}]"
                    cursor.execute(f"TRUNCATE TABLE {tgt_tbl}")
                else:
                    cursor.execute(f'TRUNCATE TABLE "{schema_name}"."{table}"')
                logger.info(f"api_call_upsert: truncated {schema_name}.{table} before insert")

            if is_mssql:
                tgt_table = f"[{schema_name}].[{table}]" if schema_name else f"[{table}]"
                col_list = ", ".join(f"[{c}]" for c in columns)

                if operation == "upsert" and pk_column:
                    # ── MSSQL: use MERGE statement ──
                    src_cols = ", ".join(f"src.[{c}]" for c in columns)
                    update_cols = [c for c in columns if c != pk_column]
                    update_set = ", ".join(f"tgt.[{c}] = src.[{c}]" for c in update_cols)
                    for row in mapped_rows:
                        vals = [row.get(c) for c in columns]
                        merge_sql = f"""
                            MERGE {tgt_table} AS tgt
                            USING (SELECT {', '.join(f'%s AS [{c}]' for c in columns)}) AS src
                            ON tgt.[{pk_column}] = src.[{pk_column}]
                            WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET {update_set}
                            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT ({col_list}) VALUES ({src_cols});
                        """
                        cursor.execute(merge_sql, vals)
                        total += 1
                else:
                    # ── MSSQL: plain INSERT (all rows, including duplicates) ──
                    placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(columns))
                    for row in mapped_rows:
                        cursor.execute(f"INSERT INTO {tgt_table} ({col_list}) VALUES ({placeholders})",
                                       [row.get(c) for c in columns])
                        total += 1
                connection.commit()
            else:
                # ── PostgreSQL ──
                col_str = ", ".join(f'"{c}"' for c in columns)
                placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(columns))
                qualified = f'"{schema_name}"."{table}"'

                if operation == "upsert" and pk_column:
                    update_cols = [c for c in columns if c != pk_column]
                    if not update_cols:
                        sql = f'INSERT INTO {qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders}) ON CONFLICT ("{pk_column}") DO NOTHING'
                    else:
                        update_parts = ", ".join(f'"{c}" = EXCLUDED."{c}"' for c in update_cols)
                        sql = (
                            f'INSERT INTO {qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders}) '
                            f'ON CONFLICT ("{pk_column}") DO UPDATE SET {update_parts}'
                        )
                else:
                    # plain INSERT — all rows, including duplicates
                    sql = f'INSERT INTO {qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders})'

                for row in mapped_rows:
                    cursor.execute(sql, [row.get(c) for c in columns])
                    total += cursor.rowcount
                connection.commit()

            cursor.close()

            op_label = "Truncate+Insert" if truncate_first else ("Upserted" if operation == "upsert" else "Inserted")
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "phase": "complete",
                "rows_affected": total,
                "rows_parsed": len(mapped_rows),
                "operation": operation,
                "truncate_first": truncate_first,
                "pk_column": pk_column if operation == "upsert" else None,
                "api_status_code": api_result.get("status_code"),
                "message": f"{op_label} {total} rows into {schema_name}.{table}" + (f" (PK: {pk_column})" if pk_column and operation == "upsert" else ""),
                "data": mapped_rows[:5],
            }

    except Exception as e:
        if connection:
            try:
                connection.rollback()
            except Exception:
                pass
        err_msg = getattr(e, 'detail', None) or str(e) or f"{type(e).__name__}: {repr(e)}"
        logger.error(f"api_call_upsert DB phase failed: {err_msg}")
        return {
            "status": "failed",
            "phase": "upsert",
            "error": err_msg,
            "api_status_code": api_result.get("status_code"),
            "api_rows_received": len(mapped_rows),
        }
    finally:
        if connection:
            try:
                connection.close()
            except Exception:
                pass

    return {"status": "skipped"}


def _exec_truncate_table(step_config: dict, context: dict) -> dict:
    """Truncate (delete all rows from) a database table.
    Supports CloudSQL/PostgreSQL, BigQuery, and custom server connections.
    Config keys:
      - db_type: 'cloudsql' | 'bigquery' | 'server:<id>'
      - database: database/dataset name
      - table: table name to truncate
      - schema_name: schema (default 'public', ignored for BigQuery)
      - confirm: must be true to actually execute (safety guard)
    """
    cfg = step_config
    db_type = cfg.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    database = cfg.get("database", "")
    table = cfg.get("table", "")
    schema_name = cfg.get("schema_name", "public")
    confirm = cfg.get("confirm", False)

    if not database:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "database name is required"}
    if not table:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "table name is required"}
    if not confirm:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "Safety guard: set 'confirm' to true to allow truncation"}

    # Resolve server_id for custom servers
    server_id = None
    if db_type.startswith("server:"):
        extracted = db_type.replace("server:", "")
        if extracted not in ("cloudsql", "bigquery", ""):
            server_id = extracted
            db_type = "server"
        else:
            db_type = extracted or "cloudsql"

    connection = None
    try:
        if server_id:
            connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
        elif db_type == "bigquery":
            # BigQuery: use DELETE with no WHERE (TRUNCATE not supported on all table types)
            full_table = f"`{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{database}.{table}`"
            # Use TRUNCATE TABLE for BigQuery (supported since 2020)
            truncate_sql = f"TRUNCATE TABLE {full_table}"
            try:
                bq_client.query(truncate_sql).result()
            except Exception:
                # Fallback: DELETE all rows
                delete_sql = f"DELETE FROM {full_table} WHERE TRUE"
                bq_client.query(delete_sql).result()
            return {"status": "success", "operation": "truncate", "table": f"{database}.{table}", "message": "Table truncated successfully"}
        else:
            connection = get_db_connection(database)

        if connection:
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            qualified = f'"{schema_name}"."{table}"'
            cursor.execute(f"TRUNCATE TABLE {qualified}")
            connection.commit()
            cursor.close()
            return {"status": "success", "operation": "truncate", "table": f"{schema_name}.{table}", "message": "Table truncated successfully"}

    except Exception as e:
        if connection:
            connection.rollback()
        err_msg = getattr(e, 'detail', None) or str(e) or f"{type(e).__name__}: {repr(e)}"
        logger.error(f"truncate_table failed: {err_msg}")
        return {"status": "failed", "error": err_msg}
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

    return {"status": "skipped"}


def _exec_db_mapping(step_config: dict, context: dict) -> dict:
    """Map data from context to a DB table (insert / update / delete)."""
    cfg = step_config
    source_path = cfg.get("source_path", "")
    if not source_path:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "source_path is required — e.g. 'steps.fetch_data.data'"}

    data = _resolve_path(context, source_path)
    if data is None:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": f"No data found at path: '{source_path}'. Available steps: {list(context.get('steps', {}).keys())}"}

    if isinstance(data, dict):
        data = [data]
    if not isinstance(data, list):
        return {"status": "failed", "error": f"Source data at '{source_path}' is not a list/dict, got {type(data).__name__}"}

    db_type = cfg.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    database = cfg.get("database", "")
    table = cfg.get("table", "")
    schema_name = cfg.get("schema_name", "public")
    operation = cfg.get("operation", "insert")
    mapping = cfg.get("mapping", [])
    pk_column = cfg.get("pk_column", "") or cfg.get("update_key", "id")

    if not database:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "database name is required"}
    if not table:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "table name is required"}
    if not mapping:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "At least one column mapping (source_field → target_column) is required"}

    # Map source fields to target columns
    mapped_rows = []
    for row in data:
        mapped = {}
        for m in mapping:
            src = m.get("source_field", "")
            tgt = m.get("target_column", "")
            if src and tgt:
                val = _resolve_path(row, src) if isinstance(row, dict) else row
                mapped[tgt] = val
        if mapped:
            mapped_rows.append(mapped)

    if not mapped_rows:
        return {"status": "skipped", "message": "No rows after mapping"}

    # Resolve server_id — db_type can be "cloudsql", "bigquery", or "server:<id>"
    server_id = None
    if db_type.startswith("server:"):
        extracted = db_type.replace("server:", "")
        # Guard: "cloudsql" / "bigquery" are built-in types, not server IDs
        if extracted not in ("cloudsql", "bigquery", ""):
            server_id = extracted
            db_type = "server"  # normalize for clarity
        else:
            db_type = extracted or "cloudsql"  # treat "server:cloudsql" as "cloudsql"

    connection = None
    try:
        if server_id:
            connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
        elif db_type == "bigquery":
            # BigQuery operations
            table_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{database}.{table}"
            if operation == "insert":
                errors = bq_client.insert_rows_json(table_ref, mapped_rows)
                if errors:
                    return {"status": "failed", "error": str(errors), "rows_affected": 0}
                return {"status": "success", "rows_affected": len(mapped_rows), "operation": operation}
            elif operation == "upsert":
                # BigQuery MERGE for upsert
                if not pk_column:
                    return {"status": "failed", "error": "upsert requires a primary key column (update_key)"}
                columns = list(mapped_rows[0].keys())
                update_cols = [c for c in columns if c != pk_column]
                if not update_cols:
                    return {"status": "failed", "error": "No non-PK columns to update"}
                # Build a MERGE using UNNEST of a JSON array
                full_table = f"`{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{database}.{table}`"
                # Use a temp table approach: insert into temp, MERGE into target
                import json as _json
                # Serialize datetimes
                clean_rows = []
                for row in mapped_rows:
                    clean = {}
                    for k, v in row.items():
                        if hasattr(v, 'isoformat'):
                            clean[k] = v.isoformat()
                        else:
                            clean[k] = v
                    clean_rows.append(clean)
                # First insert into target (streaming), then use DML to resolve conflicts
                # BQ doesn't support ON CONFLICT, so use MERGE with a staging approach
                # Insert rows, then deduplicate via MERGE
                errors = bq_client.insert_rows_json(table_ref, clean_rows)
                if errors:
                    return {"status": "failed", "error": str(errors), "rows_affected": 0}
                return {"status": "success", "rows_affected": len(clean_rows), "operation": "upsert",
                        "message": f"Upserted {len(clean_rows)} rows into BigQuery (streaming insert — existing PKs will have new row versions)"}
            else:
                return {"status": "skipped", "message": f"BigQuery {operation} via mapping not yet supported"}
        else:
            connection = get_db_connection(database)

        cursor = connection.cursor()

        if operation == "insert":
            columns = list(mapped_rows[0].keys())
            col_str = ", ".join(f'"{c}"' for c in columns)
            placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(columns))
            qualified = f'"{schema_name}"."{table}"'
            sql = f"INSERT INTO {qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders})"
            total = 0
            for row in mapped_rows:
                cursor.execute(sql, [row.get(c) for c in columns])
                total += cursor.rowcount
            connection.commit()
            return {"status": "success", "rows_affected": total, "operation": "insert"}

        elif operation == "update":
            total = 0
            for row in mapped_rows:
                pk_val = row.pop(pk_column, None)
                if pk_val is None:
                    continue
                set_parts = []
                values = []
                for col, val in row.items():
                    set_parts.append(f'"{col}" = %s')
                    values.append(val)
                values.append(pk_val)
                qualified = f'"{schema_name}"."{table}"'
                sql = f'UPDATE {qualified} SET {", ".join(set_parts)} WHERE "{pk_column}" = %s'
                cursor.execute(sql, values)
                total += cursor.rowcount
            connection.commit()
            return {"status": "success", "rows_affected": total, "operation": "update"}

        elif operation == "delete":
            pk_values = [row.get(pk_column) for row in mapped_rows if row.get(pk_column) is not None]
            if not pk_values:
                return {"status": "skipped", "message": "No PK values to delete"}
            placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(pk_values))
            qualified = f'"{schema_name}"."{table}"'
            sql = f'DELETE FROM {qualified} WHERE "{pk_column}" IN ({placeholders})'
            cursor.execute(sql, pk_values)
            connection.commit()
            return {"status": "success", "rows_affected": cursor.rowcount, "operation": "delete"}

        elif operation == "upsert":
            # INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (pk_column) DO UPDATE — upserts rows by primary key
            if not pk_column:
                return {"status": "failed", "error": "upsert requires a primary key column (update_key)"}
            columns = list(mapped_rows[0].keys())
            col_str = ", ".join(f'"{c}"' for c in columns)
            placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(columns))
            update_cols = [c for c in columns if c != pk_column]
            if not update_cols:
                return {"status": "failed", "error": "No non-PK columns to update"}
            update_parts = ", ".join(f'"{c}" = EXCLUDED."{c}"' for c in update_cols)
            qualified = f'"{schema_name}"."{table}"'
            sql = (
                f'INSERT INTO {qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders}) '
                f'ON CONFLICT ("{pk_column}") DO UPDATE SET {update_parts}'
            )
            total = 0
            for row in mapped_rows:
                cursor.execute(sql, [row.get(c) for c in columns])
                total += cursor.rowcount
            connection.commit()
            return {"status": "success", "rows_affected": total, "operation": "upsert",
                    "message": f"Upserted {total} rows using PK '{pk_column}'"}

    except Exception as e:
        if connection:
            connection.rollback()
        err_msg = getattr(e, 'detail', None) or str(e) or f"{type(e).__name__}: {repr(e)}"
        logger.error(f"db_mapping failed: {err_msg}")
        return {"status": "failed", "error": err_msg}
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

    return {"status": "skipped"}


# ── Row transformation engine ──────────────────────────────────────


def _safe_expression_eval(expression: str, row: dict) -> Any:
    """Safely evaluate a simple expression against a row dict.
    Supports: column references, string concatenation (+), arithmetic (+,-,*,/),
    and whitelisted functions: upper, lower, trim, left, right, replace,
    substring, coalesce, len, abs, round, int, float, str.
    """
    import ast
    import operator

    _SAFE_FUNCS = {
        "upper": lambda s: str(s).upper() if s is not None else "",
        "lower": lambda s: str(s).lower() if s is not None else "",
        "trim": lambda s: str(s).strip() if s is not None else "",
        "left": lambda s, n: str(s)[:int(n)] if s is not None else "",
        "right": lambda s, n: str(s)[-int(n):] if s is not None else "",
        "replace": lambda s, old, new: str(s).replace(str(old), str(new)) if s is not None else "",
        "substring": lambda s, start, length=None: str(s)[int(start):int(start) + int(length)] if length else str(s)[int(start):],
        "coalesce": lambda *args: next((a for a in args if a is not None and a != ""), None),
        "len": lambda s: len(str(s)) if s is not None else 0,
        "abs": lambda n: abs(float(n)),
        "round": lambda n, d=0: round(float(n), int(d)),
        "int": lambda n: int(float(n)) if n is not None else 0,
        "float": lambda n: float(n) if n is not None else 0.0,
        "str": lambda n: str(n) if n is not None else "",
        "concat": lambda *args: "".join(str(a) if a is not None else "" for a in args),
        "iif": lambda cond, t, f: t if cond else f,
        "now": lambda: datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        "today": lambda: datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
    }

    expr = expression.strip()
    if not expr:
        return None

    func_pattern = re.compile(r'(\w+)\s*\(')
    col_pattern = re.compile(r'\b([a-zA-Z_]\w*)\b')

    func_match = func_pattern.match(expr)
    if func_match:
        func_name = func_match.group(1).lower()
        if func_name in _SAFE_FUNCS:
            paren_start = expr.index("(")
            depth = 0
            args_str = ""
            for i, ch in enumerate(expr[paren_start:], paren_start):
                if ch == "(":
                    depth += 1
                elif ch == ")":
                    depth -= 1
                    if depth == 0:
                        args_str = expr[paren_start + 1:i]
                        break

            args = []
            current = ""
            depth = 0
            for ch in args_str:
                if ch == "(":
                    depth += 1
                    current += ch
                elif ch == ")":
                    depth -= 1
                    current += ch
                elif ch == "," and depth == 0:
                    args.append(current.strip())
                    current = ""
                else:
                    current += ch
            if current.strip():
                args.append(current.strip())

            resolved_args = []
            for arg in args:
                arg = arg.strip()
                if (arg.startswith('"') and arg.endswith('"')) or (arg.startswith("'") and arg.endswith("'")):
                    resolved_args.append(arg[1:-1])
                elif arg.replace(".", "", 1).replace("-", "", 1).isdigit():
                    resolved_args.append(float(arg) if "." in arg else int(arg))
                elif func_pattern.match(arg):
                    resolved_args.append(_safe_expression_eval(arg, row))
                elif arg in row:
                    resolved_args.append(row.get(arg))
                else:
                    resolved_args.append(arg)

            return _SAFE_FUNCS[func_name](*resolved_args)

    if "+" in expr and not any(expr.strip().startswith(f) for f in _SAFE_FUNCS):
        parts = expr.split("+")
        results = []
        all_numeric = True
        for part in parts:
            part = part.strip()
            if (part.startswith('"') and part.endswith('"')) or (part.startswith("'") and part.endswith("'")):
                results.append(part[1:-1])
                all_numeric = False
            elif part.replace(".", "", 1).replace("-", "", 1).isdigit():
                results.append(float(part) if "." in part else int(part))
            elif part in row:
                val = row.get(part)
                results.append(val)
                if not isinstance(val, (int, float)):
                    all_numeric = False
            else:
                results.append(part)
                all_numeric = False

        if all_numeric:
            return sum(float(r) for r in results)
        return "".join(str(r) if r is not None else "" for r in results)

    for op_char, op_func in [("*", operator.mul), ("-", operator.sub), ("/", operator.truediv)]:
        if op_char in expr and not any(expr.strip().startswith(f) for f in _SAFE_FUNCS):
            parts = expr.split(op_char, 1)
            if len(parts) == 2:
                left = parts[0].strip()
                right = parts[1].strip()
                left_val = row.get(left, left)
                right_val = row.get(right, right)
                try:
                    return op_func(float(left_val), float(right_val))
                except (ValueError, TypeError, ZeroDivisionError):
                    return None

    if (expr.startswith('"') and expr.endswith('"')) or (expr.startswith("'") and expr.endswith("'")):
        return expr[1:-1]
    if expr.replace(".", "", 1).replace("-", "", 1).isdigit():
        return float(expr) if "." in expr else int(expr)
    if expr in row:
        return row.get(expr)

    template_pattern = re.compile(r'\{(\w+)\}')
    if template_pattern.search(expr):
        def repl(m):
            col = m.group(1)
            val = row.get(col, "")
            return str(val) if val is not None else ""
        return template_pattern.sub(repl, expr)

    return expr


def _apply_row_transformations(rows: list, transformations: list, context: dict = None) -> tuple:
    """Apply a pipeline of transformations to row dicts.
    Returns (transformed_rows, transform_log).
    """
    if not transformations or not rows:
        return rows, {}

    transform_log = {"steps": [], "total_in": len(rows), "total_out": 0}
    current_rows = list(rows)

    for t_cfg in transformations:
        if not t_cfg.get("enabled", True):
            continue
        t_type = t_cfg.get("type", "")
        t_conf = t_cfg.get("config", t_cfg)
        step_log = {"type": t_type, "rows_in": len(current_rows), "errors": []}

        try:
            if t_type == "filter_rows":
                column = t_conf.get("column", "")
                op = t_conf.get("operator", "eq")
                value = t_conf.get("value")
                filtered = []
                for row in current_rows:
                    cond = {"field": column, "op": op, "value": value}
                    if _evaluate_condition(cond, row):
                        filtered.append(row)
                current_rows = filtered

            elif t_type == "add_column":
                target_col = t_conf.get("target_column", "")
                expr_type = t_conf.get("expression_type", "static")
                expression = t_conf.get("expression", "")

                for row in current_rows:
                    try:
                        if expr_type == "static":
                            row[target_col] = expression
                        elif expr_type == "column_ref":
                            row[target_col] = row.get(expression)
                        elif expr_type == "concat":
                            template_pattern = re.compile(r'\{(\w+)\}')
                            def repl(m):
                                val = row.get(m.group(1), "")
                                return str(val) if val is not None else ""
                            row[target_col] = template_pattern.sub(repl, expression)
                        elif expr_type in ("math", "expression"):
                            row[target_col] = _safe_expression_eval(expression, row)
                        else:
                            row[target_col] = _safe_expression_eval(expression, row)
                    except Exception as e:
                        if len(step_log["errors"]) < 10:
                            step_log["errors"].append(str(e)[:200])
                        row[target_col] = None

            elif t_type == "validate":
                column = t_conf.get("column", "")
                rule = t_conf.get("rule", "not_null")
                rule_config = t_conf.get("rule_config", {})
                on_fail = t_conf.get("on_fail", "skip")
                valid_rows = []

                for row in current_rows:
                    val = row.get(column)
                    is_valid = True
                    try:
                        if rule == "not_null":
                            is_valid = val is not None and val != ""
                        elif rule == "regex":
                            pattern = rule_config.get("pattern", "")
                            is_valid = bool(re.match(pattern, str(val))) if val is not None else False
                        elif rule == "range":
                            min_val = rule_config.get("min")
                            max_val = rule_config.get("max")
                            num_val = float(val) if val is not None else None
                            if num_val is None:
                                is_valid = False
                            else:
                                if min_val is not None and num_val < float(min_val):
                                    is_valid = False
                                if max_val is not None and num_val > float(max_val):
                                    is_valid = False
                        elif rule == "type_check":
                            expected = rule_config.get("expected_type", "str")
                            if expected == "int":
                                int(val)
                            elif expected == "float":
                                float(val)
                            elif expected == "date":
                                datetime.fromisoformat(str(val))
                        elif rule == "unique":
                            pass
                    except (ValueError, TypeError):
                        is_valid = False

                    if is_valid:
                        valid_rows.append(row)
                    elif on_fail == "fail":
                        raise ValueError(f"Validation failed for column '{column}': value={val}, rule={rule}")
                    else:
                        if len(step_log["errors"]) < 10:
                            step_log["errors"].append(f"Row skipped: {column}={val} failed {rule}")

                if rule == "unique":
                    seen = set()
                    unique_rows = []
                    for row in valid_rows:
                        key = row.get(column)
                        if key not in seen:
                            seen.add(key)
                            unique_rows.append(row)
                        else:
                            if len(step_log["errors"]) < 10:
                                step_log["errors"].append(f"Duplicate skipped: {column}={key}")
                    valid_rows = unique_rows

                current_rows = valid_rows

            elif t_type == "rename_column":
                from_col = t_conf.get("from_column", "")
                to_col = t_conf.get("to_column", "")
                if from_col and to_col:
                    for row in current_rows:
                        if from_col in row:
                            row[to_col] = row.pop(from_col)

            elif t_type == "type_cast":
                column = t_conf.get("column", "")
                target_type = t_conf.get("target_type", "string")
                fmt = t_conf.get("format", "")
                on_error = t_conf.get("on_error", "skip")

                for row in current_rows:
                    val = row.get(column)
                    if val is None:
                        continue
                    try:
                        if target_type == "string":
                            row[column] = str(val)
                        elif target_type == "int":
                            row[column] = int(float(val))
                        elif target_type == "float":
                            row[column] = float(val)
                        elif target_type == "bool":
                            row[column] = str(val).lower() in ("true", "1", "yes", "t", "y")
                        elif target_type == "date":
                            if fmt:
                                row[column] = datetime.strptime(str(val), fmt).isoformat()
                            else:
                                row[column] = datetime.fromisoformat(str(val)).isoformat()
                    except (ValueError, TypeError) as e:
                        if on_error == "fail":
                            raise
                        if len(step_log["errors"]) < 10:
                            step_log["errors"].append(f"Cast failed: {column}={val} to {target_type}: {e}")

            elif t_type == "deduplicate":
                columns = t_conf.get("columns", [])
                if isinstance(columns, str):
                    columns = [c.strip() for c in columns.split(",") if c.strip()]
                keep = t_conf.get("keep", "first")

                if columns:
                    if keep == "last":
                        current_rows = list(reversed(current_rows))
                    seen = set()
                    unique = []
                    for row in current_rows:
                        key = tuple(row.get(c) for c in columns)
                        if key not in seen:
                            seen.add(key)
                            unique.append(row)
                    if keep == "last":
                        unique = list(reversed(unique))
                    current_rows = unique

            elif t_type == "custom_expression":
                target_col = t_conf.get("target_column", "")
                expression = t_conf.get("expression", "")
                on_error = t_conf.get("on_error", "skip")

                for row in current_rows:
                    try:
                        row[target_col] = _safe_expression_eval(expression, row)
                    except Exception as e:
                        if on_error == "fail":
                            raise
                        if len(step_log["errors"]) < 10:
                            step_log["errors"].append(f"Expression failed: {e}")
                        row[target_col] = None

        except Exception as e:
            step_log["error"] = str(e)[:500]
            step_log["rows_out"] = len(current_rows)
            transform_log["steps"].append(step_log)
            transform_log["total_out"] = len(current_rows)
            transform_log["status"] = "failed"
            raise

        step_log["rows_out"] = len(current_rows)
        transform_log["steps"].append(step_log)

    transform_log["total_out"] = len(current_rows)
    transform_log["status"] = "success"
    return current_rows, transform_log


def _exec_db_transfer(step_config: dict, context: dict) -> dict:
    """Transfer data directly from one DB table to another.
    Supports same/different DB, same/different server, CloudSQL/BigQuery/custom servers.
    Uses chunked read/write to avoid OOM on large tables.
    """
    cfg = step_config
    BATCH_SIZE = int(cfg.get("batch_size", 2000))  # rows per chunk

    # Source config
    src_db_type   = cfg.get("source_db_type", "cloudsql")
    src_database  = cfg.get("source_database", "")
    src_table     = cfg.get("source_table", "")
    src_schema    = cfg.get("source_schema", "public")
    src_query     = cfg.get("source_query", "")            # Optional: custom query instead of SELECT *
    src_limit     = cfg.get("source_limit", 0)             # 0 = no limit

    # Target config
    tgt_db_type   = cfg.get("target_db_type", "cloudsql")
    tgt_database  = cfg.get("target_database", "")
    tgt_table     = cfg.get("target_table", "")
    tgt_schema    = cfg.get("target_schema", "public")
    tgt_operation = cfg.get("target_operation", "insert")   # insert | upsert | replace
    tgt_pk        = cfg.get("target_pk", "")                # PK column for upsert

    # Column mapping (optional) — if empty, uses same column names
    mapping       = cfg.get("mapping", [])
    only_matching = cfg.get("only_matching_columns", False)   # auto-filter to columns that exist in target
    on_error      = cfg.get("on_error", "fail")               # fail | skip  — skip = skip bad rows, continue
    transformations = cfg.get("transformations", [])

    # Pagination config — for large tables, auto-split by a column (e.g. RowID)
    paginate_col  = (cfg.get("pagination_column") or "").strip()  # e.g. "RowID"
    paginate_size = int(cfg.get("pagination_size", 50000))        # rows per page/chunk

    if not src_database or not src_table:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "Source database and table are required"}
    if not tgt_database or not tgt_table:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "Target database and table are required"}

    # ── Helper: get target table columns ────────────────────
    _target_columns_cache = []   # populated lazily on first batch

    def _get_target_columns(tgt_conn, is_mssql, is_bq):
        """Return a list of column names from the target table."""
        if is_bq:
            tbl = bq_client.get_table(f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{tgt_database}.{tgt_table}")
            return [f.name for f in tbl.schema]
        cur = tgt_conn.cursor()
        if is_mssql:
            cur.execute(
                "SELECT COLUMN_NAME FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_NAME = %s AND TABLE_SCHEMA = %s ORDER BY ORDINAL_POSITION",
                (tgt_table, tgt_schema or 'dbo')
            )
        else:
            cur.execute(
                "SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_name = %s AND table_schema = %s ORDER BY ordinal_position",
                (tgt_table, tgt_schema or 'public')
            )
        cols = [r[0] for r in cur.fetchall()]
        cur.close()
        return cols

    # ── Helper: apply column mapping to a batch of rows ─────
    valid_mapping = [m for m in (mapping or []) if m.get("source_field") and m.get("target_column")]

    def _map_batch(rows, tgt_conn=None, is_mssql=False, is_bq=False):
        nonlocal _target_columns_cache
        # Explicit mapping takes priority
        if valid_mapping:
            mapped = []
            for row in rows:
                m_row = {}
                for m in valid_mapping:
                    m_row[m["target_column"]] = row.get(m["source_field"])
                mapped.append(m_row)
            return mapped
        # Auto-match: keep only columns that exist in the target table
        if only_matching and not valid_mapping:
            if not _target_columns_cache:
                _target_columns_cache = _get_target_columns(tgt_conn, is_mssql, is_bq)
            keep_lower = {c.lower(): c for c in _target_columns_cache}
            return [{keep_lower[k.lower()]: v for k, v in row.items() if k.lower() in keep_lower} for row in rows]
        return rows

    # ── Helper: write a batch to the target ─────────────────
    skipped_rows = []   # collect sample of skipped rows (max 20 total)

    def _write_batch(rows, tgt_conn, is_first_batch, is_tgt_mssql):
        if not rows:
            return 0, 0
        columns = list(rows[0].keys())
        if not columns:
            return 0, 0  # No columns matched — skip write and do NOT truncate
        total = 0
        skipped = 0

        if is_tgt_mssql:
            tgt_tbl = f"[{tgt_schema}].[{tgt_table}]" if tgt_schema else f"[{tgt_table}]"
            col_list = ", ".join(f"[{c}]" for c in columns)
            placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(columns))

            if tgt_operation == "replace" and is_first_batch:
                tgt_conn.cursor().execute(f"TRUNCATE TABLE {tgt_tbl}")

            cursor = tgt_conn.cursor()
            if tgt_operation == "upsert" and tgt_pk:
                src_cols = ", ".join(f"src.[{c}]" for c in columns)
                update_cols = [c for c in columns if c != tgt_pk]
                update_set = ", ".join(f"tgt.[{c}] = src.[{c}]" for c in update_cols)
                for row in rows:
                    vals = [row.get(c) for c in columns]
                    merge_sql = f"""
                        MERGE {tgt_tbl} AS tgt
                        USING (SELECT {', '.join(f'%s AS [{c}]' for c in columns)}) AS src
                        ON tgt.[{tgt_pk}] = src.[{tgt_pk}]
                        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET {update_set}
                        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT ({col_list}) VALUES ({src_cols});
                    """
                    try:
                        cursor.execute(merge_sql, vals)
                        total += 1
                    except Exception as row_err:
                        if on_error == "skip":
                            skipped += 1
                            if len(skipped_rows) < 20:
                                skipped_rows.append({"row": row, "error": str(row_err)[:200]})
                        else:
                            raise
            else:
                for row in rows:
                    try:
                        cursor.execute(f"INSERT INTO {tgt_tbl} ({col_list}) VALUES ({placeholders})",
                                       [row.get(c) for c in columns])
                        total += 1
                    except Exception as row_err:
                        if on_error == "skip":
                            skipped += 1
                            if len(skipped_rows) < 20:
                                skipped_rows.append({"row": row, "error": str(row_err)[:200]})
                        else:
                            raise
            tgt_conn.commit()
            cursor.close()
        else:
            # PostgreSQL target — use SAVEPOINTs for skip mode
            col_str = ", ".join(f'"{c}"' for c in columns)
            placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(columns))
            tgt_qualified = f'"{tgt_schema}"."{tgt_table}"'

            if tgt_operation == "replace" and is_first_batch:
                tgt_conn.cursor().execute(f'TRUNCATE TABLE {tgt_qualified}')

            cursor = tgt_conn.cursor()
            if tgt_operation == "upsert" and tgt_pk:
                update_parts = ", ".join(f'"{c}" = EXCLUDED."{c}"' for c in columns if c != tgt_pk)
                if update_parts:
                    sql = f'INSERT INTO {tgt_qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders}) ON CONFLICT ("{tgt_pk}") DO UPDATE SET {update_parts}'
                else:
                    sql = f'INSERT INTO {tgt_qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders}) ON CONFLICT ("{tgt_pk}") DO NOTHING'
            else:
                sql = f'INSERT INTO {tgt_qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders})'

            for row in rows:
                vals = [row.get(c) for c in columns]
                if on_error == "skip":
                    try:
                        cursor.execute("SAVEPOINT row_sp")
                        cursor.execute(sql, vals)
                        cursor.execute("RELEASE SAVEPOINT row_sp")
                        total += cursor.rowcount
                    except Exception as row_err:
                        cursor.execute("ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT row_sp")
                        skipped += 1
                        if len(skipped_rows) < 20:
                            skipped_rows.append({"row": row, "error": str(row_err)[:200]})
                else:
                    cursor.execute(sql, vals)
                    total += cursor.rowcount
            tgt_conn.commit()
            cursor.close()

        return total, skipped

    # ── Resolve target connection up-front ───────────────────
    tgt_server_id = None
    if tgt_db_type.startswith("server:"):
        extracted = tgt_db_type.replace("server:", "")
        if extracted not in ("cloudsql", "bigquery", ""):
            tgt_server_id = extracted
        else:
            tgt_db_type = extracted or "cloudsql"

    is_tgt_mssql = False
    is_tgt_bq = tgt_db_type == "bigquery"

    # ── 1. READ from source (chunked) & WRITE in batches ────
    src_connection = None
    tgt_connection = None
    total_read = 0
    total_written = 0
    total_skipped = 0
    sample_data = []
    src_columns = []

    try:
        # Build query
        if src_query:
            query = src_query
        else:
            # Detect source dialect: look up the actual server type rather than
            # assuming "server:" prefix always means MSSQL (it can be PostgreSQL too).
            _src_server_id_peek = None
            if src_db_type.startswith("server:"):
                _candidate = src_db_type.replace("server:", "")
                if _candidate not in ("cloudsql", "bigquery", ""):
                    _src_server_id_peek = _candidate
            if _src_server_id_peek:
                _srv_type = _server_store.get(_src_server_id_peek, {}).get("db_type", "cloudsql")
                _src_is_mssql = _srv_type == "mssql"
            else:
                _src_is_mssql = False
            if _src_is_mssql:
                _sch = src_schema if src_schema else 'dbo'
                src_qualified = f'[{_sch}].[{src_table}]'
                query = f'SELECT * FROM {src_qualified}'
                if src_limit and int(src_limit) > 0:
                    query += f' ORDER BY (SELECT NULL) OFFSET 0 ROWS FETCH NEXT {int(src_limit)} ROWS ONLY'
            else:
                _sch = src_schema if src_schema else 'public'
                src_qualified = f'"{_sch}"."{src_table}"'
                query = f'SELECT * FROM {src_qualified}'
                if src_limit and int(src_limit) > 0:
                    query += f' LIMIT {int(src_limit)}'

        # Resolve source connection
        src_server_id = None
        if src_db_type.startswith("server:"):
            extracted = src_db_type.replace("server:", "")
            if extracted not in ("cloudsql", "bigquery", ""):
                src_server_id = extracted
            else:
                src_db_type = extracted or "cloudsql"

        # ── BigQuery source (streams into memory — paginated by BQ client) ──
        if not src_server_id and src_db_type == "bigquery":
            full_table = f"`{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{src_database}.{src_table}`"
            bq_query = src_query if src_query else f"SELECT * FROM {full_table}"
            if src_limit and int(src_limit) > 0 and not src_query:
                bq_query += f" LIMIT {int(src_limit)}"
            query_job = bq_client.query(bq_query)
            result = query_job.result()
            src_columns = [f.name for f in result.schema]

            # Open target
            if tgt_server_id:
                tgt_connection = _get_server_connection(tgt_server_id, tgt_database)
                is_tgt_mssql = hasattr(tgt_connection, 'tds_version') or type(tgt_connection).__module__.startswith('pymssql')
            elif not is_tgt_bq:
                tgt_connection = get_db_connection(tgt_database)

            batch = []
            is_first = True
            for row in result:
                batch.append(dict(row))
                if len(batch) >= BATCH_SIZE:
                    mapped = _map_batch(batch, tgt_conn=tgt_connection, is_mssql=is_tgt_mssql, is_bq=is_tgt_bq)
                    if transformations:
                        mapped, _ = _apply_row_transformations(mapped, transformations, context)
                    if not sample_data:
                        sample_data = mapped[:5]
                    if is_tgt_bq:
                        table_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{tgt_database}.{tgt_table}"
                        clean = [{k: v.isoformat() if hasattr(v, 'isoformat') else v for k, v in r.items()} for r in mapped]
                        errors = bq_client.insert_rows_json(table_ref, clean)
                        if errors:
                            return {"status": "failed", "error": str(errors), "source_rows": total_read}
                        total_written += len(clean)
                    else:
                        w, s = _write_batch(mapped, tgt_connection, is_first, is_tgt_mssql)
                        total_written += w
                        total_skipped += s
                    total_read += len(batch)
                    batch = []
                    is_first = False
            # Flush remaining
            if batch:
                mapped = _map_batch(batch, tgt_conn=tgt_connection, is_mssql=is_tgt_mssql, is_bq=is_tgt_bq)
                if transformations:
                    mapped, _ = _apply_row_transformations(mapped, transformations, context)
                if not sample_data:
                    sample_data = mapped[:5]
                if is_tgt_bq:
                    table_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{tgt_database}.{tgt_table}"
                    clean = [{k: v.isoformat() if hasattr(v, 'isoformat') else v for k, v in r.items()} for r in mapped]
                    errors = bq_client.insert_rows_json(table_ref, clean)
                    if errors:
                        return {"status": "failed", "error": str(errors), "source_rows": total_read}
                    total_written += len(clean)
                else:
                    w, s = _write_batch(mapped, tgt_connection, is_first, is_tgt_mssql)
                    total_written += w
                    total_skipped += s
                total_read += len(batch)

        else:
            # ── SQL source (PostgreSQL / MSSQL) — chunked fetchmany ──
            if src_server_id:
                src_connection = _get_server_connection(src_server_id, src_database)
            else:
                src_connection = get_db_connection(src_database)

            # Detect if source is MSSQL for quoting
            _src_is_mssql = hasattr(src_connection, 'tds_version') or type(src_connection).__module__.startswith('pymssql')

            # Open target
            if tgt_server_id:
                tgt_connection = _get_server_connection(tgt_server_id, tgt_database)
                is_tgt_mssql = hasattr(tgt_connection, 'tds_version') or type(tgt_connection).__module__.startswith('pymssql')
            elif is_tgt_bq:
                pass  # BigQuery doesn't need a connection object
            else:
                tgt_connection = get_db_connection(tgt_database)

            is_first = True

            # ── If pagination_column is set, auto-split into range queries ──
            if paginate_col and not src_query:
                # First, get the max value of the pagination column
                _sch = src_schema if src_schema and src_schema != 'public' else ('dbo' if _src_is_mssql else 'public')
                if _src_is_mssql:
                    _src_tbl = f'[{_sch}].[{src_table}]'
                    _col_ref = f'[{paginate_col}]'
                else:
                    _src_tbl = f'"{_sch}"."{src_table}"'
                    _col_ref = f'"{paginate_col}"'

                max_cursor = src_connection.cursor()
                max_cursor.execute(f'SELECT MAX({_col_ref}) FROM {_src_tbl}')
                max_val = max_cursor.fetchone()[0] or 0
                max_cursor.close()
                logger.info(f"db_transfer: paginating by {paginate_col}, max={max_val}, chunk={paginate_size}")

                page_start = 1
                while page_start <= max_val:
                    page_end = page_start + paginate_size - 1
                    page_query = f'SELECT * FROM {_src_tbl} WHERE {_col_ref} BETWEEN {page_start} AND {page_end}'
                    logger.info(f"db_transfer: page {page_start}-{page_end}")

                    pg_cursor = src_connection.cursor()
                    pg_cursor.execute(page_query)
                    if not src_columns:
                        src_columns = [desc[0] for desc in pg_cursor.description]

                    while True:
                        raw_rows = pg_cursor.fetchmany(BATCH_SIZE)
                        if not raw_rows:
                            break
                        batch = [dict(row) if isinstance(row, dict) else dict(zip(src_columns, row)) for row in raw_rows]
                        mapped = _map_batch(batch, tgt_conn=tgt_connection, is_mssql=is_tgt_mssql, is_bq=is_tgt_bq)
                        if transformations:
                            mapped, _ = _apply_row_transformations(mapped, transformations, context)
                        if not sample_data:
                            sample_data = mapped[:5]

                        if is_tgt_bq:
                            table_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{tgt_database}.{tgt_table}"
                            clean = [{k: v.isoformat() if hasattr(v, 'isoformat') else v for k, v in r.items()} for r in mapped]
                            errors = bq_client.insert_rows_json(table_ref, clean)
                            if errors:
                                return {"status": "failed", "error": str(errors), "source_rows": total_read}
                            total_written += len(clean)
                        else:
                            w, s = _write_batch(mapped, tgt_connection, is_first, is_tgt_mssql)
                            total_written += w
                            total_skipped += s

                        total_read += len(batch)
                        is_first = False

                    pg_cursor.close()
                    page_start = page_end + 1

            else:
                # ── Standard single-query path ──
                src_cursor = src_connection.cursor()
                src_cursor.execute(query)
                src_columns = [desc[0] for desc in src_cursor.description]

                while True:
                    raw_rows = src_cursor.fetchmany(BATCH_SIZE)
                    if not raw_rows:
                        break
                    batch = [dict(row) if isinstance(row, dict) else dict(zip(src_columns, row)) for row in raw_rows]
                    mapped = _map_batch(batch, tgt_conn=tgt_connection, is_mssql=is_tgt_mssql, is_bq=is_tgt_bq)
                    if transformations:
                        mapped, _ = _apply_row_transformations(mapped, transformations, context)
                    if not sample_data:
                        sample_data = mapped[:5]

                    if is_tgt_bq:
                        table_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{tgt_database}.{tgt_table}"
                        clean = [{k: v.isoformat() if hasattr(v, 'isoformat') else v for k, v in r.items()} for r in mapped]
                        errors = bq_client.insert_rows_json(table_ref, clean)
                        if errors:
                            return {"status": "failed", "error": str(errors), "source_rows": total_read}
                        total_written += len(clean)
                    else:
                        w, s = _write_batch(mapped, tgt_connection, is_first, is_tgt_mssql)
                        total_written += w
                        total_skipped += s

                    total_read += len(batch)
                    is_first = False

                src_cursor.close()

        if total_read == 0:
            return {"status": "skipped", "message": "No rows found in source", "source_rows": 0}

        result_msg = f"Transferred {total_written} rows in batches of {BATCH_SIZE}"
        if total_skipped > 0:
            result_msg += f" ({total_skipped} rows skipped due to errors)"

        return {
            "status": "success",
            "source_rows": total_read,
            "rows_affected": total_written,
            "rows_skipped": total_skipped,
            "operation": tgt_operation,
            "batch_size": BATCH_SIZE,
            "message": result_msg,
            "data": sample_data,
            **(({"skipped_sample": skipped_rows}) if skipped_rows else {}),
        }

    except Exception as e:
        if tgt_connection:
            try:
                tgt_connection.rollback()
            except Exception:
                pass
        err_msg = getattr(e, 'detail', None) or str(e) or f"{type(e).__name__}: {repr(e)}"
        return {"status": "failed", "error": f"Transfer failed: {err_msg}", "source_rows": total_read, "rows_written": total_written}
    finally:
        if src_connection:
            try:
                src_connection.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
        if tgt_connection:
            try:
                tgt_connection.close()
            except Exception:
                pass


def _exec_email_trigger(step_config: dict, context: dict) -> dict:
    """Send emails based on conditions. Uses do-not-reply SMTP."""
    cfg = step_config
    # Accept both 'to' (frontend) and 'recipients' field names
    recipients = cfg.get("to", "") or cfg.get("recipients", [])
    if isinstance(recipients, str):
        recipients = [r.strip() for r in recipients.split(",") if r.strip()]
    elif isinstance(recipients, list):
        recipients = [r.strip() for r in recipients if isinstance(r, str) and r.strip()]

    subject = _render_template(cfg.get("subject", "Scheduler Notification"), context)
    body = _render_template(cfg.get("body", ""), context)
    from_email = cfg.get("from_email", SCHEDULER_FROM_EMAIL)

    if not recipients:
        return {"status": "skipped", "message": "No recipients"}

    try:
        server = smtplib.SMTP(SCHEDULER_SMTP_SERVER, timeout=30)
        server.set_debuglevel(0)

        sent = 0
        failed = 0
        for idx, to_email in enumerate(recipients[:SCHEDULER_MAX_EMAILS]):
            try:
                msg = MIMEMultipart("alternative")
                msg["Subject"] = subject
                msg["From"] = from_email
                msg["To"] = to_email

                is_html = "<" in body and ">" in body
                msg_type = "html" if is_html else "plain"
                msg.attach(MIMEText(body, msg_type))

                server.send_message(msg, from_addr=from_email)
                sent += 1

                if idx < len(recipients) - 1:
                    time.sleep(SCHEDULER_EMAIL_RATE_LIMIT)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Email failed to {to_email}: {e}")
                failed += 1

        server.quit()
        return {"status": "success", "sent": sent, "failed": failed}
    except Exception as e:
        err_msg = getattr(e, 'detail', None) or str(e) or f"{type(e).__name__}: {repr(e)}"
        return {"status": "failed", "error": err_msg}


def _exec_gcs_operation(step_config: dict, context: dict) -> dict:
    """Perform GCS bucket operations (list / delete / upload / move)."""
    cfg = step_config
    bucket_name = _render_template(cfg.get("bucket", ""), context)
    operation = cfg.get("operation", "list")
    # 'prefix' doubles as the object path for delete/upload/move on the frontend
    file_path = _render_template(cfg.get("file_path", "") or cfg.get("prefix", ""), context)
    prefix = _render_template(cfg.get("prefix", ""), context)

    if not gcs_client:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "GCS client not initialized"}
    if not bucket_name:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "No bucket name provided"}

    try:
        bucket = gcs_client.bucket(bucket_name)

        if operation == "list":
            blobs = list(bucket.list_blobs(prefix=prefix or None, max_results=500))
            files = [{"name": b.name, "size": b.size, "updated": b.updated.isoformat() if b.updated else ""} for b in blobs]
            return {"status": "success", "files": files, "count": len(files)}

        elif operation == "delete":
            if not file_path:
                return {"status": "failed", "error": "No file_path for delete"}
            # Support comma-separated or list
            paths = [p.strip() for p in file_path.split(",") if p.strip()] if isinstance(file_path, str) else [file_path]
            deleted = 0
            for p in paths:
                blob = bucket.blob(p)
                if blob.exists():
                    blob.delete()
                    deleted += 1
            return {"status": "success", "deleted": deleted}

        elif operation == "upload":
            # Upload data from context (accept both 'source_path' and 'source_data_path')
            source_path = cfg.get("source_path", "") or cfg.get("source_data_path", "")
            data = _resolve_path(context, source_path) if source_path else cfg.get("upload_content", "")
            if data is None:
                return {"status": "failed", "error": "No data to upload"}
            content = json.dumps(data, default=str) if not isinstance(data, str) else data
            blob = bucket.blob(file_path)
            blob.upload_from_string(content, content_type=cfg.get("content_type", "application/json"))
            return {"status": "success", "uploaded": file_path, "size": len(content)}

        elif operation == "move":
            dest_path = _render_template(cfg.get("dest_path", "") or cfg.get("destination", ""), context)
            if not file_path or not dest_path:
                return {"status": "failed", "error": "Need file_path and dest_path for move"}
            source_blob = bucket.blob(file_path)
            bucket.copy_blob(source_blob, bucket, dest_path)
            source_blob.delete()
            return {"status": "success", "moved": f"{file_path} → {dest_path}"}

        return {"status": "failed", "error": f"Unknown GCS operation: {operation}"}
    except Exception as e:
        err_msg = getattr(e, 'detail', None) or str(e) or f"{type(e).__name__}: {repr(e)}"
        return {"status": "failed", "error": err_msg}


def _parse_csv_content(content: str, delimiter: str = ",", has_header: bool = True) -> tuple:
    """Parse CSV string into (columns, rows) where rows is a list of dicts."""
    import io as _io
    reader = csv.reader(_io.StringIO(content), delimiter=delimiter)
    all_rows = list(reader)
    if not all_rows:
        return [], []
    if has_header:
        columns = [c.strip() for c in all_rows[0]]
        data_rows = all_rows[1:]
    else:
        columns = [f"col_{i}" for i in range(len(all_rows[0]))]
        data_rows = all_rows
    rows = []
    for row in data_rows:
        if not any(cell.strip() for cell in row):
            continue  # skip blank lines
        d = {}
        for i, col in enumerate(columns):
            val = row[i].strip() if i < len(row) else None
            # Auto-cast: numbers, booleans, nulls
            if val is None or val == "":
                d[col] = None
            elif val.lower() in ("null", "none"):
                d[col] = None
            elif val.lower() in ("true", "false"):
                d[col] = val.lower() == "true"
            else:
                try:
                    if "." in val:
                        d[col] = float(val)
                    else:
                        d[col] = int(val)
                except ValueError:
                    d[col] = val
        rows.append(d)
    return columns, rows


def _exec_file_import(step_config: dict, context: dict) -> dict:
    """Import data from a CSV or SQL file into a database table.

    Supports three file sources:
      1. GCS bucket:  gcs_bucket + gcs_path
      2. URL:         file_url (HTTP GET to download)
      3. Inline:      file_content (CSV/SQL text stored in step config)

    For CSV files:
      - Parses rows using delimiter (default comma)
      - Supports has_header toggle
      - INSERT or UPSERT into target table (pk_column required for upsert)
      - Optional column mapping

    For SQL files:
      - Executes each statement sequentially against the target database
      - Supports INSERT, COPY, and any DML

    Config keys:
      file_type         : "csv" | "sql"  (default "csv")
      file_source       : "gcs" | "url" | "inline"
      gcs_bucket        : GCS bucket name   (when file_source = "gcs")
      gcs_path          : object path       (when file_source = "gcs")
      file_url          : URL to download   (when file_source = "url")
      file_content      : raw text content  (when file_source = "inline")
      delimiter         : CSV delimiter      (default ",")
      has_header        : bool               (default true)
      db_type           : "cloudsql" | "bigquery" | "server:<id>"
      database          : target database
      table             : target table        (CSV mode only)
      schema_name       : schema              (default "public")
      operation         : "insert" | "upsert" (default "insert")
      pk_column         : primary key column  (required for upsert)
      mapping           : [{source_field, target_column}, …] (optional)
    """
    cfg = step_config

    # ── 1. Resolve file content ──────────────────────────────
    file_type = cfg.get("file_type", "csv").lower()
    file_source = cfg.get("file_source", "inline")
    content = None

    if file_source == "gcs":
        bucket_name = _render_template(cfg.get("gcs_bucket", ""), context)
        gcs_path = _render_template(cfg.get("gcs_path", ""), context)
        if not bucket_name or not gcs_path:
            return {"status": "failed", "error": "GCS bucket and path are required"}
        if not gcs_client:
            return {"status": "failed", "error": "GCS client not initialized"}
        try:
            bucket = gcs_client.bucket(bucket_name)
            blob = bucket.blob(gcs_path)
            content = blob.download_as_text(encoding="utf-8")
        except Exception as e:
            return {"status": "failed", "error": f"Failed to download from GCS: {e}"}

    elif file_source == "url":
        file_url = _render_template(cfg.get("file_url", ""), context)
        if not file_url:
            return {"status": "failed", "error": "file_url is required"}
        try:
            resp = http_requests.get(file_url, timeout=60, verify=False)
            resp.raise_for_status()
            content = resp.text
        except Exception as e:
            return {"status": "failed", "error": f"Failed to download file from URL: {e}"}

    elif file_source == "inline":
        content = cfg.get("file_content", "")
