        if not content:
            # Try getting content from a previous step in context
            data_path = cfg.get("content_data_path", "")
            if data_path:
                content = _resolve_path(context, data_path)
                if content and not isinstance(content, str):
                    content = str(content)
    else:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": f"Unknown file_source: {file_source}"}

    if not content or not content.strip():
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "No file content to import (empty file or failed download)"}

    # ── 2. Parse content ─────────────────────────────────────
    db_type = cfg.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    database = cfg.get("database", "")
    table = cfg.get("table", "")
    schema_name = cfg.get("schema_name", "public")
    operation = cfg.get("operation", "insert")
    pk_column = cfg.get("pk_column", "")
    mapping = cfg.get("mapping", [])

    if not database:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "database name is required"}

    # Resolve server_id
    server_id = None
    if db_type.startswith("server:"):
        extracted = db_type.replace("server:", "")
        if extracted not in ("cloudsql", "bigquery", ""):
            server_id = extracted
            db_type = "server"
        else:
            db_type = extracted or "cloudsql"

    # ── SQL file mode ────────────────────────────────────────
    if file_type == "sql":
        connection = None
        try:
            if server_id:
                connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
                is_mssql = hasattr(connection, 'tds_version') or type(connection).__module__.startswith('pymssql')
            elif db_type == "bigquery":
                # Execute SQL statements via BigQuery client
                statements = [s.strip() for s in content.split(";") if s.strip()]
                executed = 0
                for stmt in statements:
                    try:
                        bq_client.query(stmt).result()
                        executed += 1
                    except Exception as e:
                        return {
                            "status": "failed",
                            "error": f"BigQuery statement #{executed + 1} failed: {e}",
                            "statements_executed": executed,
                            "total_statements": len(statements),
                        }
                return {
                    "status": "success",
                    "file_type": "sql",
                    "statements_executed": executed,
                    "message": f"Executed {executed} SQL statement(s) on BigQuery {database}",
                }
            else:
                connection = get_db_connection(database)
                is_mssql = False

            if connection:
                cursor = connection.cursor()
                statements = [s.strip() for s in content.split(";") if s.strip()]
                executed = 0
                total_rows = 0
                for stmt in statements:
                    cursor.execute(stmt)
                    if hasattr(cursor, 'rowcount') and cursor.rowcount and cursor.rowcount > 0:
                        total_rows += cursor.rowcount
                    executed += 1
                connection.commit()
                cursor.close()
                return {
                    "status": "success",
                    "file_type": "sql",
                    "statements_executed": executed,
                    "rows_affected": total_rows,
                    "message": f"Executed {executed} SQL statement(s), {total_rows} row(s) affected",
                }

        except Exception as e:
            if connection:
                try:
                    connection.rollback()
                except Exception:
                    pass
            err_msg = getattr(e, 'detail', None) or str(e) or f"{type(e).__name__}: {repr(e)}"
            return {"status": "failed", "error": f"SQL execution error: {err_msg}"}
        finally:
            if connection:
                try:
                    connection.close()
                except Exception:
                    pass
        return {"status": "skipped"}

    # ── CSV file mode ────────────────────────────────────────
    if not table:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "table name is required for CSV import"}

    delimiter = cfg.get("delimiter", ",")
    has_header = cfg.get("has_header", True)

    columns, rows = _parse_csv_content(content, delimiter=delimiter, has_header=has_header)
    if not rows:
        return {"status": "success", "message": "CSV file is empty or has no data rows", "rows_affected": 0}

    # Apply column mapping if set
    valid_mapping = [m for m in mapping if m.get("source_field") and m.get("target_column")]
    if valid_mapping:
        mapped_rows = []
        for row in rows:
            mapped = {}
            for m in valid_mapping:
                mapped[m["target_column"]] = row.get(m["source_field"])
            if mapped:
                mapped_rows.append(mapped)
        rows = mapped_rows
        columns = [m["target_column"] for m in valid_mapping]

    if not rows:
        return {"status": "success", "message": "No rows after column mapping", "rows_affected": 0}

    # ── Write to DB ──────────────────────────────────────────
    connection = None
    try:
        if server_id:
            connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
            is_mssql = hasattr(connection, 'tds_version') or type(connection).__module__.startswith('pymssql')
        elif db_type == "bigquery":
            table_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{database}.{table}"
            clean_rows = []
            for row in rows:
                clean = {}
                for k, v in row.items():
                    if hasattr(v, 'isoformat'):
                        clean[k] = v.isoformat()
                    else:
                        clean[k] = v
                clean_rows.append(clean)
            errors = bq_client.insert_rows_json(table_ref, clean_rows)
            if errors:
                return {"status": "failed", "error": str(errors), "rows_parsed": len(rows)}
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "file_type": "csv",
                "rows_parsed": len(rows),
                "rows_affected": len(clean_rows),
                "operation": operation,
                "message": f"Imported {len(clean_rows)} rows into BigQuery {database}.{table}",
            }
        else:
            connection = get_db_connection(database)
            is_mssql = False

        if connection:
            cursor = connection.cursor()
            cols = list(rows[0].keys())

            if is_mssql:
                if operation == "upsert" and pk_column:
                    col_list = ", ".join(f"[{c}]" for c in cols)
                    src_cols = ", ".join(f"src.[{c}]" for c in cols)
                    update_cols = [c for c in cols if c != pk_column]
                    update_set = ", ".join(f"tgt.[{c}] = src.[{c}]" for c in update_cols)
                    tgt_table = f"[{schema_name}].[{table}]" if schema_name else f"[{table}]"
                    total = 0
                    for row in rows:
                        vals = [row.get(c) for c in cols]
                        merge_sql = f"""
                            MERGE {tgt_table} AS tgt
                            USING (SELECT {', '.join(f'%s AS [{c}]' for c in cols)}) AS src
                            ON tgt.[{pk_column}] = src.[{pk_column}]
                            WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET {update_set}
                            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT ({col_list}) VALUES ({src_cols});
                        """
                        cursor.execute(merge_sql, vals)
                        total += 1
                else:
                    tgt_table = f"[{schema_name}].[{table}]" if schema_name else f"[{table}]"
                    col_list = ", ".join(f"[{c}]" for c in cols)
                    placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(cols))
                    total = 0
                    for row in rows:
                        cursor.execute(f"INSERT INTO {tgt_table} ({col_list}) VALUES ({placeholders})",
                                       [row.get(c) for c in cols])
                        total += 1
                connection.commit()
            else:
                # PostgreSQL
                col_str = ", ".join(f'"{c}"' for c in cols)
                placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(cols))
                qualified = f'"{schema_name}"."{table}"'

                if operation == "upsert" and pk_column:
                    update_cols = [c for c in cols if c != pk_column]
                    if update_cols:
                        update_parts = ", ".join(f'"{c}" = EXCLUDED."{c}"' for c in update_cols)
                        sql = (
                            f'INSERT INTO {qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders}) '
                            f'ON CONFLICT ("{pk_column}") DO UPDATE SET {update_parts}'
                        )
                    else:
                        sql = f'INSERT INTO {qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders}) ON CONFLICT ("{pk_column}") DO NOTHING'
                else:
                    sql = f'INSERT INTO {qualified} ({col_str}) VALUES ({placeholders})'

                total = 0
                for row in rows:
                    cursor.execute(sql, [row.get(c) for c in cols])
                    total += cursor.rowcount
                connection.commit()

            cursor.close()
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "file_type": "csv",
                "rows_parsed": len(rows),
                "columns": cols,
                "rows_affected": total,
                "operation": operation,
                "pk_column": pk_column if operation == "upsert" else None,
                "message": f"{'Upserted' if operation == 'upsert' else 'Inserted'} {total} rows into {schema_name}.{table}",
                "data": rows[:5],  # sample for context
            }

    except Exception as e:
        if connection:
            try:
                connection.rollback()
            except Exception:
                pass
        err_msg = getattr(e, 'detail', None) or str(e) or f"{type(e).__name__}: {repr(e)}"
        logger.error(f"file_import failed: {err_msg}")
        return {"status": "failed", "error": err_msg, "rows_parsed": len(rows) if rows else 0}
    finally:
        if connection:
            try:
                connection.close()
            except Exception:
                pass

    return {"status": "skipped"}


def _exec_sql_script(step_config: dict, context: dict) -> dict:
    """Execute multiple SQL statements in a single transaction.

    Config keys:
      db_type          : 'cloudsql' | 'bigquery' | 'server:<id>'
      database         : database name
      schema_name      : schema (default 'public', ignored for BigQuery)
      transaction_mode : wrap all statements in BEGIN/COMMIT/ROLLBACK (default True)
      on_error         : 'rollback' | 'continue' (default 'rollback')
      statements       : list of {id, name, sql, enabled, capture_result, validation}
      variables        : dict of custom template variables available as {{key}}
    """
    cfg = step_config
    db_type = cfg.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    database = cfg.get("database", "")
    schema_name = cfg.get("schema_name", "public")
    transaction_mode = cfg.get("transaction_mode", True)
    on_error = cfg.get("on_error", "rollback")
    statements = cfg.get("statements", [])
    custom_variables = cfg.get("variables", {})

    if not database:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "database name is required"}
    if not statements:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "at least one SQL statement is required"}

    enabled_stmts = [s for s in statements if s.get("enabled", True)]
    if not enabled_stmts:
        return {"status": "skipped", "message": "all statements are disabled"}

    # Resolve server_id for custom servers
    server_id = None
    if db_type.startswith("server:"):
        extracted = db_type.replace("server:", "")
        if extracted not in ("cloudsql", "bigquery", ""):
            server_id = extracted
            db_type = "server"
        else:
            db_type = extracted or "cloudsql"

    # Build enriched context with custom variables
    exec_context = dict(context)
    exec_context["vars"] = {}
    for k, v in custom_variables.items():
        resolved = _render_template(str(v), context) if isinstance(v, str) else v
        exec_context["vars"][k] = resolved
        if k not in exec_context:
            exec_context[k] = resolved

    statement_results = []
    total_affected = 0
    executed_count = 0
    overall_status = "success"

    # ── BigQuery path ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    if db_type == "bigquery":
        try:
            for stmt in enabled_stmts:
                stmt_name = stmt.get("name", f"statement_{executed_count + 1}")
                raw_sql = stmt.get("sql", "").strip()
                if not raw_sql:
                    statement_results.append({"name": stmt_name, "status": "skipped", "message": "empty SQL"})
                    continue

                rendered_sql = _render_template(raw_sql, exec_context)
                stmt_start = datetime.now()
                try:
                    job = bq_client.query(rendered_sql)
                    result_rows = list(job.result())
                    affected = job.num_dml_affected_rows if job.num_dml_affected_rows is not None else len(result_rows)
                    duration_ms = round((datetime.now() - stmt_start).total_seconds() * 1000, 2)

                    stmt_result = {
                        "name": stmt_name,
                        "status": "success",
                        "affected_rows": affected,
                        "duration_ms": duration_ms,
                        "sql_preview": rendered_sql[:200],
                    }

                    if stmt.get("capture_result") and result_rows:
                        rows_data = [dict(r) for r in result_rows[:1000]]
                        stmt_result["data"] = rows_data
                        exec_context.setdefault("sql_script_results", {})[stmt_name] = rows_data

                    # Validation
                    validation = stmt.get("validation", {})
                    if validation.get("enabled"):
                        passed, err_msg = _validate_sql_statement_result(affected, validation)
                        stmt_result["validation"] = {"passed": passed, "message": err_msg}
                        if not passed and validation.get("fail_action", "abort") == "abort":
                            stmt_result["status"] = "failed"
                            statement_results.append(stmt_result)
                            overall_status = "failed"
                            break

                    statement_results.append(stmt_result)
                    total_affected += affected
                    executed_count += 1

                except Exception as e:
                    duration_ms = round((datetime.now() - stmt_start).total_seconds() * 1000, 2)
                    err_msg = str(e)[:500]
                    stmt_result = {
                        "name": stmt_name, "status": "failed", "error": err_msg,
                        "duration_ms": duration_ms, "sql_preview": rendered_sql[:200],
                    }
                    statement_results.append(stmt_result)
                    overall_status = "failed" if on_error == "rollback" else "partial"
                    if on_error == "rollback":
                        break

            return {
                "status": overall_status,
                "statements_executed": executed_count,
                "statements_total": len(enabled_stmts),
                "total_affected_rows": total_affected,
                "transaction_mode": False,
                "note": "BigQuery does not support explicit transaction wrapping in this mode",
                "statement_results": statement_results,
                "message": f"Executed {executed_count}/{len(enabled_stmts)} statements, {total_affected} total rows affected",
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {"status": "failed", "error": str(e)[:500], "statement_results": statement_results}

    # ── CloudSQL / MSSQL path ──────────────────────────────────────
    connection = None
    is_mssql = False
    try:
        if server_id:
            connection = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
            s_info = _load_server(server_id)
            is_mssql = s_info.get("type", "").lower() in ("mssql", "sqlserver", "sql_server")
        else:
            connection = get_db_connection(database)

        if not connection:
            return {"status": "failed", "error": "could not establish database connection"}

        if is_mssql:
            cursor = connection.cursor(as_dict=True)
            if transaction_mode:
                cursor.execute("BEGIN TRANSACTION")
        else:
            cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=psycopg2.extras.RealDictCursor)

        for stmt in enabled_stmts:
            stmt_name = stmt.get("name", f"statement_{executed_count + 1}")
            raw_sql = stmt.get("sql", "").strip()
            if not raw_sql:
                statement_results.append({"name": stmt_name, "status": "skipped", "message": "empty SQL"})
                continue

            rendered_sql = _render_template(raw_sql, exec_context)
            stmt_start = datetime.now()
            try:
                cursor.execute(rendered_sql)
                affected = cursor.rowcount if cursor.rowcount >= 0 else 0
                duration_ms = round((datetime.now() - stmt_start).total_seconds() * 1000, 2)

                stmt_result = {
                    "name": stmt_name,
                    "status": "success",
                    "affected_rows": affected,
                    "duration_ms": duration_ms,
                    "sql_preview": rendered_sql[:200],
                }

                # Capture SELECT results if requested
                if stmt.get("capture_result"):
                    try:
                        rows = cursor.fetchall()
                        rows_data = [dict(r) for r in rows[:1000]]
                        stmt_result["data"] = rows_data
                        stmt_result["row_count"] = len(rows_data)
                        exec_context.setdefault("sql_script_results", {})[stmt_name] = rows_data
                    except psycopg2.ProgrammingError:
                        pass
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                # Validation
                validation = stmt.get("validation", {})
                if validation.get("enabled"):
                    passed, val_msg = _validate_sql_statement_result(affected, validation)
                    stmt_result["validation"] = {"passed": passed, "message": val_msg}
                    if not passed:
                        fail_action = validation.get("fail_action", "abort")
                        if fail_action == "abort":
                            stmt_result["status"] = "failed"
                            statement_results.append(stmt_result)
                            overall_status = "failed"
                            if transaction_mode:
                                if is_mssql:
                                    cursor.execute("ROLLBACK TRANSACTION")
                                else:
                                    connection.rollback()
                            break
                        elif fail_action == "warn":
                            stmt_result["status"] = "warning"

                statement_results.append(stmt_result)
                total_affected += affected
                executed_count += 1

            except Exception as e:
                duration_ms = round((datetime.now() - stmt_start).total_seconds() * 1000, 2)
                err_msg = str(e)[:500]
                stmt_result = {
                    "name": stmt_name, "status": "failed", "error": err_msg,
                    "duration_ms": duration_ms, "sql_preview": rendered_sql[:200],
                }
                statement_results.append(stmt_result)

                if on_error == "rollback":
                    overall_status = "failed"
                    if transaction_mode:
                        if is_mssql:
                            cursor.execute("ROLLBACK TRANSACTION")
                        else:
                            connection.rollback()
                    break
                else:
                    overall_status = "partial"

        # Commit on success
        if overall_status != "failed":
            if transaction_mode:
                if is_mssql:
                    cursor.execute("COMMIT TRANSACTION")
                else:
                    connection.commit()
            else:
                connection.commit()

        cursor.close()
        return {
            "status": overall_status,
            "statements_executed": executed_count,
            "statements_total": len(enabled_stmts),
            "total_affected_rows": total_affected,
            "transaction_mode": transaction_mode,
            "statement_results": statement_results,
            "message": f"Executed {executed_count}/{len(enabled_stmts)} statements, {total_affected} total rows affected",
        }

    except Exception as e:
        if connection:
            try:
                connection.rollback()
            except Exception:
                pass
        err_msg = str(e)[:500]
        logger.error(f"sql_script failed: {err_msg}")
        return {"status": "failed", "error": err_msg, "statement_results": statement_results}
    finally:
        if connection:
            try:
                connection.close()
            except Exception:
                pass


def _validate_sql_statement_result(affected_rows: int, validation: dict) -> tuple:
    """Check affected_rows against validation config. Returns (passed, message)."""
    op = validation.get("operator", "gte")
    try:
        expected = int(validation.get("value", 0))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        expected = 0

    ops = {
        "eq": affected_rows == expected,
        "neq": affected_rows != expected,
        "gt": affected_rows > expected,
        "gte": affected_rows >= expected,
        "lt": affected_rows < expected,
        "lte": affected_rows <= expected,
    }
    passed = ops.get(op, True)
    if not passed:
        return False, f"Validation failed: {affected_rows} rows (expected {op} {expected})"
    return True, None


def _exec_conditional(step_config: dict, context: dict) -> dict:
    """Evaluate a condition against the execution context and return a result.

    Config keys:
      condition_field    : dot-path into context to evaluate, e.g. "steps.fetch.response.count"
      condition_operator : eq | neq | gt | lt | gte | lte | contains | not_empty | is_empty | exists
      condition_value    : expected value for comparison
      then_action        : "continue" | "skip" | "fail"   (when condition IS met)
      else_action        : "continue" | "skip" | "fail"   (when condition is NOT met)
      then_message       : optional message logged when condition is met
      else_message       : optional message logged when condition is NOT met

    The returned dict contains:
      status             : "success" | "skipped" | "failed"
      condition_result   : True / False
      action             : the chosen action string
      skip_remaining     : True if the orchestrator should stop processing further steps
    """
    cfg = step_config
    condition_field    = cfg.get("condition_field", "")
    condition_operator = cfg.get("condition_operator", "eq")
    condition_value    = cfg.get("condition_value", "")
    then_action        = cfg.get("then_action", "continue")
    else_action        = cfg.get("else_action", "skip")
    then_message       = cfg.get("then_message", "")
    else_message       = cfg.get("else_message", "")

    condition = {"field": condition_field, "op": condition_operator, "value": condition_value}
    condition_met = _evaluate_condition(condition, context)

    chosen_action  = then_action  if condition_met else else_action
    chosen_message = then_message if condition_met else else_message

    if chosen_action == "continue":
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "condition_result": condition_met,
            "action": chosen_action,
            "message": chosen_message or f"Condition {'met' if condition_met else 'not met'} → continue",
        }
    elif chosen_action == "skip":
        return {
            "status": "skipped",
            "condition_result": condition_met,
            "action": chosen_action,
            "skip_remaining": True,
            "message": chosen_message or f"Condition {'met' if condition_met else 'not met'} → skipping remaining steps",
        }
    else:  # "fail"
        return {
            "status": "failed",
            "condition_result": condition_met,
            "action": chosen_action,
            "message": chosen_message or f"Condition {'met' if condition_met else 'not met'} → failing job",
        }


# ── Job execution orchestrator ───────────────────────────────────────


def _execute_scheduler_job(job_id: str, run_id: str = None) -> dict:
    """Execute all steps of a scheduler job in order."""
    job = _load_scheduler_job(job_id)
    if not job:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "Job not found"}

    # ── Check dependencies — all must have last_run_status == 'success' ──
    deps = job.get("dependencies", [])
    if deps:
        failed_deps = []
        for dep_id in deps:
            dep_job = _load_scheduler_job(dep_id)
            if not dep_job:
                failed_deps.append(f"{dep_id} (not found)")
            elif dep_job.get("last_run_status") != "success":
                dep_status = dep_job.get("last_run_status") or "never run"
                failed_deps.append(f"{dep_job.get('name', dep_id)} ({dep_status})")
        if failed_deps:
            # Save a run log showing the dependency failure
            if not run_id:
                run_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:12]
            run = {
                "id": run_id,
                "job_id": job_id,
                "status": "failed",
                "started_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "finished_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "duration_ms": 0,
                "steps_log": [],
                "error": f"Dependency check failed: {', '.join(failed_deps)}",
            }
            _save_run_log(run)
            job["last_run_at"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
            job["last_run_status"] = "failed"
            _save_scheduler_job(job)
            return run

    if not run_id:
        run_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:12]
    start_time = datetime.now()
    run = {
        "id": run_id,
        "job_id": job_id,
        "status": "running",
        "started_at": start_time.isoformat(),
        "steps_log": [],
    }
    _save_run_log(run)

    context = {"job_id": job_id, "run_id": run_id, "steps": {}}

    sorted_steps = sorted(job.get("steps", []), key=lambda s: s.get("order", 0))
    overall_status = "success"

    for step in sorted_steps:
        if not step.get("enabled", True):
            continue

        step_name = step.get("name", step.get("id", "unknown"))
        step_type = step.get("type", "")
        step_cfg = step.get("config", {})
        step_conditions = step.get("conditions", [])

        # Check conditions
        if not _evaluate_conditions(step_conditions, context):
            step_log = {"name": step_name, "type": step_type, "status": "skipped", "message": "Conditions not met"}
            run["steps_log"].append(step_log)
            context["steps"][step_name] = step_log
            continue

        step_start = datetime.now()
        try:
            if step_type == "api_call":
                result = _exec_api_call(step_cfg, context)
            elif step_type == "api_call_upsert":
                result = _exec_api_call_upsert(step_cfg, context)
            elif step_type == "db_mapping":
                result = _exec_db_mapping(step_cfg, context)
            elif step_type == "db_transfer":
                result = _exec_db_transfer(step_cfg, context)
            elif step_type == "email_trigger":
                result = _exec_email_trigger(step_cfg, context)
            elif step_type == "gcs_operation":
                result = _exec_gcs_operation(step_cfg, context)
            elif step_type == "truncate_table":
                result = _exec_truncate_table(step_cfg, context)
            elif step_type == "file_import":
                result = _exec_file_import(step_cfg, context)
            elif step_type == "conditional":
                result = _exec_conditional(step_cfg, context)
            elif step_type == "sql_script":
                result = _exec_sql_script(step_cfg, context)
            else:
                result = {"status": "failed", "error": f"Unknown step type: {step_type}"}
        except Exception as e:
            err_msg = getattr(e, 'detail', None) or str(e) or f"{type(e).__name__}: {repr(e)}"
            result = {"status": "failed", "error": err_msg}

        step_elapsed = (datetime.now() - step_start).total_seconds() * 1000
        step_log = {
            "name": step_name,
            "type": step_type,
            "status": result.get("status", "unknown"),
            "duration_ms": round(step_elapsed, 2),
            **{k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != "status"},
        }
        run["steps_log"].append(step_log)
        context["steps"][step_name] = result

        # Conditional step can signal to skip all remaining steps
        if result.get("skip_remaining"):
            break

        if result.get("status") == "failed":
            overall_status = "failed"
            # On fail, check if we should stop
            if step_cfg.get("stop_on_fail", True):
                break

    elapsed = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000
    run["status"] = overall_status
    run["finished_at"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
    run["duration_ms"] = round(elapsed, 2)
    _save_run_log(run)

    # Update job last_run
    job["last_run_at"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
    job["last_run_status"] = overall_status
    _save_scheduler_job(job)

    return run


# ── Background scheduler thread ─────────────────────────────────────

_scheduler_running = False


def _scheduler_loop():
    """Background thread: check for interval/cron jobs and run them."""
    global _scheduler_running
    _scheduler_running = True
    logger.info("Scheduler background thread started")

    # Track last run times for interval jobs
    last_runs: Dict[str, datetime] = {}

    while _scheduler_running:
        try:
            jobs = _load_all_scheduler_jobs()
            now = datetime.now()

            for job in jobs:
                if not job.get("enabled"):
                    continue
                stype = job.get("schedule_type", "manual")
                scfg = job.get("schedule_config", {})
                jid = job["id"]

                if stype == "interval":
                    interval_min = scfg.get("interval_minutes", 60)
                    last = last_runs.get(jid)
                    # On first check after startup, seed from DB to avoid spurious immediate fire
                    if last is None:
                        db_last = job.get("last_run_at")
                        if db_last:
                            try:
                                last = datetime.fromisoformat(db_last)
                                last_runs[jid] = last
                            except Exception:
                                pass
                    if last is None or (now - last).total_seconds() >= interval_min * 60:
                        logger.info(f"Scheduler: running interval job '{job['name']}' ({jid})")
                        try:
                            if job.get("execution_mode") == "cloud_job":
                                _create_or_update_cloud_run_job(jid, job["name"], job.get("cloud_job_config", {}))
                                _execute_cloud_run_job(jid, job.get("cloud_job_config", {}))
                            else:
                                _execute_scheduler_job(jid)
                        except Exception as e:
                            logger.error(f"Scheduler job {jid} failed: {e}")
                        last_runs[jid] = now

                elif stype == "cron":
                    # Cron: evaluate hour/minute/days_of_week expressions
                    cron_hour = scfg.get("hour")
                    cron_minute = scfg.get("minute", 0)
                    cron_days = scfg.get("days_of_week")  # e.g. [0,1,2,3,4] = Mon-Fri

                    def _cron_field_match(expr, value: int) -> bool:
                        """Match a single cron field: *, */n, n-m, n,m,p, or plain n."""
                        s = str(expr).strip()
                        if s == '*':
                            return True
                        if s.startswith('*/'):
                            step = int(s[2:])
                            return value % step == 0
                        if '-' in s:
                            lo, hi = s.split('-', 1)
                            return int(lo) <= value <= int(hi)
                        if ',' in s:
                            return value in [int(x) for x in s.split(',')]
                        return int(s) == value

                    if cron_hour is not None:
                        hour_match = _cron_field_match(cron_hour, now.hour)
                        minute_match = _cron_field_match(cron_minute, now.minute)
                        # days_of_week may be ints (0=Mon) or strings ('mon','tue',...)
                        _day_map = {'mon': 0, 'tue': 1, 'wed': 2, 'thu': 3, 'fri': 4, 'sat': 5, 'sun': 6}
                        if cron_days is not None:
                            _normalized = [_day_map.get(str(d).lower(), d) if not str(d).isdigit() else int(d) for d in cron_days]
                            day_match = now.weekday() in _normalized
                        else:
                            day_match = True

                        if hour_match and minute_match and day_match:
                            last = last_runs.get(jid)
                            # Seed from DB on first run after startup
                            if last is None:
                                db_last = job.get("last_run_at")
                                if db_last:
                                    try:
                                        last = datetime.fromisoformat(db_last)
                                        last_runs[jid] = last
                                    except Exception:
                                        pass
                            # Don't run again within the same minute
                            if last is None or (now - last).total_seconds() >= 120:
                                logger.info(f"Scheduler: running cron job '{job['name']}' ({jid})")
                                try:
                                    if job.get("execution_mode") == "cloud_job":
                                        _create_or_update_cloud_run_job(jid, job["name"], job.get("cloud_job_config", {}))
                                        _execute_cloud_run_job(jid, job.get("cloud_job_config", {}))
                                    else:
                                        _execute_scheduler_job(jid)
                                except Exception as e:
                                    logger.error(f"Scheduler job {jid} failed: {e}")
                                last_runs[jid] = now

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Scheduler loop error: {e}")

        time.sleep(60)  # Check every 60 seconds


# Start the background scheduler (skip in Cloud Run Job mode)
if not os.getenv("SCHEDULER_JOB_ID"):
    _scheduler_thread = threading.Thread(target=_scheduler_loop, daemon=True)
    _scheduler_thread.start()


# ── REST API endpoints ───────────────────────────────────────────────


@app.post("/api/scheduler/jobs")
async def create_scheduler_job(config: SchedulerJobConfig, request: Request):
    """Create a new scheduler job."""
    user_id = _get_user_id(request)
    config.id = config.id or str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
    config.created_at = datetime.now().isoformat()
    config.updated_at = config.created_at
    for step in config.steps:
        step.id = step.id or str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]

    job = config.model_dump()
    job["created_by"] = user_id
    _save_scheduler_job(job)
    logger.info(f"Scheduler job created by {user_id}: {config.id} – {config.name}")
    return {"success": True, "job": job}


@app.get("/api/scheduler/jobs")
async def list_scheduler_jobs(request: Request):
    """List scheduler jobs — scoped to user; super-admin sees all."""
    user_id = _get_user_id(request)
    all_jobs = _load_all_scheduler_jobs()
    if _is_super_admin(user_id):
        jobs = all_jobs
    else:
        jobs = [j for j in all_jobs if j.get("created_by", "") == user_id or j.get("created_by", "") == ""]
    return {"success": True, "jobs": jobs, "count": len(jobs)}


@app.get("/api/scheduler/jobs/{job_id}")
async def get_scheduler_job(job_id: str = Path(...)):
    """Get a single scheduler job."""
    job = _load_scheduler_job(job_id)
    if not job:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Job not found")
    return {"success": True, "job": job}


@app.put("/api/scheduler/jobs/{job_id}")
async def update_scheduler_job(job_id: str = Path(...), config: SchedulerJobConfig = None, request: Request = None):
    """Update a scheduler job."""
    user_id = _get_user_id(request) if request else ""
    existing = _load_scheduler_job(job_id)
    if not existing:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Job not found")
    owner = existing.get("created_by", "")
    if owner and not _is_super_admin(user_id) and owner != user_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not authorised to update this job")
    updated = config.model_dump()
    updated["id"] = job_id
    updated["created_at"] = existing.get("created_at")
    updated["updated_at"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
    updated["last_run_at"] = existing.get("last_run_at", "")
    updated["last_run_status"] = existing.get("last_run_status", "")
    updated["created_by"] = existing.get("created_by", user_id)
    for step in updated.get("steps", []):
        if not step.get("id"):
            step["id"] = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
    _save_scheduler_job(updated)
    return {"success": True, "job": updated}


@app.delete("/api/scheduler/jobs/{job_id}")
async def delete_scheduler_job_endpoint(job_id: str = Path(...), request: Request = None):
    """Delete a scheduler job and its run history."""
    user_id = _get_user_id(request) if request else ""
    job = _load_scheduler_job(job_id)
    if not job:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Job not found")
    owner = job.get("created_by", "")
    if owner and not _is_super_admin(user_id) and owner != user_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not authorised to delete this job")
    _delete_scheduler_job(job_id)
    return {"success": True, "deleted": job["name"]}


@app.post("/api/scheduler/jobs/{job_id}/run")
async def run_scheduler_job(job_id: str = Path(...)):
    """Manually run a scheduler job now — returns immediately, executes in background."""
    job = _load_scheduler_job(job_id)
    if not job:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Job not found")

    execution_mode = job.get("execution_mode", "in_app")

    if execution_mode == "cloud_job":
        try:
            cloud_config = job.get("cloud_job_config", {})
            _create_or_update_cloud_run_job(job_id, job["name"], cloud_config)
            run = _execute_cloud_run_job(job_id, cloud_config)
            job["last_run_at"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
            job["last_run_status"] = "running"
            _save_scheduler_job(job)
            return {"success": True, "run": run, "execution_mode": "cloud_job",
                    "message": "Job submitted to Cloud Run. Check history for results."}
        except Exception as e:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to launch Cloud Run Job: {str(e)}")

    import threading
    run_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:12]
    pending_run = {
        "id": run_id,
        "job_id": job_id,
        "status": "running",
        "started_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "steps_log": [],
    }
    _save_run_log(pending_run)
    t = threading.Thread(target=_execute_scheduler_job, args=(job_id, run_id), daemon=True)
    t.start()
    return {"success": True, "run": pending_run, "execution_mode": "in_app",
            "message": "Job started in background. Check history for results."}


@app.post("/api/scheduler/jobs/{job_id}/toggle")
async def toggle_scheduler_job(job_id: str = Path(...)):
    """Enable or disable a scheduler job."""
    job = _load_scheduler_job(job_id)
    if not job:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Job not found")
    job["enabled"] = not job.get("enabled", True)
    job["updated_at"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
    _save_scheduler_job(job)
    return {"success": True, "enabled": job["enabled"]}


@app.get("/api/scheduler/jobs/{job_id}/history")
async def get_scheduler_job_history(job_id: str = Path(...), limit: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100)):
    """Get run history for a scheduler job."""
    runs = _load_run_history(job_id, limit)
    return {"success": True, "runs": runs, "count": len(runs)}


@app.post("/api/scheduler/tick")
async def scheduler_tick():
    """Trigger a scheduler check. Call this from Cloud Scheduler every minute."""
    import asyncio
    jobs = _load_all_scheduler_jobs()
    now = datetime.now()
    triggered = []
    for job in jobs:
        if not job.get("enabled"):
            continue
        stype = job.get("schedule_type", "manual")
        if stype == "interval":
            scfg = job.get("schedule_config", {})
            interval_min = scfg.get("interval_minutes", 60)
            last_run = job.get("last_run_at")
            if last_run:
                try:
                    last_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(last_run)
                    if (now - last_dt).total_seconds() < interval_min * 60:
                        continue
                except Exception:
                    pass
            if job.get("execution_mode") == "cloud_job":
                try:
                    cloud_config = job.get("cloud_job_config", {})
                    await asyncio.to_thread(_create_or_update_cloud_run_job, job["id"], job["name"], cloud_config)
                    run = await asyncio.to_thread(_execute_cloud_run_job, job["id"], cloud_config)
                    triggered.append({"job_id": job["id"], "name": job["name"], "status": "submitted", "execution_mode": "cloud_job"})
                except Exception as e:
                    triggered.append({"job_id": job["id"], "name": job["name"], "status": "failed", "error": str(e)})
            else:
                run = await asyncio.to_thread(_execute_scheduler_job, job["id"])
                triggered.append({"job_id": job["id"], "name": job["name"], "status": run.get("status")})
    return {"success": True, "triggered": triggered, "checked": len(jobs)}


@app.get("/api/scheduler/servers")
async def get_scheduler_servers():
    """Get available servers/databases for the scheduler DB mapping step."""
    servers_list = []
    # Default servers
    servers_list.append({"id": "cloudsql", "name": "Default CloudSQL", "type": "cloudsql"})
    servers_list.append({"id": "bigquery", "name": "BigQuery", "type": "bigquery"})
    # Custom servers
    for srv in _server_store.values():
        if not srv.get("is_default"):
            servers_list.append({"id": f"server:{srv['id']}", "name": srv.get("name", srv["host"]), "type": "custom", "host": srv.get("host")})
    return {"success": True, "servers": servers_list}


@app.get("/api/scheduler/buckets")

    """List accessible GCS buckets."""
    if not gcs_client:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="GCS client not initialized")
    try:
        buckets = list(gcs_client.list_buckets(max_results=100))
        return {"success": True, "buckets": [{"name": b.name} for b in buckets]}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

def _save_user_history(user_id: str, query_text: str, database_name: str = "",
                       db_type: str = "cloudsql", row_count: int = 0, execution_time_ms: float = 0):
    """Persist a query to user history (fire-and-forget)."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {USER_HISTORY_TABLE}
                (user_id, query_text, database_name, db_type, row_count, execution_time_ms)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
        """, (user_id, query_text[:4000], database_name, db_type, row_count, execution_time_ms))
        # Keep only last 200 entries per user
        cursor.execute(f"""
            DELETE FROM {USER_HISTORY_TABLE}
            WHERE id NOT IN (
                SELECT id FROM {USER_HISTORY_TABLE}
                WHERE user_id = %s ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 200
            ) AND user_id = %s
        """, (user_id, user_id))
        connection.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Save user history failed: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.get("/api/user/history")
def get_user_history(request: Request, limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200)):
    """Get query history for the current user."""
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT id, query_text, database_name, db_type, row_count, execution_time_ms, created_at
            FROM {USER_HISTORY_TABLE}
            WHERE user_id = %s
            ORDER BY created_at DESC
            LIMIT %s
        """, (user_id, limit))
        rows = [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]
        # Serialize timestamps
        for r in rows:
            if hasattr(r.get("created_at"), "isoformat"):
                r["created_at"] = r["created_at"].isoformat()
        return {"success": True, "history": rows}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

@app.post("/api/user/favorites")
def add_user_favorite(request: Request, body: dict = Body(...)):
    """Add a query to favorites."""
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    query_text = body.get("query", "").strip()
    label = body.get("label", "").strip()
    if not query_text:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="query is required")
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        # Prevent duplicates
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT id FROM {USER_FAVORITES_TABLE}
            WHERE user_id = %s AND query_text = %s LIMIT 1
        """, (user_id, query_text))
        if cursor.fetchone():
            return {"success": True, "message": "Already in favorites"}
        cursor.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {USER_FAVORITES_TABLE} (user_id, query_text, label)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s) RETURNING id, created_at
        """, (user_id, query_text, label))
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True, "favorite": {"id": row[0], "query_text": query_text, "label": label,
                "created_at": row[1].isoformat() if hasattr(row[1], "isoformat") else str(row[1])}}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

async def remove_user_favorite(fav_id: int = Path(...), request: Request = None):

        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.get("/api/audit")
async def get_audit_log(
    request: Request,
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1, description="Page number (1-based)"),
    page_size: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=500, description="Rows per page"),
    user: str = Query(None, description="Filter by user ID"),
    search: str = Query(None, description="Search across query, table, database, action"),
    action: str = Query(None, description="Filter by action type"),
    database_name: str = Query(None, description="Filter by database name"),
    db_type: str = Query(None, description="Filter by db type"),
    date_from: str = Query(None, description="Start date (YYYY-MM-DD)"),
    date_to: str = Query(None, description="End date (YYYY-MM-DD)"),
    sort_by: str = Query("created_at", description="Sort column"),
    sort_order: str = Query("desc", pattern="^(asc|desc)$"),
):
    """Get audit log with server-side pagination, search, and filters.
    Super-admin can see all; others see only their own."""
    caller = _require_user(request)

    # Permission check
    if not _is_super_admin(caller):
        user = caller  # non-admins can only see their own

    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)

        # Build WHERE conditions dynamically
        conditions = []
        params = []

        if user:
            conditions.append("user_id = %s")
            params.append(user.strip().lower())

        if action:
            conditions.append("action = %s")
            params.append(action)

        if database_name:
            conditions.append("database_name = %s")
            params.append(database_name)

        if db_type:
            conditions.append("db_type = %s")
            params.append(db_type)

        if date_from:
            conditions.append("created_at >= %s::timestamp")
            params.append(date_from + " 00:00:00")

        if date_to:
            conditions.append("created_at <= %s::timestamp")
            params.append(date_to + " 23:59:59")

        if search:
            search_term = f"%{search.strip()}%"
            conditions.append(
                "(query_text ILIKE %s OR table_name ILIKE %s OR database_name ILIKE %s "
                "OR user_id ILIKE %s OR action ILIKE %s OR ip_address ILIKE %s)"
            )
            params.extend([search_term] * 6)

        where_clause = (" WHERE " + " AND ".join(conditions)) if conditions else ""

        # Validate sort column to prevent SQL injection
        allowed_sort = {"id", "user_id", "action", "database_name", "table_name",
                        "db_type", "ip_address", "created_at"}
        if sort_by not in allowed_sort:
            sort_by = "created_at"

        # Count total matching rows
        count_sql = f"SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM {AUDIT_TABLE}{where_clause}"
        cursor.execute(count_sql, params)
        total = cursor.fetchone()["total"]

        # Fetch paginated results
        offset = (page - 1) * page_size
        data_sql = f"""
            SELECT * FROM {AUDIT_TABLE}{where_clause}
            ORDER BY {sort_by} {sort_order}
            LIMIT %s OFFSET %s
        """
        cursor.execute(data_sql, params + [page_size, offset])
        rows = [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]
        for r in rows:
            if hasattr(r.get("created_at"), "isoformat"):
                r["created_at"] = r["created_at"].isoformat()

        # Fetch distinct filter values for dropdowns (lightweight)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT DISTINCT action FROM {AUDIT_TABLE} WHERE action != '' ORDER BY action")
        all_actions = [r["action"] for r in cursor.fetchall()]

        cursor.execute(f"SELECT DISTINCT database_name FROM {AUDIT_TABLE} WHERE database_name != '' ORDER BY database_name")
        all_databases = [r["database_name"] for r in cursor.fetchall()]

        cursor.execute(f"SELECT DISTINCT user_id FROM {AUDIT_TABLE} ORDER BY user_id")
        all_users = [r["user_id"] for r in cursor.fetchall()]

        total_pages = max(1, -(-total // page_size))  # ceil division

        return {
            "success": True,
            "logs": rows,
            "count": len(rows),
            "total": total,
            "page": page,
            "page_size": page_size,
            "total_pages": total_pages,
            "filters": {
                "actions": all_actions,
                "databases": all_databases,
                "users": all_users,
            },
        }
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Audit log query failed: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

async def get_current_user(request: Request):
    """Return user info — VZID, is_super_admin flag."""

        }
    }


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  SCHEMA EXPLORER / ERD
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

@app.get("/api/schema/erd")
async def get_erd_data(
    database: str = Query(..., description="Database name"),
    type: str = Query("cloudsql", pattern="^(cloudsql|bigquery|mssql)$"),
    request: Request = None,
):
    """Return tables, columns, and foreign-key relationships for ERD."""
    _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        if type == "bigquery":
            # BigQuery: list all tables + columns in the dataset
            dataset_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{database}"
            tables_iter = bq_client.list_tables(dataset_ref)
            tables = []
            for tbl in tables_iter:
                full_ref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{database}.{tbl.table_id}"
                bq_table = bq_client.get_table(full_ref)
                cols = [
                    {
                        "column_name": f.name,
                        "data_type": f.field_type,
                        "is_nullable": "YES" if f.mode != "REQUIRED" else "NO",
                        "is_primary": False,
                    }
                    for f in bq_table.schema
                ]
                tables.append({
                    "table_schema": database,
                    "table_name": tbl.table_id,
                    "columns": cols,
                    "row_count": bq_table.num_rows,
                })
            return {"success": True, "database": database, "tables": tables, "relationships": []}

        # CloudSQL / PostgreSQL
        connection = get_db_connection(database)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)

        # All columns with PK flag
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT
                c.table_schema,
                c.table_name,
                c.column_name,
                c.data_type,
                c.is_nullable,
                CASE WHEN kcu.column_name IS NOT NULL THEN true ELSE false END AS is_primary
            FROM information_schema.columns c
            LEFT JOIN information_schema.table_constraints tc
                ON tc.table_schema = c.table_schema
                AND tc.table_name = c.table_name
                AND tc.constraint_type = 'PRIMARY KEY'
            LEFT JOIN information_schema.key_column_usage kcu
                ON kcu.constraint_name = tc.constraint_name
                AND kcu.table_schema = tc.table_schema
                AND kcu.column_name = c.column_name
            WHERE c.table_schema NOT IN ('pg_catalog', 'information_schema')
            ORDER BY c.table_schema, c.table_name, c.ordinal_position
        """)
        rows = cursor.fetchall()

        # Group into tables
        table_map = {}
        for r in rows:
            key = f"{r['table_schema']}.{r['table_name']}"
            if key not in table_map:
                table_map[key] = {
                    "table_schema": r["table_schema"],
                    "table_name": r["table_name"],
                    "columns": [],
                }
            table_map[key]["columns"].append({
                "column_name": r["column_name"],
                "data_type": r["data_type"],
                "is_nullable": r["is_nullable"],
                "is_primary": r["is_primary"],
            })
        tables = list(table_map.values())

        # Foreign keys
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT
                kcu.table_schema  AS source_schema,
                kcu.table_name    AS source_table,
                kcu.column_name   AS source_column,
                ccu.table_schema  AS target_schema,
                ccu.table_name    AS target_table,
                ccu.column_name   AS target_column,
                tc.constraint_name
            FROM information_schema.table_constraints tc
            JOIN information_schema.key_column_usage kcu
                ON tc.constraint_name = kcu.constraint_name
                AND tc.table_schema = kcu.table_schema
            JOIN information_schema.constraint_column_usage ccu
                ON ccu.constraint_name = tc.constraint_name
                AND ccu.table_schema = tc.table_schema
            WHERE tc.constraint_type = 'FOREIGN KEY'
              AND tc.table_schema NOT IN ('pg_catalog', 'information_schema')
        """)
        fk_rows = cursor.fetchall()
        relationships = [dict(r) for r in fk_rows]

        return {
            "success": True,
            "database": database,
            "tables": tables,
            "relationships": relationships,
        }
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"ERD data error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  QUERY LIBRARY  — community saved queries
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE = "sql_editor_query_library"
QUERY_LIKES_TABLE = "sql_editor_query_likes"
QUERY_FAVORITES_TABLE = "sql_editor_query_library_favorites"
QUERY_COMMENTS_TABLE = "sql_editor_query_comments"


def _ensure_query_library_tables():
    """Create query-library tables."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} (
                id            SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                title         VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
                description   TEXT DEFAULT '',
                query_text    TEXT NOT NULL,
                database_name VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                db_type       VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT 'cloudsql',
                tags          TEXT DEFAULT '',
                user_id       VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                is_public     BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
                like_count    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                fav_count     INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                use_count     INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                created_at    TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
                updated_at    TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ql_user ON {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} (user_id)
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ql_search ON {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE}
                USING gin(to_tsvector('english', title || ' ' || description || ' ' || tags))
        """)

        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {QUERY_LIKES_TABLE} (
                id         SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                query_id   INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE}(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                user_id    VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW(),
                UNIQUE(query_id, user_id)
            )
        """)

        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {QUERY_FAVORITES_TABLE} (
                id         SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                query_id   INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE}(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                user_id    VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW(),
                UNIQUE(query_id, user_id)
            )
        """)

        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {QUERY_COMMENTS_TABLE} (
                id         SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                query_id   INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE}(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                user_id    VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                comment    TEXT NOT NULL,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_qc_query ON {QUERY_COMMENTS_TABLE} (query_id, created_at)
        """)

        connection.commit()
        logger.info("Query-library tables ensured")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to create query-library tables: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


_ensure_query_library_tables()


# ── Save a query to the library ──────────────────────────────────────
@app.post("/api/library/queries")
async def save_library_query(request: Request, body: dict = Body(...)):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    title = (body.get("title") or "").strip()
    description = (body.get("description") or "").strip()
    query_text = (body.get("query_text") or "").strip()
    database_name = body.get("database_name", "")
    db_type = body.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    tags = body.get("tags", "")
    if not title or not query_text:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="title and query_text are required")
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE}
                (title, description, query_text, database_name, db_type, tags, user_id)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s) RETURNING id
        """, (title, description, query_text, database_name, db_type, tags, user_id))
        new_id = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        connection.commit()
        await _log_audit_async(user_id, "library_save", database_name=database_name, query_text=query_text[:500])
        return {"success": True, "id": new_id}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── List / search queries ────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.get("/api/library/queries")
async def list_library_queries(
    request: Request,
    search: str = Query(""),
    tag: str = Query(""),
    sort: str = Query("recent"),
    mine: bool = Query(False),
    favorites_only: bool = Query(False),
    limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=500),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)

        where_parts = ["q.is_public = true OR q.user_id = %s"]
        params = [user_id]

        if mine:
            where_parts = ["q.user_id = %s"]
            params = [user_id]

        if favorites_only:
            where_parts.append(f"EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM {QUERY_FAVORITES_TABLE} f WHERE f.query_id = q.id AND f.user_id = %s)")
            params.append(user_id)

        if search:
            where_parts.append(
                "(q.title ILIKE %s OR q.description ILIKE %s OR q.tags ILIKE %s OR q.query_text ILIKE %s)"
            )
            s = f"%{search}%"
            params.extend([s, s, s, s])

        if tag:
            where_parts.append("q.tags ILIKE %s")
            params.append(f"%{tag}%")

        order = {
            "recent": "q.created_at DESC",
            "popular": "q.like_count DESC, q.created_at DESC",
            "most_used": "q.use_count DESC, q.created_at DESC",
        }.get(sort, "q.created_at DESC")

        where_sql = " AND ".join(f"({w})" for w in where_parts)

        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT q.*,
                   EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {QUERY_LIKES_TABLE} l WHERE l.query_id = q.id AND l.user_id = %s) AS liked_by_me,
                   EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {QUERY_FAVORITES_TABLE} f WHERE f.query_id = q.id AND f.user_id = %s) AS faved_by_me,
                   (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {QUERY_COMMENTS_TABLE} c WHERE c.query_id = q.id) AS comment_count
            FROM {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} q
            WHERE {where_sql}
            ORDER BY {order}
            LIMIT %s OFFSET %s
        """, [user_id, user_id] + params + [limit, offset])
        rows = [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]
        for r in rows:
            for k in ("created_at", "updated_at"):
                if hasattr(r.get(k), "isoformat"):
                    r[k] = r[k].isoformat()

        # total count
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} q WHERE {where_sql}", params)
        total = cursor.fetchone()["count"]

        return {"success": True, "queries": rows, "total": total}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Library list error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── Get single query detail ──────────────────────────────────────────
@app.get("/api/library/queries/{query_id}")
async def get_library_query(query_id: int = Path(...), request: Request = None):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT q.*,
                   EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {QUERY_LIKES_TABLE} l WHERE l.query_id = q.id AND l.user_id = %s) AS liked_by_me,
                   EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {QUERY_FAVORITES_TABLE} f WHERE f.query_id = q.id AND f.user_id = %s) AS faved_by_me,
                   (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {QUERY_COMMENTS_TABLE} c WHERE c.query_id = q.id) AS comment_count
            FROM {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} q WHERE q.id = %s
        """, (user_id, user_id, query_id))
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if not row:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Query not found")
        r = dict(row)
        for k in ("created_at", "updated_at"):
            if hasattr(r.get(k), "isoformat"):
                r[k] = r[k].isoformat()
        # Increment use_count
        cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} SET use_count = use_count + 1 WHERE id = %s", (query_id,))
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True, "query": r}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── Update query ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.put("/api/library/queries/{query_id}")
async def update_library_query(query_id: int = Path(...), request: Request = None, body: dict = Body(...)):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT user_id FROM {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (query_id,))
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if not row:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Query not found")
        if row[0] != user_id and not _is_super_admin(user_id):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not authorized")
        sets, vals = [], []
        for col in ("title", "description", "query_text", "database_name", "db_type", "tags", "is_public"):
            if col in body:
                sets.append(f"{col} = %s")
                vals.append(body[col])
        if sets:
            sets.append("updated_at = NOW()")
            vals.append(query_id)
            cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} SET {', '.join(sets)} WHERE id = %s", vals)
            connection.commit()
        return {"success": True}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

# ── Like / Unlike toggle ─────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.post("/api/library/queries/{query_id}/like")
async def toggle_like_query(query_id: int = Path(...), request: Request = None):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT id FROM {QUERY_LIKES_TABLE} WHERE query_id = %s AND user_id = %s", (query_id, user_id))
        existing = cursor.fetchone()
        if existing:
            cursor.execute(f"DELETE FROM {QUERY_LIKES_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (existing[0],))
            cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} SET like_count = GREATEST(like_count - 1, 0) WHERE id = %s", (query_id,))
            liked = False
        else:
            cursor.execute(f"INSERT INTO {QUERY_LIKES_TABLE} (query_id, user_id) VALUES (%s, %s)", (query_id, user_id))
            cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} SET like_count = like_count + 1 WHERE id = %s", (query_id,))
            liked = True
        connection.commit()
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT like_count FROM {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (query_id,))
        count = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        return {"success": True, "liked": liked, "like_count": count}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── Favorite / Unfavorite toggle ──────────────────────────────────────
@app.post("/api/library/queries/{query_id}/favorite")
async def toggle_favorite_query(query_id: int = Path(...), request: Request = None):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT id FROM {QUERY_FAVORITES_TABLE} WHERE query_id = %s AND user_id = %s", (query_id, user_id))
        existing = cursor.fetchone()
        if existing:
            cursor.execute(f"DELETE FROM {QUERY_FAVORITES_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (existing[0],))
            cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} SET fav_count = GREATEST(fav_count - 1, 0) WHERE id = %s", (query_id,))
            faved = False
        else:
            cursor.execute(f"INSERT INTO {QUERY_FAVORITES_TABLE} (query_id, user_id) VALUES (%s, %s)", (query_id, user_id))
            cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} SET fav_count = fav_count + 1 WHERE id = %s", (query_id,))
            faved = True
        connection.commit()
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT fav_count FROM {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (query_id,))
        count = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        return {"success": True, "favorited": faved, "fav_count": count}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()
