# ── Get all unique tags ──────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.get("/api/library/tags")
async def get_library_tags(request: Request):
    _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT DISTINCT unnest(string_to_array(tags, ',')) AS tag FROM {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE} WHERE tags != '' ORDER BY tag")
        tags = [r[0].strip() for r in cursor.fetchall() if r[0].strip()]
        return {"success": True, "tags": tags}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
# ║               DATA PIPELINE BUILDER + DASHBOARD BUILDER            ║
# ╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

PIPELINE_TABLE = "sql_editor_pipelines"
PIPELINE_RUNS_TABLE = "sql_editor_pipeline_runs"
DASHBOARD_TABLE = "sql_editor_dashboards"
DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE = "sql_editor_dashboard_widgets"


def _ensure_pipeline_tables():
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {PIPELINE_TABLE} (
                id            VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
                name          VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
                description   TEXT DEFAULT '',
                nodes         TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
                edges         TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
                schedule_type VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'manual',
                schedule_config TEXT DEFAULT '{{}}',
                enabled       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
                user_id       VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                status        VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft',
                last_run_at   VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT '',
                last_run_status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT '',
                created_at    TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
                updated_at    TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {PIPELINE_RUNS_TABLE} (
                id            SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                pipeline_id   VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL REFERENCES {PIPELINE_TABLE}(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                status        VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'running',
                started_at    TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW(),
                finished_at   TIMESTAMP,
                duration_ms   INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                node_results  TEXT DEFAULT '{{}}',
                error_message TEXT DEFAULT '',
                rows_processed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                user_id       VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT ''
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pipeline_user ON {PIPELINE_TABLE} (user_id)")
        cursor.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pipeline_runs_pid ON {PIPELINE_RUNS_TABLE} (pipeline_id)")
        connection.commit()
        logger.info("Pipeline tables ensured")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error creating pipeline tables: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def _ensure_dashboard_tables():
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {DASHBOARD_TABLE} (
                id            VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
                name          VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
                description   TEXT DEFAULT '',
                layout        TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
                is_public     BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
                auto_refresh  INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                theme         VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'light',
                user_id       VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                created_at    TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
                updated_at    TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE} (
                id            VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
                dashboard_id  VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL REFERENCES {DASHBOARD_TABLE}(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                widget_type   VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'chart',
                title         VARCHAR(500) DEFAULT '',
                query_text    TEXT DEFAULT '',
                database_name VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                db_type       VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'cloudsql',
                chart_type    VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'bar',
                chart_config  TEXT DEFAULT '{{}}',
                position_x    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                position_y    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                width         INTEGER DEFAULT 6,
                height        INTEGER DEFAULT 4,
                refresh_interval INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                last_data     TEXT DEFAULT '',
                last_refreshed TIMESTAMP,
                created_at    TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_dashboard_user ON {DASHBOARD_TABLE} (user_id)")
        cursor.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_dwidget_dash ON {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE} (dashboard_id)")
        # Add share_token column if not exists
        cursor.execute(f"""
            DO $$ BEGIN
                ALTER TABLE {DASHBOARD_TABLE} ADD COLUMN share_token VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT '';
            EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_column THEN NULL;
            END $$;
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_dashboard_share_token ON {DASHBOARD_TABLE} (share_token)")
        connection.commit()
        logger.info("Dashboard tables ensured")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error creating dashboard tables: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


_ensure_pipeline_tables()
_ensure_dashboard_tables()


# ── Pipeline CRUD ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@app.get("/api/pipelines")
async def list_pipelines(request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {PIPELINE_TABLE} WHERE user_id = %s ORDER BY updated_at DESC", (user_id,))
        result = []
        for p in cursor.fetchall():
            result.append({
                "id": p["id"], "name": p["name"], "description": p["description"],
                "nodes": json.loads(p["nodes"] or "[]"), "edges": json.loads(p["edges"] or "[]"),
                "schedule_type": p["schedule_type"], "schedule_config": json.loads(p["schedule_config"] or "{}"),
                "enabled": p["enabled"], "status": p["status"],
                "last_run_at": p["last_run_at"] or "", "last_run_status": p["last_run_status"] or "",
                "created_at": str(p["created_at"]), "updated_at": str(p["updated_at"]),
            })
        return {"success": True, "pipelines": result}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.post("/api/pipelines")
async def create_pipeline(request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    body = await request.json()
    pipeline_id = f"pipe-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {PIPELINE_TABLE} (id, name, description, nodes, edges, schedule_type, schedule_config, enabled, user_id, status)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, 'draft')
        """, (
            pipeline_id, body.get("name", "Untitled Pipeline"), body.get("description", ""),
            json.dumps(body.get("nodes", [])), json.dumps(body.get("edges", [])),
            body.get("schedule_type", "manual"), json.dumps(body.get("schedule_config", {})),
            body.get("enabled", True), user_id,
        ))
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True, "id": pipeline_id}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

async def get_pipeline(pipeline_id: str, request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)

                "schedule_type": p["schedule_type"], "schedule_config": json.loads(p["schedule_config"] or "{}"),
                "enabled": p["enabled"], "status": p["status"],
                "last_run_at": p["last_run_at"] or "", "last_run_status": p["last_run_status"] or "",
                "created_at": str(p["created_at"]), "updated_at": str(p["updated_at"]),
            }
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.put("/api/pipelines/{pipeline_id}")
async def update_pipeline(pipeline_id: str, request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    body = await request.json()
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        fields, vals = [], []
        for key in ["name", "description", "schedule_type", "enabled", "status"]:
            if key in body:
                fields.append(f"{key} = %s"); vals.append(body[key])
        if "nodes" in body:
            fields.append("nodes = %s"); vals.append(json.dumps(body["nodes"]))
        if "edges" in body:
            fields.append("edges = %s"); vals.append(json.dumps(body["edges"]))
        if "schedule_config" in body:
            fields.append("schedule_config = %s"); vals.append(json.dumps(body["schedule_config"]))
        fields.append("updated_at = NOW()")
        vals.extend([pipeline_id, user_id])
        cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {PIPELINE_TABLE} SET {', '.join(fields)} WHERE id = %s AND user_id = %s", vals)
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

@app.post("/api/pipelines/{pipeline_id}/run")
async def run_pipeline(pipeline_id: str, request: Request):
    """Execute a pipeline by running each node in topological order."""
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {PIPELINE_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND user_id = %s", (pipeline_id, user_id))
        p = cursor.fetchone()
        if not p:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Pipeline not found")

        nodes = json.loads(p["nodes"] or "[]")
        edges = json.loads(p["edges"] or "[]")
        start_time = time.time()
        node_map = {n["id"]: n for n in nodes}
        node_results = {}
        total_rows = 0
        error_msg = ""
        run_status = "success"

        # Topological sort
        in_degree = {n["id"]: 0 for n in nodes}
        adj = {n["id"]: [] for n in nodes}
        for edge in edges:
            src, tgt = edge.get("source", ""), edge.get("target", "")
            if src in adj and tgt in in_degree:
                adj[src].append(tgt)
                in_degree[tgt] += 1
        queue = [nid for nid, deg in in_degree.items() if deg == 0]
        exec_order = []
        while queue:
            nid = queue.pop(0)
            exec_order.append(nid)
            for child in adj.get(nid, []):
                in_degree[child] -= 1
                if in_degree[child] == 0:
                    queue.append(child)

        for nid in exec_order:
            node = node_map.get(nid)
            if not node:
                continue
            ndata = node.get("data", {})
            node_type = ndata.get("nodeType", "")
            cfg = ndata.get("config", {})
            try:
                if node_type == "source":
                    db_name = cfg.get("database", "")
                    db_tp = cfg.get("dbType", "cloudsql")
                    query = cfg.get("query", "")
                    if db_name and query:
                        if db_tp == "bigquery":
                            job = bq_client.query(_normalize_bq_query(query))
                            rows = [dict(r) for r in job.result()]
                        else:
                            c2 = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database=db_name, user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
                            cu2 = c2.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
                            cu2.execute(query)
                            rows = cu2.fetchall()
                            c2.close()
                        node_results[nid] = {"rows": rows, "count": len(rows), "status": "success"}
                        total_rows += len(rows)
                    else:
                        node_results[nid] = {"rows": [], "count": 0, "status": "skipped", "message": "Missing db/query"}

                elif node_type == "transform":
                    parent_data = []
                    for pe in [e for e in edges if e.get("target") == nid]:
                        parent_data.extend(node_results.get(pe.get("source"), {}).get("rows", []))
                    tt = cfg.get("transformType", "filter")

                    if tt == "filter":
                        col, op, val = cfg.get("column", ""), cfg.get("operator", "="), cfg.get("value", "")
                        filtered = []
                        for row in parent_data:
                            rv = str(row.get(col, ""))
                            if (op == "=" and rv == val) or (op == "!=" and rv != val) or \
                               (op == ">" and rv > val) or (op == "<" and rv < val) or \
                               (op == "contains" and val.lower() in rv.lower()) or \
                               (op == "not_null" and rv and rv != "None"):
                                filtered.append(row)
                        node_results[nid] = {"rows": filtered, "count": len(filtered), "status": "success"}

                    elif tt == "aggregate":
                        gcol, acol, afn = cfg.get("groupBy", ""), cfg.get("aggColumn", ""), cfg.get("aggFunction", "COUNT")
                        groups = {}
                        for row in parent_data:
                            gk = str(row.get(gcol, ""))
                            groups.setdefault(gk, []).append(row)
                        agg_rows = []
                        for gk, rg in groups.items():
                            vals = [float(r.get(acol, 0) or 0) for r in rg]
                            av = len(rg) if afn == "COUNT" else sum(vals) if afn == "SUM" else \
                                 (sum(vals)/len(vals) if vals else 0) if afn == "AVG" else \
                                 (min(vals) if vals else 0) if afn == "MIN" else (max(vals) if vals else 0)
                            agg_rows.append({gcol: gk, f"{afn}_{acol}": av})
                        node_results[nid] = {"rows": agg_rows, "count": len(agg_rows), "status": "success"}

                    elif tt == "sort":
                        sc, sd = cfg.get("sortColumn", ""), cfg.get("sortDirection", "ASC")
                        node_results[nid] = {"rows": sorted(parent_data, key=lambda r: str(r.get(sc, "")), reverse=(sd == "DESC")), "count": len(parent_data), "status": "success"}

                    elif tt == "limit":
                        lim = int(cfg.get("limit", 100))
                        node_results[nid] = {"rows": parent_data[:lim], "count": min(lim, len(parent_data)), "status": "success"}

                    elif tt == "select_columns":
                        cols = [c.strip() for c in cfg.get("columns", "").split(",") if c.strip()]
                        projected = [{c: r.get(c) for c in cols if c in r} for r in parent_data] if cols else parent_data
                        node_results[nid] = {"rows": projected, "count": len(projected), "status": "success"}

                    elif tt == "rename":
                        rm = cfg.get("renameMap", {})
                        node_results[nid] = {"rows": [{rm.get(k, k): v for k, v in r.items()} for r in parent_data], "count": len(parent_data), "status": "success"}

                    elif tt == "deduplicate":
                        seen, deduped = set(), []
                        dedup_col = cfg.get("column", "")
                        for row in parent_data:
                            key = str(row.get(dedup_col, "")) if dedup_col else json.dumps(row, default=str, sort_keys=True)
                            if key not in seen:
                                seen.add(key); deduped.append(row)
                        node_results[nid] = {"rows": deduped, "count": len(deduped), "status": "success"}

                    elif tt == "add_column":
                        new_col = cfg.get("columnName", "new_col")
                        expr = cfg.get("expression", "")
                        for row in parent_data:
                            row[new_col] = expr
                        node_results[nid] = {"rows": parent_data, "count": len(parent_data), "status": "success"}

                    else:
                        node_results[nid] = {"rows": parent_data, "count": len(parent_data), "status": "passthrough"}

                elif node_type == "destination":
                    parent_data_d = []
                    for pe in [e for e in edges if e.get("target") == nid]:
                        parent_data_d.extend(node_results.get(pe.get("source"), {}).get("rows", []))
                    dest_type = cfg.get("destType", "table")
                    dest_db = cfg.get("database", "")
                    dest_table = cfg.get("tableName", "")
                    dest_db_type = cfg.get("dbType", "cloudsql")
                    wm = cfg.get("writeMode", "append")

                    if dest_type == "table" and dest_db and dest_table and parent_data_d:
                        if dest_db_type == "bigquery":
                            from google.cloud.bigquery import LoadJobConfig, WriteDisposition
                            tref = f"{GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT}.{dest_db}.{dest_table}"
                            jc = LoadJobConfig()
                            jc.write_disposition = WriteDisposition.WRITE_TRUNCATE if wm == "replace" else WriteDisposition.WRITE_APPEND
                            jc.autodetect = True
                            bq_client.load_table_from_json(parent_data_d, tref, job_config=jc).result()
                            node_results[nid] = {"rows": [], "count": len(parent_data_d), "status": "success", "message": f"Loaded {len(parent_data_d)} rows to {tref}"}
                        else:
                            c3 = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database=dest_db, user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
                            cu3 = c3.cursor()
                            if wm == "replace":
                                cu3.execute(f"DELETE FROM {dest_table}")
                            if parent_data_d:
                                cols = list(parent_data_d[0].keys())
                                for row in parent_data_d:
                                    cu3.execute(f"INSERT INTO {dest_table} ({', '.join(cols)}) VALUES ({', '.join(['%s']*len(cols))})", [row.get(c) for c in cols])
                            c3.commit(); c3.close()
                            node_results[nid] = {"rows": [], "count": len(parent_data_d), "status": "success", "message": f"Loaded {len(parent_data_d)} rows"}
                    elif dest_type == "gcs":
                        bucket = cfg.get("bucket", "")
                        fp = cfg.get("filePath", f"pipeline_output_{pipeline_id}.csv")
                        if bucket and parent_data_d:
                            sc = gcs_storage.Client(project=GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT)
                            bo = sc.bucket(bucket)
                            blob = bo.blob(fp)
                            output = io.StringIO()
                            writer = csv.DictWriter(output, fieldnames=parent_data_d[0].keys())
                            writer.writeheader(); writer.writerows(parent_data_d)
                            blob.upload_from_string(output.getvalue(), content_type="text/csv")
                            node_results[nid] = {"rows": [], "count": len(parent_data_d), "status": "success", "message": f"Exported to gs://{bucket}/{fp}"}
                        else:
                            node_results[nid] = {"rows": [], "count": 0, "status": "skipped"}
                    else:
                        node_results[nid] = {"rows": [], "count": len(parent_data_d), "status": "success", "message": "Preview only"}
                else:
                    node_results[nid] = {"rows": [], "count": 0, "status": "skipped"}

            except Exception as ne:
                node_results[nid] = {"rows": [], "count": 0, "status": "error", "message": str(ne)}
                error_msg = f"Node {nid}: {str(ne)}"; run_status = "failed"; break

        duration_ms = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
        results_summary = {nid: {"count": r.get("count", 0), "status": r.get("status", ""), "message": r.get("message", "")} for nid, r in node_results.items()}

        cursor2 = connection.cursor()
        cursor2.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {PIPELINE_RUNS_TABLE} (pipeline_id, status, duration_ms, node_results, error_message, rows_processed, user_id)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
        """, (pipeline_id, run_status, duration_ms, json.dumps(results_summary), error_msg, total_rows, user_id))
        cursor2.execute(f"UPDATE {PIPELINE_TABLE} SET last_run_at = NOW(), last_run_status = %s, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = %s", (run_status, pipeline_id))
        connection.commit()

        return {"success": True, "status": run_status, "duration_ms": duration_ms, "rows_processed": total_rows, "error": error_msg, "node_results": results_summary}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.get("/api/pipelines/{pipeline_id}/runs")
async def get_pipeline_runs(pipeline_id: str, request: Request, limit: int = 20):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {PIPELINE_RUNS_TABLE} WHERE pipeline_id = %s ORDER BY started_at DESC LIMIT %s", (pipeline_id, limit))
        runs = []
        for r in cursor.fetchall():
            runs.append({
                "id": r["id"], "pipeline_id": r["pipeline_id"], "status": r["status"],
                "started_at": str(r["started_at"]) if r["started_at"] else "",
                "finished_at": str(r["finished_at"]) if r["finished_at"] else "",
                "duration_ms": r["duration_ms"], "rows_processed": r["rows_processed"],
                "node_results": json.loads(r["node_results"] or "{}"),
                "error_message": r["error_message"] or "",
            })
        return {"success": True, "runs": runs}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── Dashboard CRUD ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

@app.get("/api/dashboards")
async def list_dashboards(request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT d.*, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE} WHERE dashboard_id = d.id) AS widget_count
            FROM {DASHBOARD_TABLE} d WHERE d.user_id = %s OR d.is_public = TRUE ORDER BY d.updated_at DESC
        """, (user_id,))
        dashboards = []
        for d in cursor.fetchall():
            dashboards.append({
                "id": d["id"], "name": d["name"], "description": d["description"],
                "is_public": d["is_public"], "auto_refresh": d["auto_refresh"],
                "theme": d["theme"], "user_id": d["user_id"], "widget_count": d["widget_count"],
                "created_at": str(d["created_at"]), "updated_at": str(d["updated_at"]),
            })
        return {"success": True, "dashboards": dashboards}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

@app.get("/api/dashboards/public/{token}")
async def get_public_dashboard(token: str):
    """Access a shared dashboard by its share token — no auth required."""
    if not token or len(token) < 5:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid share token")
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {DASHBOARD_TABLE} WHERE share_token = %s", (token,))
        d = cursor.fetchone()
        if not d:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Dashboard not found or link expired")
        # Load widgets with their last cached data
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE} WHERE dashboard_id = %s ORDER BY position_y, position_x", (d["id"],))
        widgets = []
        for w in cursor.fetchall():
            last_data = w.get("last_data") or "[]"
            try:
                parsed_data = json.loads(last_data) if isinstance(last_data, str) else last_data
            except:
                parsed_data = []
            widgets.append({
                "id": w["id"], "dashboard_id": w["dashboard_id"], "widget_type": w["widget_type"],
                "title": w["title"], "database_name": w["database_name"], "db_type": w["db_type"],
                "chart_type": w["chart_type"], "chart_config": json.loads(w["chart_config"] or "{}"),
                "position_x": w["position_x"], "position_y": w["position_y"],
                "width": w["width"], "height": w["height"],
                "last_data": parsed_data,
                "last_refreshed": str(w["last_refreshed"]) if w["last_refreshed"] else "",
            })
        return {
            "success": True,
            "dashboard": {
                "id": d["id"], "name": d["name"], "description": d["description"],
                "is_public": d["is_public"], "auto_refresh": d["auto_refresh"],
                "theme": d["theme"],
            },
            "widgets": widgets,
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.get("/api/dashboards/{dash_id}")
async def get_dashboard(dash_id: str, request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {DASHBOARD_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND (user_id = %s OR is_public = TRUE)", (dash_id, user_id))
        d = cursor.fetchone()
        if not d:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Dashboard not found")
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE} WHERE dashboard_id = %s ORDER BY position_y, position_x", (dash_id,))
        widgets = []
        for w in cursor.fetchall():
            widgets.append({
                "id": w["id"], "dashboard_id": w["dashboard_id"], "widget_type": w["widget_type"],
                "title": w["title"], "query_text": w["query_text"],
                "database_name": w["database_name"], "db_type": w["db_type"],
                "chart_type": w["chart_type"], "chart_config": json.loads(w["chart_config"] or "{}"),
                "position_x": w["position_x"], "position_y": w["position_y"],
                "width": w["width"], "height": w["height"], "refresh_interval": w["refresh_interval"],
                "last_refreshed": str(w["last_refreshed"]) if w["last_refreshed"] else "",
            })
        return {
            "success": True,
            "dashboard": {
                "id": d["id"], "name": d["name"], "description": d["description"],
                "is_public": d["is_public"], "auto_refresh": d["auto_refresh"],
                "theme": d["theme"], "user_id": d["user_id"],
                "layout": json.loads(d["layout"] or "[]"),
                "created_at": str(d["created_at"]), "updated_at": str(d["updated_at"]),
            },
            "widgets": widgets,
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.put("/api/dashboards/{dash_id}")
async def update_dashboard(dash_id: str, request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    body = await request.json()
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        fields, vals = [], []
        for key in ["name", "description", "is_public", "auto_refresh", "theme"]:
            if key in body:
                fields.append(f"{key} = %s"); vals.append(body[key])
        if "layout" in body:
            fields.append("layout = %s"); vals.append(json.dumps(body["layout"]))
        fields.append("updated_at = NOW()")
        vals.extend([dash_id, user_id])
        cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {DASHBOARD_TABLE} SET {', '.join(fields)} WHERE id = %s AND user_id = %s", vals)
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

            INSERT INTO {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE}
            (id, dashboard_id, widget_type, title, query_text, database_name, db_type, chart_type, chart_config, position_x, position_y, width, height, refresh_interval)
            VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s)
        """, (widget_id, dash_id, body.get("widget_type","chart"), body.get("title",""),
              body.get("query_text",""), body.get("database_name",""), body.get("db_type","cloudsql"),
              body.get("chart_type","bar"), json.dumps(body.get("chart_config",{})),
