@app.put("/api/dashboards/{dash_id}/widgets/{widget_id}")
async def update_widget(dash_id: str, widget_id: str, request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    body = await request.json()
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        fields, vals = [], []
        for key in ["widget_type","title","query_text","database_name","db_type","chart_type","position_x","position_y","width","height","refresh_interval"]:
            if key in body:
                fields.append(f"{key} = %s"); vals.append(body[key])
        if "chart_config" in body:
            fields.append("chart_config = %s"); vals.append(json.dumps(body["chart_config"]))
        if "last_data" in body:
            fields.append("last_data = %s"); vals.append(json.dumps(body["last_data"]) if isinstance(body["last_data"], (dict,list)) else body["last_data"])
            fields.append("last_refreshed = NOW()")
        if not fields:
            return {"success": True}
        vals.extend([widget_id, dash_id])
        cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE} SET {', '.join(fields)} WHERE id = %s AND dashboard_id = %s", vals)
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.delete("/api/dashboards/{dash_id}/widgets/{widget_id}")
async def delete_widget(dash_id: str, widget_id: str, request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"DELETE FROM {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND dashboard_id = %s", (widget_id, dash_id))
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.post("/api/dashboards/{dash_id}/widgets/{widget_id}/refresh")
async def refresh_widget(dash_id: str, widget_id: str, request: Request):
    """Re-execute the widget's query and return fresh data."""
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND dashboard_id = %s", (widget_id, dash_id))
        w = cursor.fetchone()
        if not w:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Widget not found")
        query = w["query_text"]
        db_name = w["database_name"]
        db_tp = w["db_type"] or "cloudsql"
        if not query or not db_name:
            return {"success": True, "data": [], "columns": []}
        if db_tp == "bigquery":
            job = bq_client.query(_normalize_bq_query(query))
            rows = [dict(r) for r in job.result()]
            columns = list(rows[0].keys()) if rows else []
        else:
            c2 = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database=db_name, user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
            cu2 = c2.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
            cu2.execute(query)
            rows = cu2.fetchall()
            columns = [desc[0] for desc in cu2.description] if cu2.description else []
            c2.close()
        serialized = []
        for row in rows:
            r = {}
            for k, v in (row if isinstance(row, dict) else {}).items():
                r[k] = v.isoformat() if isinstance(v, datetime) else v
            serialized.append(r)
        cursor2 = connection.cursor()
        cursor2.execute(f"UPDATE {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE} SET last_data = %s, last_refreshed = NOW() WHERE id = %s",
                        (json.dumps(serialized[:500], default=str), widget_id))
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True, "data": serialized, "columns": columns, "row_count": len(serialized)}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── Dashboard Sharing ────────────────────────────────────────────────

@app.post("/api/dashboards/{dash_id}/share")
async def share_dashboard(dash_id: str, request: Request):
    """Generate or return a share token for view-only access to a dashboard."""
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT id, share_token FROM {DASHBOARD_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND user_id = %s", (dash_id, user_id))
        d = cursor.fetchone()
        if not d:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Dashboard not found")
        token = d.get("share_token") or ""
        if not token:
            token = f"shr-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]}"
            cursor2 = connection.cursor()
            cursor2.execute(f"UPDATE {DASHBOARD_TABLE} SET share_token = %s WHERE id = %s", (token, dash_id))
            connection.commit()
        return {"success": True, "share_token": token}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
# ║                DATA LINEAGE & IMPACT ANALYSIS                      ║
# ╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

@app.get("/api/lineage/scan")
async def scan_lineage(request: Request, database: str = Query(""), table: str = Query(""), column: str = Query("")):
    """
    Scan across all system tables to build a full lineage graph:
      Column → Table → Query (library) → API → Scheduler Job → Pipeline → Dashboard Widget
    If a specific table.column is provided, returns only the subgraph impacted.
    """
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)

        nodes = []
        edges = []
        node_ids = set()

        def add_node(nid, ntype, label, meta=None):
            if nid not in node_ids:
                node_ids.add(nid)
                nodes.append({"id": nid, "type": ntype, "label": label, "meta": meta or {}})

        def add_edge(src, tgt, rel="uses"):
            edges.append({"source": src, "target": tgt, "relation": rel})

        search_table = table.lower().strip() if table else ""
        search_column = column.lower().strip() if column else ""
        search_db = database.lower().strip() if database else ""

        # 1) Scan Query Library — find queries referencing the table/column
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT id, title, query_text, database_name, user_id FROM {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE}")
        lib_queries = cursor.fetchall()
        for q in lib_queries:
            sql_lower = (q["query_text"] or "").lower()
            if search_table and search_table not in sql_lower:
                continue
            if search_column and search_column not in sql_lower:
                continue
            if search_db and (q["database_name"] or "").lower() != search_db:
                continue
            qid = f"query_{q['id']}"
            add_node(qid, "query", q["title"] or f"Query #{q['id']}", {"id": q["id"], "user": q["user_id"], "database": q["database_name"]})
            # Extract table references from SQL
            tbl_refs = set(re.findall(r'(?:FROM|JOIN|INTO|UPDATE|TABLE)\s+(?:"?(\w+)"?\.)?(?:"?(\w+)"?)', sql_lower, re.IGNORECASE))
            for schema_ref, tbl_ref in tbl_refs:
                if tbl_ref:
                    tid = f"table_{q.get('database_name','') or ''}_{tbl_ref}"
                    add_node(tid, "table", tbl_ref, {"database": q.get("database_name", ""), "schema": schema_ref})
                    add_edge(tid, qid, "queried_by")

        # 2) Scan Custom APIs — find APIs with SQL referencing the table
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT id, name, query_template, database_name, owner FROM {API_BUILDER_TABLE}")
        apis = cursor.fetchall()
        for api in apis:
            sql_lower = (api["query_template"] or "").lower()
            if search_table and search_table not in sql_lower:
                continue
            if search_column and search_column not in sql_lower:
                continue
            if search_db and (api["database_name"] or "").lower() != search_db:
                continue
            aid = f"api_{api['id']}"
            add_node(aid, "api", api["name"] or api["id"], {"id": api["id"], "owner": api["owner"], "database": api["database_name"]})
            tbl_refs = set(re.findall(r'(?:FROM|JOIN|INTO|UPDATE|TABLE)\s+(?:"?(\w+)"?\.)?(?:"?(\w+)"?)', sql_lower, re.IGNORECASE))
            for schema_ref, tbl_ref in tbl_refs:
                if tbl_ref:
                    tid = f"table_{api.get('database_name','') or ''}_{tbl_ref}"
                    add_node(tid, "table", tbl_ref, {"database": api.get("database_name", "")})
                    add_edge(tid, aid, "used_by_api")

        # 3) Scan Scheduler Jobs — find jobs with steps referencing the table
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT id, name, steps, user_id FROM {SCHEDULER_JOBS_TABLE}")
        jobs = cursor.fetchall()
        for job in jobs:
            steps_raw = job.get("steps") or "[]"
            try:
                steps = json.loads(steps_raw) if isinstance(steps_raw, str) else steps_raw
            except:
                steps = []
            steps_text = json.dumps(steps).lower()
            if search_table and search_table not in steps_text:
                continue
            if search_column and search_column not in steps_text:
                continue
            jid = f"job_{job['id']}"
            add_node(jid, "scheduler", job["name"] or job["id"], {"id": job["id"], "user": job["user_id"]})
            # Check step configs for table references
            for step in steps:
                cfg = step.get("config", {})
                for key in ["source_table", "target_table", "table", "source_query", "url"]:
                    val = str(cfg.get(key, "")).lower()
                    if search_table and search_table in val:
                        tbl_refs2 = re.findall(r'(?:FROM|JOIN|INTO|UPDATE|TABLE)\s+(?:"?\w+"?\.)?(?:"?(\w+)"?)', val, re.IGNORECASE)
                        for tr in tbl_refs2:
                            if tr:
                                tid = f"table_{search_db}_{tr}"
                                add_node(tid, "table", tr, {"database": search_db})
                                add_edge(tid, jid, "used_by_job")
                        if not tbl_refs2 and search_table in val:
                            tid = f"table_{search_db}_{search_table}"
                            add_node(tid, "table", search_table, {"database": search_db})
                            add_edge(tid, jid, "used_by_job")

        # 4) Scan Pipelines — find pipelines with nodes referencing the table
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT id, name, nodes, edges, user_id FROM {PIPELINE_TABLE}")
        pipes = cursor.fetchall()
        for pipe in pipes:
            nodes_raw = pipe.get("nodes") or "[]"
            try:
                pnodes = json.loads(nodes_raw) if isinstance(nodes_raw, str) else nodes_raw
            except:
                pnodes = []
            pipe_text = json.dumps(pnodes).lower()
            if search_table and search_table not in pipe_text:
                continue
            if search_column and search_column not in pipe_text:
                continue
            pid = f"pipeline_{pipe['id']}"
            add_node(pid, "pipeline", pipe["name"] or pipe["id"], {"id": pipe["id"], "user": pipe["user_id"]})
            for pn in pnodes:
                pcfg = pn.get("data", {}).get("config", {})
                for key in ["query", "tableName", "database"]:
                    val = str(pcfg.get(key, "")).lower()
                    if search_table and search_table in val:
                        tid = f"table_{search_db}_{search_table}"
                        add_node(tid, "table", search_table, {"database": search_db})
                        add_edge(tid, pid, "used_by_pipeline")
                        break

        # 5) Scan Dashboard Widgets — find widgets with queries referencing the table
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT id, dashboard_id, title, query_text, database_name FROM {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE}")
        wdgs = cursor.fetchall()
        for wdg in wdgs:
            sql_lower = (wdg["query_text"] or "").lower()
            if search_table and search_table not in sql_lower:
                continue
            if search_column and search_column not in sql_lower:
                continue
            wid = f"widget_{wdg['id']}"
            add_node(wid, "widget", wdg["title"] or wdg["id"], {"id": wdg["id"], "dashboard_id": wdg["dashboard_id"], "database": wdg["database_name"]})
            tbl_refs = set(re.findall(r'(?:FROM|JOIN|INTO|UPDATE|TABLE)\s+(?:"?(\w+)"?\.)?(?:"?(\w+)"?)', sql_lower, re.IGNORECASE))
            for schema_ref, tbl_ref in tbl_refs:
                if tbl_ref:
                    tid = f"table_{wdg.get('database_name','') or ''}_{tbl_ref}"
                    add_node(tid, "table", tbl_ref, {"database": wdg.get("database_name", "")})
                    add_edge(tid, wid, "used_by_widget")

        # 6) If a specific column was requested, add column node and column-level edges
        if search_column and search_table:
            col_id = f"column_{search_db}_{search_table}_{search_column}"
            add_node(col_id, "column", f"{search_table}.{search_column}", {"database": search_db, "table": search_table})
            # Link column to table
            for n in nodes:
                if n["type"] == "table" and search_table in n["label"].lower():
                    add_edge(col_id, n["id"], "belongs_to")

        # Build impact summary
        impact = {
            "queries": len([n for n in nodes if n["type"] == "query"]),
            "apis": len([n for n in nodes if n["type"] == "api"]),
            "scheduler_jobs": len([n for n in nodes if n["type"] == "scheduler"]),
            "pipelines": len([n for n in nodes if n["type"] == "pipeline"]),
            "widgets": len([n for n in nodes if n["type"] == "widget"]),
            "tables": len([n for n in nodes if n["type"] == "table"]),
        }

        return {
            "success": True,
            "nodes": nodes,
            "edges": edges,
            "impact": impact,
            "search": {"database": search_db, "table": search_table, "column": search_column},
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.get("/api/lineage/impact")
async def get_impact_analysis(request: Request, database: str = Query(""), table: str = Query(""), column: str = Query("")):
    """
    Quick impact summary: 'What breaks if I drop this table/column?'
    Returns categorized list of all dependent objects.
    """
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)

        search_table = table.lower().strip()
        search_column = column.lower().strip()
        search_db = database.lower().strip()

        if not search_table:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Table name is required")

        affected = {"queries": [], "apis": [], "jobs": [], "pipelines": [], "widgets": []}

        # Check queries
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT id, title, query_text, database_name, user_id FROM {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE}")
        for q in cursor.fetchall():
            sql_l = (q["query_text"] or "").lower()
            if search_table in sql_l and (not search_column or search_column in sql_l):
                if not search_db or (q["database_name"] or "").lower() == search_db:
                    affected["queries"].append({"id": q["id"], "name": q["title"], "user": q["user_id"], "database": q["database_name"]})

        # Check APIs
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT id, name, query_template, database_name, owner FROM {API_BUILDER_TABLE}")
        for api in cursor.fetchall():
            sql_l = (api["query_template"] or "").lower()
            if search_table in sql_l and (not search_column or search_column in sql_l):
                if not search_db or (api["database_name"] or "").lower() == search_db:
                    affected["apis"].append({"id": api["id"], "name": api["name"], "owner": api["owner"], "database": api["database_name"]})

        # Check scheduler jobs
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT id, name, steps, user_id FROM {SCHEDULER_JOBS_TABLE}")
        for job in cursor.fetchall():
            steps_text = json.dumps(job.get("steps") or "[]").lower()
            if search_table in steps_text and (not search_column or search_column in steps_text):
                affected["jobs"].append({"id": job["id"], "name": job["name"], "user": job["user_id"]})

        # Check pipelines
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT id, name, nodes, user_id FROM {PIPELINE_TABLE}")
        for pipe in cursor.fetchall():
            pipe_text = (pipe.get("nodes") or "").lower() if isinstance(pipe.get("nodes"), str) else json.dumps(pipe.get("nodes") or []).lower()
            if search_table in pipe_text and (not search_column or search_column in pipe_text):
                affected["pipelines"].append({"id": pipe["id"], "name": pipe["name"], "user": pipe["user_id"]})

        # Check dashboard widgets
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT w.id, w.title, w.query_text, w.database_name, w.dashboard_id, d.name as dash_name FROM {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE} w LEFT JOIN {DASHBOARD_TABLE} d ON w.dashboard_id = d.id")
        for wdg in cursor.fetchall():
            sql_l = (wdg["query_text"] or "").lower()
            if search_table in sql_l and (not search_column or search_column in sql_l):
                if not search_db or (wdg["database_name"] or "").lower() == search_db:
                    affected["widgets"].append({"id": wdg["id"], "title": wdg["title"], "dashboard": wdg["dash_name"], "dashboard_id": wdg["dashboard_id"]})

        total = sum(len(v) for v in affected.values())
        severity = "critical" if total >= 5 else "warning" if total >= 2 else "low" if total >= 1 else "safe"

        return {
            "success": True,
            "target": {"database": search_db, "table": search_table, "column": search_column},
            "affected": affected,
            "total_affected": total,
            "severity": severity,
            "message": f"Dropping {'column ' + search_column + ' from ' if search_column else ''}{search_table} would impact {total} object(s)."
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.get("/api/lineage/tables")
async def get_lineage_tables(request: Request, database: str = Query("")):
    """List all tables referenced across queries, APIs, jobs, pipelines, widgets for type-ahead."""
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        all_sql = []

        cursor.execute(f"SELECT query_text FROM {QUERY_LIBRARY_TABLE}")
        all_sql.extend([r["query_text"] or "" for r in cursor.fetchall()])
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT query_template FROM {API_BUILDER_TABLE}")
        all_sql.extend([r["query_template"] or "" for r in cursor.fetchall()])
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT query_text FROM {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE}")
        all_sql.extend([r["query_text"] or "" for r in cursor.fetchall()])

        SQL_KEYWORDS = {
            'select', 'where', 'set', 'values', 'and', 'or', 'on', 'as', 'if', 'not', 'exists',
            'case', 'when', 'then', 'else', 'end', 'null', 'true', 'false', 'in', 'between',
            'like', 'is', 'by', 'order', 'group', 'having', 'limit', 'offset', 'union', 'all',
            'distinct', 'count', 'sum', 'avg', 'min', 'max', 'cast', 'coalesce', 'inner',
            'outer', 'left', 'right', 'cross', 'full', 'natural', 'lateral', 'with', 'recursive',
            'asc', 'desc', 'primary', 'key', 'foreign', 'references', 'constraint', 'index',
            'create', 'alter', 'drop', 'insert', 'delete', 'update', 'begin', 'commit', 'rollback',
        }
        tables = set()
        for sql in all_sql:
            refs = re.findall(r'(?:FROM|JOIN|INTO|UPDATE|TABLE|MERGE\s+INTO)\s+(?:"?\w+"?\.)?(?:"?(\w+)"?)', sql, re.IGNORECASE)
            for r in refs:
                if r and r.lower() not in SQL_KEYWORDS and len(r) > 1:
                    tables.add(r)

        # Also get actual tables from the database if provided
        if database:
            try:
                c2 = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database=database, user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
                cu2 = c2.cursor()
                cu2.execute("SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema = 'public'")
                for row in cu2.fetchall():
                    tables.add(row[0])
                c2.close()
            except:
                pass

        return {"success": True, "tables": sorted(tables)}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
# ║                QUERY PERFORMANCE ANALYZER                          ║
# ╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

SLOW_QUERY_TABLE = "sql_editor_slow_queries"


def _ensure_slow_query_table():
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {SLOW_QUERY_TABLE} (
                id          SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                user_id     VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                database_name VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                db_type     VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'cloudsql',
                query_text  TEXT NOT NULL,
                duration_ms INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                row_count   INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                plan_json   TEXT DEFAULT '',
                plan_text   TEXT DEFAULT '',
                suggestions TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
                cost_estimate FLOAT DEFAULT 0,
                created_at  TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_slow_q_user ON {SLOW_QUERY_TABLE} (user_id)")
        cursor.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_slow_q_dur ON {SLOW_QUERY_TABLE} (duration_ms DESC)")
        connection.commit()
        logger.info("Slow query table ensured")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error creating slow query table: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


_ensure_slow_query_table()


@app.post("/api/analyze/explain")
async def analyze_explain(request: Request):
    """
    Run EXPLAIN ANALYZE on a query and return the execution plan
    with parsed node tree, cost, timing, and index suggestions.
    """
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    body = await request.json()
    query = body.get("query", "").strip()
    database = body.get("database", "").strip()
    db_type = body.get("db_type", "cloudsql")

    if not query:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Query is required")
    if not database:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Database is required")

    # Safety: only allow SELECT or WITH for EXPLAIN
    q_upper = query.strip().upper()
    if not (q_upper.startswith("SELECT") or q_upper.startswith("WITH") or q_upper.startswith("(")):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="EXPLAIN is only allowed for SELECT queries")

    connection = None
    try:
        if db_type == "bigquery":
            # BigQuery dry run for cost estimation
            from google.cloud.bigquery import QueryJobConfig
            query = _normalize_bq_query(query)
            job_config = QueryJobConfig(dry_run=True, use_query_cache=False)
            job = bq_client.query(query, job_config=job_config)
            bytes_processed = job.total_bytes_processed or 0
            cost_tb = 6.25  # $6.25 per TB on-demand
            est_cost = (bytes_processed / (1024**4)) * cost_tb

            return {
                "success": True,
                "db_type": "bigquery",
                "plan_text": f"Estimated bytes processed: {bytes_processed:,}\nEstimated cost: ${est_cost:.6f}",
                "plan_tree": [{
                    "node_type": "BigQuery Scan",
                    "bytes_processed": bytes_processed,
                    "cost_estimate": round(est_cost, 6),
                    "details": f"{bytes_processed / (1024**2):.1f} MB scanned"
                }],
                "cost_estimate": round(est_cost, 6),
                "bytes_processed": bytes_processed,
                "suggestions": _bq_suggestions(query, bytes_processed),
                "duration_ms": 0,
            }

        # CloudSQL — EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, COSTS, BUFFERS, FORMAT JSON)
        start_time = time.time()
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database=database, user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        connection.set_session(readonly=True, autocommit=True)
        cursor = connection.cursor()

        # Get JSON plan
        cursor.execute(f"EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, COSTS, BUFFERS, FORMAT JSON) {query}")
        plan_json_raw = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        plan_json = plan_json_raw if isinstance(plan_json_raw, list) else json.loads(plan_json_raw) if isinstance(plan_json_raw, str) else [plan_json_raw]

        # Get text plan for display
        cursor2 = connection.cursor()
        cursor2.execute(f"EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, COSTS, BUFFERS) {query}")
        plan_text = "\n".join([row[0] for row in cursor2.fetchall()])

        duration_ms = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)

        # Parse the plan tree
        root_plan = plan_json[0].get("Plan", {}) if plan_json else {}
        plan_tree = _parse_plan_node(root_plan)
        total_cost = root_plan.get("Total Cost", 0)
        actual_time = root_plan.get("Actual Total Time", 0)
        rows_returned = root_plan.get("Actual Rows", 0)

        # Generate suggestions
        suggestions = _generate_suggestions(root_plan, query, plan_text)

        # Log slow query (>500ms)
        if duration_ms > 500:
            try:
                c3 = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
                cu3 = c3.cursor()
                cu3.execute(f"""
                    INSERT INTO {SLOW_QUERY_TABLE} (user_id, database_name, db_type, query_text, duration_ms, row_count, plan_json, plan_text, suggestions, cost_estimate)
                    VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                """, (user_id, database, db_type, query, duration_ms, rows_returned,
                      json.dumps(plan_json, default=str), plan_text, json.dumps(suggestions), total_cost))
                c3.commit()
                c3.close()
            except:
                pass

        return {
            "success": True,
            "db_type": "cloudsql",
            "plan_text": plan_text,
            "plan_tree": plan_tree,
            "plan_json": plan_json,
            "cost_estimate": total_cost,
            "actual_time_ms": actual_time,
            "rows_returned": rows_returned,
            "duration_ms": duration_ms,
            "suggestions": suggestions,
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def _parse_plan_node(node, depth=0):
    """Recursively parse EXPLAIN JSON plan into a flat tree for the frontend."""
    if not node:
        return []
    result = [{
        "depth": depth,
        "node_type": node.get("Node Type", "Unknown"),
        "relation": node.get("Relation Name", ""),
        "alias": node.get("Alias", ""),
        "join_type": node.get("Join Type", ""),
        "index_name": node.get("Index Name", ""),
        "index_cond": node.get("Index Cond", ""),
        "filter": node.get("Filter", ""),
        "hash_cond": node.get("Hash Cond", ""),
        "merge_cond": node.get("Merge Cond", ""),
        "sort_key": node.get("Sort Key", []),
        "startup_cost": node.get("Startup Cost", 0),
        "total_cost": node.get("Total Cost", 0),
        "plan_rows": node.get("Plan Rows", 0),
        "actual_rows": node.get("Actual Rows", 0),
        "actual_time": node.get("Actual Total Time", 0),
        "actual_startup": node.get("Actual Startup Time", 0),
        "loops": node.get("Actual Loops", 1),
        "shared_hit": node.get("Shared Hit Blocks", 0),
        "shared_read": node.get("Shared Read Blocks", 0),
        "rows_removed_by_filter": node.get("Rows Removed by Filter", 0),
        "width": node.get("Plan Width", 0),
    }]
    for child in node.get("Plans", []):
        result.extend(_parse_plan_node(child, depth + 1))
    return result


def _generate_suggestions(plan, query, plan_text):
    """Analyze EXPLAIN output and generate actionable optimization suggestions."""
    suggestions = []
    query_lower = query.lower()
    plan_lower = plan_text.lower()

    # 1) Sequential scan on large tables
    def _check_seq_scans(node):
        if node.get("Node Type") == "Seq Scan" and node.get("Actual Rows", 0) > 1000:
            rel = node.get("Relation Name", "?")
            filt = node.get("Filter", "")
            removed = node.get("Rows Removed by Filter", 0)
            if removed > 100:
                suggestions.append({
                    "type": "index",
                    "severity": "high",
                    "title": f"Sequential scan on '{rel}' with heavy filtering",
                    "detail": f"Seq Scan read {node.get('Actual Rows',0) + removed} rows but only kept {node.get('Actual Rows',0)}. {removed} rows removed by filter: {filt}",
                    "recommendation": f"CREATE INDEX idx_{rel}_<column> ON {rel} (<filter_columns>);",
                })
            elif node.get("Actual Rows", 0) > 10000:
                suggestions.append({
                    "type": "index",
                    "severity": "medium",
                    "title": f"Large sequential scan on '{rel}'",
                    "detail": f"Full table scan returning {node.get('Actual Rows',0)} rows.",
                    "recommendation": f"Consider adding an index on '{rel}' for the columns used in WHERE/JOIN clauses.",
                })
        for child in node.get("Plans", []):
            _check_seq_scans(child)

    _check_seq_scans(plan)

    # 2) Sort / disk-based sort
    if "sort method: external" in plan_lower or "sort method: disk" in plan_lower:
        suggestions.append({
            "type": "memory",
            "severity": "high",
            "title": "Sort spilled to disk",
            "detail": "The sort operation exceeded work_mem and spilled to disk, significantly slowing the query.",
            "recommendation": "SET work_mem = '256MB'; or add an index matching the ORDER BY columns.",
        })

    # 3) Hash join with large build
    def _check_hash(node):
        if node.get("Node Type") == "Hash" and node.get("Actual Rows", 0) > 100000:
            suggestions.append({
                "type": "memory",
                "severity": "medium",
                "title": "Large hash table in memory",
                "detail": f"Hash node built with {node.get('Actual Rows',0)} rows. May consume significant memory.",
                "recommendation": "Consider increasing work_mem or restructuring the JOIN.",
            })
        for child in node.get("Plans", []):
            _check_hash(child)

    _check_hash(plan)

    # 4) Nested loop with high loops count
    def _check_nested(node):
        if node.get("Node Type") == "Nested Loop" and node.get("Actual Loops", 1) > 1000:
            suggestions.append({
                "type": "rewrite",
                "severity": "high",
                "title": "Nested loop with excessive iterations",
                "detail": f"Nested Loop executed {node.get('Actual Loops',1)} times. This is often a sign of missing indexes on the inner relation.",
                "recommendation": "Add an index on the inner table's join column, or rewrite the query to use a hash join.",
            })
        for child in node.get("Plans", []):
            _check_nested(child)

    _check_nested(plan)

    # 5) SELECT * warning
    if "select *" in query_lower:
        suggestions.append({
            "type": "rewrite",
            "severity": "low",
            "title": "SELECT * detected",
            "detail": "Fetching all columns increases I/O and network transfer.",
            "recommendation": "List only the columns you need: SELECT col1, col2, ... FROM ...",
        })

    # 6) Missing LIMIT
    if "limit" not in query_lower and plan.get("Actual Rows", 0) > 10000:
        suggestions.append({
            "type": "rewrite",
            "severity": "medium",
            "title": "No LIMIT clause on large result set",
            "detail": f"Query returned {plan.get('Actual Rows',0)} rows without a LIMIT.",
            "recommendation": "Add LIMIT to reduce data transfer if you don't need all rows.",
        })

    # 7) Row estimate vs actual mismatch
    est = plan.get("Plan Rows", 0)
    act = plan.get("Actual Rows", 0)
    if est > 0 and act > 0 and (act / est > 10 or est / act > 10):
        suggestions.append({
            "type": "statistics",
            "severity": "medium",
            "title": "Row estimate significantly off",
            "detail": f"Planner estimated {est} rows but got {act}. Stale statistics can lead to suboptimal plans.",
            "recommendation": "Run ANALYZE on the involved tables to update statistics.",
        })

    if not suggestions:
        suggestions.append({
            "type": "info",
            "severity": "low",
            "title": "Query looks well-optimized",
            "detail": "No major performance issues detected in the execution plan.",
            "recommendation": "",
        })

    return suggestions


def _bq_suggestions(query, bytes_processed):
    """Generate BigQuery-specific optimization suggestions."""
    suggestions = []
    q_lower = query.lower()
    gb = bytes_processed / (1024**3)

    if "select *" in q_lower:
        suggestions.append({"type": "rewrite", "severity": "high", "title": "SELECT * on BigQuery", "detail": "BigQuery is columnar — SELECT * scans all columns and dramatically increases cost.", "recommendation": "Select only needed columns."})
    if gb > 1:
        suggestions.append({"type": "partitioning", "severity": "high", "title": f"Large scan: {gb:.1f} GB", "detail": "Consider partitioning/clustering the table or adding date filters.", "recommendation": "Add WHERE _PARTITIONDATE or cluster key filters."})
    if "order by" in q_lower and "limit" not in q_lower:
        suggestions.append({"type": "rewrite", "severity": "medium", "title": "ORDER BY without LIMIT", "detail": "Sorting the entire result set is expensive.", "recommendation": "Add a LIMIT clause."})
    if not suggestions:
        suggestions.append({"type": "info", "severity": "low", "title": "Query cost looks reasonable", "detail": f"{gb:.2f} GB scanned.", "recommendation": ""})
    return suggestions


@app.get("/api/analyze/slow-queries")
async def get_slow_queries(request: Request, limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=500)):
    """Get the slow query log for the current user (or all for admin)."""
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        if _is_super_admin(user_id):
            cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {SLOW_QUERY_TABLE} ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT %s", (limit,))
        else:
            cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {SLOW_QUERY_TABLE} WHERE user_id = %s ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT %s", (user_id, limit))
        rows = []
        for r in cursor.fetchall():
            rows.append({
                "id": r["id"], "user_id": r["user_id"], "database_name": r["database_name"],
                "db_type": r["db_type"], "query_text": r["query_text"], "duration_ms": r["duration_ms"],
                "row_count": r["row_count"], "cost_estimate": r["cost_estimate"],
                "suggestions": json.loads(r["suggestions"] or "[]"),
                "created_at": r["created_at"].isoformat() if hasattr(r["created_at"], "isoformat") else str(r["created_at"]),
            })
        return {"success": True, "queries": rows}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.post("/api/analyze/estimate")
async def estimate_query_cost(request: Request):
    """Quick cost pre-estimation without running the query."""
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    body = await request.json()
    query = body.get("query", "").strip()
    database = body.get("database", "").strip()
    db_type = body.get("db_type", "cloudsql")

    if not query or not database:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Query and database required")

    q_upper = query.strip().upper()
    if not (q_upper.startswith("SELECT") or q_upper.startswith("WITH") or q_upper.startswith("(")):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Only SELECT queries supported")

    connection = None
    try:
        if db_type == "bigquery":
            from google.cloud.bigquery import QueryJobConfig
            query = _normalize_bq_query(query)
            job_config = QueryJobConfig(dry_run=True, use_query_cache=False)
            job = bq_client.query(query, job_config=job_config)
            bytes_processed = job.total_bytes_processed or 0
            est_cost = (bytes_processed / (1024**4)) * 6.25
            return {
                "success": True,
                "db_type": "bigquery",
                "bytes_processed": bytes_processed,
                "estimated_cost_usd": round(est_cost, 6),
                "human": f"{bytes_processed / (1024**2):.1f} MB — ${est_cost:.6f}",
            }

        # CloudSQL — EXPLAIN without ANALYZE (plan only, no execution)
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database=database, user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        connection.set_session(readonly=True, autocommit=True)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"EXPLAIN (COSTS, FORMAT JSON) {query}")
        plan_json = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        plan = plan_json[0] if isinstance(plan_json, list) else json.loads(plan_json)[0] if isinstance(plan_json, str) else plan_json
        root = plan.get("Plan", {})
        total_cost = root.get("Total Cost", 0)
        est_rows = root.get("Plan Rows", 0)
        est_width = root.get("Plan Width", 0)

        return {
            "success": True,
            "db_type": "cloudsql",
            "total_cost": total_cost,
            "estimated_rows": est_rows,
            "estimated_width": est_width,
            "human": f"Cost: {total_cost:.1f} — Est. {est_rows} rows × {est_width} bytes",
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


@app.get("/api/analyze/index-suggestions")
async def get_index_suggestions(request: Request, database: str = Query(""), table: str = Query("")):
    """Analyze a table's indexes and suggest missing ones based on usage patterns."""
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    if not database or not table:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Database and table required")
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database=database, user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)

        # Get existing indexes
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT indexname, indexdef, tablename
            FROM pg_indexes WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND tablename = %s
        """, (table,))
        existing_indexes = [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]

        # Get table stats
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT reltuples::bigint AS row_estimate,
                   pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size(%s)) AS total_size,
                   pg_total_relation_size(%s) AS size_bytes
            FROM pg_class WHERE relname = %s
        """, (table, table, table))
        stats_row = cursor.fetchone()
        table_stats = dict(stats_row) if stats_row else {"row_estimate": 0, "total_size": "0", "size_bytes": 0}

        # Get column stats
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT attname, n_distinct, null_frac, avg_width, correlation
            FROM pg_stats WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND tablename = %s
        """, (table,))
        col_stats = [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]

        # Get sequential/index scan counts
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT seq_scan, seq_tup_read, idx_scan, idx_tup_fetch,
                   n_tup_ins, n_tup_upd, n_tup_del, n_live_tup, n_dead_tup,
                   last_vacuum, last_autovacuum, last_analyze, last_autoanalyze
            FROM pg_stat_user_tables WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND relname = %s
        """, (table,))
        usage_row = cursor.fetchone()
        usage_stats = {}
        if usage_row:
            usage_stats = dict(usage_row)
            for k, v in usage_stats.items():
                if hasattr(v, "isoformat"):
                    usage_stats[k] = v.isoformat()

        # Generate index suggestions
        suggestions = []
        indexed_cols = set()
        for idx in existing_indexes:
            # Extract column names from index definition
            cols = re.findall(r'\((.+?)\)', idx.get("indexdef", ""))
            if cols:
                for c in cols[0].split(","):
                    indexed_cols.add(c.strip().lower())

        # Suggest indexes for high-cardinality unindexed columns
        for cs in col_stats:
            col = cs["attname"]
            if col.lower() in indexed_cols:
                continue
            nd = cs.get("n_distinct", 0) or 0
            nf = cs.get("null_frac", 0) or 0
            if nd < -0.5 or nd > 100:  # high cardinality
                suggestions.append({
                    "column": col,
                    "reason": f"High cardinality (n_distinct={nd}), not indexed. Frequently used in WHERE/JOIN would benefit from an index.",
                    "sql": f"CREATE INDEX idx_{table}_{col} ON {table} ({col});",
                    "priority": "medium",
                })

        # Check if seq scans >> idx scans
        seq = usage_stats.get("seq_scan", 0) or 0
        idx = usage_stats.get("idx_scan", 0) or 0
        if seq > 100 and (idx == 0 or seq / max(idx, 1) > 10):
            suggestions.append({
                "column": "*",
                "reason": f"Table has {seq} seq scans vs {idx} index scans — heavily scanned without indexes.",
                "sql": "Review WHERE/JOIN columns in your queries and add targeted indexes.",
                "priority": "high",
            })

        # Check dead tuples
        dead = usage_stats.get("n_dead_tup", 0) or 0
        live = usage_stats.get("n_live_tup", 0) or 0
        if dead > 10000 and live > 0 and dead / live > 0.1:
            suggestions.append({
                "column": "*",
                "reason": f"{dead} dead tuples ({(dead/live*100):.0f}% of live). Table bloat impacts performance.",
                "sql": f"VACUUM ANALYZE {table};",
                "priority": "high",
            })

        return {
            "success": True,
            "table": table,
            "database": database,
            "table_stats": table_stats,
            "usage_stats": usage_stats,
            "existing_indexes": existing_indexes,
            "column_stats": col_stats,
            "suggestions": suggestions,
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
# ║  FEATURE: Embedded BI – shareable iframe/JS embed tokens           ║
# ╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

EMBED_TOKENS_TABLE = "sql_editor_embed_tokens"


def _ensure_embed_tables():
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {EMBED_TOKENS_TABLE} (
                id            VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
                token         VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
                dashboard_id  VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
                widget_id     VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT '',
                title         VARCHAR(500) DEFAULT '',
                user_id       VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                settings      TEXT DEFAULT '{{}}',
                allowed_domains TEXT DEFAULT '*',
                is_active     BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
                expires_at    TIMESTAMP,
                created_at    TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
                updated_at    TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_embed_token ON {EMBED_TOKENS_TABLE} (token)")
        cursor.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_embed_user ON {EMBED_TOKENS_TABLE} (user_id)")
        connection.commit()
        logger.info("Embed tokens table ensured")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error creating embed tables: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


_ensure_embed_tables()


# ── POST /api/embed/create — generate embed token ───────────────────
@app.post("/api/embed/create")
async def create_embed_token(request: Request, body: dict = Body(...)):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        dashboard_id = body.get("dashboard_id", "")
        widget_id = body.get("widget_id", "")
        title = body.get("title", "")
        settings = json.dumps(body.get("settings", {}))
        allowed_domains = body.get("allowed_domains", "*")
        expires_days = body.get("expires_days", 0)

        if not dashboard_id:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="dashboard_id is required")

        embed_id = f"emb_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
        expires_at = None
        if expires_days and int(expires_days) > 0:
            expires_at = f"NOW() + INTERVAL '{int(expires_days)} days'"

        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()

        if expires_at:
            cursor.execute(f"""
                INSERT INTO {EMBED_TOKENS_TABLE} (id, token, dashboard_id, widget_id, title, user_id, settings, allowed_domains, expires_at)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, {expires_at})
            """, (embed_id, token, dashboard_id, widget_id, title, user_id, settings, allowed_domains))
        else:
            cursor.execute(f"""
                INSERT INTO {EMBED_TOKENS_TABLE} (id, token, dashboard_id, widget_id, title, user_id, settings, allowed_domains)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
            """, (embed_id, token, dashboard_id, widget_id, title, user_id, settings, allowed_domains))

        connection.commit()
        _log_audit(user_id, "embed_create", details=f"Created embed token for dashboard {dashboard_id}")
        return {"success": True, "id": embed_id, "token": token}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── GET /api/embed/list — list user's embed tokens ───────────────────
@app.get("/api/embed/list")
async def list_embed_tokens(request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT e.*, d.name as dashboard_name
            FROM {EMBED_TOKENS_TABLE} e
            LEFT JOIN {DASHBOARD_TABLE} d ON d.id = e.dashboard_id
            WHERE e.user_id = %s
            ORDER BY e.created_at DESC
        """, (user_id,))
        tokens = cursor.fetchall()
        for t in tokens:
            for k, v in t.items():
                if isinstance(v, datetime):
                    t[k] = v.isoformat()
        return {"success": True, "tokens": tokens}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── PUT /api/embed/{embed_id} — update embed token ──────────────────
@app.put("/api/embed/{embed_id}")
async def update_embed_token(embed_id: str, request: Request, body: dict = Body(...)):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        fields = []
        values = []
        for col in ["title", "allowed_domains"]:
            if col in body:
                fields.append(f"{col} = %s")
                values.append(body[col])
        if "settings" in body:
            fields.append("settings = %s")
            values.append(json.dumps(body["settings"]))
        if "is_active" in body:
            fields.append("is_active = %s")
            values.append(bool(body["is_active"]))
        if not fields:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No fields to update")
        fields.append("updated_at = NOW()")
        values.extend([embed_id, user_id])
        cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {EMBED_TOKENS_TABLE} SET {', '.join(fields)} WHERE id = %s AND user_id = %s", values)
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True, "updated": cursor.rowcount}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── DELETE /api/embed/{embed_id} — revoke embed token ────────────────
@app.delete("/api/embed/{embed_id}")
async def delete_embed_token(embed_id: str, request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"DELETE FROM {EMBED_TOKENS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND user_id = %s", (embed_id, user_id))
        connection.commit()
        _log_audit(user_id, "embed_delete", details=f"Deleted embed token {embed_id}")
        return {"success": True, "deleted": cursor.rowcount}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── GET /api/embed/{token}/render — PUBLIC: get widget data by token ─
@app.get("/api/embed/{token}/render")
async def render_embed(token: str):
    """Public endpoint — no auth required. Returns widget data for embedding."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)

        # Validate token
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT * FROM {EMBED_TOKENS_TABLE}
            WHERE token = %s AND is_active = TRUE
            AND (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > NOW())
        """, (token,))
        embed = cursor.fetchone()
        if not embed:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Invalid or expired embed token")

        widget_id = embed.get("widget_id", "")
        dashboard_id = embed["dashboard_id"]

        if widget_id:
            # Single widget
            cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND dashboard_id = %s", (widget_id, dashboard_id))
            widget = cursor.fetchone()
            if not widget:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Widget not found")
            # Execute widget query to get fresh data
            widget_data = _execute_embed_widget_query(widget)
            for k, v in widget.items():
                if isinstance(v, datetime):
                    widget[k] = v.isoformat()
            return {"success": True, "type": "widget", "widget": widget, "data": widget_data}
        else:
            # Full dashboard
            cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {DASHBOARD_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (dashboard_id,))
            dashboard = cursor.fetchone()
            if not dashboard:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Dashboard not found")
            cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {DASHBOARD_WIDGETS_TABLE} WHERE dashboard_id = %s", (dashboard_id,))
            widgets = cursor.fetchall()
            for w in widgets:
                w["live_data"] = _execute_embed_widget_query(w)
                for k, v in w.items():
                    if isinstance(v, datetime):
                        w[k] = v.isoformat()
            for k, v in dashboard.items():
                if isinstance(v, datetime):
                    dashboard[k] = v.isoformat()
            return {"success": True, "type": "dashboard", "dashboard": dashboard, "widgets": widgets}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def _execute_embed_widget_query(widget: dict) -> dict:
    """Execute a widget's query and return result data."""
    query = widget.get("query_text", "")
    database = widget.get("database_name", "postgres")
    db_type = widget.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    if not query:
        return {"columns": [], "rows": [], "row_count": 0}
    conn2 = None
    try:
        if db_type == "bigquery" and bq_client:
            job = bq_client.query(_normalize_bq_query(query))
            results = job.result()
            columns = [f.name for f in results.schema]
            rows = [dict(zip(columns, row.values())) for row in results]
            return {"columns": columns, "rows": rows[:500], "row_count": len(rows)}
        else:
            conn2 = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database=database, user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
            c2 = conn2.cursor()
            c2.execute(query)
            columns = [desc[0] for desc in c2.description] if c2.description else []
            rows_raw = c2.fetchmany(500)
            rows = [dict(zip(columns, r)) for r in rows_raw]
            return {"columns": columns, "rows": rows, "row_count": len(rows)}
    except Exception as ex:
        return {"columns": [], "rows": [], "row_count": 0, "error": str(ex)}
    finally:
        if conn2:
            conn2.close()


# ── GET /api/embed/{token}/snippet — returns embed code ──────────────
@app.get("/api/embed/{token}/snippet")
async def get_embed_snippet(token: str, request: Request):
    """Returns HTML iframe and JS snippet code for embedding."""
    base_url = str(request.base_url).rstrip("/")
    iframe_url = f"{base_url}/api/embed/{token}/render"
    iframe_code = f'<iframe src="{iframe_url}" width="100%" height="500" frameborder="0" style="border:none;border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)"></iframe>'
    js_code = f"""<div id="sql-embed-{token[:8]}"></div>
<script>
(function() {{
  fetch('{iframe_url}')
    .then(r => r.json())
    .then(data => {{
      const el = document.getElementById('sql-embed-{token[:8]}');
      if (data.success) {{
        const table = document.createElement('table');
        table.style.cssText = 'width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-family:sans-serif;font-size:13px';
        const d = data.type === 'widget' ? data.data : (data.widgets?.[0]?.live_data || {{}});
        if (d.columns) {{
          const thead = '<tr>' + d.columns.map(c => '<th style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd;background:#f5f5f5">' + c + '</th>').join('') + '</tr>';
          const tbody = (d.rows || []).slice(0, 100).map(r => '<tr>' + d.columns.map(c => '<td style="padding:8px;border:1px solid #ddd">' + (r[c] ?? '') + '</td>').join('') + '</tr>').join('');
          table.innerHTML = thead + tbody;
        }}
        el.appendChild(table);
      }} else {{
        el.textContent = 'Failed to load embed';
      }}
    }});
}})();
</script>"""
    return {"success": True, "iframe_code": iframe_code, "js_code": js_code, "render_url": iframe_url}

QUERY_FORMS_TABLE = "sql_editor_query_forms"


def _ensure_forms_tables():
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {QUERY_FORMS_TABLE} (
                id            VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
                name          VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
                description   TEXT DEFAULT '',
                query_text    TEXT NOT NULL,
                database_name VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT 'postgres',
                db_type       VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT 'cloudsql',
                parameters    TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
                user_id       VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                is_public     BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
                share_token   VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE,
                executions    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                created_at    TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
                updated_at    TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_forms_user ON {QUERY_FORMS_TABLE} (user_id)")
        cursor.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_forms_token ON {QUERY_FORMS_TABLE} (share_token)")
        connection.commit()
        logger.info("Query forms table ensured")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error creating forms tables: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


_ensure_forms_tables()


# ── POST /api/forms — create a form from parameterized query ─────────
@app.post("/api/forms")
async def create_query_form(request: Request, body: dict = Body(...)):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        name = body.get("name", "").strip()
        description = body.get("description", "")
        query_text = body.get("query_text", "").strip()
        database_name = body.get("database_name", "postgres")
        db_type = body.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
        parameters = body.get("parameters", [])
        is_public = body.get("is_public", False)

        if not name:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="name is required")
        if not query_text:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="query_text is required")

        # Auto-detect parameters from {{param_name}} pattern if none provided
        if not parameters:
            found = re.findall(r'\{\{(\w+)\}\}', query_text)
            parameters = [{"name": p, "label": p.replace("_", " ").title(), "type": "text", "default": "", "required": True} for p in dict.fromkeys(found)]

        form_id = f"form_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
        share_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(24) if is_public else secrets.token_urlsafe(24)

        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {QUERY_FORMS_TABLE} (id, name, description, query_text, database_name, db_type, parameters, user_id, is_public, share_token)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
        """, (form_id, name, description, query_text, database_name, db_type, json.dumps(parameters), user_id, is_public, share_token))
        connection.commit()
        _log_audit(user_id, "form_create", details=f"Created query form '{name}'")
        return {"success": True, "id": form_id, "share_token": share_token, "parameters": parameters}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── GET /api/forms — list user's forms ───────────────────────────────
@app.get("/api/forms")
async def list_query_forms(request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {QUERY_FORMS_TABLE} WHERE user_id = %s ORDER BY updated_at DESC", (user_id,))
        forms = cursor.fetchall()
        for f in forms:
            if isinstance(f.get("parameters"), str):
                f["parameters"] = json.loads(f["parameters"])
            for k, v in f.items():
                if isinstance(v, datetime):
                    f[k] = v.isoformat()
        return {"success": True, "forms": forms}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

# ── PUT /api/forms/{form_id} — update form ──────────────────────────
@app.put("/api/forms/{form_id}")
async def update_query_form(form_id: str, request: Request, body: dict = Body(...)):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        fields = []
        values = []
        for col in ["name", "description", "query_text", "database_name", "db_type"]:
            if col in body:
                fields.append(f"{col} = %s")
                values.append(body[col])
        if "parameters" in body:
            fields.append("parameters = %s")
            values.append(json.dumps(body["parameters"]))
        if "is_public" in body:
            fields.append("is_public = %s")
            values.append(bool(body["is_public"]))
        if not fields:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No fields to update")
        fields.append("updated_at = NOW()")
        values.extend([form_id, user_id])
        cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {QUERY_FORMS_TABLE} SET {', '.join(fields)} WHERE id = %s AND user_id = %s", values)
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True, "updated": cursor.rowcount}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

# ── POST /api/forms/{form_id}/execute — execute form with params ─────
@app.post("/api/forms/{form_id}/execute")
async def execute_query_form(form_id: str, request: Request, body: dict = Body(...)):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {QUERY_FORMS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND user_id = %s", (form_id, user_id))
        form = cursor.fetchone()
        if not form:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Form not found")

        result = _execute_form_query(form, body.get("params", {}))

        # Increment execution count
        cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {QUERY_FORMS_TABLE} SET executions = executions + 1 WHERE id = %s", (form_id,))
        connection.commit()

        _log_audit(user_id, "form_execute", database_name=form.get("database_name", ""), details=f"Executed form '{form.get('name')}'")
        return {"success": True, **result}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()

    """Public: execute a shared form with user-supplied parameters."""
    connection = None

# ── POST /api/forms/parse-params — auto-detect params in SQL ─────────
@app.post("/api/forms/parse-params")
async def parse_form_params(request: Request, body: dict = Body(...)):
    _require_user(request)
    query_text = body.get("query_text", "")
    found = re.findall(r'\{\{(\w+)\}\}', query_text)
    parameters = [{"name": p, "label": p.replace("_", " ").title(), "type": "text", "default": "", "required": True} for p in dict.fromkeys(found)]
    return {"success": True, "parameters": parameters}


def _execute_form_query(form: dict, params: dict) -> dict:
    """Replace {{param}} placeholders and execute the query safely."""
    query_text = form.get("query_text", "")
    database_name = form.get("database_name", "postgres")
    db_type = form.get("db_type", "cloudsql")

    # Parse parameter definitions
    param_defs = form.get("parameters", "[]")
    if isinstance(param_defs, str):
        param_defs = json.loads(param_defs)

    # Validate required params
    for pdef in param_defs:
        pname = pdef.get("name", "")
        if pdef.get("required", False) and pname not in params:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Missing required parameter: {pname}")

    # Build safe substitution — use parameterized queries where possible
    # Replace {{param}} with %s placeholders for safe execution
    ordered_params = []
    def replacer(match):
        pname = match.group(1)
        val = params.get(pname, "")
        # Apply type coercion from param defs
        ptype = "text"
        for pdef in param_defs:
            if pdef.get("name") == pname:
                ptype = pdef.get("type", "text")
                if not val and pdef.get("default"):
                    val = pdef["default"]
                break
        if ptype == "number":
            try:
                val = float(val) if "." in str(val) else int(val)
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                val = 0
        elif ptype == "date":
            val = str(val)
        else:
            val = str(val)
        ordered_params.append(val)
        return "%s"

    safe_query = re.sub(r'\{\{(\w+)\}\}', replacer, query_text)

    conn2 = None
    try:
        if db_type == "bigquery" and bq_client:
            # BigQuery: replace %s back with actual values for BQ
            final_query = query_text
            for pdef in param_defs:
                pname = pdef.get("name", "")
                val = params.get(pname, pdef.get("default", ""))
                ptype = pdef.get("type", "text")
                if ptype == "number":
                    final_query = final_query.replace("{{" + pname + "}}", str(val))
                else:
                    final_query = final_query.replace("{{" + pname + "}}", f"'{str(val)}'")
            job = bq_client.query(_normalize_bq_query(final_query))
            results = job.result()
            columns = [f.name for f in results.schema]
            rows = [dict(zip(columns, row.values())) for row in results]
            return {"columns": columns, "rows": rows[:1000], "row_count": len(rows)}
        else:
            conn2 = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database=database_name, user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
            c2 = conn2.cursor()
            c2.execute(safe_query, tuple(ordered_params))
            if c2.description:
                columns = [desc[0] for desc in c2.description]
                rows_raw = c2.fetchmany(1000)
                rows = [dict(zip(columns, r)) for r in rows_raw]
                return {"columns": columns, "rows": rows, "row_count": len(rows)}
            else:
                conn2.commit()
                return {"columns": [], "rows": [], "row_count": 0, "message": f"Query executed. Rows affected: {c2.rowcount}"}
    except Exception as ex:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Query execution error: {str(ex)}")
    finally:
        if conn2:
            conn2.close()


# ╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
# ║  FEATURE: Database Migration Tool                                  ║
# ╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

MIGRATIONS_TABLE = "sql_editor_migrations"


def _ensure_migration_tables():
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {MIGRATIONS_TABLE} (
                id              VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
                name            VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
                source_db       VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                target_db       VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                source_db_type  VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'cloudsql',
                target_db_type  VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'cloudsql',
                diff_json       TEXT DEFAULT '{{}}',
                alter_script    TEXT DEFAULT '',
                rollback_script TEXT DEFAULT '',
                status          VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending',
                executed_at     TIMESTAMP,
                execution_log   TEXT DEFAULT '',
                user_id         VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                created_at      TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
                updated_at      TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_migration_user ON {MIGRATIONS_TABLE} (user_id)")
        connection.commit()
        logger.info("Migration table ensured")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error creating migration tables: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


_ensure_migration_tables()


def _get_schema_info(database: str, db_type: str = "cloudsql") -> dict:
    """Get comprehensive schema info for a database — tables, columns, indexes, constraints."""
    schema_info = {"tables": {}, "indexes": {}, "constraints": {}}
    conn = None
    try:
        if db_type == "bigquery" and bq_client:
            dataset_ref = bq_client.dataset(database)
            tables = list(bq_client.list_tables(dataset_ref))
            for t in tables:
                tbl = bq_client.get_table(t.reference)
                cols = {}
                for f in tbl.schema:
                    cols[f.name] = {
                        "type": f.field_type,
                        "nullable": f.mode != "REQUIRED",
                        "default": None,
                    }
                schema_info["tables"][t.table_id] = cols
            return schema_info

        conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database=database, user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)

        # Get all tables and columns
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT table_name, column_name, data_type, is_nullable, column_default,
                   character_maximum_length, numeric_precision, numeric_scale
            FROM information_schema.columns
            WHERE table_schema = 'public'
            ORDER BY table_name, ordinal_position
        """)
        for row in cur.fetchall():
            tbl = row["table_name"]
            if tbl not in schema_info["tables"]:
                schema_info["tables"][tbl] = {}
            col_type = row["data_type"]
            if row.get("character_maximum_length"):
                col_type += f"({row['character_maximum_length']})"
            elif row.get("numeric_precision"):
                col_type += f"({row['numeric_precision']}"
                if row.get("numeric_scale"):
                    col_type += f",{row['numeric_scale']}"
                col_type += ")"
            schema_info["tables"][tbl][row["column_name"]] = {
                "type": col_type,
                "nullable": row["is_nullable"] == "YES",
                "default": row.get("column_default"),
            }

        # Get indexes
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT tablename, indexname, indexdef
            FROM pg_indexes
            WHERE schemaname = 'public'
        """)
        for row in cur.fetchall():
            tbl = row["tablename"]
            if tbl not in schema_info["indexes"]:
                schema_info["indexes"][tbl] = []
            schema_info["indexes"][tbl].append({"name": row["indexname"], "definition": row["indexdef"]})

        # Get constraints (PK, FK, UNIQUE, CHECK)
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT tc.table_name, tc.constraint_name, tc.constraint_type,
                   kcu.column_name,
                   ccu.table_name AS foreign_table, ccu.column_name AS foreign_column
            FROM information_schema.table_constraints tc
            JOIN information_schema.key_column_usage kcu
                ON tc.constraint_name = kcu.constraint_name AND tc.table_schema = kcu.table_schema
            LEFT JOIN information_schema.constraint_column_usage ccu
                ON tc.constraint_name = ccu.constraint_name AND tc.table_schema = ccu.table_schema
            WHERE tc.table_schema = 'public'
            ORDER BY tc.table_name, tc.constraint_name
        """)
        for row in cur.fetchall():
            tbl = row["table_name"]
            if tbl not in schema_info["constraints"]:
                schema_info["constraints"][tbl] = []
            schema_info["constraints"][tbl].append({
                "name": row["constraint_name"],
                "type": row["constraint_type"],
                "column": row["column_name"],
                "foreign_table": row.get("foreign_table"),
                "foreign_column": row.get("foreign_column"),
            })

        return schema_info
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error getting schema info for {database}: {e}")
        return schema_info
    finally:
        if conn:
            conn.close()


def _compute_schema_diff(source: dict, target: dict) -> dict:
    """Compare two schema_info dicts and produce a diff."""
    diff = {
        "tables_to_add": [],
        "tables_to_drop": [],
        "columns_to_add": [],
        "columns_to_drop": [],
        "columns_to_alter": [],
        "indexes_to_add": [],
        "indexes_to_drop": [],
        "summary": "",
    }
    src_tables = set(source.get("tables", {}).keys())
    tgt_tables = set(target.get("tables", {}).keys())

    # Tables only in source → need to CREATE in target
    for tbl in sorted(src_tables - tgt_tables):
        diff["tables_to_add"].append({"table": tbl, "columns": source["tables"][tbl]})

    # Tables only in target → DROP from target to match source
    for tbl in sorted(tgt_tables - src_tables):
        diff["tables_to_drop"].append(tbl)

    # Common tables → compare columns
    for tbl in sorted(src_tables & tgt_tables):
        src_cols = source["tables"][tbl]
        tgt_cols = target["tables"][tbl]
        src_names = set(src_cols.keys())
        tgt_names = set(tgt_cols.keys())

        for col in sorted(src_names - tgt_names):
            diff["columns_to_add"].append({"table": tbl, "column": col, **src_cols[col]})

        for col in sorted(tgt_names - src_names):
            diff["columns_to_drop"].append({"table": tbl, "column": col})

        for col in sorted(src_names & tgt_names):
            sc = src_cols[col]
            tc = tgt_cols[col]
            if sc.get("type") != tc.get("type") or sc.get("nullable") != tc.get("nullable"):
                diff["columns_to_alter"].append({
                    "table": tbl, "column": col,
                    "from_type": tc.get("type"), "to_type": sc.get("type"),
                    "from_nullable": tc.get("nullable"), "to_nullable": sc.get("nullable"),
                })

    # Compare indexes
    src_idx = source.get("indexes", {})
    tgt_idx = target.get("indexes", {})
    src_idx_names = set()
    tgt_idx_names = set()
    src_idx_map = {}
    tgt_idx_map = {}
    for tbl, idxs in src_idx.items():
        for idx in idxs:
            src_idx_names.add(idx["name"])
            src_idx_map[idx["name"]] = idx
    for tbl, idxs in tgt_idx.items():
        for idx in idxs:
            tgt_idx_names.add(idx["name"])
            tgt_idx_map[idx["name"]] = idx

    for iname in sorted(src_idx_names - tgt_idx_names):
        diff["indexes_to_add"].append(src_idx_map[iname])
    for iname in sorted(tgt_idx_names - src_idx_names):
        diff["indexes_to_drop"].append(tgt_idx_map[iname])

    total = (len(diff["tables_to_add"]) + len(diff["tables_to_drop"]) +
             len(diff["columns_to_add"]) + len(diff["columns_to_drop"]) +
             len(diff["columns_to_alter"]) + len(diff["indexes_to_add"]) + len(diff["indexes_to_drop"]))
    diff["summary"] = f"{total} changes detected" if total else "Schemas are identical"
    return diff


def _generate_alter_script(diff: dict) -> str:
    """Generate ALTER SQL from diff."""
