    lines = ["-- Migration Script (AUTO-GENERATED)", f"-- Generated at {datetime.now().isoformat()}", ""]

    for item in diff.get("tables_to_add", []):
        tbl = item["table"]
        cols_sql = []
        for cname, cinfo in item["columns"].items():
            col_def = f'    "{cname}" {cinfo["type"]}'
            if not cinfo.get("nullable", True):
                col_def += " NOT NULL"
            if cinfo.get("default"):
                col_def += f" DEFAULT {cinfo['default']}"
            cols_sql.append(col_def)
        lines.append(f'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "{tbl}" (')
        lines.append(",\n".join(cols_sql))
        lines.append(");")
        lines.append("")

    for tbl in diff.get("tables_to_drop", []):
        lines.append(f'DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "{tbl}" CASCADE;')

    for item in diff.get("columns_to_add", []):
        col_def = f'{item["type"]}'
        if not item.get("nullable", True):
            col_def += " NOT NULL"
        if item.get("default"):
            col_def += f" DEFAULT {item['default']}"
        lines.append(f'ALTER TABLE "{item["table"]}" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "{item["column"]}" {col_def};')

    for item in diff.get("columns_to_drop", []):
        lines.append(f'ALTER TABLE "{item["table"]}" DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS "{item["column"]}";')

    for item in diff.get("columns_to_alter", []):
        lines.append(f'ALTER TABLE "{item["table"]}" ALTER COLUMN "{item["column"]}" TYPE {item["to_type"]};')
        if item.get("to_nullable") and not item.get("from_nullable"):
            lines.append(f'ALTER TABLE "{item["table"]}" ALTER COLUMN "{item["column"]}" DROP NOT NULL;')
        elif not item.get("to_nullable") and item.get("from_nullable"):
            lines.append(f'ALTER TABLE "{item["table"]}" ALTER COLUMN "{item["column"]}" SET NOT NULL;')

    for idx in diff.get("indexes_to_add", []):
        lines.append(f'{idx["definition"]};')

    for idx in diff.get("indexes_to_drop", []):
        lines.append(f'DROP INDEX IF EXISTS "{idx["name"]}";')

    return "\n".join(lines)


def _generate_rollback_script(diff: dict) -> str:
    """Generate rollback (reverse) SQL from diff."""
    lines = ["-- Rollback Script (AUTO-GENERATED)", f"-- Generated at {datetime.now().isoformat()}", ""]

    # Reverse: drop tables that were added
    for item in diff.get("tables_to_add", []):
        lines.append(f'DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "{item["table"]}" CASCADE;')

    # Reverse: recreate tables that were dropped (we don't have the full schema, so just note)
    for tbl in diff.get("tables_to_drop", []):
        lines.append(f'-- TODO: Recreate table "{tbl}" (original schema not captured)')

    # Reverse: drop columns that were added
    for item in diff.get("columns_to_add", []):
        lines.append(f'ALTER TABLE "{item["table"]}" DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS "{item["column"]}";')

    # Reverse: re-add columns that were dropped (minimal)
    for item in diff.get("columns_to_drop", []):
        lines.append(f'-- TODO: Re-add column "{item["column"]}" to "{item["table"]}"')

    # Reverse: alter columns back
    for item in diff.get("columns_to_alter", []):
        lines.append(f'ALTER TABLE "{item["table"]}" ALTER COLUMN "{item["column"]}" TYPE {item["from_type"]};')

    for idx in diff.get("indexes_to_add", []):
        lines.append(f'DROP INDEX IF EXISTS "{idx["name"]}";')

    for idx in diff.get("indexes_to_drop", []):
        lines.append(f'{idx.get("definition", "-- re-create index " + idx["name"])};')

    return "\n".join(lines)


# ── POST /api/migrate/diff — compare two database schemas ───────────
@app.post("/api/migrate/diff")
async def migrate_diff(request: Request, body: dict = Body(...)):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    try:
        source_db = body.get("source_db", "")
        target_db = body.get("target_db", "")
        source_db_type = body.get("source_db_type", "cloudsql")
        target_db_type = body.get("target_db_type", "cloudsql")

        if not source_db or not target_db:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="source_db and target_db are required")

        source_schema = _get_schema_info(source_db, source_db_type)
        target_schema = _get_schema_info(target_db, target_db_type)
        diff = _compute_schema_diff(source_schema, target_schema)

        return {
            "success": True,
            "source_db": source_db,
            "target_db": target_db,
            "source_table_count": len(source_schema.get("tables", {})),
            "target_table_count": len(target_schema.get("tables", {})),
            "diff": diff,
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


# ── POST /api/migrate/generate — generate scripts from diff ─────────
@app.post("/api/migrate/generate")
async def migrate_generate(request: Request, body: dict = Body(...)):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        name = body.get("name", "Migration " + datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"))
        source_db = body.get("source_db", "")
        target_db = body.get("target_db", "")
        source_db_type = body.get("source_db_type", "cloudsql")
        target_db_type = body.get("target_db_type", "cloudsql")
        diff = body.get("diff", {})

        if not diff:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="diff data is required — run /api/migrate/diff first")

        alter_script = _generate_alter_script(diff)
        rollback_script = _generate_rollback_script(diff)

        mig_id = f"mig_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"

        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {MIGRATIONS_TABLE} (id, name, source_db, target_db, source_db_type, target_db_type, diff_json, alter_script, rollback_script, user_id)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
        """, (mig_id, name, source_db, target_db, source_db_type, target_db_type, json.dumps(diff), alter_script, rollback_script, user_id))
        connection.commit()

        _log_audit(user_id, "migration_generate", details=f"Generated migration '{name}' ({source_db} → {target_db})")
        return {"success": True, "id": mig_id, "alter_script": alter_script, "rollback_script": rollback_script}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── GET /api/migrate/history — list past migrations ──────────────────
@app.get("/api/migrate/history")
async def migrate_history(request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {MIGRATIONS_TABLE} WHERE user_id = %s ORDER BY created_at DESC", (user_id,))
        migrations = cursor.fetchall()
        for m in migrations:
            for k, v in m.items():
                if isinstance(v, datetime):
                    m[k] = v.isoformat()
            if isinstance(m.get("diff_json"), str):
                try:
                    m["diff_json"] = json.loads(m["diff_json"])
                except Exception:
                    pass
        return {"success": True, "migrations": migrations}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── GET /api/migrate/{mig_id} — get migration details ───────────────
@app.get("/api/migrate/{mig_id}")
async def get_migration(mig_id: str, request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {MIGRATIONS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND user_id = %s", (mig_id, user_id))
        mig = cursor.fetchone()
        if not mig:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Migration not found")
        for k, v in mig.items():
            if isinstance(v, datetime):
                mig[k] = v.isoformat()
        if isinstance(mig.get("diff_json"), str):
            try:
                mig["diff_json"] = json.loads(mig["diff_json"])
            except Exception:
                pass
        return {"success": True, "migration": mig}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── POST /api/migrate/execute — execute migration on target DB ───────
@app.post("/api/migrate/execute")
async def execute_migration(request: Request, body: dict = Body(...)):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    if not _is_super_admin(user_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Only super admin can execute migrations")
    connection = None
    target_conn = None
    try:
        mig_id = body.get("id", "")
        if not mig_id:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Migration id is required")

        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {MIGRATIONS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND user_id = %s", (mig_id, user_id))
        mig = cursor.fetchone()
        if not mig:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Migration not found")
        if mig.get("status") == "executed":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Migration already executed")

        alter_script = mig.get("alter_script", "")
        target_db = mig.get("target_db", "")
        target_db_type = mig.get("target_db_type", "cloudsql")

        if not alter_script.strip():
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No alter script to execute")

        execution_log = []
        statements = [s.strip() for s in alter_script.split(";") if s.strip() and not s.strip().startswith("--")]

        if target_db_type == "bigquery":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="BigQuery migrations must be executed manually")

        target_conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database=target_db, user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        tc = target_conn.cursor()

        for stmt in statements:
            try:
                tc.execute(stmt)
                execution_log.append(f"OK: {stmt[:100]}...")
            except Exception as ex:
                execution_log.append(f"ERROR: {stmt[:100]}... → {str(ex)}")
                target_conn.rollback()
                # Update status to failed
                cursor2 = connection.cursor()
                cursor2.execute(f"UPDATE {MIGRATIONS_TABLE} SET status = 'failed', execution_log = %s, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = %s",
                               ("\n".join(execution_log), mig_id))
                connection.commit()
                raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Migration failed: {str(ex)}")

        target_conn.commit()

        # Update status to executed
        cursor2 = connection.cursor()
        cursor2.execute(f"UPDATE {MIGRATIONS_TABLE} SET status = 'executed', executed_at = NOW(), execution_log = %s, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = %s",
                       ("\n".join(execution_log), mig_id))
        connection.commit()

        _log_audit(user_id, "migration_execute", details=f"Executed migration {mig_id} on {target_db}")
        return {"success": True, "status": "executed", "log": execution_log}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if target_conn:
            target_conn.close()
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── POST /api/migrate/rollback — execute rollback script ─────────────
@app.post("/api/migrate/rollback")
async def rollback_migration(request: Request, body: dict = Body(...)):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    if not _is_super_admin(user_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Only super admin can execute rollbacks")
    connection = None
    target_conn = None
    try:
        mig_id = body.get("id", "")
        if not mig_id:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Migration id is required")

        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {MIGRATIONS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND user_id = %s", (mig_id, user_id))
        mig = cursor.fetchone()
        if not mig:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Migration not found")

        rollback_script = mig.get("rollback_script", "")
        target_db = mig.get("target_db", "")
        target_db_type = mig.get("target_db_type", "cloudsql")

        if not rollback_script.strip():
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No rollback script available")
        if target_db_type == "bigquery":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="BigQuery rollbacks must be executed manually")

        target_conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database=target_db, user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        tc = target_conn.cursor()

        log_lines = []
        statements = [s.strip() for s in rollback_script.split(";") if s.strip() and not s.strip().startswith("--")]
        for stmt in statements:
            try:
                tc.execute(stmt)
                log_lines.append(f"OK: {stmt[:100]}...")
            except Exception as ex:
                log_lines.append(f"ERROR: {stmt[:100]}... → {str(ex)}")

        target_conn.commit()

        # Update status
        cursor2 = connection.cursor()
        cursor2.execute(f"UPDATE {MIGRATIONS_TABLE} SET status = 'rolled_back', execution_log = %s, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = %s",
                       ("\n".join(log_lines), mig_id))
        connection.commit()

        _log_audit(user_id, "migration_rollback", details=f"Rolled back migration {mig_id}")
        return {"success": True, "status": "rolled_back", "log": log_lines}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if target_conn:
            target_conn.close()
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ── DELETE /api/migrate/{mig_id} — delete migration record ──────────
@app.delete("/api/migrate/{mig_id}")
async def delete_migration(mig_id: str, request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"DELETE FROM {MIGRATIONS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND user_id = %s", (mig_id, user_id))
        connection.commit()
        return {"success": True, "deleted": cursor.rowcount}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# ██  FILE IMPORT  –  Direct CSV / SQL file upload to Database
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

@app.post("/api/import/file")
async def import_file_to_db(
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    db_type: str = Form("cloudsql"),
    database: str = Form(...),
    table: str = Form(""),
    schema_name: str = Form("public"),
    operation: str = Form("insert"),
    pk_column: str = Form(""),
    delimiter: str = Form(","),
    has_header: str = Form("true"),
    mapping: str = Form("[]"),
    vz_id: str = Header(None, alias="vz-id"),
):
    """Upload a CSV or SQL file and import data into a database table.

    CSV files:  Parsed into rows and inserted/upserted into the target table.
    SQL files:  Each statement (split by ';') is executed sequentially.
    """
    import json as _json

    if not database:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="database is required")

    filename = file.filename or ""
    raw = await file.read()
    content = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")

    if not content.strip():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Uploaded file is empty")

    ext = filename.rsplit(".", 1)[-1].lower() if "." in filename else ""
    file_type = "sql" if ext == "sql" else "csv"

    has_header_bool = has_header.lower() in ("true", "1", "yes")
    try:
        col_mapping = _json.loads(mapping)
    except Exception:
        col_mapping = []

    step_cfg = {
        "file_type": file_type,
        "file_source": "inline",
        "file_content": content,
        "db_type": db_type,
        "database": database,
        "table": table,
        "schema_name": schema_name,
        "operation": operation,
        "pk_column": pk_column,
        "delimiter": delimiter,
        "has_header": has_header_bool,
        "mapping": col_mapping,
    }

    result = _exec_file_import(step_cfg, context={})

    if result.get("status") == "failed":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=result.get("error", "Import failed"))

    return result


@app.post("/api/import/preview")
async def import_file_preview(
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    delimiter: str = Form(","),
    has_header: str = Form("true"),
    max_rows: str = Form("20"),
):
    """Preview CSV file contents — returns detected columns and first N rows."""
    raw = await file.read()
    content = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")

    if not content.strip():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Uploaded file is empty")

    has_header_bool = has_header.lower() in ("true", "1", "yes")
    max_n = min(int(max_rows or "20"), 200)

    columns, rows = _parse_csv_content(content, delimiter=delimiter, has_header=has_header_bool)
    return {
        "columns": columns,
        "total_rows": len(rows),
        "preview_rows": rows[:max_n],
    }


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  AI SQL COPILOT — Natural Language → SQL Generation
#  Phase 1: Schema Metadata Catalog (auto-refresh from INFORMATION_SCHEMA)
#  Phase 2: Two-stage NL→SQL (table selection → SQL generation)
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

AI_CATALOG_TABLE = "sql_editor_schema_catalog"
AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE = "sql_editor_nl_query_log"
EMBEDDING_MODEL_NAME = "text-embedding-005"

# In-memory schema cache: {cache_key: {"tables": [...], "expires_at": float}}
_schema_cache: Dict[str, dict] = {}
_SCHEMA_CACHE_TTL = 300  # 5 minutes default

# Embedding model (lazy-loaded)
_embedding_model = None

def _get_embedding_model():
    global _embedding_model
    if _embedding_model is None:
        try:
            _embedding_model = TextEmbeddingModel.from_pretrained(EMBEDDING_MODEL_NAME)
            logger.info(f"Embedding model {EMBEDDING_MODEL_NAME} loaded")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load embedding model: {e}")
    return _embedding_model


def _embed_text(text: str) -> list:
    """Generate embedding for a text string. Returns list of floats."""
    model = _get_embedding_model()
    if not model:
        return []
    try:
        embeddings = model.get_embeddings([text])
        return embeddings[0].values if embeddings else []
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Embedding failed: {e}")
        return []


def _cosine_similarity(a: list, b: list) -> float:
    """Compute cosine similarity between two vectors."""
    if not a or not b or len(a) != len(b):
        return 0.0
    dot = sum(x * y for x, y in zip(a, b))
    norm_a = sum(x * x for x in a) ** 0.5
    norm_b = sum(x * x for x in b) ** 0.5
    if norm_a == 0 or norm_b == 0:
        return 0.0
    return dot / (norm_a * norm_b)


def _ensure_ai_copilot_tables():
    """Create tables for AI SQL Copilot — schema catalog + NL query log."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {AI_CATALOG_TABLE} (
                id              SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                server_id       VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'default',
                db_type         VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT 'cloudsql',
                database_name   VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                schema_name     VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT 'public',
                table_name      VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                column_name     VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                data_type       VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                is_nullable     VARCHAR(10)  DEFAULT 'YES',
                is_pk           BOOLEAN      DEFAULT FALSE,
                description     TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                sample_values   TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                ordinal_position INTEGER     DEFAULT 0,
                last_refreshed  TIMESTAMP    DEFAULT NOW(),
                UNIQUE(server_id, database_name, schema_name, table_name, column_name)
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_catalog_db_table
            ON {AI_CATALOG_TABLE} (server_id, database_name, table_name)
        """)
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sql_editor_table_descriptions (
                id              SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                server_id       VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'default',
                database_name   VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                schema_name     VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT 'public',
                table_name      VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                description     TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                row_count_approx BIGINT      DEFAULT 0,
                last_refreshed  TIMESTAMP    DEFAULT NOW(),
                UNIQUE(server_id, database_name, schema_name, table_name)
            )
        """)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE} (
                id              SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                user_id         VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT '',
                server_id       VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'default',
                db_type         VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT 'cloudsql',
                database_name   VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                nl_question     TEXT         NOT NULL,
                generated_sql   TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                tables_used     TEXT         DEFAULT '[]',
                was_executed    BOOLEAN      DEFAULT FALSE,
                was_edited      BOOLEAN      DEFAULT FALSE,
                edited_sql      TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                feedback        VARCHAR(20)  DEFAULT '',
                question_embedding TEXT      DEFAULT '',
                result_summary  TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                created_at      TIMESTAMP    DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        # Add embedding column to existing tables (idempotent)
        try:
            cursor.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE} ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS question_embedding TEXT DEFAULT ''")
            cursor.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE} ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS result_summary TEXT DEFAULT ''")
        except Exception:
            pass
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_nl_log_db
            ON {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE} (database_name, created_at DESC)
        """)
        connection.commit()
        logger.info("AI Copilot tables ensured (catalog + query log)")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to create AI Copilot tables: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


_ensure_ai_copilot_tables()


# ── Schema Catalog: Refresh from INFORMATION_SCHEMA ──────────────────

def _refresh_schema_catalog(database: str, db_type: str = "cloudsql",
                            server_id: str = "default", schema_name: str = "public") -> dict:
    """Query INFORMATION_SCHEMA and update the catalog for a given database."""
    src_conn = None
    pg_conn = None
    try:
        is_mssql = False
        if server_id and server_id not in ("default", "cloudsql", "bigquery"):
            src_conn = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
            srv = _server_store.get(server_id, {})
            is_mssql = srv.get("db_type") == "mssql"
        elif db_type == "bigquery":
            pass
        else:
            src_conn = get_db_connection(database)

        tables_info = []

        if db_type == "bigquery":
            bq_query = f"""
                SELECT table_name, column_name, data_type, is_nullable, ordinal_position
                FROM `{database}.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS`
                ORDER BY table_name, ordinal_position
            """
            rows = list(bq_client.query(bq_query).result())
            current_table = None
            current_cols = []
            for r in rows:
                if r.table_name != current_table:
                    if current_table:
                        tables_info.append({"table_name": current_table, "schema": database, "columns": current_cols})
                    current_table = r.table_name
                    current_cols = []
                current_cols.append({
                    "name": r.column_name, "type": r.data_type,
                    "nullable": r.is_nullable, "is_pk": False, "ordinal": r.ordinal_position
                })
            if current_table:
                tables_info.append({"table_name": current_table, "schema": database, "columns": current_cols})

        elif is_mssql:
            cursor = src_conn.cursor()
            sch = schema_name if schema_name and schema_name != 'public' else 'dbo'
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT c.TABLE_NAME, c.COLUMN_NAME, c.DATA_TYPE, c.IS_NULLABLE, c.ORDINAL_POSITION,
                       CASE WHEN pk.COLUMN_NAME IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS IS_PK
                FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS c
                LEFT JOIN (
                    SELECT ku.TABLE_NAME, ku.COLUMN_NAME
                    FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS tc
                    JOIN INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE ku
                      ON tc.CONSTRAINT_NAME = ku.CONSTRAINT_NAME
                    WHERE tc.CONSTRAINT_TYPE = 'PRIMARY KEY'
                ) pk ON c.TABLE_NAME = pk.TABLE_NAME AND c.COLUMN_NAME = pk.COLUMN_NAME
                WHERE c.TABLE_SCHEMA = %s
                ORDER BY c.TABLE_NAME, c.ORDINAL_POSITION
            """, (sch,))
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            current_table = None
            current_cols = []
            for r in rows:
                tbl = r["TABLE_NAME"]
                if tbl != current_table:
                    if current_table:
                        tables_info.append({"table_name": current_table, "schema": sch, "columns": current_cols})
                    current_table = tbl
                    current_cols = []
                current_cols.append({
                    "name": r["COLUMN_NAME"], "type": r["DATA_TYPE"],
                    "nullable": r["IS_NULLABLE"], "is_pk": bool(r["IS_PK"]), "ordinal": r["ORDINAL_POSITION"]
                })
            if current_table:
                tables_info.append({"table_name": current_table, "schema": sch, "columns": current_cols})
            cursor.close()

        else:
            cursor = src_conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT c.table_name, c.column_name, c.data_type, c.is_nullable, c.ordinal_position,
                       CASE WHEN pk.column_name IS NOT NULL THEN true ELSE false END AS is_pk
                FROM information_schema.columns c
                LEFT JOIN (
                    SELECT a.attname AS column_name, i.indrelid::regclass::text AS table_name
                    FROM pg_index i
                    JOIN pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid = i.indrelid AND a.attnum = ANY(i.indkey)
                    WHERE i.indisprimary
                ) pk ON c.table_name = pk.table_name AND c.column_name = pk.column_name
                WHERE c.table_schema = %s
                  AND c.table_name NOT LIKE 'pg_%%'
                  AND c.table_name NOT LIKE 'sql_editor_%%'
                ORDER BY c.table_name, c.ordinal_position
            """, (schema_name,))
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            current_table = None
            current_cols = []
            for r in rows:
                tbl = r["table_name"]
                if tbl != current_table:
                    if current_table:
                        tables_info.append({"table_name": current_table, "schema": schema_name, "columns": current_cols})
                    current_table = tbl
                    current_cols = []
                current_cols.append({
                    "name": r["column_name"], "type": r["data_type"],
                    "nullable": r["is_nullable"], "is_pk": bool(r.get("is_pk")), "ordinal": r["ordinal_position"]
                })
            if current_table:
                tables_info.append({"table_name": current_table, "schema": schema_name, "columns": current_cols})
            cursor.close()

        # Fetch sample values (first 3 rows, top 10 columns per table)
        sample_map = {}
        for tinfo in tables_info[:100]:
            tbl = tinfo["table_name"]
            try:
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    sq = f"SELECT * FROM `{database}.{tbl}` LIMIT 3"
                    sample_rows = [dict(r) for r in bq_client.query(sq).result()]
                elif is_mssql:
                    sch = tinfo["schema"]
                    sc = src_conn.cursor()
                    sc.execute(f"SELECT TOP 3 * FROM [{sch}].[{tbl}]")
                    sample_rows = sc.fetchall()
                    sc.close()
                else:
                    sc = src_conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
                    sc.execute(f'SELECT * FROM "{tinfo["schema"]}"."{tbl}" LIMIT 3')
                    sample_rows = [dict(r) for r in sc.fetchall()]
                    sc.close()
                col_samples = {}
                for col_info in tinfo["columns"][:10]:
                    cname = col_info["name"]
                    vals = []
                    for row in sample_rows:
                        v = row.get(cname)
                        if v is not None:
                            sv = str(v)[:50]
                            if sv not in vals:
                                vals.append(sv)
                    col_samples[cname] = ", ".join(vals[:3])
                sample_map[tbl] = col_samples
            except Exception:
                sample_map[tbl] = {}

        # Save to PostgreSQL catalog
        pg_conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        pg_cursor = pg_conn.cursor()
        pg_cursor.execute(
            f"DELETE FROM {AI_CATALOG_TABLE} WHERE server_id = %s AND database_name = %s",
            (server_id, database)
        )
        pg_cursor.execute(
            "DELETE FROM sql_editor_table_descriptions WHERE server_id = %s AND database_name = %s",
            (server_id, database)
        )
        total_cols = 0
        for tinfo in tables_info:
            tbl = tinfo["table_name"]
            sch = tinfo["schema"]
            for col in tinfo["columns"]:
                samples = sample_map.get(tbl, {}).get(col["name"], "")
                pg_cursor.execute(f"""
                    INSERT INTO {AI_CATALOG_TABLE}
                        (server_id, db_type, database_name, schema_name, table_name,
                         column_name, data_type, is_nullable, is_pk, sample_values, ordinal_position, last_refreshed)
                    VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, NOW())
                """, (server_id, db_type, database, sch, tbl,
                      col["name"], col["type"], col["nullable"], col["is_pk"],
                      samples, col["ordinal"]))
                total_cols += 1
            pg_cursor.execute("""
                INSERT INTO sql_editor_table_descriptions
                    (server_id, database_name, schema_name, table_name, description, row_count_approx, last_refreshed)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, '', 0, NOW())
            """, (server_id, database, sch, tbl))
        pg_conn.commit()
        pg_cursor.close()

        cache_key = f"{server_id}:{database}"
        _schema_cache[cache_key] = {
            "tables": tables_info, "samples": sample_map,
            "expires_at": _time.time() + _SCHEMA_CACHE_TTL,
        }
        return {"tables": len(tables_info), "columns": total_cols, "database": database, "server_id": server_id}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Schema catalog refresh failed for {database}: {e}")
        raise
    finally:
        if src_conn:
            try: src_conn.close()
            except: pass
        if pg_conn:
            try: pg_conn.close()
            except: pass


def _get_catalog_for_db(database: str, server_id: str = "default") -> dict:
    """Get schema catalog from cache or PostgreSQL."""
    cache_key = f"{server_id}:{database}"
    cached = _schema_cache.get(cache_key)
    if cached and cached["expires_at"] > _time.time():
        return cached
    pg_conn = None
    try:
        pg_conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = pg_conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT table_name, schema_name, column_name, data_type, is_nullable, is_pk, sample_values, ordinal_position
            FROM {AI_CATALOG_TABLE}
            WHERE server_id = %s AND database_name = %s
            ORDER BY table_name, ordinal_position
        """, (server_id, database))
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT table_name, schema_name, description, row_count_approx
            FROM sql_editor_table_descriptions
            WHERE server_id = %s AND database_name = %s
        """, (server_id, database))
        desc_map = {r["table_name"]: r for r in cursor.fetchall()}
        tables = []
        samples = {}
        current_table = None
        current_cols = []
        for r in rows:
            tbl = r["table_name"]
            if tbl != current_table:
                if current_table:
                    desc_info = desc_map.get(current_table, {})
                    tables.append({
                        "table_name": current_table,
                        "schema": current_cols[0]["schema"] if current_cols else "public",
                        "description": desc_info.get("description", ""),
                        "row_count_approx": desc_info.get("row_count_approx", 0),
                        "columns": [{"name": c["name"], "type": c["type"], "nullable": c["nullable"], "is_pk": c["is_pk"]} for c in current_cols],
                    })
                current_table = tbl
                current_cols = []
            sv = r.get("sample_values", "")
            current_cols.append({"name": r["column_name"], "type": r["data_type"], "nullable": r["is_nullable"], "is_pk": r["is_pk"], "schema": r["schema_name"]})
            if sv:
                samples.setdefault(tbl, {})[r["column_name"]] = sv
        if current_table:
            desc_info = desc_map.get(current_table, {})
            tables.append({
                "table_name": current_table,
                "schema": current_cols[0]["schema"] if current_cols else "public",
                "description": desc_info.get("description", ""),
                "row_count_approx": desc_info.get("row_count_approx", 0),
                "columns": [{"name": c["name"], "type": c["type"], "nullable": c["nullable"], "is_pk": c["is_pk"]} for c in current_cols],
            })
        cursor.close()
        result = {"tables": tables, "samples": samples, "expires_at": _time.time() + _SCHEMA_CACHE_TTL}
        _schema_cache[cache_key] = result
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to load catalog for {database}: {e}")
        return {"tables": [], "samples": {}}
    finally:
        if pg_conn:
            pg_conn.close()


def _generate_table_descriptions(database: str, server_id: str = "default") -> int:
    """Use Gemini to auto-generate short descriptions for tables without one."""
    catalog = _get_catalog_for_db(database, server_id)
    tables_needing_desc = [t for t in catalog.get("tables", []) if not t.get("description")]
    if not tables_needing_desc:
        return 0
    table_summaries = []
    for t in tables_needing_desc[:50]:
        cols = ", ".join(f"{c['name']} ({c['type']})" for c in t["columns"][:15])
        samps = catalog.get("samples", {}).get(t["table_name"], {})
        sample_str = "; ".join(f"{k}: {v}" for k, v in list(samps.items())[:5])
        table_summaries.append(f"- {t['table_name']}: columns=[{cols}]" + (f" samples=[{sample_str}]" if sample_str else ""))

    prompt = f"""For each database table below, write a concise 1-line description (max 100 chars) of what the table likely stores.
Return ONLY a JSON object: {{"table_name": "description", ...}}

Tables in database "{database}":
{chr(10).join(table_summaries)}"""

    try:
        model = GenerativeModel(GEMINI_MODEL)
        response = model.generate_content(prompt)
        text = response.text.strip()
        if "```" in text:
            text = text.split("```")[1]
            if text.startswith("json"):
                text = text[4:]
            text = text.strip()
        descriptions = json.loads(text)
        pg_conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10)
        cursor = pg_conn.cursor()
        count = 0
        for tbl_name, desc in descriptions.items():
            cursor.execute("""
                UPDATE sql_editor_table_descriptions
                SET description = %s, last_refreshed = NOW()
                WHERE server_id = %s AND database_name = %s AND table_name = %s AND (description = '' OR description IS NULL)
            """, (str(desc)[:200], server_id, database, tbl_name))
            count += cursor.rowcount
        pg_conn.commit()
        cursor.close()
        pg_conn.close()
        _schema_cache.pop(f"{server_id}:{database}", None)
        return count
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Auto-describe tables failed: {e}")
        return 0


# ── NL→SQL Two-Stage Generation ──────────────────────────────────────

def _find_similar_examples(question: str, database: str, top_k: int = 5) -> list:
    """Find similar past queries using embedding cosine similarity."""
    q_emb = _embed_text(question)
    if not q_emb:
        return []
    pg_conn = None
    try:
        pg_conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cursor = pg_conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT nl_question, COALESCE(NULLIF(edited_sql, ''), generated_sql) AS sql,
                   question_embedding, feedback, was_edited
            FROM {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE}
            WHERE database_name = %s
              AND question_embedding != ''
              AND (was_executed = true OR was_edited = true OR feedback = 'good')
            ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 100
        """, (database,))
        candidates = cursor.fetchall()
        cursor.close()
        if not candidates:
            return []
        scored = []
        for c in candidates:
            try:
                c_emb = json.loads(c["question_embedding"])
                sim = _cosine_similarity(q_emb, c_emb)
                if sim > 0.5:  # threshold
                    # Boost score for user-edited and verified examples (gold training data)
                    bonus = 0.0
                    if c.get("was_edited"):
                        bonus += 0.1   # user corrected this SQL — high value
                    if c.get("feedback") == "good":
                        bonus += 0.05  # user verified this SQL
                    elif c.get("feedback") == "edited":
                        bonus += 0.08  # user edited and ran
                    effective_score = min(sim + bonus, 1.0)
                    scored.append({"question": c["nl_question"], "sql": c["sql"],
                                   "score": effective_score, "feedback": c.get("feedback", ""),
                                   "was_edited": c.get("was_edited", False)})
            except Exception:
                pass
        scored.sort(key=lambda x: -x["score"])
        return scored[:top_k]
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Embedding search failed: {e}")
        return []
    finally:
        if pg_conn:
            pg_conn.close()


def _nl_to_sql(question: str, database: str, db_type: str = "cloudsql",
               server_id: str = "default", schema_name: str = "public",
               conversation_history: list = None, action_mode: bool = False) -> dict:
    """Two-stage NL→SQL: 1) select tables, 2) generate SQL with conversation context."""
    # Auto-correct schema_name per database type
    if db_type == "mssql" and schema_name == "public":
        schema_name = "dbo"
    elif db_type == "bigquery":
        schema_name = database  # In BigQuery, schema = dataset name

    catalog = _get_catalog_for_db(database, server_id)
    tables = catalog.get("tables", [])
    if not tables:
        return {"error": "No schema catalog found. Please refresh the catalog first.", "sql": None, "tables_used": []}

    # Stage 1: Table Selection
    compact_catalog = []
    for t in tables:
        col_summary = ", ".join(c["name"] for c in t["columns"][:12])
        if len(t["columns"]) > 12:
            col_summary += f", ... (+{len(t['columns'])-12} more)"
        desc = t.get("description", "")
        line = f"- {t['table_name']}: {desc}" if desc else f"- {t['table_name']}"
        line += f"  [{col_summary}]"
        compact_catalog.append(line)

    stage1_prompt = f"""You are a database expert. Given a user's question and available tables, identify which tables are needed.

Database: {database} ({db_type})
Available tables:
{chr(10).join(compact_catalog)}
"""
    # Extract tables from conversation history for follow-up fallback
    prev_tables = []
    if conversation_history:
        conv_lines = []
        for h in conversation_history[-5:]:
            if h.get("role") == "user":
                conv_lines.append(f'User: "{h.get("content", "")}"')
            elif h.get("tables_used"):
                prev_tables = h["tables_used"]  # keep latest
                conv_lines.append(f'Used tables: {", ".join(h["tables_used"])}')
            if h.get("sql"):
                conv_lines.append(f'SQL: {h["sql"][:150]}')
            if h.get("result_summary"):
                conv_lines.append(f'Result: {h["result_summary"][:100]}')
        if conv_lines:
            stage1_prompt += "\nConversation so far:\n" + "\n".join(conv_lines) + "\n"
            stage1_prompt += "\nIMPORTANT: If this is a follow-up question (e.g. 'is that correct?', 'how many of those...', 'filter by...', 'show me more'), reuse the tables from the previous query.\n"

    stage1_prompt += f"""
User question: "{question}"

Return ONLY a JSON array of table names needed. Example: ["users", "orders"]
If this is clearly a follow-up to the conversation above, return the same tables used previously.
If no tables match at all, return [].
"""
    model = GenerativeModel(GEMINI_MODEL)
    try:
        resp1 = model.generate_content(stage1_prompt)
        text1 = resp1.text.strip()
        if "```" in text1:
            text1 = text1.split("```")[1]
            if text1.startswith("json"):
                text1 = text1[4:]
            text1 = text1.strip()
        selected_tables = json.loads(text1)
        if not isinstance(selected_tables, list):
            selected_tables = []
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Stage 1 (table selection) failed: {e}")
        selected_tables = [t["table_name"] for t in tables[:5]]

    if not selected_tables:
        # Fallback: if this is a follow-up question, reuse tables from the last conversation turn
        if prev_tables:
            selected_tables = prev_tables
            logger.info(f"Follow-up detected — reusing prior tables: {selected_tables}")
        else:
            return {"error": "Could not identify relevant tables for your question.", "sql": None, "tables_used": []}

    # Stage 2: SQL Generation
    is_bq = db_type == "bigquery"
    is_mssql = db_type == "mssql"
    if is_bq:
        q_char_open, q_char_close = "`", "`"
    elif is_mssql:
        q_char_open, q_char_close = "[", "]"
    else:
        q_char_open, q_char_close = '"', '"'
    schema_details = []
    for tbl_name in selected_tables:
        tbl_info = next((t for t in tables if t["table_name"] == tbl_name), None)
        if not tbl_info:
            continue
        cols = []
        for c in tbl_info["columns"]:
            pk_marker = " PK" if c.get("is_pk") else ""
            nullable = " NOT NULL" if c.get("nullable") == "NO" else ""
            cols.append(f"  {c['name']} {c['type']}{pk_marker}{nullable}")
        samps = catalog.get("samples", {}).get(tbl_name, {})
        sample_lines = [f"  -- {cn}: {sv}" for cn, sv in list(samps.items())[:8] if sv]
        sch = tbl_info.get("schema", schema_name)
        block = f'TABLE {q_char_open}{sch}{q_char_close}.{q_char_open}{tbl_name}{q_char_close} (\n' + ",\n".join(cols) + "\n)"
        if sample_lines:
            block += "\nSample values:\n" + "\n".join(sample_lines)
        schema_details.append(block)

    # Few-shot examples — Embedding-based similarity search first, fallback to recent
    few_shot = ""
    try:
        similar = _find_similar_examples(question, database, top_k=5)
        if similar:
            lines = []
            for ex in similar:
                if ex.get("was_edited"):
                    prefix = "[user-corrected] "
                elif ex.get("feedback") == "good":
                    prefix = "[verified] "
                elif ex.get("feedback") == "edited":
                    prefix = "[user-corrected] "
                else:
                    prefix = ""
                lines.append(f'{prefix}Q: "{ex["question"]}"\nSQL: {ex["sql"]}')
            few_shot = "\n\nSimilar past queries (ranked by relevance):\n" + "\n\n".join(lines)
        else:
            # Fallback: recent successful queries
            pg_conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
            cur = pg_conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
            cur.execute(f"""
                SELECT nl_question, COALESCE(edited_sql, generated_sql) AS sql
                FROM {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE}
                WHERE database_name = %s AND (was_executed = true OR was_edited = true)
                ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 5
            """, (database,))
            examples = cur.fetchall()
            cur.close()
            pg_conn.close()
            if examples:
                lines = [f'Q: "{ex["nl_question"]}"\nSQL: {ex["sql"]}' for ex in examples]
                few_shot = "\n\nRecent successful queries for this database:\n" + "\n\n".join(lines)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Build conversation context for Stage 2
    conv_context = ""
    if conversation_history:
        conv_lines = []
        for h in conversation_history[-6:]:
            if h.get("role") == "user":
                conv_lines.append(f'User: "{h.get("content", "")}"')
            elif h.get("sql"):
                sql_preview = h["sql"][:200]
                summary_text = h.get("result_summary", "")
                conv_lines.append(f'SQL: {sql_preview}')
                if summary_text:
                    conv_lines.append(f'Result: {summary_text[:150]}')
        if conv_lines:
            conv_context = "\n\nConversation history (for context — the user may refer to previous results):\n" + "\n".join(conv_lines)

    dialect = "PostgreSQL" if db_type == "cloudsql" else ("T-SQL (MS SQL Server)" if db_type == "mssql" else "BigQuery Standard SQL")

    # Fetch business glossary context (column hints / domain terms)
    glossary_context = _get_glossary_context(database, server_id)

    # Fetch JOIN relationship hints for selected tables
    join_context = _get_join_context(database, db_type, server_id, selected_tables, schema_name)

    # Dialect-aware quoting instruction for the prompt
    if is_bq:
        bq_dataset = database  # In BigQuery, the "database" parameter IS the dataset name
        qualify_instruction = f"Always qualify table names with dataset using backticks: `{bq_dataset}.table_name`. NEVER use double quotes for identifiers in BigQuery — double quotes are string literals in BigQuery."
    elif db_type == "mssql":
        qualify_instruction = f'Always qualify table names with schema: [{schema_name}].[table_name]'
    else:
        qualify_instruction = f'Always qualify table names with schema: "{schema_name}"."table_name"'

    # Dialect-aware LIMIT instruction
    if db_type == "mssql":
        limit_instruction = "Use TOP 100 (e.g. SELECT TOP 100 ...) unless the user asks for a specific count or aggregation. Do NOT use LIMIT — MSSQL does not support LIMIT."
        string_instruction = "For string comparisons use LIKE (MSSQL is case-insensitive by default)"
    elif is_bq:
        limit_instruction = "Add LIMIT 100 unless the user asks for a specific count or aggregation"
        string_instruction = "For case-insensitive string comparisons use LOWER(col) LIKE LOWER('%value%')"
    else:
        limit_instruction = "Add LIMIT 100 unless the user asks for a specific count or aggregation"
        string_instruction = "For case-insensitive string comparisons use ILIKE"

    # Action Mode rules
    action_rules = ""
    if action_mode:
        action_rules = """\n- ACTION MODE IS ON: The user wants to MODIFY data (UPDATE, INSERT). Generate UPDATE or INSERT statements as appropriate.
- ABSOLUTELY FORBIDDEN in Action Mode: DELETE, DROP, TRUNCATE, ALTER DROP, RENAME. If the user asks for any destructive operation, return: -- CANNOT_ANSWER: Destructive operations (DELETE, DROP, TRUNCATE) are not allowed in Action Mode.
- For UPDATE queries: ALWAYS include a WHERE clause to target specific rows. Never generate an UPDATE without WHERE.
- For INSERT queries: Generate standard INSERT INTO ... VALUES statements."""
    else:
        action_rules = "\n- Generate only SELECT queries (read-only). Do NOT generate UPDATE, INSERT, DELETE, DROP, or TRUNCATE."

    stage2_prompt = f"""You are an expert {dialect} query generator. Generate a single SQL query to answer the user's question.

RULES:
- Use ONLY the tables and columns listed below
- {qualify_instruction}
- {string_instruction}
- {limit_instruction}
- Return ONLY the SQL query, no explanation, no markdown, no code blocks
- If you cannot answer with the available schema, return: -- CANNOT_ANSWER: <reason>
- If the user refers to previous results (e.g. "how many of those", "filter those by", "break that down"), use the conversation history to understand context and generate a NEW query that addresses the follow-up
- FOLLOW-UP HANDLING: If the user asks a vague follow-up like "is that correct?", "are you sure?", "can you verify?", "check that", or "show me", generate a verification or confirmation query using the same tables/conditions from the previous query in the conversation history. For example, re-run the previous query with additional detail, or provide a count with a breakdown to verify.
- When JOINing tables, use the known relationships listed below. Prefer explicit JOIN ON conditions over WHERE-based joins.{action_rules}

Schema:
{chr(10).join(schema_details)}
{join_context}
{glossary_context}
{few_shot}
{conv_context}

User question: "{question}"

SQL:"""

    try:
        resp2 = model.generate_content(stage2_prompt)
        sql = resp2.text.strip()
        if sql.startswith("```"):
            sql = sql.split("```")[1]
            if sql.startswith("sql"):
                sql = sql[3:]
            sql = sql.strip()
        if sql.endswith("```"):
            sql = sql[:-3].strip()
        cannot_answer = sql.startswith("-- CANNOT_ANSWER")
        return {
            "sql": sql if not cannot_answer else None,
            "tables_used": selected_tables,
            "error": sql.replace("-- CANNOT_ANSWER:", "").strip() if cannot_answer else None,
            "dialect": dialect,
        }
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Stage 2 (SQL generation) failed: {e}")
        return {"error": f"SQL generation failed: {str(e)}", "sql": None, "tables_used": selected_tables}


def _log_nl_query(user_id: str, database: str, db_type: str, server_id: str,
                  question: str, sql: str, tables_used: list, result_summary: str = ""):
    """Log a NL→SQL interaction with embedding for future similarity search."""
    try:
        # Generate embedding for the question (async-safe since called via executor)
        q_emb = _embed_text(question)
        emb_json = json.dumps(q_emb) if q_emb else ""

        pg_conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cursor = pg_conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE}
                (user_id, server_id, db_type, database_name, nl_question, generated_sql, tables_used, question_embedding, result_summary)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
        """, (user_id, server_id, db_type, database, question, sql or "", json.dumps(tables_used), emb_json, result_summary or ""))
        pg_conn.commit()
        cursor.close()
        pg_conn.close()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Failed to log NL query: {e}")


# ── AI Copilot API Endpoints ─────────────────────────────────────────

@app.post("/api/ai/catalog/refresh")
async def refresh_schema_catalog(body: dict = Body(...), request: Request = None):
    """Refresh the schema catalog for a database."""
    database = body.get("database", "")
    db_type = body.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    server_id = body.get("server_id", "default")
    schema_name = body.get("schema_name", "public")
    auto_describe = body.get("auto_describe", True)
    if not database:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="database is required")
    result = await asyncio.to_thread(_refresh_schema_catalog, database, db_type, server_id, schema_name)
    desc_count = 0
    if auto_describe:
        desc_count = await asyncio.to_thread(_generate_table_descriptions, database, server_id)
    return {"success": True, "tables_indexed": result["tables"], "columns_indexed": result["columns"],
            "descriptions_generated": desc_count, "database": database, "server_id": server_id}


@app.get("/api/ai/catalog")
async def get_schema_catalog(database: str = Query(...), server_id: str = Query("default")):
    """Get the cached schema catalog for a database."""
    catalog = _get_catalog_for_db(database, server_id)
    tables = catalog.get("tables", [])
    return {"success": True, "tables": tables, "count": len(tables), "database": database,
            "server_id": server_id, "has_catalog": len(tables) > 0}


@app.post("/api/ai/generate-sql")
async def generate_sql_from_nl(body: dict = Body(...), request: Request = None):
    """Generate SQL from natural language. Core NL→SQL endpoint."""
    question = (body.get("question") or "").strip()
    database = body.get("database", "")
    db_type = body.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    server_id = body.get("server_id", "default")
    schema_name = body.get("schema_name", "public")
    auto_execute = body.get("auto_execute", True)
    action_mode = body.get("action_mode", False)
    conversation_history = body.get("conversation_history", [])
    if not question:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="question is required")
    if not database:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="database is required")
    user_id = _get_user_id(request) if request else ""

    # Auto-refresh catalog if empty
    catalog = _get_catalog_for_db(database, server_id)
    if not catalog.get("tables"):
        await asyncio.to_thread(_refresh_schema_catalog, database, db_type, server_id, schema_name)
        await asyncio.to_thread(_generate_table_descriptions, database, server_id)

    result = await asyncio.to_thread(_nl_to_sql, question, database, db_type, server_id, schema_name, conversation_history, action_mode)
    sql = result.get("sql")
    tables_used = result.get("tables_used", [])

    # ── Action Mode: block destructive SQL at the endpoint level as a safety net ──
    if action_mode and sql:
        sql_upper = sql.strip().upper()
        for forbidden in ("DELETE ", "DELETE\n", "DROP ", "DROP\n", "TRUNCATE ", "TRUNCATE\n"):
            if sql_upper.startswith(forbidden):
                return {"success": True, "sql": sql, "tables_used": tables_used,
                        "error": "Destructive operations (DELETE, DROP, TRUNCATE) are not allowed in Action Mode.",
                        "dialect": result.get("dialect"), "execution": None,
                        "question": question, "summary": None, "chart_config": None,
                        "action_mode": True, "is_mutation": True, "blocked": True}

    # ── Action Mode: detect mutation queries → return without auto-executing ──
    is_mutation = False
    if sql:
        sql_upper = sql.strip().upper()
        is_mutation = sql_upper.startswith("UPDATE ") or sql_upper.startswith("INSERT ")

    if action_mode and is_mutation:
        # Don't auto-execute mutations — return SQL for user review
        return {"success": True, "sql": sql, "tables_used": tables_used,
                "error": result.get("error"), "dialect": result.get("dialect"),
                "execution": None, "question": question,
                "summary": None, "chart_config": None,
                "action_mode": True, "is_mutation": True, "blocked": False}

    # Auto-execute if requested (read-only queries)
    exec_result = None
    if auto_execute and sql and not sql.startswith("--"):
        try:
            if db_type == "bigquery":
                exec_result = execute_bigquery(sql, database, limit=100)
            elif server_id and server_id not in ("default", "cloudsql", "bigquery"):
                conn = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
                cursor = conn.cursor()
                cursor.execute(sql)
                if cursor.description:
                    cols = [d[0] for d in cursor.description]
                    data_rows = cursor.fetchall()
                    if data_rows and isinstance(data_rows[0], dict):
                        exec_result = {"success": True, "data": data_rows[:100], "columns": cols, "row_count": len(data_rows)}
                    else:
                        exec_result = {"success": True, "data": [dict(zip(cols, r)) for r in data_rows[:100]], "columns": cols, "row_count": len(data_rows)}
                conn.close()
            else:
                exec_result = execute_query(database, sql, limit=100)
        except Exception as e:
            exec_result = {"success": False, "error": str(e)}

    # ── AI Summarization + Chart Suggestion ──
    summary = None
    chart_config = None
    if exec_result and exec_result.get("success") and exec_result.get("data"):
        data_rows = exec_result["data"]
        columns = exec_result.get("columns", list(data_rows[0].keys()) if data_rows else [])
        row_count = exec_result.get("row_count", len(data_rows))

        # Build a compact data snapshot for the LLM (max 15 rows)
        preview_rows = data_rows[:15]
        preview_text = ""
        if preview_rows:
            header = " | ".join(str(c) for c in columns[:12])
            preview_text = header + "\n"
            for pr in preview_rows:
                vals = " | ".join(str(pr.get(c, ""))[:40] for c in columns[:12])
                preview_text += vals + "\n"

        # Compute basic stats for numeric columns
        numeric_stats = {}
        for col in columns[:12]:
            vals = []
            for r in data_rows:
                v = r.get(col)
                if v is not None:
                    try:
                        vals.append(float(v))
                    except (ValueError, TypeError):
                        pass
            if vals:
                numeric_stats[col] = {"min": min(vals), "max": max(vals), "avg": round(sum(vals)/len(vals), 2), "count": len(vals)}

        # Compute unique value counts for categorical columns (low cardinality)
        cat_stats = {}
        for col in columns[:12]:
            if col in numeric_stats:
                continue
            val_counts = {}
            for r in data_rows:
                v = str(r.get(col, ""))[:50]
                val_counts[v] = val_counts.get(v, 0) + 1
            if 1 < len(val_counts) <= 20:
                cat_stats[col] = dict(sorted(val_counts.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])[:10])

        stats_text = ""
        if numeric_stats:
            stats_text += "\nNumeric column stats:\n"
            for cn, st in numeric_stats.items():
                stats_text += f"  {cn}: min={st['min']}, max={st['max']}, avg={st['avg']}, non-null={st['count']}\n"
        if cat_stats:
            stats_text += "\nCategorical value distributions:\n"
            for cn, vc in cat_stats.items():
                dist = ", ".join(f"{k}: {v}" for k, v in list(vc.items())[:8])
                stats_text += f"  {cn}: {dist}\n"

        summary_prompt = f"""You are a data analyst. Analyze this SQL query result and provide deep, specific insights.

User asked: "{question}"
Query executed: {sql}
Total rows returned: {row_count}
Columns: {', '.join(str(c) for c in columns)}
{stats_text}
Sample data (first {len(preview_rows)} rows):
{preview_text}

Provide TWO things in a JSON response:

1. "summary" — A detailed 2-4 sentence analysis. Be SPECIFIC: mention actual numbers, counts, percentages, column values, trends, patterns, outliers, or notable findings from the data. Do NOT be generic. For example:
   - "The query returned 15 users. 3 are admins (20%), 12 are active. The most recent signup was 2024-01-15."
   - "Revenue ranges from $1,200 to $89,000. The top 3 customers account for 45% of total revenue."

2. "chart" — A chart recommendation. Pick the best chart type for this data.

Return ONLY valid JSON:
{{
  "summary": "Your specific data analysis here...",
  "chart": {{
    "type": "bar|line|pie|none",
    "x_column": "exact_column_name",
    "y_column": "exact_column_name",
    "title": "Short descriptive title"
  }}
}}

Rules for chart:
- Use "none" if data has only 1 row, is all text with no grouping, or has no meaningful numeric column
- For pie: x_column=label column, y_column=numeric value column (max 12 slices)
- For bar: good for comparing categories
- For line: good for time series or ordered data
- Column names must EXACTLY match: {columns}"""

        try:
            model = GenerativeModel(GEMINI_MODEL)
            resp = model.generate_content(summary_prompt)
            text = resp.text.strip()
            if "```" in text:
                text = text.split("```")[1]
                if text.startswith("json"):
                    text = text[4:]
                text = text.strip()
            parsed = json.loads(text)
            summary = parsed.get("summary", "")
            chart_cfg = parsed.get("chart", {})
            if chart_cfg.get("type") and chart_cfg["type"] != "none":
                # Validate columns exist
                x_col = chart_cfg.get("x_column", "")
                y_col = chart_cfg.get("y_column", "")
                col_names_lower = [str(c).lower() for c in columns]
                if x_col.lower() in col_names_lower and y_col.lower() in col_names_lower:
                    x_idx = col_names_lower.index(x_col.lower())
                    y_idx = col_names_lower.index(y_col.lower())
                    chart_config = {
                        "type": chart_cfg["type"],
                        "x_column": str(columns[x_idx]),
                        "y_column": str(columns[y_idx]),
                        "title": chart_cfg.get("title", ""),
                    }
            logger.info(f"AI summary generated: {len(summary)} chars, chart: {chart_config}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"AI summarization failed: {e}")
            # Provide a basic fallback summary
            summary = f"Query returned {row_count} row{'s' if row_count != 1 else ''} with {len(columns)} columns ({', '.join(str(c) for c in columns[:6])})."
            if numeric_stats:
                first_num = list(numeric_stats.items())[0]
                summary += f" {first_num[0]} ranges from {first_num[1]['min']} to {first_num[1]['max']} (avg: {first_num[1]['avg']})."

    # Log the query with embedding + result summary (after summarization)
    if sql:
        asyncio.get_event_loop().run_in_executor(
            None, _log_nl_query, user_id, database, db_type, server_id, question, sql, tables_used, summary or ""
        )

    return {"success": True, "sql": sql, "tables_used": tables_used, "error": result.get("error"),
            "dialect": result.get("dialect"), "execution": exec_result, "question": question,
            "summary": summary, "chart_config": chart_config,
            "action_mode": action_mode, "is_mutation": is_mutation, "blocked": False}


@app.post("/api/ai/action-preview")
async def action_preview(body: dict = Body(...)):
    """Fetch current data that will be affected by an action-mode mutation query.
    Parses the UPDATE/INSERT SQL to build a SELECT preview."""
    sql = (body.get("sql") or "").strip()
    database = body.get("database", "")
    db_type = body.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    server_id = body.get("server_id", "default")
    if not sql or not database:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="sql and database are required")

    # Build a SELECT query from the mutation to preview affected rows
    sql_upper = sql.strip().upper()
    preview_sql = None
    try:
        if sql_upper.startswith("UPDATE "):
            # Extract table and WHERE clause from UPDATE ... SET ... WHERE ...
            import re as _re
            # Pattern: UPDATE [schema.]table SET ... WHERE ...
            m = _re.match(
                r'UPDATE\s+(.+?)\s+SET\s+.+?(\s+WHERE\s+.+?)?(;?)\s*$',
                sql.strip(), _re.IGNORECASE | _re.DOTALL
            )
            if m:
                table_ref = m.group(1).strip()
                where_clause = (m.group(2) or "").strip()
                preview_sql = f"SELECT * FROM {table_ref} {where_clause} LIMIT 50"
                if db_type == "mssql":
                    preview_sql = f"SELECT TOP 50 * FROM {table_ref} {where_clause}"
        elif sql_upper.startswith("INSERT "):
            # For INSERT, preview the target table's recent rows
            import re as _re
            m = _re.match(r'INSERT\s+INTO\s+(.+?)\s*\(', sql.strip(), _re.IGNORECASE)
            if m:
                table_ref = m.group(1).strip()
                preview_sql = f"SELECT * FROM {table_ref} ORDER BY 1 DESC LIMIT 20"
                if db_type == "mssql":
                    preview_sql = f"SELECT TOP 20 * FROM {table_ref} ORDER BY 1 DESC"
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Action preview parse error: {e}")

    if not preview_sql:
        return {"success": False, "error": "Could not generate preview query from the SQL."}

    # Execute the preview
    try:
        if db_type == "bigquery":
            result = execute_bigquery(preview_sql, database, limit=50)
        elif server_id and server_id not in ("default", "cloudsql", "bigquery"):
            conn = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute(preview_sql)
            if cursor.description:
                cols = [d[0] for d in cursor.description]
                rows = cursor.fetchall()
                result = {"success": True, "data": [dict(zip(cols, r)) if not isinstance(r, dict) else r for r in rows[:50]],
                          "columns": cols, "row_count": len(rows)}
            else:
                result = {"success": True, "data": [], "columns": [], "row_count": 0}
            conn.close()
        else:
            result = execute_query(database, preview_sql, limit=50)
        return {"success": True, "preview": result, "preview_sql": preview_sql}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"success": False, "error": f"Preview failed: {str(e)}"}


@app.post("/api/ai/action-execute")
async def action_execute(body: dict = Body(...), request: Request = None):
    """Execute a mutation query (UPDATE/INSERT) after user confirmation.
    Returns the after-state of affected rows."""
    sql = (body.get("sql") or "").strip()
    database = body.get("database", "")
    db_type = body.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    server_id = body.get("server_id", "default")
    if not sql or not database:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="sql and database are required")
    user_id = _get_user_id(request) if request else ""

    # Safety: block destructive operations
    sql_upper = sql.strip().upper()
    for forbidden in ("DELETE", "DROP", "TRUNCATE"):
        if sql_upper.startswith(forbidden):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f"{forbidden} operations are not allowed in Action Mode.")

    # Execute the mutation
    try:
        if db_type == "bigquery":
            exec_result = execute_bigquery(sql, database)
        elif server_id and server_id not in ("default", "cloudsql", "bigquery"):
            conn = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute(sql)
            conn.commit()
            rows_affected = cursor.rowcount
            conn.close()
            exec_result = {"success": True, "rows_affected": rows_affected}
        else:
            conn = get_db_connection(database)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute(sql)
            conn.commit()
            rows_affected = cursor.rowcount
            cursor.close()
            conn.close()
            exec_result = {"success": True, "rows_affected": rows_affected}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}

    # Fetch after-state preview
    after_data = None
    try:
        import re as _re
        if sql_upper.startswith("UPDATE "):
            m = _re.match(r'UPDATE\s+(.+?)\s+SET\s+.+?(\s+WHERE\s+.+?)?(;?)\s*$', sql.strip(), _re.IGNORECASE | _re.DOTALL)
            if m:
                table_ref = m.group(1).strip()
                where_clause = (m.group(2) or "").strip()
                after_sql = f"SELECT * FROM {table_ref} {where_clause} LIMIT 50"
                if db_type == "mssql":
                    after_sql = f"SELECT TOP 50 * FROM {table_ref} {where_clause}"
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    after_data = execute_bigquery(after_sql, database, limit=50)
                elif server_id and server_id not in ("default", "cloudsql", "bigquery"):
                    conn = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
                    cursor = conn.cursor()
                    cursor.execute(after_sql)
                    if cursor.description:
                        cols = [d[0] for d in cursor.description]
                        rows = cursor.fetchall()
                        after_data = {"success": True, "data": [dict(zip(cols, r)) if not isinstance(r, dict) else r for r in rows[:50]], "columns": cols, "row_count": len(rows)}
                    conn.close()
                else:
                    after_data = execute_query(database, after_sql, limit=50)
        elif sql_upper.startswith("INSERT "):
            m = _re.match(r'INSERT\s+INTO\s+(.+?)\s*\(', sql.strip(), _re.IGNORECASE)
            if m:
                table_ref = m.group(1).strip()
                after_sql = f"SELECT * FROM {table_ref} ORDER BY 1 DESC LIMIT 20"
                if db_type == "mssql":
                    after_sql = f"SELECT TOP 20 * FROM {table_ref} ORDER BY 1 DESC"
                if db_type == "bigquery":
                    after_data = execute_bigquery(after_sql, database, limit=20)
                elif server_id and server_id not in ("default", "cloudsql", "bigquery"):
                    conn = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
                    cursor = conn.cursor()
                    cursor.execute(after_sql)
                    if cursor.description:
                        cols = [d[0] for d in cursor.description]
                        rows = cursor.fetchall()
                        after_data = {"success": True, "data": [dict(zip(cols, r)) if not isinstance(r, dict) else r for r in rows[:20]], "columns": cols, "row_count": len(rows)}
                    conn.close()
                else:
                    after_data = execute_query(database, after_sql, limit=20)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"After-state fetch failed: {e}")

    # Audit log
    if user_id:
        try:
            _log_audit(user_id, database, sql, "success", exec_result.get("rows_affected", 0))
        except Exception:
            pass

    return {"success": True, "execution": exec_result, "after_data": after_data}


@app.post("/api/ai/feedback")
async def ai_query_feedback(body: dict = Body(...), request: Request = None):
    """Submit feedback on generated SQL for learning."""
    question = body.get("question", "")
    edited_sql = body.get("edited_sql", "")
    feedback = body.get("feedback", "")
    database = body.get("database", "")
    was_executed = body.get("was_executed", False)
    if not question:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="question is required")
    try:
        pg_conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cursor = pg_conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            UPDATE {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE}
            SET feedback = %s, was_executed = %s, was_edited = %s, edited_sql = %s
            WHERE id = (
                SELECT id FROM {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE}
                WHERE database_name = %s AND nl_question = %s
                ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1
            )
        """, (feedback, was_executed, bool(edited_sql), edited_sql or "", database, question))
        pg_conn.commit()
        cursor.close()
        pg_conn.close()
        return {"success": True}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}
