# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Feature 1: Query Explanation — explain any SQL in plain English
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

@app.post("/api/ai/explain-sql")
async def explain_sql(body: dict = Body(...)):
    """Explain a SQL query in plain English using Gemini."""
    sql = body.get("sql", "").strip()
    db_type = body.get("db_type", "cloudsql")
    if not sql:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="sql is required")
    dialect = "PostgreSQL" if db_type == "cloudsql" else ("T-SQL (MS SQL Server)" if db_type == "mssql" else "BigQuery Standard SQL")
    prompt = f"""You are a SQL expert. Explain the following {dialect} query in clear, concise plain English.

Break it down step by step:
1. What tables/data sources are involved
2. What filters or conditions are applied
3. What aggregations or transformations happen
4. What the final output looks like

If there are JOINs, explain how the tables are connected.
If there are subqueries, explain their purpose.
Keep the explanation concise but thorough. Use bullet points.

SQL:
{sql}

Explanation:"""
    try:
        model = GenerativeModel(GEMINI_MODEL)
        resp = await asyncio.to_thread(model.generate_content, prompt)
        return {"success": True, "explanation": resp.text.strip()}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"SQL explanation failed: {e}")
        return {"success": False, "explanation": "", "error": str(e)}


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Feature 2: Auto-Suggest Questions — smart question suggestions per DB
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

@app.get("/api/ai/suggest-questions")
async def suggest_questions(database: str = Query(""), server_id: str = Query("default")):
    """Generate smart question suggestions based on the schema catalog."""
    if not database:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="database is required")
    try:
        catalog = _get_catalog_for_db(database, server_id)
        tables = catalog.get("tables", [])
        if not tables:
            return {"success": True, "suggestions": [
                "Show me the top 10 rows from any table",
                "How many records are in each table?",
                "What are the unique values in the most common column?",
            ]}
        # Build a compact schema summary for Gemini
        schema_summary = []
        for t in tables[:20]:
            cols = ", ".join(c["name"] for c in t["columns"][:8])
            desc = t.get("description", "")
            line = f"- {t['table_name']}: {desc}" if desc else f"- {t['table_name']}"
            line += f" [{cols}]"
            schema_summary.append(line)
        prompt = f"""Given this database schema, suggest 8 useful and diverse natural-language questions a data analyst would ask. Include a mix of:
- Simple lookups (top N, distinct values)
- Aggregations (counts, sums, averages grouped by)
- Comparisons (between categories, time periods)
- Data quality checks (nulls, duplicates)
- JOINs across related tables

Database: {database}
Tables:
{chr(10).join(schema_summary)}

Return ONLY a JSON array of question strings. Example: ["How many orders per month?", "Show top 10 customers by revenue"]
"""
        model = GenerativeModel(GEMINI_MODEL)
        resp = await asyncio.to_thread(model.generate_content, prompt)
        text = resp.text.strip()
        if "```" in text:
            text = text.split("```")[1]
            if text.startswith("json"):
                text = text[4:]
            text = text.strip()
        suggestions = json.loads(text)
        if not isinstance(suggestions, list):
            suggestions = []
        return {"success": True, "suggestions": suggestions[:8]}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Suggest questions failed: {e}")
        return {"success": True, "suggestions": [
            f"Show me the top 10 rows from {tables[0]['table_name']}" if tables else "Describe the database",
            "How many records are in each table?",
            "What columns have the most null values?",
        ]}

# Feature 3: Column Hints / Business Glossary — admin-managed terms
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def _ensure_glossary_table():
    """Create the business glossary table if it doesn't exist."""
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sql_editor_business_glossary (
                id              SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                server_id       VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'default',
                database_name   VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                term            VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                definition      TEXT         NOT NULL,
                sql_expression  TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                examples        TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                created_by      VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT '',
                created_at      TIMESTAMP    DEFAULT NOW(),
                updated_at      TIMESTAMP    DEFAULT NOW(),
                UNIQUE(server_id, database_name, term)
            )
        """)
        conn.commit()
        cursor.close()
        conn.close()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Failed to create glossary table: {e}")

_ensure_glossary_table()


@app.get("/api/ai/glossary")
async def get_glossary(database: str = Query(""), server_id: str = Query("default")):
    """Get all business glossary terms for a database."""
    pg_conn = None
    try:
        pg_conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cursor = pg_conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        if database:
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM sql_editor_business_glossary
                WHERE (database_name = %s OR database_name = '*') AND server_id = %s
                ORDER BY term
            """, (database, server_id))
        else:
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM sql_editor_business_glossary ORDER BY database_name, term")
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        cursor.close()
        return {"success": True, "glossary": [dict(r) for r in rows], "count": len(rows)}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"success": False, "glossary": [], "error": str(e)}
    finally:
        if pg_conn:
            pg_conn.close()


@app.post("/api/ai/glossary")
async def add_glossary_term(body: dict = Body(...), request: Request = None):
    """Add or update a business glossary term."""
    database = body.get("database", "")
    term = body.get("term", "").strip()
    definition = body.get("definition", "").strip()
    sql_expression = body.get("sql_expression", "").strip()
    examples = body.get("examples", "").strip()
    server_id = body.get("server_id", "default")
    if not term or not definition:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="term and definition are required")
    user_id = ""
    if request:
        try:
            user_id = request.headers.get("X-User-Id", "") or request.headers.get("x-user-id", "")
        except Exception:
            pass
    pg_conn = None
    try:
        pg_conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cursor = pg_conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("""
            INSERT INTO sql_editor_business_glossary (server_id, database_name, term, definition, sql_expression, examples, created_by)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
            ON CONFLICT (server_id, database_name, term) DO UPDATE
            SET definition = EXCLUDED.definition, sql_expression = EXCLUDED.sql_expression,
                examples = EXCLUDED.examples, updated_at = NOW()
        """, (server_id, database or '*', term, definition, sql_expression, examples, user_id))
        pg_conn.commit()
        cursor.close()
        return {"success": True}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}
    finally:
        if pg_conn:
            pg_conn.close()

def _get_glossary_context(database: str, server_id: str = "default") -> str:
    """Fetch glossary terms to inject into Stage 2 prompt."""
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cursor = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT term, definition, sql_expression FROM sql_editor_business_glossary
            WHERE (database_name = %s OR database_name = '*') AND server_id = %s
            ORDER BY term
        """, (database, server_id))
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        cursor.close()
        conn.close()
        if not rows:
            return ""
        lines = []
        for r in rows:
            line = f'- "{r["term"]}" means: {r["definition"]}'
            if r.get("sql_expression"):
                line += f' → SQL: {r["sql_expression"]}'
            lines.append(line)
        return "\n\nBusiness Glossary (use these definitions when the user mentions these terms):\n" + "\n".join(lines)
    except Exception:
        return ""


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Feature 4: JOIN Inference — detect FK relationships between tables
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def _detect_join_relationships(database: str, db_type: str = "cloudsql",
                                server_id: str = "default", schema_name: str = "public") -> list:
    """Detect foreign key and implicit join relationships."""
    relationships = []
    try:
        if db_type == "cloudsql":
            if server_id and server_id not in ("default", "cloudsql", "bigquery"):
                conn = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
            else:
                conn = get_db_connection(database)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            # Get actual FK constraints
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT
                    tc.table_name AS source_table,
                    kcu.column_name AS source_column,
                    ccu.table_name AS target_table,
                    ccu.column_name AS target_column,
                    tc.constraint_name
                FROM information_schema.table_constraints tc
                JOIN information_schema.key_column_usage kcu
                    ON tc.constraint_name = kcu.constraint_name AND tc.table_schema = kcu.table_schema
                JOIN information_schema.constraint_column_usage ccu
                    ON ccu.constraint_name = tc.constraint_name AND ccu.table_schema = tc.table_schema
                WHERE tc.constraint_type = 'FOREIGN KEY' AND tc.table_schema = %s
            """, (schema_name,))
            for row in cursor.fetchall():
                relationships.append({
                    "type": "foreign_key",
                    "source_table": row[0], "source_column": row[1],
                    "target_table": row[2], "target_column": row[3],
                    "constraint": row[4],
                })
            cursor.close()
            conn.close()
        elif db_type == "mssql":
            conn = _get_server_connection(server_id, database)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT
                    tp.name AS source_table,
                    cp.name AS source_column,
                    tr.name AS target_table,
                    cr.name AS target_column,
                    fk.name AS constraint_name
                FROM sys.foreign_keys fk
                JOIN sys.foreign_key_columns fkc ON fk.object_id = fkc.constraint_object_id
                JOIN sys.tables tp ON fkc.parent_object_id = tp.object_id
                JOIN sys.columns cp ON fkc.parent_object_id = cp.object_id AND fkc.parent_column_id = cp.column_id
                JOIN sys.tables tr ON fkc.referenced_object_id = tr.object_id
                JOIN sys.columns cr ON fkc.referenced_object_id = cr.object_id AND fkc.referenced_column_id = cr.column_id
            """)
            for row in cursor.fetchall():
                relationships.append({
                    "type": "foreign_key",
                    "source_table": row[0], "source_column": row[1],
                    "target_table": row[2], "target_column": row[3],
                    "constraint": row[4],
                })
            cursor.close()
            conn.close()

        # Also detect implicit joins: columns with matching names ending in _id
        pg_conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cursor = pg_conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT table_name, column_name, is_pk FROM {AI_CATALOG_TABLE}
            WHERE server_id = %s AND database_name = %s
            ORDER BY table_name, column_name
        """, (server_id, database))
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        cursor.close()
        pg_conn.close()

        # Build lookup: pk columns per table, all columns per table
        pk_map = {}  # table_name -> [pk columns]
        col_map = {}  # table_name -> set of columns
        for r in rows:
            tbl = r["table_name"]
            col = r["column_name"]
            col_map.setdefault(tbl, set()).add(col)
            if r.get("is_pk"):
                pk_map.setdefault(tbl, []).append(col)

        # Find implicit FK: if table A has column "{table_b}_id" and table B has "id" PK
        existing = {(r["source_table"], r["source_column"], r["target_table"], r["target_column"]) for r in relationships}
        for tbl_a, cols in col_map.items():
            for col in cols:
                if col.endswith("_id") and col != "id":
                    # Guess target table name from column (e.g., "customer_id" -> "customers" or "customer")
                    base = col[:-3]  # strip "_id"
                    for candidate in [base, base + "s", base + "es"]:
                        if candidate in col_map and candidate != tbl_a:
                            target_pks = pk_map.get(candidate, [])
                            target_col = "id" if "id" in col_map[candidate] else (target_pks[0] if target_pks else None)
                            if target_col and (tbl_a, col, candidate, target_col) not in existing:
                                relationships.append({
                                    "type": "implicit",
                                    "source_table": tbl_a, "source_column": col,
                                    "target_table": candidate, "target_column": target_col,
                                })
                                existing.add((tbl_a, col, candidate, target_col))
                            break
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"FK detection failed: {e}")
    return relationships


# Cache for relationships
_relationship_cache = {}

def _get_join_context(database: str, db_type: str, server_id: str, selected_tables: list, schema_name: str = "public") -> str:
    """Build a JOIN hint context string for selected tables."""
    cache_key = f"{server_id}:{database}"
    if cache_key not in _relationship_cache or _relationship_cache[cache_key].get("expires", 0) < _time.time():
        rels = _detect_join_relationships(database, db_type, server_id, schema_name)
        _relationship_cache[cache_key] = {"rels": rels, "expires": _time.time() + 600}
    all_rels = _relationship_cache[cache_key]["rels"]
    # Filter to only relationships involving selected tables
    relevant = [r for r in all_rels if r["source_table"] in selected_tables or r["target_table"] in selected_tables]
    if not relevant:
        return ""
    lines = []
    for r in relevant:
        rel_type = "FK" if r["type"] == "foreign_key" else "implicit"
        lines.append(f'  {r["source_table"]}.{r["source_column"]} → {r["target_table"]}.{r["target_column"]} ({rel_type})')
    return "\n\nKnown JOIN relationships between these tables:\n" + "\n".join(lines) + "\nUse these relationships for JOINs when combining data across tables."


@app.get("/api/ai/relationships")
async def get_relationships(database: str = Query(""), server_id: str = Query("default"),
                            db_type: str = Query("cloudsql")):
    """Get detected FK + implicit join relationships for a database."""
    if not database:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="database is required")
    try:
        rels = await asyncio.to_thread(_detect_join_relationships, database, db_type, server_id)
        return {"success": True, "relationships": rels, "count": len(rels)}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"success": False, "relationships": [], "error": str(e)}


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Feature 5: Admin Training Dashboard — manage training examples
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

@app.get("/api/ai/training")
async def get_training_data(
    database: str = Query(""),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=500),
    search: str = Query(""),
    feedback: str = Query(""),
    was_edited: str = Query(""),
):
    """Get paginated training examples from the NL query log (admin view)."""
    pg_conn = None
    try:
        pg_conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cursor = pg_conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        conditions = []
        params = []
        if database:
            conditions.append("database_name = %s")
            params.append(database)
        if search:
            conditions.append("(nl_question ILIKE %s OR generated_sql ILIKE %s OR edited_sql ILIKE %s)")
            params.extend([f"%{search}%"] * 3)
        if feedback:
            conditions.append("feedback = %s")
            params.append(feedback)
        if was_edited == "true":
            conditions.append("was_edited = true")
        elif was_edited == "false":
            conditions.append("was_edited = false")
        where = "WHERE " + " AND ".join(conditions) if conditions else ""
        # Count
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE} {where}", params)
        total = cursor.fetchone()["count"]
        # Data
        offset = (page - 1) * page_size
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT id, user_id, database_name, nl_question, generated_sql, edited_sql,
                   tables_used, feedback, was_executed, was_edited, result_summary, created_at
            FROM {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE} {where}
            ORDER BY created_at DESC
            LIMIT %s OFFSET %s
        """, params + [page_size, offset])
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        # Stats
        cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT
                COUNT(*) AS total,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE feedback = 'good') AS good,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE feedback = 'edited') AS edited,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE feedback = 'bad') AS bad,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE was_edited = true) AS user_corrected,
                COUNT(DISTINCT user_id) AS unique_users,
                COUNT(DISTINCT database_name) AS unique_databases
            FROM {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE}
        """)
        stats = dict(cursor.fetchone())
        cursor.close()
        return {
            "success": True,
            "examples": [dict(r) for r in rows],
            "total": total, "page": page, "page_size": page_size,
            "total_pages": max(1, -(-total // page_size)),
            "stats": stats,
        }
    except Exception as e:
        return {"success": False, "examples": [], "error": str(e)}
    finally:
        if pg_conn:
            pg_conn.close()


@app.put("/api/ai/training/{example_id}")
async def update_training_example(example_id: int, body: dict = Body(...)):
    """Update a training example (edit the gold SQL, feedback, etc.)."""
    pg_conn = None
    try:
        pg_conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cursor = pg_conn.cursor()
        sets = []
        params = []
        if "edited_sql" in body:
            sets.append("edited_sql = %s")
            params.append(body["edited_sql"])
            sets.append("was_edited = true")
        if "feedback" in body:
            sets.append("feedback = %s")
            params.append(body["feedback"])
        if "nl_question" in body:
            sets.append("nl_question = %s")
            params.append(body["nl_question"])
        if not sets:
            return {"success": False, "error": "nothing to update"}
        params.append(example_id)
        cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE} SET {', '.join(sets)} WHERE id = %s", params)
        pg_conn.commit()
        cursor.close()
        return {"success": True, "updated": True}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}
    finally:
        if pg_conn:
            pg_conn.close()

async def delete_training_example(example_id: int):
    """Delete a training example."""
    pg_conn = None
    try:
        pg_conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cursor = pg_conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"DELETE FROM {AI_QUERY_LOG_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (example_id,))
        pg_conn.commit()
        cursor.close()
        return {"success": True}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}
    finally:
        if pg_conn:
            pg_conn.close()


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# LOGIC FLOW BUILDER
# Phases 1-4: Step Sequencer, Conditions, Transforms, Loops, HTTP, AI Copilot
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

import re as _re
import operator as _op
from copy import deepcopy

LOGIC_FLOWS_TABLE = "sql_editor_logic_flows"
FLOW_RUNS_TABLE   = "sql_editor_flow_runs"

# ── Server cache reference (already populated by server-management code) ──────
# _server_cache is expected to be a module-level dict {server_id: {...}}
# We add a safe accessor so this section compiles even if the dict lives
# elsewhere in main.py.
if "_server_cache" not in dir():
    _server_cache: Dict[str, Any] = {}


def _ensure_flow_tables():
    """Create logic-flow tables if they don't exist."""
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {LOGIC_FLOWS_TABLE} (
                id          VARCHAR(100) PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::text,
                name        VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                description TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                api_id      VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT '',
                steps       JSONB        NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
                params      JSONB        NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
                tags        TEXT[]       DEFAULT ARRAY[]::TEXT[],
                created_by  VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT '',
                created_at  TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
                updated_at  TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cur.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {FLOW_RUNS_TABLE} (
                id                VARCHAR(100) PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::text,
                flow_id           VARCHAR(100) REFERENCES {LOGIC_FLOWS_TABLE}(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                status            VARCHAR(50)  DEFAULT 'pending',
                input_params      JSONB        DEFAULT '{{}}',
                result            JSONB        DEFAULT NULL,
                error             TEXT         DEFAULT NULL,
                step_logs         JSONB        DEFAULT '[]',
                execution_time_ms FLOAT        DEFAULT 0,
                executed_by       VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT '',
                created_at        TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cur.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_flow_runs_fid  ON {FLOW_RUNS_TABLE}  (flow_id, created_at DESC)")
        cur.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_flows_created   ON {LOGIC_FLOWS_TABLE} (created_by, created_at DESC)")
        conn.commit()
        logger.info("Logic flow tables ensured")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to ensure flow tables: {e}")
    finally:
        if conn:
            conn.close()


_ensure_flow_tables()


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Pydantic Models
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class FlowParam(BaseModel):
    name: str
    type: str = "string"          # string | integer | float | boolean | array | object
    required: bool = False
    default: Optional[Any] = None
    description: str = ""


class CreateFlowRequest(BaseModel):
    name: str
    description: str = ""
    api_id: str = ""
    steps: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    params: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    tags: List[str] = []


class UpdateFlowRequest(BaseModel):
    name: Optional[str] = None
    description: Optional[str] = None
    steps: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None
    params: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None
    tags: Optional[List[str]] = None
    api_id: Optional[str] = None


class ExecuteFlowRequest(BaseModel):
    params: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    database: Optional[str] = None
    db_type: str = "cloudsql"
    server_id: Optional[str] = None


class GenerateFlowRequest(BaseModel):
    description: str
    database: Optional[str] = None
    available_tables: Optional[List[str]] = None
    db_type: str = "cloudsql"


class SuggestStepsRequest(BaseModel):
    current_steps: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    database: Optional[str] = None
    context: str = ""
    db_type: str = "cloudsql"


class ExplainFlowRequest(BaseModel):
    steps: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    params: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    name: str = ""


class DebugFlowRequest(BaseModel):
    flow_id: Optional[str] = None
    run_id: Optional[str] = None
    steps: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None
    error: Optional[str] = None
    step_logs: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None
    params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Expression / Template Engine
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def _get_ctx_value(path: str, ctx: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
    """Navigate dot-notation path. Supports .length / .count / .size."""
    parts = path.split(".")
    cur: Any = ctx
    for part in parts:
        if part in ("length", "count", "size") and isinstance(cur, list):
            cur = len(cur)
        elif isinstance(cur, dict):
            cur = cur.get(part)
        elif isinstance(cur, list):
            try:
                cur = cur[int(part)]
            except (ValueError, IndexError):
                return None
        else:
            return None
        if cur is None:
            return None
    return cur


def _resolve_value(val: Any, ctx: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
    """Recursively resolve {{variable}} placeholders in a value."""
    if isinstance(val, str):
        stripped = val.strip()
        full_match = _re.fullmatch(r'\{\{([^}]+)\}\}', stripped)
        if full_match:
            return _get_ctx_value(full_match.group(1).strip(), ctx)
        def _rep(m):
            v = _get_ctx_value(m.group(1).strip(), ctx)
            return "" if v is None else str(v)
        return _re.sub(r'\{\{([^}]+)\}\}', _rep, val)
    elif isinstance(val, dict):
        return {k: _resolve_value(v, ctx) for k, v in val.items()}
    elif isinstance(val, list):
        return [_resolve_value(item, ctx) for item in val]
    return val


def _evaluate_expr(expr: Any, ctx: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """Evaluate a condition expression dict (supports compound and/or)."""
    if not expr:
        return True
    if isinstance(expr, bool):
        return expr
    if not isinstance(expr, dict):
        return bool(expr)

    op_str = expr.get("operator", "==")

    # Compound
    if op_str.lower() == "and":
        return all(_evaluate_expr(sub, ctx) for sub in expr.get("conditions", []))
    if op_str.lower() == "or":
        return any(_evaluate_expr(sub, ctx) for sub in expr.get("conditions", []))

    left  = _resolve_value(expr.get("left",  ""), ctx)
    right = _resolve_value(expr.get("right", ""), ctx)

    try:
        if op_str == "==":           return left == right
        if op_str == "!=":           return left != right
        if op_str == ">":            return float(left) >  float(right)
        if op_str == "<":            return float(left) <  float(right)
        if op_str == ">=":           return float(left) >= float(right)
        if op_str == "<=":           return float(left) <= float(right)
        if op_str == "is_empty":     return not left or (isinstance(left, (list, dict, str)) and len(left) == 0)
        if op_str == "is_not_empty": return bool(left) and (not isinstance(left, (list, dict, str)) or len(left) > 0)
        if op_str == "contains":     return (str(right) in str(left)) if left is not None else False
        if op_str == "not_contains": return (str(right) not in str(left)) if left is not None else True
        if op_str == "starts_with":  return str(left).startswith(str(right))
        if op_str == "ends_with":    return str(left).endswith(str(right))
        if op_str == "in":           return left in (right if isinstance(right, list) else [right])
        if op_str == "not_in":       return left not in (right if isinstance(right, list) else [right])
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return False
    return False


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Flow Executor — Step Handlers
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def _pg_connect(database: str):
    return psycopg2.connect(
        host=DB_HOST, database=database,
        user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
    )


async def _exec_sql_step(cfg: Dict, ctx: Dict) -> Any:
    database  = str(_resolve_value(cfg.get("database") or ctx.get("_database", "postgres"), ctx))
    db_type   = str(_resolve_value(cfg.get("db_type")  or ctx.get("_db_type",  "cloudsql"), ctx))
    server_id = _resolve_value(cfg.get("server_id") or ctx.get("_server_id"), ctx)
    query     = str(_resolve_value(cfg.get("query", ""), ctx))
    limit     = cfg.get("limit")
    bind_to   = cfg.get("bind_result_to", "result")

    if not query.strip():
        ctx[bind_to] = []
        return []

    if limit and "LIMIT" not in query.upper():
        query = f"{query.rstrip(';')} LIMIT {int(limit)}"

    rows: List[Dict] = []

    if db_type == "cloudsql":
        conn = await asyncio.to_thread(_pg_connect, database)
        try:
            cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
            await asyncio.to_thread(cur.execute, query)
            if cur.description:
                rows = [dict(r) for r in await asyncio.to_thread(cur.fetchall)]
            await asyncio.to_thread(conn.commit)
        finally:
            await asyncio.to_thread(conn.close)
    elif db_type == "mssql" and _HAS_PYMSSQL and server_id and server_id in _server_cache:
        sc = _server_cache[server_id]
        def _run_mssql():
            c = pymssql.connect(server=sc["host"], user=sc["username"],
                                password=sc["password"], database=database,
                                port=int(sc.get("port", 1433)))
            try:
                cr = c.cursor(as_dict=True)
                cr.execute(query)
                return cr.fetchall() if cr.description else []
            finally:
                c.close()
        rows = await asyncio.to_thread(_run_mssql)

    ctx[bind_to] = rows
    return rows


def _exec_transform_step(cfg: Dict, ctx: Dict) -> Any:
    source  = cfg.get("source", "result")
    bind_to = cfg.get("bind_result_to") or source
    data    = list(ctx.get(source, []))

    for op in cfg.get("operations", []):
        op_type = op.get("op", "")

        if op_type == "filter":
            field    = op.get("field", "")
            operator = op.get("operator", "==")
            value    = _resolve_value(op.get("value"), ctx)
            expr     = {"left": f"{{{{_item.{field}}}}}", "operator": operator, "right": value}
            filtered = []
            for item in data:
                ctx["_item"] = item
                if _evaluate_expr({"left": item.get(field), "operator": operator, "right": value}, ctx):
                    filtered.append(item)
            ctx.pop("_item", None)
            data = filtered

        elif op_type == "rename":
            frm, to = op.get("from", ""), op.get("to", "")
            if frm and to:
                data = [{(to if k == frm else k): v for k, v in row.items()} for row in data]

        elif op_type == "add_field":
            field = op.get("field", "new_field")
            expr_val = op.get("value", "")
            new_data = []
            for item in data:
                ctx["_item"] = item
                item = dict(item)
                item[field] = _resolve_value(expr_val, ctx)
                new_data.append(item)
            ctx.pop("_item", None)
            data = new_data

        elif op_type == "remove_field":
            field = op.get("field", "")
            data = [{k: v for k, v in row.items() if k != field} for row in data]

        elif op_type == "sort":
            field = op.get("field", "")
            reverse = op.get("direction", "asc").lower() == "desc"
            if field:
                data = sorted(data, key=lambda r: (r.get(field) is None, r.get(field)), reverse=reverse)

        elif op_type == "limit":
            n = int(op.get("count", 100))
            data = data[:n]

        elif op_type == "offset":
            n = int(op.get("count", 0))
            data = data[n:]

        elif op_type == "unique":
            field = op.get("field", "")
            if field:
                seen, deduped = set(), []
                for row in data:
                    v = row.get(field)
                    if v not in seen:
                        seen.add(v)
                        deduped.append(row)
                data = deduped

        elif op_type == "group_by":
            field = op.get("field", "")
            agg_field = op.get("agg_field", "count")
            agg_func  = op.get("agg_func", "count")
            if field:
                groups: Dict[Any, List] = {}
                for row in data:
                    key = row.get(field)
                    groups.setdefault(key, []).append(row)
                grouped = []
                for key, rows in groups.items():
                    entry: Dict[str, Any] = {field: key}
                    if agg_func == "count":
                        entry[agg_field] = len(rows)
                    elif agg_func == "sum":
                        entry[agg_field] = sum(r.get(agg_field, 0) or 0 for r in rows)
                    elif agg_func == "avg":
                        vals = [r.get(agg_field, 0) or 0 for r in rows]
                        entry[agg_field] = sum(vals) / len(vals) if vals else 0
                    elif agg_func == "min":
                        entry[agg_field] = min((r.get(agg_field) for r in rows if r.get(agg_field) is not None), default=None)
                    elif agg_func == "max":
                        entry[agg_field] = max((r.get(agg_field) for r in rows if r.get(agg_field) is not None), default=None)
                    grouped.append(entry)
                data = grouped

        elif op_type == "pick_fields":
            fields = op.get("fields", [])
            if fields:
                data = [{f: row.get(f) for f in fields} for row in data]

    ctx[bind_to] = data
    return data


async def _exec_http_step(cfg: Dict, ctx: Dict) -> Any:
    method  = str(_resolve_value(cfg.get("method", "GET"), ctx)).upper()
    url     = str(_resolve_value(cfg.get("url", ""), ctx))
    headers = _resolve_value(cfg.get("headers", {}), ctx) or {}
    body    = _resolve_value(cfg.get("body"), ctx)
    timeout = int(cfg.get("timeout", 30))
    bind_to = cfg.get("bind_result_to", "http_result")

    if not url:
        ctx[bind_to] = None
        return None

    def _do_request():
        if method == "GET":
            return http_requests.get(url, headers=headers, timeout=timeout)
        elif method == "POST":
            return http_requests.post(url, json=body, headers=headers, timeout=timeout)
        elif method == "PUT":
            return http_requests.put(url, json=body, headers=headers, timeout=timeout)
        elif method == "DELETE":
            return http_requests.delete(url, headers=headers, timeout=timeout)
        elif method == "PATCH":
            return http_requests.patch(url, json=body, headers=headers, timeout=timeout)
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown HTTP method: {method}")

    resp = await asyncio.to_thread(_do_request)
    try:
        result = resp.json()
    except Exception:
        result = resp.text

    ctx[bind_to] = result
    ctx[f"{bind_to}_status"] = resp.status_code
    return result


# ── Recursive step executor ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

async def _execute_step(step: Dict, ctx: Dict, logs: List) -> Dict:
    step_id   = step.get("id", str(uuid.uuid4())[:8])
    step_name = step.get("name", step_id)
    step_type = step.get("type", "")
    cfg       = step.get("config", {})
    on_error  = step.get("on_error", "stop")

    log_entry: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "step_id": step_id,
        "step_name": step_name,
        "type": step_type,
        "status": "running",
        "started_at": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
        "output": None,
        "error": None,
    }
    logs.append(log_entry)

    try:
        result: Any = None

        if step_type == "sql_query":
            result = await _exec_sql_step(cfg, ctx)

        elif step_type == "condition":
            expr        = cfg.get("expression", {})
            then_steps  = cfg.get("then_steps", [])
            else_steps  = cfg.get("else_steps", [])
            branch_taken = _evaluate_expr(expr, ctx)
            log_entry["branch"] = "then" if branch_taken else "else"
            chosen = then_steps if branch_taken else else_steps
            branch_logs: List = []
            for sub in chosen:
                sub_result = await _execute_step(sub, ctx, branch_logs)
                if sub_result.get("_flow_return"):
                    logs.extend(branch_logs)
                    log_entry["status"] = "completed"
                    return sub_result
            logs.extend(branch_logs)
            result = branch_taken

        elif step_type == "transform":
            result = _exec_transform_step(cfg, ctx)

        elif step_type == "loop":
            source   = cfg.get("iterate_over", "result")
            item_var = cfg.get("item_variable", "item")
            sub_steps = cfg.get("sub_steps", [])
            items    = ctx.get(source, [])
            bind_to  = cfg.get("bind_result_to", f"{source}_processed")
            processed = []
            loop_logs: List = []
            for idx, item in enumerate(items):
                ctx[item_var] = item
                ctx[f"{item_var}_index"] = idx
                for sub in sub_steps:
                    sub_res = await _execute_step(sub, ctx, loop_logs)
                    if sub_res.get("_flow_return"):
                        logs.extend(loop_logs)
                        log_entry["status"] = "completed"
                        return sub_res
                processed.append(deepcopy(ctx.get("_loop_output", item)))
            ctx.pop(item_var, None)
            ctx.pop(f"{item_var}_index", None)
            ctx[bind_to] = processed
            logs.extend(loop_logs)
            result = processed

        elif step_type == "http_call":
            result = await _exec_http_step(cfg, ctx)

        elif step_type == "set_variable":
            var_name  = cfg.get("variable", "var")
            var_value = _resolve_value(cfg.get("value"), ctx)
            ctx[var_name] = var_value
            result = var_value

        elif step_type == "return":
            status_code = int(_resolve_value(cfg.get("status_code", 200), ctx) or 200)
            data        = _resolve_value(cfg.get("data"), ctx)
            message     = str(_resolve_value(cfg.get("message", ""), ctx))
            extra        = _resolve_value(cfg.get("extra", {}), ctx) or {}
            payload: Dict[str, Any] = {"success": True, "status_code": status_code, "message": message}
            if data is not None:
                payload["data"] = data
                if isinstance(data, list):
                    payload["count"] = len(data)
            payload.update(extra)
            log_entry["status"] = "completed"
            log_entry["output"] = {"status_code": status_code}
            return {"_flow_return": True, "payload": payload, "status_code": status_code}

        elif step_type == "error_handler":
            status_code = int(_resolve_value(cfg.get("status_code", 500), ctx) or 500)
            message     = str(_resolve_value(cfg.get("message", "An error occurred"), ctx))
            return {"_flow_return": True, "payload": {"success": False, "error": message}, "status_code": status_code}

        elif step_type == "merge":
            sources  = cfg.get("sources", [])
            bind_to  = cfg.get("bind_result_to", "merged")
            strategy = cfg.get("strategy", "concat")  # concat | zip | dict
            resolved_sources = [ctx.get(s, []) for s in sources]
            if strategy == "concat":
                merged: Any = []
                for s in resolved_sources:
                    if isinstance(s, list):
                        merged.extend(s)
            elif strategy == "zip":
                merged = [dict(**a, **b) for a, b in zip(*resolved_sources)] if len(resolved_sources) >= 2 else []
            else:
                merged = {s: ctx.get(s) for s in sources}
            ctx[bind_to] = merged
            result = merged

        else:
            logger.warning(f"Unknown step type: {step_type}")
            result = None

        log_entry["status"] = "completed"
        log_entry["output"] = str(result)[:500] if result is not None else None

    except Exception as e:
        log_entry["status"] = "error"
        log_entry["error"]  = str(e)
        if on_error == "stop":
            raise
        # on_error == "continue": swallow and move on

    log_entry["finished_at"] = datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
    return {"_flow_return": False}


async def _execute_flow(steps: List[Dict], ctx: Dict, logs: List) -> Dict:
    """Execute a list of steps in order.  Returns the final payload."""
    for step in steps:
        result = await _execute_step(step, ctx, logs)
        if result.get("_flow_return"):
            return result
    # If no explicit Return step, return everything in ctx (minus internals)
    public_ctx = {k: v for k, v in ctx.items() if not k.startswith("_")}
    return {
        "_flow_return": True,
        "payload": {"success": True, "data": public_ctx},
        "status_code": 200,
    }

        "tags": ["enrich", "http", "join"],
        "params": [
            {"name": "database",    "type": "string", "required": True,  "description": "Database name"},
            {"name": "external_url","type": "string", "required": True,  "description": "External API base URL"},
        ],
        "steps": [
            {"id": "s1", "type": "sql_query",  "name": "Fetch base data",
             "config": {"database": "{{params.database}}", "query": "SELECT * FROM your_table LIMIT 50", "bind_result_to": "rows"}},
            {"id": "s2", "type": "http_call",  "name": "Call external API",
             "config": {"method": "GET", "url": "{{params.external_url}}", "bind_result_to": "external_data"}},
            {"id": "s3", "type": "return",     "name": "Return merged",
             "config": {"status_code": 200, "data": "{{rows}}", "extra": {"external": "{{external_data}}"}, "message": "Enriched"}},
        ],
    },
    {
        "id": "tpl_conditional_role",
        "name": "Role-Based Response",
        "description": "Return different data based on a user-role parameter",
        "tags": ["role", "conditional", "access-control"],
        "params": [
            {"name": "database", "type": "string", "required": True,  "description": "Database name"},
            {"name": "role",     "type": "string", "required": True,  "description": "User role: admin | user"},
        ],
        "steps": [
            {"id": "s1", "type": "condition", "name": "Check admin role",
             "config": {
                 "expression": {"left": "{{params.role}}", "operator": "==", "right": "admin"},
                 "then_steps": [
                     {"id": "s1a", "type": "sql_query", "name": "Admin query",
                      "config": {"database": "{{params.database}}", "query": "SELECT * FROM your_table", "bind_result_to": "data"}},
                     {"id": "s1b", "type": "return", "name": "Admin response",
                      "config": {"status_code": 200, "data": "{{data}}", "message": "Admin view"}},
                 ],
                 "else_steps": [
                     {"id": "s1c", "type": "sql_query", "name": "User query",
                      "config": {"database": "{{params.database}}", "query": "SELECT id, name FROM your_table WHERE is_public = true", "bind_result_to": "data"}},
                     {"id": "s1d", "type": "return", "name": "User response",
                      "config": {"status_code": 200, "data": "{{data}}", "message": "User view"}},
                 ],
             }},
        ],
    },
    {
        "id": "tpl_multi_query_join",
        "name": "Multi-Query Merge",
        "description": "Run two queries and merge results into a single response",
        "tags": ["multi-query", "merge", "join"],
        "params": [
            {"name": "database", "type": "string", "required": True, "description": "Database name"},
        ],
        "steps": [
            {"id": "s1", "type": "sql_query", "name": "Query A",
             "config": {"database": "{{params.database}}", "query": "SELECT * FROM table_a LIMIT 100", "bind_result_to": "data_a"}},
            {"id": "s2", "type": "sql_query", "name": "Query B",
             "config": {"database": "{{params.database}}", "query": "SELECT * FROM table_b LIMIT 100", "bind_result_to": "data_b"}},
            {"id": "s3", "type": "return",     "name": "Return both",
             "config": {"status_code": 200, "extra": {"table_a": "{{data_a}}", "table_b": "{{data_b}}"}, "message": "Both datasets"}},
        ],
    },
    {
        "id": "tpl_paginate",
        "name": "Paginated List",
        "description": "Standard pagination with total count",
        "tags": ["pagination", "list"],
        "params": [
            {"name": "database",  "type": "string",  "required": True,  "description": "Database"},
            {"name": "page",      "type": "integer", "required": False, "default": 1,  "description": "Page"},
            {"name": "page_size", "type": "integer", "required": False, "default": 20, "description": "Size"},
        ],
        "steps": [
            {"id": "s1", "type": "sql_query", "name": "Count total",
             "config": {"database": "{{params.database}}", "query": "SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM your_table", "bind_result_to": "count_result"}},
            {"id": "s2", "type": "sql_query", "name": "Fetch page",
             "config": {"database": "{{params.database}}", "query": "SELECT * FROM your_table LIMIT {{params.page_size}} OFFSET 0", "bind_result_to": "rows"}},
            {"id": "s3", "type": "return",    "name": "Return with meta",
             "config": {"status_code": 200, "data": "{{rows}}", "extra": {"total": "{{count_result}}", "page": "{{params.page}}", "page_size": "{{params.page_size}}"}, "message": "OK"}},
        ],
    },
    {
        "id": "tpl_validate_insert",
        "name": "Validate & Insert",
        "description": "Check for duplicate before inserting a new record",
        "tags": ["insert", "validation", "write"],
        "params": [
            {"name": "database", "type": "string", "required": True, "description": "Database"},
            {"name": "value",    "type": "string", "required": True, "description": "Value to insert"},
        ],
        "steps": [
            {"id": "s1", "type": "sql_query", "name": "Check duplicate",
             "config": {"database": "{{params.database}}", "query": "SELECT id FROM your_table WHERE unique_field = '{{params.value}}' LIMIT 1", "bind_result_to": "existing"}},
            {"id": "s2", "type": "condition", "name": "Duplicate check",
             "config": {
                 "expression": {"left": "{{existing.length}}", "operator": ">", "right": "0"},
                 "then_steps": [{"id": "s2a", "type": "return", "name": "Conflict",
                                 "config": {"status_code": 409, "data": None, "message": "Record already exists"}}],
                 "else_steps": [],
             }},
            {"id": "s3", "type": "sql_query", "name": "Insert record",
             "config": {"database": "{{params.database}}", "query": "INSERT INTO your_table (unique_field) VALUES ('{{params.value}}') RETURNING *", "bind_result_to": "created"}},
            {"id": "s4", "type": "return",    "name": "Return created",
             "config": {"status_code": 201, "data": "{{created}}", "message": "Created"}},
        ],
    },
]

@app.post("/api/flows")
async def create_flow(body: CreateFlowRequest, request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    flow_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute(
            f"INSERT INTO {LOGIC_FLOWS_TABLE} (id, name, description, api_id, steps, params, tags, created_by) "
            f"VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s::jsonb, %s::jsonb, %s, %s)",
            (flow_id, body.name, body.description, body.api_id,
             json.dumps(body.steps), json.dumps(body.params),
             body.tags, user_id),
        )
        conn.commit()
        return {"success": True, "id": flow_id, "message": "Flow created"}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


@app.get("/api/flows")
async def list_flows(request: Request, search: str = "", tag: str = ""):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        q = f"SELECT id, name, description, api_id, tags, created_by, created_at, updated_at, jsonb_array_length(steps) as step_count FROM {LOGIC_FLOWS_TABLE} WHERE 1=1"
        params_list: List[Any] = []
        if search:
            q += " AND (name ILIKE %s OR description ILIKE %s)"
            params_list += [f"%{search}%", f"%{search}%"]
        if tag:
            q += " AND %s = ANY(tags)"
            params_list.append(tag)
        q += " ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 200"
        cur.execute(q, params_list)
        rows = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
        return {"success": True, "flows": rows, "count": len(rows)}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


@app.get("/api/flows/{flow_id}")
async def get_flow(flow_id: str, request: Request):
    _require_user(request)
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cur.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {LOGIC_FLOWS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (flow_id,))
        row = cur.fetchone()
        if not row:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Flow not found")
        return {"success": True, "flow": dict(row)}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


@app.put("/api/flows/{flow_id}")
async def update_flow(flow_id: str, body: UpdateFlowRequest, request: Request):
    _require_user(request)
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cur = conn.cursor()
        fields, vals = [], []
        if body.name        is not None: fields.append("name = %s");              vals.append(body.name)
        if body.description is not None: fields.append("description = %s");       vals.append(body.description)
        if body.api_id      is not None: fields.append("api_id = %s");            vals.append(body.api_id)
        if body.steps       is not None: fields.append("steps = %s::jsonb");      vals.append(json.dumps(body.steps))
        if body.params      is not None: fields.append("params = %s::jsonb");     vals.append(json.dumps(body.params))
        if body.tags        is not None: fields.append("tags = %s");              vals.append(body.tags)
        if not fields:
            return {"success": True, "message": "Nothing to update"}
        fields.append("updated_at = NOW()")
        vals.append(flow_id)
        cur.execute(f"UPDATE {LOGIC_FLOWS_TABLE} SET {', '.join(fields)} WHERE id = %s", vals)
        conn.commit()
        return {"success": True, "message": "Flow updated"}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()

async def delete_flow(flow_id: str, request: Request):

        return {"success": True, "message": "Flow deleted"}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


@app.post("/api/flows/{flow_id}/execute")
async def execute_flow(flow_id: str, body: ExecuteFlowRequest, request: Request):
    """Execute a saved logic flow and return the result."""
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    ip_addr = request.client.host if request.client else ""
    run_id  = str(uuid.uuid4())
    t_start = time.time()

    # Load flow
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cur.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {LOGIC_FLOWS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (flow_id,))
        flow_row = cur.fetchone()
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()

    if not flow_row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Flow not found")

    steps  = flow_row["steps"]  if isinstance(flow_row["steps"],  list) else json.loads(flow_row["steps"])
    params = flow_row["params"] if isinstance(flow_row["params"], list) else json.loads(flow_row["params"])

    # Validate required params
    for p in params:
        if p.get("required") and p["name"] not in body.params:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=f"Required param '{p['name']}' missing")

    # Build execution context
    ctx: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "params":    body.params,
        "_database": body.database or "",
        "_db_type":  body.db_type,
        "_server_id": body.server_id or "",
        "_run_id":   run_id,
        "_user_id":  user_id,
    }
    # Apply param defaults
    for p in params:
        if p["name"] not in ctx["params"] and p.get("default") is not None:
            ctx["params"][p["name"]] = p["default"]

    step_logs: List = []
    status, error_msg, payload = "completed", None, {}

    try:
        result = await _execute_flow(steps, ctx, step_logs)
        payload = result.get("payload", {})
        http_status = result.get("status_code", 200)
    except Exception as e:
        status    = "error"
        error_msg = str(e)
        payload   = {"success": False, "error": error_msg}
        http_status = 500

    exec_ms = (time.time() - t_start) * 1000

    # Persist run
    try:
        conn2 = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
        cur2  = conn2.cursor()
        cur2.execute(
            f"INSERT INTO {FLOW_RUNS_TABLE} (id, flow_id, status, input_params, result, error, step_logs, execution_time_ms, executed_by) "
            f"VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s::jsonb, %s::jsonb, %s, %s::jsonb, %s, %s)",
            (run_id, flow_id, status, json.dumps(body.params),
             json.dumps(payload, default=str), error_msg,
             json.dumps(step_logs, default=str), exec_ms, user_id),
        )
        conn2.commit()
        conn2.close()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Flow run persist failed: {e}")

    await _log_audit_async(user_id, "flow_execute", database_name=flow_id,
                           query_text=flow_row["name"], ip_address=ip_addr)

    return JSONResponse(content={**payload, "run_id": run_id, "execution_time_ms": round(exec_ms, 2)},
                        status_code=http_status)


@app.get("/api/flows/{flow_id}/runs")

        )
