# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# AI Endpoints — Flow Copilot (Phase 3)
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def _flow_ai_model() -> GenerativeModel:
    return GenerativeModel(
        GEMINI_MODEL,
        safety_settings=[
            SafetySetting(category=HarmCategory.HARM_CATEGORY_HATE_SPEECH,       threshold=HarmBlockThreshold.BLOCK_NONE),
            SafetySetting(category=HarmCategory.HARM_CATEGORY_DANGEROUS_CONTENT, threshold=HarmBlockThreshold.BLOCK_NONE),
            SafetySetting(category=HarmCategory.HARM_CATEGORY_SEXUALLY_EXPLICIT, threshold=HarmBlockThreshold.BLOCK_NONE),
            SafetySetting(category=HarmCategory.HARM_CATEGORY_HARASSMENT,        threshold=HarmBlockThreshold.BLOCK_NONE),
        ],
    )


def _clean_json_block(text: str) -> str:
    """Strip markdown code fences from AI JSON output."""
    text = text.strip()
    text = _re.sub(r'^```(?:json)?\s*', '', text, flags=_re.MULTILINE)
    text = _re.sub(r'```\s*$', '', text, flags=_re.MULTILINE)
    return text.strip()


@app.post("/api/ai/generate-flow")
async def ai_generate_flow(body: GenerateFlowRequest, request: Request):
    """Generate a full step sequence from a natural-language description."""
    _require_user(request)
    tables_hint = ""
    if body.available_tables:
        tables_hint = f"\nAvailable tables: {', '.join(body.available_tables)}"
    if body.database:
        tables_hint += f"\nDatabase: {body.database}"

    step_schema = """
Step types and their config shapes:
- sql_query:    { database, query, bind_result_to, limit? }
- condition:    { expression: {left, operator, right} | {operator:"and"|"or", conditions:[]}, then_steps:[], else_steps:[] }
- transform:    { source, bind_result_to, operations:[{op, field, ...}] }
  op values: filter, rename, add_field, remove_field, sort, limit, offset, unique, group_by, pick_fields
- loop:         { iterate_over, item_variable, sub_steps:[], bind_result_to }
- http_call:    { method, url, headers?, body?, bind_result_to, timeout? }
- set_variable: { variable, value }
- return:       { status_code, data?, message?, extra? }
- error_handler:{ status_code, message }
- merge:        { sources:[], bind_result_to, strategy:"concat"|"zip"|"dict" }

Expression operators: ==, !=, >, <, >=, <=, is_empty, is_not_empty, contains, not_contains, starts_with, ends_with, in, not_in, and, or
Variable syntax: {{variable_name}} or {{params.param_name}}
"""

    prompt = f"""You are an expert API logic designer. Generate a Logic Flow (step sequence) based on this description:

"{body.description}"
{tables_hint}

{step_schema}

Return ONLY valid JSON with this exact structure:
{{
  "name": "Flow name",
  "description": "What this flow does",
  "params": [{{ "name": "...", "type": "string|integer|float|boolean", "required": true|false, "default": null, "description": "..." }}],
  "steps": [
    {{
      "id": "s1",
      "type": "<step_type>",
      "name": "Descriptive step name",
      "config": {{ ... }},
      "on_error": "stop"
    }}
  ],
  "tags": ["tag1","tag2"]
}}

Rules:
- Always end with a "return" step
- Use {{{{params.name}}}} for input parameters
- Use descriptive step names
- Always include a condition step to handle empty results when querying data
- Generate realistic SQL queries based on the description
- Keep it production-ready
"""
    try:
        model  = _flow_ai_model()
        resp   = await asyncio.to_thread(model.generate_content, prompt)
        raw    = _clean_json_block(resp.text)
        result = json.loads(raw)
        return {"success": True, "flow": result}
    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        return {"success": False, "error": f"AI returned invalid JSON: {e}", "raw": resp.text if 'resp' in dir() else ""}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@app.post("/api/ai/suggest-steps")
async def ai_suggest_steps(body: SuggestStepsRequest, request: Request):
    """Suggest what step to add next based on what's already been built."""
    _require_user(request)
    steps_summary = json.dumps(
        [{"type": s.get("type"), "name": s.get("name"), "binds": s.get("config", {}).get("bind_result_to")}
         for s in body.current_steps],
        indent=2,
    )
    prompt = f"""You are an expert API logic designer. A user is building a logic flow.

Current steps:
{steps_summary}

Context: {body.context or "No additional context"}
Database: {body.database or "Not specified"}

Suggest the NEXT 3 steps the user should add. Be specific and practical.

Return ONLY valid JSON:
{{
  "suggestions": [
    {{
      "title": "Short title",
      "description": "What this step does and why",
      "step": {{
        "id": "suggested_1",
        "type": "<step_type>",
        "name": "Step name",
        "config": {{ ... }},
        "on_error": "stop"
      }}
    }}
  ]
}}
"""
    try:
        model  = _flow_ai_model()
        resp   = await asyncio.to_thread(model.generate_content, prompt)
        raw    = _clean_json_block(resp.text)
        result = json.loads(raw)
        return {"success": True, **result}
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return {"success": False, "error": "AI returned invalid JSON", "suggestions": []}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@app.post("/api/ai/explain-flow")
async def ai_explain_flow(body: ExplainFlowRequest, request: Request):
    """Explain a flow in plain English — for documentation."""
    _require_user(request)
    prompt = f"""You are a technical writer. Explain this API logic flow in clear, plain English.

Flow name: {body.name or "Unnamed Flow"}
Parameters: {json.dumps(body.params, indent=2)}
Steps: {json.dumps(body.steps, indent=2)}

Write a clear explanation covering:
1. What this API does (1-2 sentence summary)
2. Input parameters it accepts
3. Step-by-step walkthrough of the logic
4. What it returns
5. Error cases handled

Return JSON:
{{
  "summary": "One sentence summary",
  "full_explanation": "Full markdown explanation",
  "parameters_doc": [{{"name": "...", "description": "..."}}],
  "returns_doc": "What the API returns",
  "example_call": {{ "params": {{ ... }} }}
}}
"""
    try:
        model  = _flow_ai_model()
        resp   = await asyncio.to_thread(model.generate_content, prompt)
        raw    = _clean_json_block(resp.text)
        result = json.loads(raw)
        return {"success": True, **result}
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return {"success": False, "error": "AI returned invalid JSON",
                "summary": "Unable to generate explanation", "full_explanation": ""}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@app.post("/api/ai/debug-flow")
async def ai_debug_flow(body: DebugFlowRequest, request: Request):
    """Analyze a failed flow run and suggest fixes."""
    _require_user(request)
    # Load run details if flow_id + run_id provided
    run_info = {}
    if body.flow_id and body.run_id:
        conn = None
        try:
            conn = psycopg2.connect(host=DB_HOST, database="postgres", user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5)
            cur  = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
            cur.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {FLOW_RUNS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND flow_id = %s", (body.run_id, body.flow_id))
            row  = cur.fetchone()
            if row:
                run_info = dict(row)
        finally:
            if conn: conn.close()

    steps     = body.steps     or (run_info.get("result") and []) or []
    error_msg = body.error     or run_info.get("error", "")
    step_logs = body.step_logs or (run_info.get("step_logs") if isinstance(run_info.get("step_logs"), list) else [])
    params    = body.params    or (run_info.get("input_params") if isinstance(run_info.get("input_params"), dict) else {})

    prompt = f"""You are a debugging expert for API logic flows.

Error: {error_msg}
Input params used: {json.dumps(params, indent=2)}
Step execution logs: {json.dumps(step_logs, indent=2)}
Flow steps definition: {json.dumps(steps, indent=2)}

Analyze the error and provide:
1. Root cause of the failure
2. Which step failed and why
3. Specific fix (modified step config or new steps to add)
4. How to prevent this in the future

Return JSON:
{{
  "root_cause": "Clear explanation of what went wrong",
  "failed_step": "Step name or ID",
  "fix_description": "What to change",
  "fixed_steps": [<corrected step objects if applicable>],
  "prevention": "How to avoid this in the future"
}}
"""
    try:
        model  = _flow_ai_model()
        resp   = await asyncio.to_thread(model.generate_content, prompt)
        raw    = _clean_json_block(resp.text)
        result = json.loads(raw)
        return {"success": True, **result}
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return {"success": False, "error": "AI returned invalid JSON",
                "root_cause": "Could not analyze error automatically. Please review step logs manually."}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@app.post("/api/ai/detect-flow-pattern")
async def ai_detect_pattern(body: SuggestStepsRequest, request: Request):
    """Detect the pattern of an in-progress flow and suggest a matching template."""
    _require_user(request)
    steps_summary = json.dumps(
        [{"type": s.get("type"), "name": s.get("name")} for s in body.current_steps],
        indent=2,
    )
    template_names = [t["name"] for t in FLOW_TEMPLATES]
    prompt = f"""Analyze these flow steps and determine if they match a known pattern.

Current steps: {steps_summary}

Known templates: {json.dumps(template_names)}

Return JSON:
{{
  "detected_pattern": "Template name or null if no match",
  "confidence": 0.0,
  "recommendation": "What template to use or why this is a custom pattern",
  "matching_template_id": "template id or null"
}}
"""
    try:
        model  = _flow_ai_model()
        resp   = await asyncio.to_thread(model.generate_content, prompt)
        raw    = _clean_json_block(resp.text)
        result = json.loads(raw)
        return {"success": True, **result}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"success": False, "detected_pattern": None, "confidence": 0, "recommendation": str(e)}


@app.post("/api/ai/auto-params")
async def ai_auto_params(request: Request, body: Dict[str, Any] = Body(...)):
    """Auto-detect params from flow steps and generate param definitions."""
    _require_user(request)
    steps = body.get("steps", [])
    steps_str = json.dumps(steps, indent=2)
    prompt = f"""Analyze these flow steps and extract all {{{{params.xxx}}}} references.

Steps: {steps_str}

Return a JSON array of param definitions:
[{{ "name": "param_name", "type": "string|integer|float|boolean", "required": true|false, "default": null, "description": "what this param is for" }}]

Return ONLY the JSON array.
"""
    try:
        model  = _flow_ai_model()
        resp   = await asyncio.to_thread(model.generate_content, prompt)
        raw    = _clean_json_block(resp.text)
        params = json.loads(raw)
        return {"success": True, "params": params}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"success": False, "params": [], "error": str(e)}


# ── PgVector Conversion ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

PGVECTOR_JOBS_TABLE = "pgvector_conversion_jobs"
PGVECTOR_EMBEDDING_MODEL = "text-embedding-005"
PGVECTOR_DIMS = 768  # text-embedding-005 output dimension


def _ensure_pgvector_jobs_table():
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {PGVECTOR_JOBS_TABLE} (
                id              VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
                source_database VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                source_schema   VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'public',
                source_table    VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                target_table    VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                text_columns    TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
                metadata_columns TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
                vector_column   VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'embedding',
                embedding_model VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT '{PGVECTOR_EMBEDDING_MODEL}',
                sync_mode       VARCHAR(20)  DEFAULT 'incremental',
                run_mode        VARCHAR(20)  DEFAULT 'manual',
                status          VARCHAR(20)  DEFAULT 'pending',
                total_rows      INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                processed_rows  INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                inserted_rows   INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                updated_rows    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                deleted_rows    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                skipped_rows    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                error_message   TEXT DEFAULT '',
                created_by      VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT '',
                created_at      TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW(),
                updated_at      TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW(),
                completed_at    TIMESTAMP
            )
        """)
        # Add new columns to existing tables (idempotent)
        for col_def in [
            "sync_mode VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'incremental'",
            "chunk_strategy VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'full_row'",
            "chunk_size INTEGER DEFAULT 500",
            "chunk_overlap INTEGER DEFAULT 50",
            "inserted_rows INTEGER DEFAULT 0",
            "updated_rows INTEGER DEFAULT 0",
            "deleted_rows INTEGER DEFAULT 0",
            "skipped_rows INTEGER DEFAULT 0",
        ]:
            try:
                cursor.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {PGVECTOR_JOBS_TABLE} ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS {col_def}")
            except Exception:
                pass
        connection.commit()
        logger.info(f"Ensured {PGVECTOR_JOBS_TABLE} table exists")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to create pgvector jobs table: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


_ensure_pgvector_jobs_table()


class PgVectorConvertRequest(BaseModel):
    source_database: str = Field(..., description="Source database name")
    source_schema: str = Field("public", description="Source schema")
    source_table: str = Field(..., description="Source table to vectorize")
    target_table: str = Field("", description="Target table name; auto-derived if empty")
    text_columns: List[str] = Field(..., description="Columns whose text will be concatenated and embedded")
    metadata_columns: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Columns stored in _metadata JSONB for RAG retrieval context")
    vector_column: str = Field("embedding", description="Name for the vector column in the target table")
    sync_mode: str = Field("incremental", description="incremental | full_refresh | recreate")
    chunk_strategy: str = Field("full_row", description="full_row | fixed — how to split text before embedding")
    chunk_size: int = Field(500, ge=50, le=8000, description="Max characters per chunk (fixed strategy)")
    chunk_overlap: int = Field(50, ge=0, le=500, description="Overlap characters between adjacent chunks")
    run_mode: str = Field("now", description="now | schedule")
    schedule_cron: str = Field("", description="Cron expression when run_mode=schedule")
    batch_size: int = Field(50, ge=1, le=500, description="Source rows to process per batch")


class PgVectorSearchRequest(BaseModel):
    database: str = Field(..., description="Database containing the vector table")
    schema: str = Field("public", description="Schema of the vector table")
    table: str = Field(..., description="Vector table name")
    question: str = Field(..., description="Natural-language question to embed and search")
    top_k: int = Field(5, ge=1, le=50, description="Number of results to return")
    vector_column: str = Field("embedding", description="Vector column name")
    metadata_filter: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict, description="Optional exact-match filters on _metadata keys")


def _pgvector_update_job(job_id: str, **fields):
    """Update job fields in postgres (fire-and-forget safe)."""
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=5,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        set_clauses = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in fields)
        values = list(fields.values()) + [job_id]
        cursor.execute(
            f"UPDATE {PGVECTOR_JOBS_TABLE} SET {set_clauses}, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = %s",
            values,
        )
        connection.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"pgvector job update failed: {e}")
    finally:
        if connection:
            connection.close()


def _pg_col_type(col: dict) -> str:
    """Map information_schema data_type to a usable PostgreSQL DDL type."""
    dt = col["data_type"]
    if dt in ("character varying", "character"):
        ml = col.get("character_maximum_length")
        return f"VARCHAR({ml})" if ml else "TEXT"
    mapping = {
        "integer": "INTEGER", "bigint": "BIGINT", "smallint": "SMALLINT",
        "boolean": "BOOLEAN", "double precision": "FLOAT", "real": "FLOAT",
        "numeric": "NUMERIC", "text": "TEXT", "jsonb": "JSONB", "json": "JSON",
        "uuid": "UUID", "date": "DATE",
        "timestamp without time zone": "TIMESTAMP",
        "timestamp with time zone": "TIMESTAMPTZ",
    }
    return mapping.get(dt, "TEXT")


def _detect_primary_keys(cursor, schema: str, table: str) -> list:
    """Return list of PK column names for the given table."""
    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT kcu.column_name
        FROM information_schema.table_constraints tc
        JOIN information_schema.key_column_usage kcu
            ON tc.constraint_name = kcu.constraint_name
            AND tc.table_schema = kcu.table_schema
        WHERE tc.constraint_type = 'PRIMARY KEY'
            AND tc.table_schema = %s
            AND tc.table_name = %s
        ORDER BY kcu.ordinal_position
    """, (schema, table))
    return [r["column_name"] for r in cursor.fetchall()]


def _make_row_key(row: dict, pk_cols: list) -> str:
    """Build a composite PK string from a row dict."""
    return "|".join(str(row.get(c, "")) for c in pk_cols)


def _make_row_hash(row: dict, text_columns: list) -> str:
    """MD5 of the concatenated text column values — used to detect changes."""
    text = " ".join(str(row.get(c, "") or "") for c in text_columns)
    return hashlib.md5(text.encode("utf-8", errors="replace")).hexdigest()


def _split_into_chunks(text: str, chunk_size: int, overlap: int) -> list:
    """Split text into overlapping fixed-size character chunks."""
    if not text:
        return ["empty"]
    if chunk_size <= 0 or len(text) <= chunk_size:
        return [text]
    step = max(1, chunk_size - overlap)
    return [text[i: i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(text), step)]


def _insert_chunks(cur, full_target: str, col_list: str, vector_column: str,
                   row: dict, all_col_names: list, base_pk: str, row_hash: str,
                   chunks: list, metadata_json: str):
    """Insert one or more chunk rows into the vector table."""
    insert_cols = (
        col_list
        + f', "{vector_column}", _base_pk, _src_pk, _row_hash, _chunk_index, _chunk_text, _metadata'
    )
    n_cols = len(all_col_names) + 7  # 7 extra: vector, _base_pk, _src_pk, _row_hash, _chunk_index, _chunk_text, _metadata (_synced_at uses DEFAULT)
    placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * n_cols)

    for idx, chunk_text in enumerate(chunks):
        src_pk = f"{base_pk}::{idx}" if len(chunks) > 1 else base_pk
        vector = _embed_text(chunk_text) or [0.0] * PGVECTOR_DIMS
        row_vals = (
            [row.get(c) for c in all_col_names]
            + [json.dumps(vector), base_pk, src_pk, row_hash, idx, chunk_text, metadata_json]
        )
        cur.execute(
            f"INSERT INTO {full_target} ({insert_cols}) VALUES ({placeholders})",
            row_vals,
        )


def _run_pgvector_conversion(job_id: str, req: dict):
    """
    Background worker that syncs a source table into a pgvector table.

    sync_mode:
      incremental  — only embed new/changed source rows; delete rows removed from source.
                     Uses _base_pk + _row_hash for change detection — safe for chunked tables.
      full_refresh — TRUNCATE vector table, re-embed all rows (structure + indexes kept).
      recreate     — DROP + CREATE vector table from scratch, then embed all rows.

    chunk_strategy:
      full_row — one vector per source row (concatenation of text_columns).
      fixed    — split concatenated text into overlapping chunks; one vector per chunk.
                 _src_pk = "{base_pk}::{chunk_index}", _base_pk = "{base_pk}".
    """
    source_db       = req["source_database"]
    source_schema   = req["source_schema"]
    source_table    = req["source_table"]
    target_table    = req["target_table"]
    text_columns    = req["text_columns"]
    metadata_columns = req.get("metadata_columns", [])
    vector_column   = req["vector_column"]
    sync_mode       = req.get("sync_mode", "incremental")
    chunk_strategy  = req.get("chunk_strategy", "full_row")
    chunk_size      = req.get("chunk_size", 500)
    chunk_overlap   = req.get("chunk_overlap", 50)
    batch_size      = req.get("batch_size", 50)

    _pgvector_update_job(job_id, status="running")
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database=source_db,
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)

        # Enable pgvector extension
        cur.execute("CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector")
        conn.commit()

        # ── Source column definitions ──────────────────────────────────
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT column_name, data_type, character_maximum_length, is_nullable
            FROM information_schema.columns
            WHERE table_schema = %s AND table_name = %s
            ORDER BY ordinal_position
        """, (source_schema, source_table))
        col_defs = cur.fetchall()
        if not col_defs:
            raise ValueError(f"Table {source_schema}.{source_table} not found or has no columns")

        all_col_names = [c["column_name"] for c in col_defs]

        # ── Primary key detection ──────────────────────────────────────
        pk_cols = _detect_primary_keys(cur, source_schema, source_table)
        if not pk_cols and sync_mode == "incremental":
            logger.warning(f"No PK found on {source_table}; falling back to full_refresh")
            sync_mode = "full_refresh"

        col_sql      = ", ".join(f'"{c["column_name"]}" {_pg_col_type(c)} DEFAULT NULL' for c in col_defs)
        col_list     = ", ".join(f'"{c}"' for c in all_col_names)
        full_target  = f'"{source_schema}"."{target_table}"'

        # ── Table existence check ──────────────────────────────────────
        chk = conn.cursor()
        chk.execute("""
            SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.tables
            WHERE table_schema = %s AND table_name = %s
        """, (source_schema, target_table))
        target_exists = chk.fetchone() is not None

        # ── Create / reset vector table ────────────────────────────────
        if sync_mode == "recreate" or not target_exists:
            cur.execute(f"DROP TABLE IF EXISTS {full_target}")
            cur.execute(f"""
                CREATE TABLE {full_target} (
                    {col_sql},
                    "{vector_column}"  vector({PGVECTOR_DIMS}),
                    _base_pk           TEXT,
                    _src_pk            TEXT,
                    _row_hash          TEXT,
                    _chunk_index       INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                    _chunk_text        TEXT,
                    _metadata          JSONB,
                    _synced_at         TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW()
                )
            """)
            cur.execute(f'CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "{target_table}_src_pk_idx" ON {full_target} (_src_pk)')
            cur.execute(f'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "{target_table}_base_pk_idx"  ON {full_target} (_base_pk)')
            cur.execute(f'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "{target_table}_{vector_column}_idx" ON {full_target} USING hnsw ("{vector_column}" vector_cosine_ops)')
            cur.execute(f'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "{target_table}_metadata_idx"  ON {full_target} USING gin (_metadata)')
            conn.commit()
            target_exists = False

        elif sync_mode == "full_refresh":
            cur.execute(f"TRUNCATE {full_target}")
            conn.commit()
            target_exists = False

        # Migrate older tables that lack the new columns (idempotent)
        for extra in [
            "_base_pk TEXT", "_src_pk TEXT", "_row_hash TEXT",
            "_chunk_index INTEGER DEFAULT 0", "_chunk_text TEXT",
            "_metadata JSONB", "_synced_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW()",
        ]:
            try:
                cur.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {full_target} ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS {extra}")
            except Exception:
                pass
        for idx_sql in [
            f'CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "{target_table}_src_pk_idx"  ON {full_target} (_src_pk)',
            f'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "{target_table}_base_pk_idx"        ON {full_target} (_base_pk)',
            f'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "{target_table}_{vector_column}_idx" ON {full_target} USING hnsw ("{vector_column}" vector_cosine_ops)',
            f'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "{target_table}_metadata_idx"        ON {full_target} USING gin (_metadata)',
        ]:
            try:
                cur.execute(idx_sql)
            except Exception:
                pass
        conn.commit()

        # ── Count source rows ──────────────────────────────────────────
        cur.execute(f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM "{source_schema}"."{source_table}"')
        total_rows = cur.fetchone()["count"]
        _pgvector_update_job(job_id, total_rows=total_rows)

        # ── Load existing index for incremental mode ───────────────────
        # Key: _base_pk (one entry per source row, regardless of chunk count)
        # Value: _row_hash
        existing: dict = {}
        if sync_mode == "incremental" and target_exists:
            idx_cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
            idx_cur.execute(f"SELECT DISTINCT _base_pk, _row_hash FROM {full_target} WHERE _base_pk IS NOT NULL")
            for r in idx_cur.fetchall():
                existing[r["_base_pk"]] = r.get("_row_hash") or ""

        # ── Stream source rows and sync ────────────────────────────────
        src_cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        src_cur.execute(f'SELECT {col_list} FROM "{source_schema}"."{source_table}"')

        inserted = updated = skipped = 0
        seen_base_pks: set = set()
        row_counter = 0

        while True:
            batch = src_cur.fetchmany(batch_size)
            if not batch:
                break

            for row in batch:
                base_pk  = _make_row_key(row, pk_cols) if pk_cols else str(row_counter)
                row_hash = _make_row_hash(row, text_columns)
                seen_base_pks.add(base_pk)
                row_counter += 1

                # Build text and chunks
                full_text = " ".join(str(row.get(c) or "") for c in text_columns).strip() or "empty"
                chunks = (
                    _split_into_chunks(full_text, chunk_size, chunk_overlap)
                    if chunk_strategy == "fixed"
                    else [full_text]
                )

                # Build metadata JSON
                meta = {c: row.get(c) for c in metadata_columns if c in row}
                metadata_json = json.dumps(meta) if meta else None

                existing_hash = existing.get(base_pk)

                if existing_hash is None:
                    # NEW source row — insert all its chunks
                    _insert_chunks(cur, full_target, col_list, vector_column,
                                   row, all_col_names, base_pk, row_hash,
                                   chunks, metadata_json)
                    inserted += 1

                elif existing_hash != row_hash:
                    # CHANGED source row — delete old chunks, insert new chunks
                    cur.execute(f"DELETE FROM {full_target} WHERE _base_pk = %s", (base_pk,))
                    _insert_chunks(cur, full_target, col_list, vector_column,
                                   row, all_col_names, base_pk, row_hash,
                                   chunks, metadata_json)
                    updated += 1

                else:
                    # UNCHANGED — skip entirely (no embedding API call)
                    skipped += 1

            conn.commit()
            processed = inserted + updated + skipped
            _pgvector_update_job(job_id,
                                 processed_rows=processed,
                                 inserted_rows=inserted,
                                 updated_rows=updated,
                                 skipped_rows=skipped)

        # ── Delete source rows that no longer exist (incremental) ──────
        deleted = 0
        if sync_mode == "incremental" and existing:
            orphans = [pk for pk in existing if pk not in seen_base_pks]
            if orphans:
                for pk in orphans:
                    cur.execute(f"DELETE FROM {full_target} WHERE _base_pk = %s", (pk,))
                    deleted += 1
                conn.commit()
                _pgvector_update_job(job_id, deleted_rows=deleted)

        _pgvector_update_job(
            job_id,
            status="completed",
            processed_rows=inserted + updated + skipped,
            inserted_rows=inserted,
            updated_rows=updated,
            deleted_rows=deleted,
            skipped_rows=skipped,
            completed_at=datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
        )
        logger.info(
            f"PgVector job {job_id} done ({chunk_strategy}) — "
            f"inserted={inserted} updated={updated} deleted={deleted} skipped={skipped}"
        )

    except Exception as e:
        err = str(e)[:1000]
        logger.error(f"PgVector job {job_id} failed: {err}")
        _pgvector_update_job(job_id, status="failed", error_message=err)
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


@app.post("/api/pgvector/convert")
async def pgvector_convert(req: PgVectorConvertRequest, request: Request):
    """Start a pgvector conversion job for a source table."""
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:16]
    target_table = req.target_table or f"{req.source_table}_vectors"

    if not req.text_columns:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="At least one text_columns entry is required")

    req_dict = req.model_dump()
    req_dict["target_table"] = target_table

    # Persist job record
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {PGVECTOR_JOBS_TABLE}
                (id, source_database, source_schema, source_table, target_table,
                 text_columns, metadata_columns, vector_column, embedding_model,
                 sync_mode, chunk_strategy, chunk_size, chunk_overlap,
                 run_mode, status, created_by)
            VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s)
        """, (
            job_id, req.source_database, req.source_schema, req.source_table, target_table,
            json.dumps(req.text_columns), json.dumps(req.metadata_columns),
            req.vector_column, PGVECTOR_EMBEDDING_MODEL,
            req.sync_mode, req.chunk_strategy, req.chunk_size, req.chunk_overlap,
            req.run_mode, "pending", user_id,
        ))
        connection.commit()
    finally:
        if connection: connection.close()

    await _log_audit_async(user_id, "pgvector_convert",
                           database_name=req.source_database,
                           table_name=req.source_table,
                           details=f"job_id={job_id} target={target_table} sync_mode={req.sync_mode} run_mode={req.run_mode}")

    if req.run_mode == "now":
        asyncio.create_task(asyncio.to_thread(_run_pgvector_conversion, job_id, req_dict))
        return {"success": True, "job_id": job_id, "status": "running",
                "message": f"Sync started ({req.sync_mode}). Target: {req.source_schema}.{target_table}"}

    # schedule mode — create a scheduler job that references this conversion
    if req.run_mode == "schedule" and req.schedule_cron:
        sched_job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:16]
        sched_conn = None
        try:
            sched_conn = psycopg2.connect(
                host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
                user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
            )
            sc = sched_conn.cursor()
            now_str = datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
            steps = json.dumps([{
                "id": str(uuid.uuid4())[:8],
                "name": "PgVector Embed",
                "type": "pgvector_embed",
                "order": 0, "enabled": True,
                "config": req_dict,
                "conditions": [],
            }])
            sc.execute(f"""
                INSERT INTO {SCHEDULER_JOBS_TABLE}
                    (id, name, description, enabled, schedule_type, schedule_config,
                     steps, created_at, updated_at, created_by)
                VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s)
            """, (
                sched_job_id,
                f"PgVector: {req.source_table} → {target_table}",
                f"Auto-embed {req.source_table} into {target_table}",
                True, "cron", json.dumps({"cron": req.schedule_cron}),
                steps, now_str, now_str, user_id,
            ))
            sched_conn.commit()
        finally:
            if sched_conn: sched_conn.close()

        _pgvector_update_job(job_id, status="scheduled")
        return {"success": True, "job_id": job_id, "scheduler_job_id": sched_job_id,
                "status": "scheduled", "message": f"Scheduled on cron: {req.schedule_cron}"}

    return {"success": True, "job_id": job_id, "status": "pending",
            "message": "Job created. Use run_mode=now to execute immediately."}


@app.get("/api/pgvector/jobs")
async def pgvector_list_jobs(request: Request, database: str = "", limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200)):
    """List pgvector conversion jobs."""
    _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        if database:
            cursor.execute(
                f"SELECT * FROM {PGVECTOR_JOBS_TABLE} WHERE source_database=%s ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT %s",
                (database, limit),
            )
        else:
            cursor.execute(
                f"SELECT * FROM {PGVECTOR_JOBS_TABLE} ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT %s", (limit,)
            )
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        jobs = []
        for r in rows:
            j = dict(r)
            j["text_columns"] = json.loads(j.get("text_columns") or "[]")
            j["metadata_columns"] = json.loads(j.get("metadata_columns") or "[]")
            jobs.append(j)
        return {"success": True, "jobs": jobs, "count": len(jobs)}
    finally:
        if connection: connection.close()


@app.get("/api/pgvector/jobs/{job_id}")
async def pgvector_get_job(job_id: str, request: Request):
    """Get status of a specific pgvector conversion job."""
    _require_user(request)
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cursor = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {PGVECTOR_JOBS_TABLE} WHERE id=%s", (job_id,))
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if not row:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Job not found")
        j = dict(row)
        j["text_columns"] = json.loads(j.get("text_columns") or "[]")
        j["metadata_columns"] = json.loads(j.get("metadata_columns") or "[]")
        total = j.get("total_rows") or 0
        processed = j.get("processed_rows") or 0
        j["progress_pct"] = round((processed / total * 100), 1) if total > 0 else 0
        return {"success": True, "job": j}
    finally:
        if connection: connection.close()


@app.post("/api/pgvector/search")
async def pgvector_search(req: PgVectorSearchRequest, request: Request):
    """
    RAG similarity search against a pgvector table.
    Embeds the question with text-embedding-005, then returns the top-k
    most similar chunks with their metadata and similarity score.
    """
    _require_user(request)

    if not req.question.strip():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="question cannot be empty")

    # Embed the question
    q_vector = await asyncio.to_thread(_embed_text, req.question)
    if not q_vector:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Failed to generate question embedding")

    connection = None
    try:
        connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database=req.database,
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = connection.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        full_target = f'"{req.schema}"."{req.table}"'
        vec_literal = "[" + ",".join(str(v) for v in q_vector) + "]"

        # Build optional metadata filter (exact-match on JSONB keys)
        filter_clause = ""
        filter_vals: list = []
        if req.metadata_filter:
            parts = []
            for k, v in req.metadata_filter.items():
                parts.append("_metadata->>%s = %s")
                filter_vals += [k, str(v)]
            filter_clause = "WHERE " + " AND ".join(parts)

        cur.execute(f"""
            SELECT *,
                   1 - ("{req.vector_column}" <=> %s::vector) AS similarity
            FROM {full_target}
            {filter_clause}
            ORDER BY "{req.vector_column}" <=> %s::vector
            LIMIT %s
        """, [vec_literal] + filter_vals + [vec_literal, req.top_k])

        rows = cur.fetchall()
        results = []
        for r in rows:
            r_dict = dict(r)
            # Parse _metadata JSONB if stored as string
            meta = r_dict.get("_metadata")
            if isinstance(meta, str):
                try:
                    meta = json.loads(meta)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            results.append({
                "similarity": round(float(r_dict.get("similarity", 0)), 4),
                "chunk_text": r_dict.get("_chunk_text", ""),
                "chunk_index": r_dict.get("_chunk_index", 0),
                "base_pk": r_dict.get("_base_pk", ""),
                "src_pk": r_dict.get("_src_pk", ""),
                "metadata": meta or {},
            })

        return {
            "success": True,
            "question": req.question,
            "table": req.table,
            "results": results,
            "count": len(results),
            "embedding_model": PGVECTOR_EMBEDDING_MODEL,
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
    finally:
        if connection: connection.close()


# ── Chat Session API ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

CHAT_APIS_TABLE     = "rag_chat_apis"
CHAT_SESSIONS_TABLE = "rag_chat_sessions"
CHAT_MESSAGES_TABLE = "rag_chat_messages"


def _ensure_chat_api_tables():
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {CHAT_APIS_TABLE} (
                id                  VARCHAR(50)  PRIMARY KEY,
                name                VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
                description         TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                system_prompt       TEXT         DEFAULT '',
                vector_database     VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                vector_schema       VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'public',
                vector_table        VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                vector_column       VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'embedding',
                top_k               INTEGER      DEFAULT 5,
                temperature         FLOAT        DEFAULT 0.7,
                max_tokens          INTEGER      DEFAULT 1024,
                max_history_turns   INTEGER      DEFAULT 10,
                auth_enabled        BOOLEAN      DEFAULT FALSE,
                auth_username       VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                auth_password_hash  VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT '',
                created_by          VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT '',
                created_at          TIMESTAMP    DEFAULT NOW(),
                updated_at          TIMESTAMP    DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cur.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {CHAT_SESSIONS_TABLE} (
                id              VARCHAR(50)  PRIMARY KEY,
                chat_api_id     VARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL,
                title           VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT 'New Conversation',
                created_by      VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT '',
                created_at      TIMESTAMP    DEFAULT NOW(),
                last_active     TIMESTAMP    DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cur.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_chat_sess_api ON {CHAT_SESSIONS_TABLE} (chat_api_id)")
        cur.execute(f"""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {CHAT_MESSAGES_TABLE} (
                id              SERIAL       PRIMARY KEY,
                session_id      VARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL,
                chat_api_id     VARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL,
                role            VARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL,
                content         TEXT         NOT NULL,
                sources         TEXT         DEFAULT '[]',
                token_count     INTEGER      DEFAULT 0,
                created_at      TIMESTAMP    DEFAULT NOW()
            )
        """)
        cur.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_chat_msg_session ON {CHAT_MESSAGES_TABLE} (session_id, created_at)")
        conn.commit()
        logger.info("Chat API tables ensured")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to create chat API tables: {e}")
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


_ensure_chat_api_tables()


# ── Pydantic models ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ChatAPIConfig(BaseModel):
    id: Optional[str] = None
    name: str = Field(..., description="Chat API name")
    description: str = ""
    system_prompt: str = Field("", description="LLM system instruction")
    vector_database: str = Field("", description="Database that holds the vector table")
    vector_schema: str = "public"
    vector_table: str = Field("", description="PgVector table to search for RAG context")
    vector_column: str = "embedding"
    top_k: int = Field(5, ge=1, le=20)
    temperature: float = Field(0.7, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    max_tokens: int = Field(1024, ge=128, le=8192)
    max_history_turns: int = Field(10, ge=1, le=50)
    auth_enabled: bool = False
    auth_username: str = ""
    auth_password: str = Field("", description="Plain text on input — stored as SHA-256 hash")


class ChatMessageRequest(BaseModel):
    message: str = Field(..., description="User message")
    session_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Existing session ID; omit to create new")


# ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _hash_pw(password: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(password.encode()).hexdigest()


def _verify_pw(password: str, stored_hash: str) -> bool:
    return _hash_pw(password) == stored_hash


def _load_chat_api(api_id: str) -> Optional[dict]:
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cur.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {CHAT_APIS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (api_id,))
        row = cur.fetchone()
        return dict(row) if row else None
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


def _check_chat_auth(api_config: dict, authorization: str) -> bool:
    """Returns True if auth passes (or auth is disabled)."""
    if not api_config.get("auth_enabled"):
        return True
    if not authorization:
        return False
    try:
        scheme, creds = authorization.split(" ", 1)
        if scheme.lower() != "basic":
            return False
        decoded = base64.b64decode(creds).decode()
        username, password = decoded.split(":", 1)
        return (
            username == api_config.get("auth_username", "")
            and _verify_pw(password, api_config.get("auth_password_hash", ""))
        )
    except Exception:
        return False


def _ensure_session(api_id: str, session_id: Optional[str], created_by: str = "") -> str:
    """Return existing session_id or create a new one."""
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        if session_id:
            cur.execute(f"SELECT id FROM {CHAT_SESSIONS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND chat_api_id = %s",
                        (session_id, api_id))
            if cur.fetchone():
                # update last_active
                conn.cursor().execute(
                    f"UPDATE {CHAT_SESSIONS_TABLE} SET last_active = NOW() WHERE id = %s", (session_id,)
                )
                conn.commit()
                return session_id
        # Create new session
        new_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:20]
        conn.cursor().execute(
            f"INSERT INTO {CHAT_SESSIONS_TABLE} (id, chat_api_id, created_by) VALUES (%s, %s, %s)",
            (new_id, api_id, created_by),
        )
        conn.commit()
        return new_id
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


def _load_history(session_id: str, max_turns: int) -> list:
    """Return last max_turns user+assistant message pairs."""
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        # Fetch last max_turns*2 messages (each turn = user+assistant)
        cur.execute(f"""
            SELECT role, content FROM {CHAT_MESSAGES_TABLE}
            WHERE session_id = %s
            ORDER BY created_at DESC
            LIMIT %s
        """, (session_id, max_turns * 2))
        rows = cur.fetchall()
        return list(reversed(rows))  # oldest first
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


def _store_messages(session_id: str, api_id: str,
                    user_msg: str, assistant_msg: str,
                    sources: list, token_count: int):
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute(
            f"INSERT INTO {CHAT_MESSAGES_TABLE} (session_id, chat_api_id, role, content) VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s)",
            (session_id, api_id, "user", user_msg),
        )
        cur.execute(
            f"INSERT INTO {CHAT_MESSAGES_TABLE} (session_id, chat_api_id, role, content, sources, token_count) VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s)",
            (session_id, api_id, "assistant", assistant_msg, json.dumps(sources), token_count),
        )
        cur.execute(
            f"UPDATE {CHAT_SESSIONS_TABLE} SET last_active = NOW(), title = CASE WHEN title = 'New Conversation' THEN %s ELSE title END WHERE id = %s",
            (user_msg[:60], session_id),
        )
        conn.commit()
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


async def _chat_invoke_core(api_config: dict, session_id: str, user_message: str) -> dict:
    """
    Core RAG + LLM invoke:
    1. Load conversation history
    2. Embed user message → search vector table
    3. Build Gemini Content list (system + history + current message with context)
    4. Call Gemini
    5. Store messages
    6. Return {response, session_id, sources, turn_id}
    """
    max_turns   = api_config.get("max_history_turns", 10)
    top_k       = api_config.get("top_k", 5)
    temperature = float(api_config.get("temperature", 0.7))
    max_tokens  = int(api_config.get("max_tokens", 1024))
    system_prompt = api_config.get("system_prompt", "You are a helpful assistant.")

    # ── Load conversation history ─────────────────────────────────────
    history_rows = await asyncio.to_thread(_load_history, session_id, max_turns)

    # ── RAG: embed query + search vector table ────────────────────────
    sources = []
    rag_context = ""
    if api_config.get("vector_table") and api_config.get("vector_database"):
        try:
            q_vec = await asyncio.to_thread(_embed_text, user_message)
            if q_vec:
                rag_conn = psycopg2.connect(
                    host=DB_HOST, database=api_config["vector_database"],
                    user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
                )
                rag_cur = rag_conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
                vec_lit = "[" + ",".join(str(v) for v in q_vec) + "]"
                vc = api_config.get("vector_column", "embedding")
                schema = api_config.get("vector_schema", "public")
                tbl = api_config["vector_table"]
                rag_cur.execute(f"""
                    SELECT _chunk_text, _metadata,
                           1 - ("{vc}" <=> %s::vector) AS similarity
                    FROM "{schema}"."{tbl}"
                    ORDER BY "{vc}" <=> %s::vector
                    LIMIT %s
                """, [vec_lit, vec_lit, top_k])
                for r in rag_cur.fetchall():
                    chunk = r.get("_chunk_text") or ""
                    meta  = r.get("_metadata") or {}
                    if isinstance(meta, str):
                        try: meta = json.loads(meta)
                        except: meta = {}
                    sim = round(float(r.get("similarity", 0)), 4)
                    sources.append({"chunk_text": chunk, "similarity": sim, "metadata": meta})
                rag_conn.close()
                if sources:
                    rag_context = "\n\n[Retrieved Context]\n" + "\n---\n".join(
                        f"(similarity {s['similarity']}) {s['chunk_text']}" for s in sources
                    )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"RAG search failed for chat API {api_config['id']}: {e}")

    # ── Build Gemini system instruction ──────────────────────────────
    system_instruction = system_prompt
    if rag_context:
        system_instruction += rag_context

    # ── Build Content list ────────────────────────────────────────────
    # History turns (alternating user / model)
    history_contents = []
    for row in history_rows:
        role = "user" if row["role"] == "user" else "model"
        history_contents.append(Content(role=role, parts=[Part.from_text(row["content"])]))

    # ── Call Gemini ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _gemini_call():
        model = GenerativeModel(
            model_name=GEMINI_MODEL,
            system_instruction=system_instruction,
        )
        chat = model.start_chat(history=history_contents)
        resp = chat.send_message(
            user_message,
            generation_config={
                "temperature": temperature,
                "max_output_tokens": max_tokens,
            },
        )
        return resp

    resp = await asyncio.to_thread(_gemini_call)
    answer = resp.text
    token_count = getattr(getattr(resp, "usage_metadata", None), "total_token_count", 0) or 0

    # ── Store messages ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    await asyncio.to_thread(
        _store_messages, session_id, api_config["id"],
        user_message, answer, sources, token_count,
    )

    return {
        "response": answer,
        "session_id": session_id,
        "sources": sources,
        "token_count": token_count,
    }

@app.post("/api/chat-apis")
async def create_chat_api(config: ChatAPIConfig, request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    api_id  = str(uuid.uuid4())[:20]
    now     = datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
    pw_hash = _hash_pw(config.auth_password) if config.auth_password else ""
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute(f"""
            INSERT INTO {CHAT_APIS_TABLE}
                (id, name, description, system_prompt,
                 vector_database, vector_schema, vector_table, vector_column,
                 top_k, temperature, max_tokens, max_history_turns,
                 auth_enabled, auth_username, auth_password_hash,
                 created_by, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s)
        """, (
            api_id, config.name, config.description, config.system_prompt,
            config.vector_database, config.vector_schema, config.vector_table, config.vector_column,
            config.top_k, config.temperature, config.max_tokens, config.max_history_turns,
            config.auth_enabled, config.auth_username, pw_hash,
            user_id, now, now,
        ))
        conn.commit()
        return {"success": True, "id": api_id}
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


@app.get("/api/chat-apis")
async def list_chat_apis(request: Request):
    _require_user(request)
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cur.execute(f"""
            SELECT id, name, description, system_prompt, vector_database, vector_schema,
                   vector_table, vector_column, top_k, temperature, max_tokens,
                   max_history_turns, auth_enabled, auth_username,
                   created_by, created_at, updated_at
            FROM {CHAT_APIS_TABLE} ORDER BY created_at DESC
        """)
        apis = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
        return {"success": True, "apis": apis, "count": len(apis)}
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


@app.get("/api/chat-apis/{api_id}")
async def get_chat_api(api_id: str, request: Request):
    _require_user(request)
    api = _load_chat_api(api_id)
    if not api:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Chat API not found")
    api.pop("auth_password_hash", None)
    return {"success": True, "api": api}


@app.put("/api/chat-apis/{api_id}")
async def update_chat_api(api_id: str, config: ChatAPIConfig, request: Request):
    _require_user(request)
    api = _load_chat_api(api_id)
    if not api:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Chat API not found")
    pw_hash = _hash_pw(config.auth_password) if config.auth_password else api.get("auth_password_hash", "")
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute(f"""
            UPDATE {CHAT_APIS_TABLE} SET
                name=%s, description=%s, system_prompt=%s,
                vector_database=%s, vector_schema=%s, vector_table=%s, vector_column=%s,
                top_k=%s, temperature=%s, max_tokens=%s, max_history_turns=%s,
                auth_enabled=%s, auth_username=%s, auth_password_hash=%s,
                updated_at=NOW()
            WHERE id=%s
        """, (
            config.name, config.description, config.system_prompt,
            config.vector_database, config.vector_schema, config.vector_table, config.vector_column,
            config.top_k, config.temperature, config.max_tokens, config.max_history_turns,
            config.auth_enabled, config.auth_username, pw_hash,
            api_id,
        ))
        conn.commit()
        return {"success": True}
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


@app.delete("/api/chat-apis/{api_id}")
async def delete_chat_api(api_id: str, request: Request):
    _require_user(request)
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute(f"DELETE FROM {CHAT_MESSAGES_TABLE} WHERE chat_api_id = %s", (api_id,))
        cur.execute(f"DELETE FROM {CHAT_SESSIONS_TABLE} WHERE chat_api_id = %s", (api_id,))
        cur.execute(f"DELETE FROM {CHAT_APIS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s", (api_id,))
        conn.commit()
        return {"success": True}
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


@app.post("/api/chat-apis/{api_id}/generate-prompt")
async def generate_system_prompt(api_id: str, request: Request, body: dict = Body(...)):
    _require_user(request)
    description = body.get("description", "").strip()
    if not description:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="description is required")
    prompt = f"""You are an expert at writing LLM system prompts.
Write a concise, effective system prompt for a chatbot described as:

\"{description}\"

The chatbot will have access to a knowledge base (RAG context) injected at runtime.
Write ONLY the system prompt text — no preamble, no quotes, no explanation.
Make it specific, clear, and professional."""
    try:
        model = GenerativeModel(GEMINI_MODEL)
        resp  = await asyncio.to_thread(model.generate_content, prompt)
        return {"success": True, "system_prompt": resp.text.strip()}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


# ── Session management ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@app.post("/api/chat-apis/{api_id}/sessions")
async def create_session(api_id: str, request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    api = _load_chat_api(api_id)
    if not api:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Chat API not found")
    session_id = await asyncio.to_thread(_ensure_session, api_id, None, user_id)
    return {"success": True, "session_id": session_id}


@app.get("/api/chat-apis/{api_id}/sessions")
async def list_sessions(api_id: str, request: Request, limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200)):
    _require_user(request)
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cur.execute(f"""
            SELECT s.id, s.title, s.created_by, s.created_at, s.last_active,
                   COUNT(m.id) AS message_count
            FROM {CHAT_SESSIONS_TABLE} s
            LEFT JOIN {CHAT_MESSAGES_TABLE} m ON m.session_id = s.id
            WHERE s.chat_api_id = %s
            GROUP BY s.id ORDER BY s.last_active DESC LIMIT %s
        """, (api_id, limit))
        sessions = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
        return {"success": True, "sessions": sessions}
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


@app.get("/api/chat-apis/{api_id}/sessions/{session_id}/history")
async def get_session_history(api_id: str, session_id: str, request: Request):
    _require_user(request)
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
        cur.execute(f"""
            SELECT id, role, content, sources, token_count, created_at
            FROM {CHAT_MESSAGES_TABLE}
            WHERE session_id = %s AND chat_api_id = %s
            ORDER BY created_at ASC
        """, (session_id, api_id))
        messages = []
        for r in cur.fetchall():
            m = dict(r)
            m["sources"] = json.loads(m.get("sources") or "[]")
            messages.append(m)
        return {"success": True, "messages": messages, "session_id": session_id}
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


@app.delete("/api/chat-apis/{api_id}/sessions/{session_id}")
async def delete_session(api_id: str, session_id: str, request: Request):
    _require_user(request)
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(
            host=DB_HOST, database="postgres",
            user=DB_USERNAME, password=DB_PASSWORD, connect_timeout=10,
        )
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute(f"DELETE FROM {CHAT_MESSAGES_TABLE} WHERE session_id = %s", (session_id,))
        cur.execute(f"DELETE FROM {CHAT_SESSIONS_TABLE} WHERE id = %s AND chat_api_id = %s",
                    (session_id, api_id))
        conn.commit()
        return {"success": True}
    finally:
        if conn: conn.close()


# ── Playground invoke (internal — requires X-User-Id) ─────────────────────────

@app.post("/api/chat-apis/{api_id}/sessions/{session_id}/message")
async def playground_message(api_id: str, session_id: str,
                             body: ChatMessageRequest, request: Request):
    user_id = _require_user(request)
    api = _load_chat_api(api_id)
    if not api:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Chat API not found")
    # Ensure session exists
    await asyncio.to_thread(_ensure_session, api_id, session_id, user_id)
    try:
        result = await _chat_invoke_core(api, session_id, body.message)
        return {"success": True, **result}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


# ── Public external endpoint (Basic Auth if configured) ───────────────────────

@app.post("/api/chat/{api_id}/chat")
async def external_chat(
    api_id: str,
    body: ChatMessageRequest,
    request: Request,
    authorization: Optional[str] = Header(None),
):
    """
    Public chat endpoint for external applications.

    Request:
        POST /api/chat/{api_id}/chat
        Authorization: Basic base64(username:password)   ← only if auth_enabled
        Content-Type: application/json
        {"message": "your question", "session_id": "optional-existing-id"}

    Response:
        {"response": "...", "session_id": "...", "sources": [...], "token_count": N}
    """
    api = _load_chat_api(api_id)
    if not api:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Chat API not found")

    if not _check_chat_auth(api, authorization or ""):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=401,
            detail="Unauthorized",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Basic"},
        )

    caller = request.client.host if request.client else "unknown"
    sid = await asyncio.to_thread(_ensure_session, api_id, body.session_id, caller)

    try:
        result = await _chat_invoke_core(api, sid, body.message)
        await _log_audit_async(
            caller, "chat_invoke",
            database_name=api.get("vector_database", ""),
            details=f"chat_api={api_id} session={sid}",
        )
        return {"success": True, **result}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    scheduler_job_id = os.getenv("SCHEDULER_JOB_ID")
    if scheduler_job_id:
        logger.info(f"Cloud Run Job mode: executing job {scheduler_job_id}")
        try:
            result = _execute_scheduler_job(scheduler_job_id)
            status = result.get("status", "unknown")
            logger.info(f"Job {scheduler_job_id} completed with status: {status}")
            if status == "failed":
                sys.exit(1)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Job {scheduler_job_id} failed: {e}")
            sys.exit(1)
    else:
        port = int(os.getenv("PORT", 8080))
        uvicorn.run("main:app", host="0.0.0.0", port=port, reload=False, log_level="info")


README.md

# SQL Editor API

A production-grade FastAPI backend for the SQL Editor platform. Supports multi-database query execution (CloudSQL/PostgreSQL, Google BigQuery, Microsoft SQL Server), a no-code API Builder with complex JOIN support, a Scheduler engine for automated data pipelines, inline CRUD operations, response caching, Basic Auth, Fernet encryption, and Gemini AI integration.

---

## Tech Stack

| Component | Technology |
|-----------|-----------|
| Framework | FastAPI 0.104 + Uvicorn |
| Databases | CloudSQL (PostgreSQL via psycopg2), BigQuery (google-cloud-bigquery), MSSQL (pymssql) |
| AI | Vertex AI (Gemini 2.5 Flash) |
| Cloud Storage | Google Cloud Storage |
| Encryption | Fernet (cryptography) |
| Validation | Pydantic v2 |

---

## Installation

```bash
# 1. Clone and install dependencies
pip install -r requirements.txt

# 2. For Microsoft SQL Server support, install FreeTDS (macOS/Linux):
brew install freetds        # macOS
apt-get install freetds-dev # Ubuntu/Debian
```

---

## Configuration

Edit the following variables at the top of `main.py`:

```python
# Database (default CloudSQL PostgreSQL for metadata storage)
DB_HOST     = "100.100.0.239"
DB_USERNAME = "datalake"
DB_PASSWORD = "datalake"

# Super-admin VZID (bypasses user-scoping on all endpoints)
SUPER_ADMIN_VZID = "anatan8"

# Gemini AI (Vertex AI)
GEMINI_MODEL          = "gemini-2.5-flash"
GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT  = "vz-it-pr-voev-voevdo-0"
GOOGLE_CLOUD_LOCATION = "us-east4"

# Scheduler email (SMTP relay, no authentication required)
SCHEDULER_SMTP_SERVER = "tpaapd1kva109.verizon.com"
SCHEDULER_FROM_EMAIL  = "do-not-reply@verizon.com"
```

> **Authentication**: All endpoints read the caller identity from the `X-User-Id` HTTP header (VZID).

## Running the Server

```bash
# Development
python main.py            # starts on port 8080

# Production
uvicorn main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8080 --workers 4

# Docker
docker build -t sqleditorapi .
docker run -p 8080:8080 sqleditorapi
```

Interactive docs available at:
- Swagger UI: `http://localhost:8080/docs`
- ReDoc:       `http://localhost:8080/redoc`

## API Reference

### Health & Root

| Method | Path | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `GET` | `/` | API info + endpoint list |
| `GET` | `/health` | Health check |

### Database & Schema Exploration

| Method | Path | Query Params | Description |
|--------|------|--------------|-------------|
| `GET` | `/api/databases` | `type` (cloudsql\|bigquery\|mssql), `search` | List all databases |
| `GET` | `/api/tables` | `database`, `type`, `search` | List tables in a database |
| `GET` | `/api/table/schema` | `database`, `table_name`, `type`, `schema` | Get column metadata |
| `GET` | `/api/table/primary-key` | `database`, `table_name`, `type`, `schema` | Get primary key columns |
| `POST` | `/api/databases/create` | — | Create database/BigQuery dataset |

**Example:**
```bash
curl "https://sqleditorapi.verizon.com/api/tables?database=my_db&type=cloudsql" \
  -H "X-User-Id: anatan8"
```

### SQL Query Execution

| Method | Path | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `POST` | `/api/query` | Execute query, return JSON |
| `POST` | `/api/query/csv` | Execute query, download CSV |
| `POST` | `/api/query/json` | Execute query, download JSON file |
| `POST` | `/api/query/clipboard` | Execute query, return formatted string |
| `POST` | `/api/server/{server_id}/query` | Execute query on a custom server |

**Request body:**
```json
{
  "database": "my_database",
  "query": "SELECT * FROM orders WHERE status = 'open' LIMIT 100",
  "limit": 500
}
```

**Response:**
```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": [{"order_id": 1, "status": "open"}],
  "columns": ["order_id", "status"],
  "row_count": 1,
  "execution_time_ms": 42.5,
  "query": "SELECT ...",
  "database": "my_database"
}
```

### CRUD Operations

Direct INSERT / UPDATE / DELETE on any table without writing SQL.

| Method | Path | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `POST` | `/api/row` | Insert one or more rows |
| `PUT` | `/api/row` | Update a row by primary key |
| `DELETE` | `/api/rows` | Delete rows by primary key values |

**Insert rows:**
```bash
curl -X POST "https://sqleditorapi.verizon.com/api/row?type=cloudsql" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "X-User-Id: anatan8" \
  -d '{
    "database": "my_db",
    "table": "orders",
    "schema_name": "public",
    "rows": [
      {"customer_id": 42, "product": "Widget", "quantity": 5},
      {"customer_id": 43, "product": "Gadget", "quantity": 2}
    ]
  }'
```

**Update row:**
```json
{
  "database": "my_db",
  "table": "orders",
  "schema_name": "public",
  "primary_key": {"order_id": 101},
  "updates": {"status": "shipped", "shipped_at": "2026-03-31"}
}
```

**Delete rows:**
```json
{
  "database": "my_db",
  "table": "orders",
  "schema_name": "public",
  "primary_key_column": "order_id",
  "primary_key_values": [101, 102, 103]
}
```

---

### Dynamic CRUD Endpoints

RESTful endpoints auto-generated for any table. Supports filtering, sorting, pagination, and column projection.

| Method | Path | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `GET` | `/api/crud/{database}/{table}` | Read rows with filtering & pagination |
| `POST` | `/api/crud/{database}/{table}` | Insert rows (JSON array body) |
| `PUT` | `/api/crud/{database}/{table}` | Update rows |
| `DELETE` | `/api/crud/{database}/{table}` | Delete rows by PK |

**Read with filters:**
```bash
# JSON filters: [{col, op, val}]
# ops: eq, neq, gt, lt, like, in, is_null, is_not_null
curl "https://sqleditorapi.verizon.com/api/crud/my_db/orders?type=cloudsql&limit=50&sort_by=created_at&sort_order=desc&filters=[{\"col\":\"status\",\"op\":\"eq\",\"val\":\"open\"}]"
```

---

### API Builder

Create persistent, named REST APIs over your database tables — with JOINs, WHERE conditions, function layers, caching, and auth.

#### Custom API CRUD

| Method | Path | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `GET` | `/api/builder/apis` | List all saved APIs |
| `POST` | `/api/builder/apis` | Create a new API |
| `GET` | `/api/builder/apis/{api_id}` | Get API config |
| `PUT` | `/api/builder/apis/{api_id}` | Update API config |
| `DELETE` | `/api/builder/apis/{api_id}` | Delete API |
| `POST` | `/api/builder/apis/preview` | Preview query without saving |

#### Execute a Custom API

| Method | Path | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `GET` | `/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute` | Read (SELECT) with optional filters |
| `POST` | `/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute` | Insert rows |
| `PUT` | `/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute` | Update rows |
| `DELETE` | `/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/execute` | Delete rows |
| `POST` | `/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/search` | POST-based search (body filters) |
| `GET` | `/api/data/{alias}` | Execute API by public alias |
| `GET` | `/api/builder/apis/{api_id}/docs` | Auto-generated API documentation |

**GET execute query params:**

| Param | Type | Description |
|-------|------|-------------|
| `limit` | int | Max rows (default: API's `default_limit`) |
| `offset` | int | Pagination offset |
| `sort_by` | string | Column to sort by |
| `sort_order` | string | `asc` or `desc` |
| `filters` | JSON | `[{"col":"status","op":"eq","val":"open"}]` |
| `{column}` | string | Simple equality filter: `?status=open` |

**Create API config example:**
```json
{
  "name": "open-incidents",
  "description": "All open IT incidents with assignee info",
  "database": "vea_dataset",
  "db_type": "bigquery",
  "base_table": "vea_sn_incident",
  "base_table_alias": "inc",
  "schema_name": "vea_dataset",
  "select_columns": ["inc.number", "inc.short_description", "inc.state", "assignee.name"],
  "default_limit": 500,
  "allowed_filters": ["state", "priority", "assigned_to"],
  "relationships": [
    {
      "name": "assignee",
      "source_table": "vea_sn_incident",
      "source_column": "assigned_to",
      "target_database": "vea_dataset",
      "target_table": "vea_sn_sys_user_all_cols",
      "target_column": "sys_id",
      "join_type": "LEFT",
      "target_db_type": "bigquery",
      "alias": "assignee"
    }
  ],
  "where_conditions": [
    {"column": "state", "operator": "!=", "value": "closed"}
  ],
  "cache_ttl": 60,
  "enable_crud": false
}
```

#### Relationships (Standalone)

| Method | Path | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `GET` | `/api/builder/relationships` | List all relationships |
| `POST` | `/api/builder/relationships` | Create a relationship |
| `DELETE` | `/api/builder/relationships/{rel_id}` | Delete a relationship |
| `POST` | `/api/builder/relationships/{rel_id}/query` | Execute a relationship query |

---

### Scheduler Engine

Build and run automated data pipeline jobs with multiple step types, dependencies, and cron/interval scheduling.

#### Job Management

| Method | Path | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `GET` | `/api/scheduler/jobs` | List scheduler jobs (scoped to user) |
| `POST` | `/api/scheduler/jobs` | Create a new job |
| `GET` | `/api/scheduler/jobs/{job_id}` | Get job config |
| `PUT` | `/api/scheduler/jobs/{job_id}` | Update job |
| `DELETE` | `/api/scheduler/jobs/{job_id}` | Delete job + run history |
| `POST` | `/api/scheduler/jobs/{job_id}/run` | Manually trigger job (async) |
| `POST` | `/api/scheduler/jobs/{job_id}/toggle` | Enable / disable job |
| `GET` | `/api/scheduler/jobs/{job_id}/history` | Get run history |
| `POST` | `/api/scheduler/tick` | Trigger scheduler check (for Cloud Scheduler integration) |
| `GET` | `/api/scheduler/servers` | Available servers for step config |
| `GET` | `/api/scheduler/buckets` | Available GCS buckets |

**Create job example:**
```json
{
  "name": "Daily Incident Sync",
  "description": "Sync ServiceNow incidents into BigQuery every night",
  "enabled": true,
  "schedule_type": "cron",
  "schedule_config": {
    "hour": 2,
    "minute": 30,
    "days_of_week": [0, 1, 2, 3, 4]
  },
  "steps": [
    {
      "name": "fetch_incidents",
      "type": "api_call_upsert",
      "order": 0,
      "enabled": true,
      "config": {
        "url": "https://api.example.com/incidents?date={{$yesterday:YYYY-MM-DD}}",
        "method": "GET",
        "auth_type": "bearer",
        "auth_config": {"token": "my-token"},
        "response_data_path": "data",
        "db_type": "cloudsql",
        "database": "incidents_db",
        "table": "sn_incidents",
        "schema_name": "public",
        "operation": "upsert",
        "pk_column": "sys_id",
        "mapping": [
          {"source_field": "sys_id", "target_column": "sys_id"},
          {"source_field": "number", "target_column": "incident_number"},
          {"source_field": "short_description", "target_column": "description"}
        ]
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

**Schedule types:**

| `schedule_type` | `schedule_config` keys | Description |
|-----------------|------------------------|-------------|
| `manual` | _(none)_ | Triggered by API call only |
| `interval` | `interval_minutes` | Run every N minutes |
| `cron` | `hour`, `minute`, `days_of_week` | Run at a specific time |

> `days_of_week` accepts integers (0=Mon, 6=Sun) or strings (`"mon"`, `"fri"`, etc.)
