## API Builder — Deep Dive

### Relationships & Complex JOINs

The API Builder supports up to **6 table JOINs** including function-based ON clauses and chained joins.

**Key fields in each relationship:**

| Field | Description |
|-------|-------------|
| `source_table` | Left-side table for the ON clause. Defaults to `base_table`. **Set this for chained JOINs** where the source is a previously joined table (e.g., B→C when base is A). |
| `source_column` | Column on the source/left side |
| `target_table` | Table being joined in |
| `target_column` | Column on the target/right side |
| `join_type` | `INNER`, `LEFT`, `RIGHT`, `FULL` |
| `alias` | Table alias (required when the same table appears multiple times) |
| `raw_on_clause` | Overrides `source_column`/`target_column` with a raw SQL expression, e.g. `RIGHT(req.cmdb_ci_link, 32) = appl.sys_id` |

**3-table chained JOIN example** (Incidents → User → Department):
```json
"relationships": [
  {
    "source_table": "incidents",
    "source_column": "assigned_to",
    "target_table": "sys_user",
    "target_column": "sys_id",
    "join_type": "LEFT",
    "alias": "assignee"
  },
  {
    "source_table": "sys_user",
    "source_column": "department",
    "target_table": "sys_department",
    "target_column": "sys_id",
    "join_type": "LEFT",
    "alias": "dept"
  }
]
```

**Function-based JOIN (raw_on_clause):**
```json
{
  "target_table": "cmdb_application",
  "join_type": "LEFT",
  "alias": "appl",
  "raw_on_clause": "RIGHT(req.cmdb_ci_link, 32) = appl.sys_id"
}
```

---

### Function Layers

Applied to query results after execution:

| Type | Config | Description |
|------|--------|-------------|
| `filter` | `{column, operator, value}` | Remove rows that don't match |
| `transform` | `{column, action}` | `lowercase`, `uppercase`, `trim`, `mask`, `hash` |
| `aggregate` | `{group_by, agg_column, agg_func}` | `sum`, `avg`, `count`, `min`, `max` |

---

### Authentication & Encryption

**Basic Auth on an API:**
```json
{
  "auth_enabled": true,
  "auth_username": "apiuser",
  "auth_password": "secret123"
}
```

Callers must include `Authorization: Basic <base64(user:pass)>` header.

**Fernet payload encryption:**
```json
{
  "encryption_enabled": true,
  "encryption_key": "your-fernet-key-base64"
}
```

GET `/api/builder/generate-key` to create a new key. With encryption enabled, all request and response payloads are Fernet-encrypted.

---

### Caching

Set `cache_ttl` (0–3600 seconds) on any custom API. Responses are cached in-memory; the same request within the TTL window returns the cached response with `"_cached": true`.

---

## Scheduler Engine — Deep Dive

### Step Types

| Type | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `api_call` | HTTP request with templated URL, headers, payload, auth |
| `api_call_upsert` | Calls API + upserts response rows into a DB table (most common ETL pattern) |
| `db_transfer` | Copies table data between any two databases (chunked, supports pagination) |
| `db_mapping` | Maps data from a previous step's output into a DB table |
| `email_trigger` | Sends email via internal SMTP relay (supports HTML bodies) |
| `gcs_operation` | `list`, `upload`, `delete`, `move` objects in a GCS bucket |
| `truncate_table` | Deletes all rows from a table (requires `confirm: true`) |
| `file_import` | Imports CSV or SQL file from GCS, URL, or inline content |
| `conditional` | Evaluates a condition — `then_action` / `else_action`: `continue`, `skip`, `fail` |

### Dynamic Variables

Use `{{$variable}}` placeholders in URLs, payloads, and email bodies:

| Variable | Output |
|----------|--------|
| `{{$now}}` | `2026-03-31 14:30:00` |
| `{{$today}}` | `2026-03-31 00:00:00` |
| `{{$yesterday}}` | `2026-03-30 00:00:00` |
| `{{$tomorrow}}` | `2026-04-01 00:00:00` |
| `{{$week_start}}` | Monday of current week |
| `{{$month_start}}` | First day of current month |
| `{{$now-1d:YYYY-MM-DD}}` | Yesterday as `2026-03-30` |
| `{{$now-1d:epoch}}` | Unix timestamp of yesterday |
| `{{$now+2h:iso}}` | ISO-8601 timestamp 2 hours from now |
| `{{steps.step_name.response.key}}` | Output from a previous step |

**Offset units:** `d` (days), `h` (hours), `m` (minutes), `w` (weeks), `M` (months), `y` (years)

### Job Dependencies

A job can list other job IDs in `dependencies`. The job will only run if **all** dependency jobs have `last_run_status == "success"`. Failed dependencies produce a `"failed"` run log with a clear explanation.

```json
{
  "name": "Load Derived Table",
  "dependencies": ["abc123", "def456"],
  "steps": [...]
}
```

---

## Deployment

### Local Development

```bash
python main.py
# API at http://localhost:8080
# Docs at http://localhost:8080/docs
```

### Production (Uvicorn + Gunicorn)

```bash
pip install gunicorn
gunicorn -w 4 -k uvicorn.workers.UvicornWorker main:app --bind 0.0.0.0:8080 --timeout 300
```

### Docker

```bash
docker build -t sqleditorapi .
docker run -p 8080:8080 sqleditorapi
```

The included `Dockerfile` uses `python:3.10-slim`, installs FreeTDS for MSSQL support, and runs on port 8080.

### Cloud Scheduler Integration

For cron jobs in production, use Google Cloud Scheduler to `POST /api/scheduler/tick` every minute. This endpoint reads `last_run_at` from the DB and is restart-safe (unlike the background thread which seeds from the DB on startup).

```
Target URL:  https://sqleditorapi.verizon.com/api/scheduler/tick
Method:      POST
Frequency:   * * * * *
```

---

## Error Responses

All endpoints return consistent error objects:

```json
{
  "detail": "Descriptive error message"
}
```

| Status | Meaning |
|--------|---------|
| `400` | Bad request — invalid query, missing params |
| `403` | Forbidden — CRUD disabled, auth failed |
| `404` | Not found — API, job, or resource doesn't exist |
| `500` | Internal server error |
| `503` | Database connection failed |

---

## Security Notes

- `X-User-Id` is trusted as-is — ensure the header is set by a trusted upstream proxy in production.
- Super-admin VZID (`anatan8`) bypasses all user-scoping; configure this appropriately.
- Dangerous SQL keywords (`DROP`, `TRUNCATE`, `ALTER`, etc.) are logged but not blocked by default — uncomment the raise in `validate_query` to enforce.
- `DELETE` via the general `/api/query` endpoint is blocked (HTTP 403); use `/api/rows` or the API Builder DELETE endpoint.
